# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST L2 — SELF-CONTAINED CUBIC-DEFECT-CANCELING M3D-3*
#
# PURPOSE
# -------
# Fresh, reproducible comparison:
#
#       adaptive M3D-2
#               vs
#       adaptive M3D-3*
#
# on the SAME deterministic flows, viscosities, truth trajectories,
# reanchor interval, projector, and integrator.
#
# NO dependence on:
#   topology_pair_K
#   RESULTS_K
#   old /content CSV files
#
# ALL RESULTS SAVE PERMANENTLY TO:
#   SAVE_DIR = /content/drive/MyDrive/M3D
#
#
# SCIENTIFIC CHANGE:
#
#     f  = F(U0)
#     A  = DF(U0)
#     Pi2 = P + q0 q0* + q1 q1*
#     R2  = I - Pi2
#
#     r2  = R2 A f
#     q2* = r2 / ||r2||
#
# Then:
#
#     Pi3 = Pi2 + q2 q2*
#     R3  = I - Pi3
#
# so ideally:
#
#     R3 A f = 0
#
# and therefore:
#
#     C3^(3) = P A R3 A f ~= 0.
#
#
# PREDECLARED IMPLEMENTATION GATES
# --------------------------------
# R3/R2 < 1e-4
# C3(rank3)/C3(rank2) < 1e-2 when rank-2 C3 is resolvable
#
#
# PREDECLARED SCIENTIFIC GATE
# ---------------------------
# ALL 12 cases at tau ~= 2:
#
#   M3D-3* target-state error < 1%
#   M3D-3* target-RHS error   < 10%
#
# Do NOT tune based on results.
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def finalize_velocity_L2(
    u,
    env,
    target_umax=0.18,
):
    U = torch.fft.fftn(
        u,
        dim=(-3, -2, -1),
    ).to(
        COMPLEX_DTYPE_D
    )

    U = dealias_D(
        project_divfree_D(
            U,
            env,
        ),
        env,
    )

    up = torch.fft.ifftn(
        U,
        dim=(-3, -2, -1),
    ).real

    speed = torch.sqrt(
        torch.sum(
            up * up,
            dim=0,
        )
    )

    umax = torch.max(
        speed
    )

    U = (
        U
        *
        (
            float(target_umax)
            /
            torch.clamp(
                umax,
                min=1e-12,
            )
        )
    )

    return U


@torch.no_grad()
def grid_L2(
    env,
):
    N = env["N"]
    L = env["L"]
    dx = env["dx"]

    coord = (
        torch.arange(
            N,
            device=DEVICE_D,
            dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
        )
        -
        N / 2
    ) * dx

    return torch.meshgrid(
        coord,
        coord,
        coord,
        indexing="ij",
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def vortex_ring_single_L2(
    env,
    variant,
):
    X, Y, Z = grid_L2(
        env
    )

    N = env["N"]
    L = env["L"]

    phase = (
        1.0
        if variant == 0
        else -1.0
    )

    x0 = (
        -0.07 * L
        if variant == 0
        else 0.09 * L
    )

    y0 = (
        0.06 * L
        if variant == 0
        else -0.08 * L
    )

    z0 = (
        -0.035 * L
        if variant == 0
        else 0.045 * L
    )

    dxp = _periodic_delta_D(
        X,
        x0,
        L,
    )

    dyp = _periodic_delta_D(
        Y,
        y0,
        L,
    )

    dzp = _periodic_delta_D(
        Z,
        z0,
        L,
    )

    rho = torch.sqrt(
        dxp * dxp
        +
        dyp * dyp
        +
        1e-10
    )

    R0 = 0.20 * L
    sigma = 0.070 * L

    ring = torch.exp(
        -(
            (rho - R0) ** 2
            +
            dzp ** 2
        )
        /
        (
            2.0
            *
            sigma ** 2
        )
    )

    u = torch.zeros(
        (
            3,
            N,
            N,
            N,
        ),
        device=DEVICE_D,
        dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
    )

    # Azimuthal ring circulation.
    u[0] = (
        phase
        *
        (
            -dyp
            /
            rho
        )
        *
        ring
    )

    u[1] = (
        phase
        *
        (
            dxp
            /
            rho
        )
        *
        ring
    )

    # Weak 3-D ring deformation.
    u[2] = (
        0.24
        *
        ring
        *
        torch.sin(
            2.0
            *
            math.pi
            *
            (
                X
                +
                0.6 * Y
            )
            /
            L
            +
            0.7 * variant
        )
    )

    return finalize_velocity_L2(
        u,
        env,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def periodic_shear_single_L2(
    env,
    variant,
):
    X, Y, Z = grid_L2(
        env
    )

    N = env["N"]
    L = env["L"]

    p = (
        0.37
        if variant == 0
        else 1.11
    )

    u = torch.zeros(
        (
            3,
            N,
            N,
            N,
        ),
        device=DEVICE_D,
        dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
    )

    # Each dominant velocity component varies mostly perpendicular
    # to itself; Leray projection removes remaining divergence exactly.
    u[0] = (
        1.00
        *
        torch.sin(
            2 * math.pi * Y / L
            +
            p
        )
        +
        0.32
        *
        torch.sin(
            4 * math.pi * Z / L
            -
            0.4 * p
        )
    )

    u[1] = (
        -0.84
        *
        torch.sin(
            2 * math.pi * Z / L
            -
            0.6 * p
        )
        +
        0.27
        *
        torch.cos(
            4 * math.pi * X / L
            +
            p
        )
    )

    u[2] = (
        0.71
        *
        torch.sin(
            2 * math.pi * X / L
            +
            0.8 * p
        )
        +
        0.23
        *
        torch.cos(
            2 * math.pi
            *
            (
                X + Y
            )
            /
            L
        )
    )

    return finalize_velocity_L2(
        u,
        env,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def skew_tubes_single_L2(
    env,
    variant,
):
    X, Y, Z = grid_L2(
        env
    )

    N = env["N"]
    L = env["L"]

    u = torch.zeros(
        (
            3,
            N,
            N,
            N,
        ),
        device=DEVICE_D,
        dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
    )

    if variant == 0:
        centers = [
            (
                -0.12 * L,
                0.08 * L,
                0.10 * L,
                +1.0,
            ),
            (
                0.13 * L,
                -0.09 * L,
                0.085 * L,
                -0.82,
            ),
        ]
    else:
        centers = [
            (
                0.11 * L,
                0.10 * L,
                0.095 * L,
                -0.93,
            ),
            (
                -0.14 * L,
                -0.07 * L,
                0.080 * L,
                +0.74,
            ),
        ]

    for j, (
        a,
        b,
        sigma,
        strength,
    ) in enumerate(
        centers
    ):

        # Rotated coordinates make the tubes oblique rather than
        # axis-aligned.
        theta = (
            0.43
            +
            0.51 * j
            +
            0.17 * variant
        )

        c = math.cos(
            theta
        )

        s = math.sin(
            theta
        )

        xp = (
            c * X
            +
            s * Z
        )

        zp = (
            -s * X
            +
            c * Z
        )

        da = _periodic_delta_D(
            xp,
            a,
            L,
        )

        db = _periodic_delta_D(
            Y,
            b,
            L,
        )

        r2 = (
            da * da
            +
            db * db
        )

        g = (
            strength
            *
            torch.exp(
                -0.5
                *
                r2
                /
                (
                    sigma
                    *
                    sigma
                )
            )
        )

        # Local tube swirl in rotated coordinates.
        ux_local = (
            -db
            /
            sigma
            *
            g
        )

        uy_local = (
            da
            /
            sigma
            *
            g
        )

        u[0] += (
            c
            *
            ux_local
        )

        u[2] += (
            s
            *
            ux_local
        )

        u[1] += (
            uy_local
        )

        u[2] += (
            0.11
            *
            g
            *
            torch.sin(
                2
                *
                math.pi
                *
                zp
                /
                L
                +
                0.6 * j
            )
        )

    # Weak interaction field.
    u[0] += (
        0.08
        *
        torch.sin(
            2
            *
            math.pi
            *
            (
                X + Y + Z
            )
            /
            L
        )
    )

    return finalize_velocity_L2(
        u,
        env,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def topology_pair_L2(
    topology,
    env,
):
    if topology == "vortex_ring":
        return (
            vortex_ring_single_L2(
                env,
                0,
            ),
            vortex_ring_single_L2(
                env,
                1,
            ),
        )

    if topology == "periodic_shear":
        return (
            periodic_shear_single_L2(
                env,
                0,
            ),
            periodic_shear_single_L2(
                env,
                1,
            ),
        )

    if topology == "skew_tubes":
        return (
            skew_tubes_single_L2(
                env,
                0,
            ),
            skew_tubes_single_L2(
                env,
                1,
            ),
        )

    if topology == "mixed_vortices":
        # This one uses the preserved deterministic baseline generator.
        return initial_flow_D(
            env
        )

    raise ValueError(
        f"Unknown topology: {topology}"
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. GENERATOR PREFLIGHT
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def divergence_ratio_L2(
    U,
    env,
):
    div = (
        env["KX"] * U[0]
        +
        env["KY"] * U[1]
        +
        env["KZ"] * U[2]
    )

    return float(
        (
            torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                div
            )
            /
            (
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                    U
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


print()
print("=" * 132)
print("GENERATOR PREFLIGHT")
print("=" * 132)

generator_rows_L2 = []

for topology in TOPOLOGIES_L2:

    U1, U2 = topology_pair_L2(
        topology,
        ENV_L2,
    )

    proj = make_projector_D(
        PROJECTOR_L2,
        ENV_L2,
    )

    target = pack_modes_D(
        U1,
        U2,
        proj["target_ids"],
    )

    full_norm = float(
        pair_norm_D(
            U1,
            U2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    target_norm = float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            target
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    frac = (
        target_norm
        /
        max(
            full_norm,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    d1 = divergence_ratio_L2(
        U1,
        ENV_L2,
    )

    d2 = divergence_ratio_L2(
        U2,
        ENV_L2,
    )

    print(
        f"{topology:16s}"
        f" | pair norm={full_norm:12.4e}"
        f" | target frac={100*frac:8.3f}%"
        f" | div1={d1:.2e}"
        f" | div2={d2:.2e}"
    )

    generator_rows_L2.append(
        {
            "topology": topology,
            "pair_norm": full_norm,
            "target_fraction": frac,
            "div1": d1,
            "div2": d2,
        }
    )

GENERATOR_L2 = pd.DataFrame(
    generator_rows_L2
)

GENERATOR_L2.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_L2_generator_preflight.csv",
    index=False,
)

del U1, U2
torch.cuda.empty_cache()


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. CENTERED JVP
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def pair_jvp_L2(
    U1,
    U2,
    V1,
    V2,
    env,
):
    vn = pair_norm_D(
        V1,
        V2,
    )

    vn_float = float(
        vn.detach().cpu()
    )

    if (
        not np.isfinite(
            vn_float
        )
        or
        vn_float < 1e-13
    ):
        return (
            torch.zeros_like(
                U1
            ),
            torch.zeros_like(
                U2
            ),
        )

    v1 = (
        V1
        /
        vn
    )

    v2 = (
        V2
        /
        vn
    )

    un = pair_norm_D(
        U1,
        U2,
    )

    eps = max(
        1e-5,
        1e-3
        *
        float(
            un.detach().cpu()
        ),
    )

    Fp1, Fp2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1 + eps * v1,
        U2 + eps * v2,
        env,
    )

    Fm1, Fm2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1 - eps * v1,
        U2 - eps * v2,
        env,
    )

    J1 = (
        Fp1 - Fm1
    ) / (
        2.0 * eps
    )

    J2 = (
        Fp2 - Fm2
    ) / (
        2.0 * eps
    )

    return (
        vn * J1,
        vn * J2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. RANK-2 COMPLEMENT
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def rank2_residual_L2(
    A1,
    A2,
    model2,
):
    tids = model2[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    R1, R2 = zero_target_modes_D(
        A1,
        A2,
        tids,
    )

    a0 = pair_inner_D(
        model2["q0_U1"],
        model2["q0_U2"],
        R1,
        R2,
    )

    R1 = (
        R1
        -
        a0
        *
        model2["q0_U1"]
    )

    R2 = (
        R2
        -
        a0
        *
        model2["q0_U2"]
    )

    a1 = pair_inner_D(
        model2["q1_U1"],
        model2["q1_U2"],
        R1,
        R2,
    )

    R1 = (
        R1
        -
        a1
        *
        model2["q1_U1"]
    )

    R2 = (
        R2
        -
        a1
        *
        model2["q1_U2"]
    )

    R1, R2 = zero_target_modes_D(
        R1,
        R2,
        tids,
    )

    return (
        R1,
        R2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. BUILD M3D-3*
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def initialize_model3_L2(
    U10,
    U20,
    projector_name,
    env,
):
    # Exact same q0/q1 construction as M3D-2.
    model = initialize_model_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        projector_name,
        env,
    )

    tids = model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    dids = model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "dep_ids"
    ]

    # f = F(U0)
    f1, f2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        env,
    )

    # Af = DF(U0)F(U0)
    Af1, Af2 = pair_jvp_L2(
        U10,
        U20,
        f1,
        f2,
        env,
    )

    # r2 = R2 Af
    r21, r22 = rank2_residual_L2(
        Af1,
        Af2,
        model,
    )

    r2_norm = pair_norm_D(
        r21,
        r22,
    )

    Af_norm = pair_norm_D(
        Af1,
        Af2,
    )

    r2_float = float(
        r2_norm.detach().cpu()
    )

    Af_float = float(
        Af_norm.detach().cpu()
    )

    q2_active = bool(
        np.isfinite(
            r2_float
        )
        and
        r2_float
        >
        max(
            1e-11,
            1e-8
            *
            max(
                Af_float,
                1e-30,
            ),
        )
    )

    if q2_active:

        q21 = (
            r21
            /
            r2_norm
        )

        q22 = (
            r22
            /
            r2_norm
        )

        q21, q22 = zero_target_modes_D(
            q21,
            q22,
            tids,
        )

        b0 = pair_inner_D(
            model["q0_U1"],
            model["q0_U2"],
            q21,
            q22,
        )

        q21 = (
            q21
            -
            b0
            *
            model["q0_U1"]
        )

        q22 = (
            q22
            -
            b0
            *
            model["q0_U2"]
        )

        b1 = pair_inner_D(
            model["q1_U1"],
            model["q1_U2"],
            q21,
            q22,
        )

        q21 = (
            q21
            -
            b1
            *
            model["q1_U1"]
        )

        q22 = (
            q22
            -
            b1
            *
            model["q1_U2"]
        )

        q21, q22 = zero_target_modes_D(
            q21,
            q22,
            tids,
        )

        nq2 = pair_norm_D(
            q21,
            q22,
        )

        if float(
            nq2.detach().cpu()
        ) < 1e-12:
            q2_active = False

            q21 = torch.zeros_like(
                model["q0_U1"]
            )

            q22 = torch.zeros_like(
                model["q0_U2"]
            )

        else:
            q21 = (
                q21
                /
                nq2
            )

            q22 = (
                q22
                /
                nq2
            )

    else:

        q21 = torch.zeros_like(
            model["q0_U1"]
        )

        q22 = torch.zeros_like(
            model["q0_U2"]
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Verify R3 Af.
    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------

    if q2_active:

        gamma = pair_inner_D(
            q21,
            q22,
            r21,
            r22,
        )

        r31 = (
            r21
            -
            gamma
            *
            q21
        )

        r32 = (
            r22
            -
            gamma
            *
            q22
        )

    else:

        r31 = r21.clone()
        r32 = r22.clone()

    r3_norm = pair_norm_D(
        r31,
        r32,
    )

    if r2_float > 1e-30:

        r3_ratio = float(
            (
                r3_norm
                /
                r2_norm
            ).detach().cpu()
        )

    else:

        r3_ratio = 0.0

    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # C3 rank-2 and rank-3
    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Ar21, Ar22 = pair_jvp_L2(
        U10,
        U20,
        r21,
        r22,
        env,
    )

    Ar31, Ar32 = pair_jvp_L2(
        U10,
        U20,
        r31,
        r32,
        env,
    )

    C32 = pack_modes_D(
        Ar21,
        Ar22,
        tids,
    )

    C33 = pack_modes_D(
        Ar31,
        Ar32,
        tids,
    )

    C32_norm = float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            C32
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    C33_norm = float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            C33
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    if C32_norm > 1e-12:

        cubic_ratio = (
            C33_norm
            /
            C32_norm
        )

    else:

        cubic_ratio = np.nan

    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Gram check
    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------

    qs = [
        (
            model["q0_U1"],
            model["q0_U2"],
        ),
        (
            model["q1_U1"],
            model["q1_U2"],
        ),
    ]

    if q2_active:

        qs.append(
            (
                q21,
                q22,
            )
        )

    gram = torch.zeros(
        (
            len(qs),
            len(qs),
        ),
        device=DEVICE_D,
        dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
    )

    for i in range(
        len(qs)
    ):

        for j in range(
            len(qs)
        ):

            gram[
                i,
                j,
            ] = pair_inner_D(
                qs[i][0],
                qs[i][1],
                qs[j][0],
                qs[j][1],
            )

    gram_error3 = float(
        torch.max(
            torch.abs(
                gram
                -
                torch.eye(
                    len(qs),
                    device=DEVICE_D,
                    dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
                )
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Extend baseline model
    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------

    model["name"] = (
        f"M3D-3star-{projector_name}"
    )

    model["q2_U1"] = q21
    model["q2_U2"] = q22

    model["q2_active"] = q2_active

    model[
        "effective_hidden_rank"
    ] = (
        3
        if q2_active
        else 2
    )

    model["z"] = torch.zeros(
        3,
        device=DEVICE_D,
        dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
    )

    q0_dep = pack_modes_D(
        model["q0_U1"],
        model["q0_U2"],
        dids,
    )

    q1_dep = pack_modes_D(
        model["q1_U1"],
        model["q1_U2"],
        dids,
    )

    q2_dep = pack_modes_D(
        q21,
        q22,
        dids,
    )

    model["Qr3"] = torch.stack(
        (
            q0_dep,
            q1_dep,
            q2_dep,
        ),
        dim=1,
    )

    model["r2_norm"] = r2_float
    model["r3_ratio"] = r3_ratio

    model[
        "c3_rank2_norm"
    ] = C32_norm

    model[
        "c3_rank3_norm"
    ] = C33_norm

    model[
        "cubic_ratio"
    ] = cubic_ratio

    model[
        "gram_error3"
    ] = gram_error3

    model[
        "cubic_cancel_ok"
    ] = bool(
        (
            not q2_active
        )
        or
        (
            r3_ratio
            <
            R3_RATIO_GATE_L2
            and
            (
                not np.isfinite(
                    cubic_ratio
                )
                or
                cubic_ratio
                <
                CUBIC_RATIO_GATE_L2
            )
        )
    )

    return model


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. M3D-3* DYNAMICS
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def reconstruct_m3d3_L2(
    x,
    z,
    U10,
    U20,
    model,
    env,
):
    tids = model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    x_phys = (
        x
        *
        model["x_scale"]
    )

    U1, U2 = unpack_modes_D(
        x_phys,
        U10,
        U20,
        tids,
    )

    hs = model[
        "hidden_scale"
    ]

    U1 = (
        U1
        +
        hs
        *
        (
            z[0] * model["q0_U1"]
            +
            z[1] * model["q1_U1"]
            +
            z[2] * model["q2_U1"]
        )
    )

    U2 = (
        U2
        +
        hs
        *
        (
            z[0] * model["q0_U2"]
            +
            z[1] * model["q1_U2"]
            +
            z[2] * model["q2_U2"]
        )
    )

    U1 = dealias_D(
        project_divfree_D(
            U1,
            env,
        ),
        env,
    )

    U2 = dealias_D(
        project_divfree_D(
            U2,
            env,
        ),
        env,
    )

    return U1, U2


@torch.no_grad()
def m3d3_rhs_L2(
    x,
    z,
    U10,
    U20,
    model,
    env,
):
    U1, U2 = reconstruct_m3d3_L2(
        x,
        z,
        U10,
        U20,
        model,
        env,
    )

    F1, F2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1,
        U2,
        env,
    )

    tids = model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    dx_phys_dt = pack_modes_D(
        F1,
        F2,
        tids,
    )

    dx_dtau = (
        TAU0_D
        *
        dx_phys_dt
        /
        model["x_scale"]
    )

    hs = model[
        "hidden_scale"
    ]

    dz0 = (
        pair_inner_D(
            model["q0_U1"],
            model["q0_U2"],
            F1,
            F2,
        )
        /
        hs
    )

    dz1 = (
        pair_inner_D(
            model["q1_U1"],
            model["q1_U2"],
            F1,
            F2,
        )
        /
        hs
    )

    dz2 = (
        pair_inner_D(
            model["q2_U1"],
            model["q2_U2"],
            F1,
            F2,
        )
        /
        hs
    )

    dz_dtau = (
        TAU0_D
        *
        torch.stack(
            (
                dz0,
                dz1,
                dz2,
            )
        ).to(
            REAL_DTYPE_D
        )
    )

    return (
        dx_dtau.to(
            REAL_DTYPE_D
        ),
        dz_dtau,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def m3d3_rk4_L2(
    model,
    d_tau,
    env,
):
    U10 = model[
        "anchor_U1"
    ]

    U20 = model[
        "anchor_U2"
    ]

    x0 = model["x"]
    z0 = model["z"]

    k1x, k1z = m3d3_rhs_L2(
        x0,
        z0,
        U10,
        U20,
        model,
        env,
    )

    k2x, k2z = m3d3_rhs_L2(
        x0
        +
        0.5 * d_tau * k1x,
        z0
        +
        0.5 * d_tau * k1z,
        U10,
        U20,
        model,
        env,
    )

    k3x, k3z = m3d3_rhs_L2(
        x0
        +
        0.5 * d_tau * k2x,
        z0
        +
        0.5 * d_tau * k2z,
        U10,
        U20,
        model,
        env,
    )

    k4x, k4z = m3d3_rhs_L2(
        x0
        +
        d_tau * k3x,
        z0
        +
        d_tau * k3z,
        U10,
        U20,
        model,
        env,
    )

    model["x"] = (
        x0
        +
        d_tau
        /
        6.0
        *
        (
            k1x
            +
            2.0 * k2x
            +
            2.0 * k3x
            +
            k4x
        )
    )

    model["z"] = (
        z0
        +
        d_tau
        /
        6.0
        *
        (
            k1z
            +
            2.0 * k2z
            +
            2.0 * k3z
            +
            k4z
        )
    )

    return (
        model["x"],
        model["z"],
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def current_full_state3_L2(
    model,
    env,
):
    return reconstruct_m3d3_L2(
        model["x"],
        model["z"],
        model["anchor_U1"],
        model["anchor_U2"],
        model,
        env,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def reanchor_model3_L2(
    model,
    env,
):
    U1, U2 = current_full_state3_L2(
        model,
        env,
    )

    return initialize_model3_L2(
        U1,
        U2,
        model["proj"]["name"],
        env,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. SCORING
# ==================================================================================================

def relative_error_L2(
    a,
    b,
):
    return float(
        (
            torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                a - b
            )
            /
            (
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                    b
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def score_model_state_L2(
    U1_model,
    U2_model,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
    projector,
):
    tids = projector[
        "target_ids"
    ]

    m = pack_modes_D(
        U1_model,
        U2_model,
        tids,
    )

    t = pack_modes_D(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
    )

    return relative_error_L2(
        m,
        t,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def score_model_rhs_L2(
    U1_model,
    U2_model,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
    env,
    projector,
):
    MF1, MF2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1_model,
        U2_model,
        env,
    )

    TF1, TF2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        env,
    )

    tids = projector[
        "target_ids"
    ]

    m = pack_modes_D(
        MF1,
        MF2,
        tids,
    )

    t = pack_modes_D(
        TF1,
        TF2,
        tids,
    )

    return relative_error_L2(
        m,
        t,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def target_vector2_L2(
    model,
):
    U1, U2 = current_full_state_D(
        model,
        ENV_L2,
    )

    return pack_modes_D(
        U1,
        U2,
        model["proj"]["target_ids"],
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def target_vector3_L2(
    model,
):
    U1, U2 = current_full_state3_L2(
        model,
        ENV_L2,
    )

    return pack_modes_D(
        U1,
        U2,
        model["proj"]["target_ids"],
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 10. CUBIC IMPLEMENTATION PREFLIGHT — ALL 12
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 132)
print("CUBIC-CANCELLATION PREFLIGHT")
print("=" * 132)

preflight_rows_L2 = []

original_nu1_L2 = float(
    nu1_D
)

original_nu2_L2 = float(
    nu2_D
)

original_tau0_L2 = float(
    TAU0_D
)

try:

    for topology in TOPOLOGIES_L2:

        for nu_mult in nu_MULTS_L2:

            nu = (
                BASE_nu_L2
                *
                float(
                    nu_mult
                )
            )

            nu1_D = nu
            nu2_D = nu
            TAU0_D = COMMON_TAU0_L2

            U10, U20 = topology_pair_L2(
                topology,
                ENV_L2,
            )

            model3 = initialize_model3_L2(
                U10,
                U20,
                PROJECTOR_L2,
                ENV_L2,
            )

            print(
                f"{topology:16s}"
                f" | nu x {nu_mult:4.2f}"
                f" | rank={model3['effective_hidden_rank']}"
                f" | R3/R2={model3['r3_ratio']:.3e}"
                f" | C3_3/C3_2={model3['cubic_ratio']:.3e}"
                f" | Gram={model3['gram_error3']:.3e}"
                f" | OK={model3['cubic_cancel_ok']}"
            )

            preflight_rows_L2.append(
                {
                    "topology": topology,
                    "nu_mult": nu_mult,
                    "q2_active": model3[
                        "q2_active"
                    ],
                    "effective_hidden_rank": model3[
                        "effective_hidden_rank"
                    ],
                    "r2_norm": model3[
                        "r2_norm"
                    ],
                    "r3_ratio": model3[
                        "r3_ratio"
                    ],
                    "c3_rank2_norm": model3[
                        "c3_rank2_norm"
                    ],
                    "c3_rank3_norm": model3[
                        "c3_rank3_norm"
                    ],
                    "cubic_ratio": model3[
                        "cubic_ratio"
                    ],
                    "gram_error3": model3[
                        "gram_error3"
                    ],
                    "cubic_cancel_ok": model3[
                        "cubic_cancel_ok"
                    ],
                }
            )

            if not model3[
                "cubic_cancel_ok"
            ]:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "\nCUBIC CANCELLATION PREFLIGHT FAILED.\n"
                    f"topology={topology}\n"
                    f"nu_mult={nu_mult}\n"
                    f"R3/R2={model3['r3_ratio']}\n"
                    f"C3 ratio={model3['cubic_ratio']}"
                )

            del U10
            del U20
            del model3

            gc.collect()
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()

finally:

    nu1_D = original_nu1_L2
    nu2_D = original_nu2_L2
    TAU0_D = original_tau0_L2


PREFLIGHT_L2 = pd.DataFrame(
    preflight_rows_L2
)

PREFLIGHT_L2.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_L2_cubic_preflight.csv",
    index=False,
)

print()
print("✅ ALL 12 CUBIC-CANCELLATION PREFLIGHT CASES PASSED.")
print("Saved preflight to Drive.")


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. RUN ALL 12 CASES
# ==================================================================================================

result_rows_L2 = []
checkpoint_rows_L2 = []
reanchor_rows_L2 = []

test_start_L2 = time.perf_counter()

original_nu1_L2 = float(
    nu1_D
)

original_nu2_L2 = float(
    nu2_D
)

original_tau0_L2 = float(
    TAU0_D
)

case_number_L2 = 0


try:

    for topology in TOPOLOGIES_L2:

        for nu_mult in nu_MULTS_L2:

            case_number_L2 += 1

            print()
            print("#" * 132)
            print(
                f"CASE {case_number_L2:02d}/12"
                f" | {topology:16s}"
                f" | nu x {nu_mult:.2f}"
            )
            print("#" * 132)

            nu = (
                BASE_nu_L2
                *
                float(
                    nu_mult
                )
            )

            nu1_D = nu
            nu2_D = nu
            TAU0_D = COMMON_TAU0_L2

            # ------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Identical initial state for truth, rank-2, rank-3*
            # ------------------------------------------------------------------

            U10, U20 = topology_pair_L2(
                topology,
                ENV_L2,
            )

            U1_truth = U10.clone()
            U2_truth = U20.clone()

            torch.cuda.synchronize()

            b2_start = time.perf_counter()

            model2 = initialize_model_D(
                U10,
                U20,
                PROJECTOR_L2,
                ENV_L2,
            )

            torch.cuda.synchronize()

            build2_s = (
                time.perf_counter()
                -
                b2_start
            )

            torch.cuda.synchronize()

            b3_start = time.perf_counter()

            model3 = initialize_model3_L2(
                U10,
                U20,
                PROJECTOR_L2,
                ENV_L2,
            )

            torch.cuda.synchronize()

            build3_s = (
                time.perf_counter()
                -
                b3_start
            )

            if not model3[
                "cubic_cancel_ok"
            ]:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Initial M3D-3* cubic implementation gate failed."
                )

            proj = model2[
                "proj"
            ]

            next_anchor_tau = (
                ANCHOR_INTERVAL_L2
            )

            report_index = 0

            reanchors2 = 0
            reanchors3 = 0

            rebuild2_total = 0.0
            rebuild3_total = 0.0

            worst_jump2 = 0.0
            worst_jump3 = 0.0

            worst_r3_ratio = float(
                model3[
                    "r3_ratio"
                ]
            )

            finite_cubic_ratios = []

            if np.isfinite(
                model3[
                    "cubic_ratio"
                ]
            ):
                finite_cubic_ratios.append(
                    float(
                        model3[
                            "cubic_ratio"
                        ]
                    )
                )

            # ------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Initial checkpoint
            # ------------------------------------------------------------------

            M21, M22 = current_full_state_D(
                model2,
                ENV_L2,
            )

            M31, M32 = current_full_state3_L2(
                model3,
                ENV_L2,
            )

            s2 = score_model_state_L2(
                M21,
                M22,
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
                proj,
            )

            s3 = score_model_state_L2(
                M31,
                M32,
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
                proj,
            )

            r2 = score_model_rhs_L2(
                M21,
                M22,
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
                ENV_L2,
                proj,
            )

            r3 = score_model_rhs_L2(
                M31,
                M32,
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
                ENV_L2,
                proj,
            )

            checkpoint_rows_L2.append(
                {
                    "case": case_number_L2,
                    "topology": topology,
                    "nu_mult": nu_mult,
                    "tau": 0.0,
                    "state_error_m3d2": s2,
                    "state_error_m3d3": s3,
                    "rhs_error_m3d2": r2,
                    "rhs_error_m3d3": r3,
                    "reanchors_m3d2": 0,
                    "reanchors_m3d3": 0,
                }
            )

            # ------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Trajectory
            # ------------------------------------------------------------------

            for step in range(
                1,
                MAX_STEPS_L2 + 1,
            ):

                # Truth
                U1_truth = full_rk4_D(
                    U1_truth,
                    DT_PHYS_L2,
                    nu1_D,
                    ENV_L2,
                )

                U2_truth = full_rk4_D(
                    U2_truth,
                    DT_PHYS_L2,
                    nu2_D,
                    ENV_L2,
                )

                # Adaptive M3D-2
                m3d_rk4_D(
                    model2,
                    DTAU_L2,
                    model2["anchor_U1"],
                    model2["anchor_U2"],
                    ENV_L2,
                )

                # Adaptive M3D-3*
                m3d3_rk4_L2(
                    model3,
                    DTAU_L2,
                    ENV_L2,
                )

                current_tau = (
                    step
                    *
                    DTAU_L2
                )

                # --------------------------------------------------------------
                # Periodic teacher-free reanchor — SAME schedule for both models.
                # --------------------------------------------------------------

                if (
                    current_tau
                    >=
                    next_anchor_tau
                    -
                    0.5 * DTAU_L2
                    and
                    next_anchor_tau
                    <
                    FINAL_TAU_L2
                    -
                    1e-12
                ):

                    # ------------------------------
                    # Rank-2
                    # ------------------------------

                    before2 = target_vector2_L2(
                        model2
                    ).clone()

                    torch.cuda.synchronize()
                    rb2_start = time.perf_counter()

                    # NO TRUTH STATE USED.
                    model2 = reanchor_model_D(
                        model2,
                        ENV_L2,
                    )

                    torch.cuda.synchronize()

                    rb2_s = (
                        time.perf_counter()
                        -
                        rb2_start
                    )

                    after2 = target_vector2_L2(
                        model2
                    )

                    jump2 = relative_error_L2(
                        after2,
                        before2,
                    )

                    reanchors2 += 1
                    rebuild2_total += rb2_s

                    worst_jump2 = max(
                        worst_jump2,
                        jump2,
                    )

                    # ------------------------------
                    # Rank-3*
                    # ------------------------------

                    before3 = target_vector3_L2(
                        model3
                    ).clone()

                    torch.cuda.synchronize()
                    rb3_start = time.perf_counter()

                    # NO TRUTH STATE USED.
                    model3 = reanchor_model3_L2(
                        model3,
                        ENV_L2,
                    )

                    torch.cuda.synchronize()

                    rb3_s = (
                        time.perf_counter()
                        -
                        rb3_start
                    )

                    after3 = target_vector3_L2(
                        model3
                    )

                    jump3 = relative_error_L2(
                        after3,
                        before3,
                    )

                    if not model3[
                        "cubic_cancel_ok"
                    ]:
                        raise RuntimeError(
                            "\nCUBIC CANCELLATION FAILED DURING REANCHOR.\n"
                            f"case={case_number_L2}\n"
                            f"topology={topology}\n"
                            f"nu_mult={nu_mult}\n"
                            f"tau={current_tau}\n"
                            f"R3/R2={model3['r3_ratio']}\n"
                            f"C3 ratio={model3['cubic_ratio']}"
                        )

                    reanchors3 += 1
                    rebuild3_total += rb3_s

                    worst_jump3 = max(
                        worst_jump3,
                        jump3,
                    )

                    worst_r3_ratio = max(
                        worst_r3_ratio,
                        float(
                            model3[
                                "r3_ratio"
                            ]
                        ),
                    )

                    if np.isfinite(
                        model3[
                            "cubic_ratio"
                        ]
                    ):
                        finite_cubic_ratios.append(
                            float(
                                model3[
                                    "cubic_ratio"
                                ]
                            )
                        )

                    reanchor_rows_L2.append(
                        {
                            "case": case_number_L2,
                            "topology": topology,
                            "nu_mult": nu_mult,
                            "reanchor": reanchors3,
                            "tau": current_tau,
                            "scheduled_tau": next_anchor_tau,
                            "m3d2_build_seconds": rb2_s,
                            "m3d3_build_seconds": rb3_s,
                            "m3d2_target_jump": jump2,
                            "m3d3_target_jump": jump3,
                            "r2_norm": model3[
                                "r2_norm"
                            ],
                            "r3_ratio": model3[
                                "r3_ratio"
                            ],
                            "c3_rank2_norm": model3[
                                "c3_rank2_norm"
                            ],
                            "c3_rank3_norm": model3[
                                "c3_rank3_norm"
                            ],
                            "cubic_ratio": model3[
                                "cubic_ratio"
                            ],
                            "gram_error3": model3[
                                "gram_error3"
                            ],
                        }
                    )

                    next_anchor_tau += (
                        ANCHOR_INTERVAL_L2
                    )

                # --------------------------------------------------------------
                # Report checkpoints
                # --------------------------------------------------------------

                if (
                    report_index
                    <
                    len(
                        REPORT_TAUS_L2
                    )
                    and
                    current_tau
                    >=
                    REPORT_TAUS_L2[
                        report_index
                    ]
                    -
                    0.5 * DTAU_L2
                ):

                    M21, M22 = current_full_state_D(
                        model2,
                        ENV_L2,
                    )

                    M31, M32 = current_full_state3_L2(
                        model3,
                        ENV_L2,
                    )

                    s2 = score_model_state_L2(
                        M21,
                        M22,
                        U1_truth,
                        U2_truth,
                        proj,
                    )

                    s3 = score_model_state_L2(
                        M31,
                        M32,
                        U1_truth,
                        U2_truth,
                        proj,
                    )

                    r2 = score_model_rhs_L2(
                        M21,
                        M22,
                        U1_truth,
                        U2_truth,
                        ENV_L2,
                        proj,
                    )

                    r3 = score_model_rhs_L2(
                        M31,
                        M32,
                        U1_truth,
                        U2_truth,
                        ENV_L2,
                        proj,
                    )

                    checkpoint_rows_L2.append(
                        {
                            "case": case_number_L2,
                            "topology": topology,
                            "nu_mult": nu_mult,
                            "tau": current_tau,
                            "state_error_m3d2": s2,
                            "state_error_m3d3": s3,
                            "rhs_error_m3d2": r2,
                            "rhs_error_m3d3": r3,
                            "reanchors_m3d2": reanchors2,
                            "reanchors_m3d3": reanchors3,
                        }
                    )

                    print(
                        f"    tau={current_tau:6.3f}"
                        f" | M2 state={100*s2:8.3f}%"
                        f" | M3 state={100*s3:8.3f}%"
                        f" | M2 RHS={100*r2:8.3f}%"
                        f" | M3 RHS={100*r3:8.3f}%"
                        f" | state gain={s2/max(s3,1e-30):7.2f}x"
                    )

                    report_index += 1

                if current_tau >= FINAL_TAU_L2:
                    break

            # ------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Final score
            # ------------------------------------------------------------------

            M21, M22 = current_full_state_D(
                model2,
                ENV_L2,
            )

            M31, M32 = current_full_state3_L2(
                model3,
                ENV_L2,
            )

            final_state2 = score_model_state_L2(
                M21,
                M22,
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
                proj,
            )

            final_state3 = score_model_state_L2(
                M31,
                M32,
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
                proj,
            )

            final_rhs2 = score_model_rhs_L2(
                M21,
                M22,
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
                ENV_L2,
                proj,
            )

            final_rhs3 = score_model_rhs_L2(
                M31,
                M32,
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
                ENV_L2,
                proj,
            )

            strict_pass = bool(
                np.isfinite(
                    final_state3
                )
                and
                np.isfinite(
                    final_rhs3
                )
                and
                final_state3
                <
                0.01
                and
                final_rhs3
                <
                0.10
            )

            result_rows_L2.append(
                {
                    "case": case_number_L2,
                    "topology": topology,
                    "nu_mult": nu_mult,
                    "final_tau": current_tau,

                    "m3d2_state_error": final_state2,
                    "m3d3_state_error": final_state3,
                    "state_improvement_2_over_3":
                        final_state2
                        /
                        max(
                            final_state3,
                            1e-30,
                        ),

                    "m3d2_rhs_error": final_rhs2,
                    "m3d3_rhs_error": final_rhs3,
                    "rhs_improvement_2_over_3":
                        final_rhs2
                        /
                        max(
                            final_rhs3,
                            1e-30,
                        ),

                    "m3d2_initial_build_s": build2_s,
                    "m3d3_initial_build_s": build3_s,

                    "reanchors_m3d2": reanchors2,
                    "reanchors_m3d3": reanchors3,

                    "rebuild_total_m3d2_s": rebuild2_total,
                    "rebuild_total_m3d3_s": rebuild3_total,

                    "worst_jump_m3d2": worst_jump2,
                    "worst_jump_m3d3": worst_jump3,

                    "worst_r3_ratio": worst_r3_ratio,

                    "worst_finite_cubic_ratio":
                        max(
                            finite_cubic_ratios
                        )
                        if finite_cubic_ratios
                        else np.nan,

                    "strict_m3d3_pass": strict_pass,
                }
            )

            print()
            print(
                f"FINAL | "
                f"M2 state={100*final_state2:.4f}% | "
                f"M3 state={100*final_state3:.4f}% | "
                f"gain={final_state2/max(final_state3,1e-30):.2f}x | "
                f"M3 RHS={100*final_rhs3:.4f}% | "
                f"PASS={strict_pass}"
            )

            # ------------------------------------------------------------------
            # CRITICAL: SAVE AFTER EVERY CASE
            # ------------------------------------------------------------------

            RESULTS_L2 = pd.DataFrame(
                result_rows_L2
            )

            CHECKPOINTS_L2 = pd.DataFrame(
                checkpoint_rows_L2
            )

            REANCHORS_L2 = pd.DataFrame(
                reanchor_rows_L2
            )

            RESULTS_L2.to_csv(
                SAVE_DIR
                /
                "m3d_L2_results_partial.csv",
                index=False,
            )

            CHECKPOINTS_L2.to_csv(
                SAVE_DIR
                /
                "m3d_L2_checkpoints_partial.csv",
                index=False,
            )

            REANCHORS_L2.to_csv(
                SAVE_DIR
                /
                "m3d_L2_reanchors_partial.csv",
                index=False,
            )

            print(
                "    💾 permanent Drive checkpoint saved"
            )

            # Clean GPU memory before next case.
            del U10
            del U20
            del U1_truth
            del U2_truth
            del M21
            del M22
            del M31
            del M32
            del model2
            del model3

            gc.collect()
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()

finally:

    nu1_D = original_nu1_L2
    nu2_D = original_nu2_L2
    TAU0_D = original_tau0_L2


torch.cuda.synchronize()

TOTAL_RUNTIME_L2 = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    test_start_L2
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 12. FINAL TABLES
# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_L2 = pd.DataFrame(
    result_rows_L2
)

CHECKPOINTS_L2 = pd.DataFrame(
    checkpoint_rows_L2
)

REANCHORS_L2 = pd.DataFrame(
    reanchor_rows_L2
)


RESULTS_L2.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_L2_12case_final.csv",
    index=False,
)

CHECKPOINTS_L2.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_L2_checkpoints.csv",
    index=False,
)

REANCHORS_L2.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_L2_reanchors.csv",
    index=False,
)


TOPOLOGY_SUMMARY_L2 = (
    RESULTS_L2
    .groupby(
        "topology",
        as_index=False,
    )
    .agg(
        m3d2_state_median=(
            "m3d2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),
        m3d3_state_median=(
            "m3d3_state_error",
            "median",
        ),
        m3d3_state_worst=(
            "m3d3_state_error",
            "max",
        ),
        m3d2_rhs_median=(
            "m3d2_rhs_error",
            "median",
        ),
        m3d3_rhs_median=(
            "m3d3_rhs_error",
            "median",
        ),
        m3d3_rhs_worst=(
            "m3d3_rhs_error",
            "max",
        ),
        state_gain_median=(
            "state_improvement_2_over_3",
            "median",
        ),
        strict_passes=(
            "strict_m3d3_pass",
            "sum",
        ),
    )
)

    RESULTS_L2
    .groupby(

    )
    .agg(
        m3d2_state_median=(
            "m3d2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),
        m3d3_state_median=(
            "m3d3_state_error",
            "median",
        ),
        m3d3_state_worst=(
            "m3d3_state_error",
            "max",
        ),
        m3d3_rhs_median=(
            "m3d3_rhs_error",
            "median",
        ),
        m3d3_rhs_worst=(
            "m3d3_rhs_error",
            "max",
        ),
        state_gain_median=(
            "state_improvement_2_over_3",
            "median",
        ),
        strict_passes=(
            "strict_m3d3_pass",
            "sum",
        ),
    )
)

    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_L2_topology_summary.csv",
    index=False,
)

    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_L2_viscosity_summary.csv",
    index=False,
)

    RESULTS_L2.to_string(

        f"{x:.8g}",
    )
)

    TOPOLOGY_SUMMARY_L2.to_string(
        index=False,

        f"{x:.8g}",
    )
)

    VISCOSITY_SUMMARY_L2.to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.8g}",
    )

# 13. FINAL DIAGNOSIS
# ==================================================================================================

all_cubic_ok_L2 = bool(
    PREFLIGHT_L2[
        "cubic_cancel_ok"
    ].all()
)

strict_passes_L2 = int(
    RESULTS_L2[
        "strict_m3d3_pass"
    ].sum()
)

median_state2_L2 = float(
    RESULTS_L2[
        "m3d2_state_error"
    ].median()
)

median_state3_L2 = float(
    RESULTS_L2[
        "m3d3_state_error"
    ].median()
)

worst_state3_L2 = float(
    RESULTS_L2[
        "m3d3_state_error"
    ].max()
)

median_rhs2_L2 = float(
    RESULTS_L2[
        "m3d2_rhs_error"
    ].median()
)

median_rhs3_L2 = float(
    RESULTS_L2[
        "m3d3_rhs_error"
    ].median()
)

worst_rhs3_L2 = float(
    RESULTS_L2[
        "m3d3_rhs_error"
    ].max()
)

median_state_gain_L2 = (
    median_state2_L2
    /
    max(
        median_state3_L2,
        1e-30,
    )
)

median_rhs_gain_L2 = (
    median_rhs2_L2
    /
    max(
        median_rhs3_L2,
        1e-30,
    )
)

PRIMARY_GATE_L2 = bool(
    all_cubic_ok_L2
    and
    strict_passes_L2
    ==
    12
)


print()
print("=" * 132)
print("M3D TEST L2 — FINAL DIAGNOSIS")
print("=" * 132)
print()

print(
    "cubic implementation gate:",
    all_cubic_ok_L2,
)

print()
print(
    "strict M3D-3* passes:",
    strict_passes_L2,
    "/ 12",
)

print()

print(
    "M3D-2 median state error:",
    f"{100*median_state2_L2:.6f}%",
)

print(
    "M3D-3* median state error:",
    f"{100*median_state3_L2:.6f}%",
)

print(
    "median state improvement:",
    f"{median_state_gain_L2:.3f}x",
)

print()

print(
    "M3D-2 median RHS error:",
    f"{100*median_rhs2_L2:.6f}%",
)

print(
    "M3D-3* median RHS error:",
    f"{100*median_rhs3_L2:.6f}%",
)

print(
    "median RHS improvement:",
    f"{median_rhs_gain_L2:.3f}x",
)

print()

print(
    "worst M3D-3* state error:",
    f"{100*worst_state3_L2:.6f}%",
)

print(
    "worst M3D-3* RHS error:",
    f"{100*worst_rhs3_L2:.6f}%",
)

print()

print(
    "total validation runtime:",
    f"{TOTAL_RUNTIME_L2:.2f}s",
)

print()

print(
    "PRIMARY ALL-12 GATE:",
    PRIMARY_GATE_L2,
)

print()

if PRIMARY_GATE_L2:

    print("🔥🔥🔥 PRIMARY PASS")
    print(
        "Cubic-defect cancellation produced a 12/12"
    )
    print(
        "teacher-free adaptive M3D-3* pass through tau≈2."
    )

elif (
    median_state3_L2
    <
    median_state2_L2
    and
    median_state_gain_L2
    >
    1.5
):

    print("✅ MATERIAL IMPROVEMENT, BUT NOT FULL GENERALIZATION.")
    print(
        "q2* substantially improved M3D-2, but at least"
    )
    print(
        "one preregistered <1% state / <10% RHS gate failed."
    )

elif (
    median_state3_L2
    <
    median_state2_L2
):

    print("🟡 SMALL/MODERATE IMPROVEMENT.")
    print(
        "The cubic direction helped, but not enough to establish"
    )
    print(
        "that cubic-defect cancellation solves long-horizon breakdown."
    )

else:

    print("❌ NO GENERAL IMPROVEMENT.")
    print(
        "The cubic-defect direction does not explain the dominant"
    )
    print(
        "long-horizon adaptive M3D-2 failure on this suite."
    )

    f.write(

        f"median_state_m3d2={median_state2_L2}\n"
        f"median_state_m3d3={median_state3_L2}\n"
        f"median_rhs_m3d2={median_rhs2_L2}\n"
        f"median_rhs_m3d3={median_rhs3_L2}\n"
    )

    "m3d_L2_config.json",
    "m3d_L2_generator_preflight.csv",

# periodic_shear   | nu x 0.25 | rank=3 | R3/R2=1.189e-07 | C3_3/C3_2=1.216e-07 | Gram=1.788e-07 | OK=True
