# ==========================================================================
# CELL 9
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST M — LOCAL TRAJECTORY-JET ORDER TEST
#
# QUESTION
# --------
# Does replacing the old second direction
#
#     q1_old ~ R1 DF(U0) q0
#
# with direct trajectory-acceleration matching
#
#     q1_star ~ R1 DF(U0) F(U0)
#
# raise resolved observable accuracy from ~O(t^3) to ~O(t^4)?
#
#
# MODELS — SAME INITIAL STATE, SAME P, SAME TRUTH
# ------------------------------------------------
#
#   P       : target directions only
#   Q0      : P + q0
#   OLD2    : P + q0 + q1_old
#   STAR2   : P + q0 + q1_star
#   STAR3   : P + q0 + q1_old + q2_star
#
#
# PREDICTED LOCAL RESOLVED ERROR ORDERS
# -------------------------------------
#
#   P      ~ t^2
#   Q0     ~ t^3
#   OLD2   ~ t^3
#   STAR2  ~ t^4
#   STAR3  ~ t^4
#
#
# NO:
#   reanchoring
#   tuning
#   old K1 data
#   fitting/training
#
# FROZEN:
#   N = 40
#   nu = 0.0025
#   tau0 = 40/3
#   projector = mixed
#
# Runs all four L2 deterministic topology families.
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import json
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch


# ==================================================================================================
# 0. PREFLIGHT
# ==================================================================================================

REQUIRED_M = [
    "small_env_D",
    "initialize_model_D",
    "_pair_rhs_D",
    "pair_inner_D",
    "pair_norm_D",
    "zero_target_modes_D",
    "pack_modes_D",
    "unpack_modes_D",
    "project_divfree_D",
    "dealias_D",
    "full_rk4_D",
    "nu1_D",
    "nu2_D",
    "TAU0_D",
    "DEVICE_D",
    "REAL_DTYPE_D",

    # Generated by the completed L2 cell:
    "topology_pair_L2",
]

missing_M = [
    x
    for x in REQUIRED_M
    if x not in globals()
]

if missing_M:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Missing Test-M dependencies:\n"
        + repr(missing_M)
        + "\n\nRun the rebuilt baseline and completed L2 cell first."
    )

if not torch.cuda.is_available():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "CUDA GPU required."
    )

if "SAVE_DIR" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "SAVE_DIR missing. Mount Drive first."
    )

SAVE_DIR = Path(
    SAVE_DIR
)

SAVE_DIR.mkdir(
    parents=True,
    exist_ok=True,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. FROZEN EXPERIMENT
# ==================================================================================================

ENV_M = small_env_D

TOPOLOGIES_M = [
    "vortex_ring",
    "periodic_shear",
    "skew_tubes",
    "mixed_vortices",
]

PROJECTOR_M = "mixed"

nu_M = 2.5e-3
TAU0_M = 40.0 / 3.0

# Smaller timestep than long-horizon L2 because this is an order test.
DT_PHYS_M = 0.005

DTAU_M = (
    DT_PHYS_M
    /
    TAU0_M
)

FINAL_TAU_M = 0.12

MAX_STEPS_M = int(
    math.ceil(
        FINAL_TAU_M
        /
        DTAU_M
    )
)

# Fixed log-log slope window.
#
# We stay early enough to measure local order but avoid the
# tiniest times where complex64 roundoff can dominate t^4 errors.
CHECKPOINT_TAUS_M = [
    0.01125,
    0.01500,
    0.02250,
    0.03000,
    0.04500,
    0.06000,
    0.09000,
    0.12000,
]

FIT_TAU_MIN_M = 0.015
FIT_TAU_MAX_M = 0.060

MODEL_NAMES_M = [
    "P",
    "Q0",
    "OLD2",
    "STAR2",
    "STAR3",
]


print("=" * 136)
print("M3D TEST M — LOCAL TRAJECTORY-JET ORDER TEST")
print("=" * 136)

print()
print(
    "GPU:",
    torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
)

print(
    "N:",
    ENV_M["N"],
)

print(
    "L:",
    ENV_M["L"],
)

print(
    "dx:",
    ENV_M["dx"],
)

print()
print(
    "topologies:",
    TOPOLOGIES_M,
)

print(
    "nu:",
    nu_M,
)

print(
    "tau0:",
    TAU0_M,
)

print(
    "physical dt:",
    DT_PHYS_M,
)

print(
    "d_tau:",
    DTAU_M,
)

print(
    "final tau:",
    FINAL_TAU_M,
)

print(
    "steps/case:",
    MAX_STEPS_M,
)

print(
    "slope-fit window:",
    (
        FIT_TAU_MIN_M,
        FIT_TAU_MAX_M,
    ),
)

print()
print(
    "expected orders:"
)

print(
    "  P     ~ 2"
)

print(
    "  Q0    ~ 3"
)

print(
    "  OLD2  ~ 3"
)

print(
    "  STAR2 ~ 4"
)

print(
    "  STAR3 ~ 4"
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. CENTERED JVP
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def pair_jvp_M(
    U1,
    U2,
    V1,
    V2,
    env,
):
    vn = pair_norm_D(
        V1,
        V2,
    )

    vn_float = float(
        vn.detach().cpu()
    )

    if (
        not np.isfinite(
            vn_float
        )
        or
        vn_float < 1e-13
    ):
        return (
            torch.zeros_like(U1),
            torch.zeros_like(U2),
        )

    v1 = (
        V1
        /
        vn
    )

    v2 = (
        V2
        /
        vn
    )

    un = pair_norm_D(
        U1,
        U2,
    )

    eps = max(
        1e-5,
        1e-3
        *
        float(
            un.detach().cpu()
        ),
    )

    Fp1, Fp2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1 + eps * v1,
        U2 + eps * v2,
        env,
    )

    Fm1, Fm2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1 - eps * v1,
        U2 - eps * v2,
        env,
    )

    J1 = (
        Fp1
        -
        Fm1
    ) / (
        2.0
        *
        eps
    )

    J2 = (
        Fp2
        -
        Fm2
    ) / (
        2.0
        *
        eps
    )

    return (
        vn * J1,
        vn * J2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. GENERIC COMPLEMENT PROJECTION
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def complement_M(
    V1,
    V2,
    tids,
    basis,
):
    R1, R2 = zero_target_modes_D(
        V1,
        V2,
        tids,
    )

    for B1, B2 in basis:

        bn = float(
            pair_norm_D(
                B1,
                B2,
            ).detach().cpu()
        )

        if bn < 1e-12:
            continue

        a = pair_inner_D(
            B1,
            B2,
            R1,
            R2,
        )

        R1 = (
            R1
            -
            a * B1
        )

        R2 = (
            R2
            -
            a * B2
        )

    R1, R2 = zero_target_modes_D(
        R1,
        R2,
        tids,
    )

    return (
        R1,
        R2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. BUILD ALL FIVE MODELS
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def build_models_M(
    U10,
    U20,
    env,
):
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Use baseline M3D-2 to establish identical:
    #
    #   P
    #   scaling
    #   q0
    #   old q1
    #
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    base = initialize_model_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        PROJECTOR_M,
        env,
    )

    tids = base[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    q0 = (
        base["q0_U1"].clone(),
        base["q0_U2"].clone(),
    )

    q1_old = (
        base["q1_U1"].clone(),
        base["q1_U2"].clone(),
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Actual trajectory derivatives:
    #
    # f  = F(U0)
    # Af = DF(U0) f = U''(0)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    f1, f2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        env,
    )

    Af1, Af2 = pair_jvp_M(
        U10,
        U20,
        f1,
        f2,
        env,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STAR2:
    #
    # q1_star = normalized R1 Af,
    # R1 = I - P - q0 q0*
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    s1, s2 = complement_M(
        Af1,
        Af2,
        tids,
        [
            q0,
        ],
    )

    ns = pair_norm_D(
        s1,
        s2,
    )

    ns_float = float(
        ns.detach().cpu()
    )

    if (
        not np.isfinite(
            ns_float
        )
        or
        ns_float < 1e-12
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "STAR2 q1* is numerically degenerate."
        )

    q1_star = (
        s1 / ns,
        s2 / ns,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STAR3:
    #
    # old q0 + old q1 + q2_star
    #
    # q2_star = normalized R2 Af
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    t1, t2 = complement_M(
        Af1,
        Af2,
        tids,
        [
            q0,
            q1_old,
        ],
    )

    nt = pair_norm_D(
        t1,
        t2,
    )

    nt_float = float(
        nt.detach().cpu()
    )

    if (
        not np.isfinite(
            nt_float
        )
        or
        nt_float < 1e-12
    ):
        q2_star = (
            torch.zeros_like(
                q0[0]
            ),
            torch.zeros_like(
                q0[1]
            ),
        )

        q2_active = False

    else:

        q2_star = (
            t1 / nt,
            t2 / nt,
        )

        q2_active = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Model basis dictionary.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    basis_dict = {
        "P": [],
        "Q0": [
            q0,
        ],
        "OLD2": [
            q0,
            q1_old,
        ],
        "STAR2": [
            q0,
            q1_star,
        ],
        "STAR3": [
            q0,
            q1_old,
            q2_star,
        ],
    }

    models = {}

    for name in MODEL_NAMES_M:

        basis = basis_dict[
            name
        ]

        model = {
            "name": name,

            "proj": base[
                "proj"
            ],

            "x_scale": base[
                "x_scale"
            ].clone(),

            "hidden_scale": base[
                "hidden_scale"
            ].clone(),

            "x": base[
                "x"
            ].clone(),

            "z": torch.zeros(
                len(
                    basis
                ),
                device=DEVICE_D,
                dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
            ),

            "anchor_U1": U10.clone(),
            "anchor_U2": U20.clone(),

            "basis": [
                (
                    a.clone(),
                    b.clone(),
                )
                for a, b
                in basis
            ],
        }

        models[
            name
        ] = model

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # MECHANISM DIAGNOSTICS
    #
    # Compare R f and R Af.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    f_norm = float(
        pair_norm_D(
            f1,
            f2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    Af_norm = float(
        pair_norm_D(
            Af1,
            Af2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    diagnostics = {}

    for name in MODEL_NAMES_M:

        basis = models[
            name
        ][
            "basis"
        ]

        Rf1, Rf2 = complement_M(
            f1,
            f2,
            tids,
            basis,
        )

        RAf1, RAf2 = complement_M(
            Af1,
            Af2,
            tids,
            basis,
        )

        Rf_ratio = float(
            (
                pair_norm_D(
                    Rf1,
                    Rf2,
                )
                /
                (
                    f_norm
                    +
                    1e-30
                )
            ).detach().cpu()
        )

        RAf_ratio = float(
            (
                pair_norm_D(
                    RAf1,
                    RAf2,
                )
                /
                (
                    Af_norm
                    +
                    1e-30
                )
            ).detach().cpu()
        )

        diagnostics[
            name
        ] = {
            "Rf_ratio": Rf_ratio,
            "RAf_ratio": RAf_ratio,
        }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Check mathematical relationship:
    #
    # q1_star should lie in span(q1_old, q2_star)
    # up to numerical error.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    star1 = q1_star[0].clone()
    star2 = q1_star[1].clone()

    span_basis = [
        q1_old,
    ]

    if q2_active:
        span_basis.append(
            q2_star
        )

    for B1, B2 in span_basis:

        a = pair_inner_D(
            B1,
            B2,
            star1,
            star2,
        )

        star1 = (
            star1
            -
            a * B1
        )

        star2 = (
            star2
            -
            a * B2
        )

    star_in_old3_residual = float(
        pair_norm_D(
            star1,
            star2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    return (
        models,
        diagnostics,
        star_in_old3_residual,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. GENERIC REDUCED DYNAMICS
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def reconstruct_M(
    model,
    env,
):
    tids = model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    x_phys = (
        model[
            "x"
        ]
        *
        model[
            "x_scale"
        ]
    )

    U1, U2 = unpack_modes_D(
        x_phys,
        model[
            "anchor_U1"
        ],
        model[
            "anchor_U2"
        ],
        tids,
    )

    hs = model[
        "hidden_scale"
    ]

    for i, (
        B1,
        B2,
    ) in enumerate(
        model[
            "basis"
        ]
    ):

        U1 = (
            U1
            +
            hs
            *
            model["z"][i]
            *
            B1
        )

        U2 = (
            U2
            +
            hs
            *
            model["z"][i]
            *
            B2
        )

    U1 = dealias_D(
        project_divfree_D(
            U1,
            env,
        ),
        env,
    )

    U2 = dealias_D(
        project_divfree_D(
            U2,
            env,
        ),
        env,
    )

    return (
        U1,
        U2,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def rhs_M(
    x,
    z,
    model,
    env,
):
    # Temporarily reconstruct from supplied RK stage state.
    old_x = model[
        "x"
    ]

    old_z = model[
        "z"
    ]

    model[
        "x"
    ] = x

    model[
        "z"
    ] = z

    U1, U2 = reconstruct_M(
        model,
        env,
    )

    model[
        "x"
    ] = old_x

    model[
        "z"
    ] = old_z

    F1, F2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1,
        U2,
        env,
    )

    tids = model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    dx_phys_dt = pack_modes_D(
        F1,
        F2,
        tids,
    )

    dx_dtau = (
        TAU0_D
        *
        dx_phys_dt
        /
        model[
            "x_scale"
        ]
    )

    if len(
        model[
            "basis"
        ]
    ) == 0:

        dz_dtau = torch.empty(
            0,
            device=DEVICE_D,
            dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
        )

    else:

        dz_phys = []

        hs = model[
            "hidden_scale"
        ]

        for B1, B2 in model[
            "basis"
        ]:

            dz_phys.append(
                pair_inner_D(
                    B1,
                    B2,
                    F1,
                    F2,
                )
                /
                hs
            )

        dz_dtau = (
            TAU0_D
            *
            torch.stack(
                dz_phys
            ).to(
                REAL_DTYPE_D
            )
        )

    return (
        dx_dtau.to(
            REAL_DTYPE_D
        ),
        dz_dtau,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def rk4_M(
    model,
    d_tau,
    env,
):
    x0 = model[
        "x"
    ]

    z0 = model[
        "z"
    ]

    k1x, k1z = rhs_M(
        x0,
        z0,
        model,
        env,
    )

    k2x, k2z = rhs_M(
        x0
        +
        0.5
        *
        d_tau
        *
        k1x,
        z0
        +
        0.5
        *
        d_tau
        *
        k1z,
        model,
        env,
    )

    k3x, k3z = rhs_M(
        x0
        +
        0.5
        *
        d_tau
        *
        k2x,
        z0
        +
        0.5
        *
        d_tau
        *
        k2z,
        model,
        env,
    )

    k4x, k4z = rhs_M(
        x0
        +
        d_tau
        *
        k3x,
        z0
        +
        d_tau
        *
        k3z,
        model,
        env,
    )

    model[
        "x"
    ] = (
        x0
        +
        d_tau
        /
        6.0
        *
        (
            k1x
            +
            2.0 * k2x
            +
            2.0 * k3x
            +
            k4x
        )
    )

    model[
        "z"
    ] = (
        z0
        +
        d_tau
        /
        6.0
        *
        (
            k1z
            +
            2.0 * k2z
            +
            2.0 * k3z
            +
            k4z
        )
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. TARGET ERROR
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def target_error_M(
    model,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
):
    U1m, U2m = reconstruct_M(
        model,
        ENV_M,
    )

    tids = model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    xm = pack_modes_D(
        U1m,
        U2m,
        tids,
    )

    xt = pack_modes_D(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
    )

    return float(
        (
            torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                xm - xt
            )
            /
            (
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                    xt
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. FIT POWER LAW
#
# error ~= C tau^p
# ==================================================================================================

def fit_slope_M(
    taus,
    errors,
):
    taus = np.asarray(
        taus,
        dtype=float,
    )

    errors = np.asarray(
        errors,
        dtype=float,
    )

    mask = (
        np.isfinite(
            taus
        )
        &
        np.isfinite(
            errors
        )
        &
        (
            taus
            >=
            FIT_TAU_MIN_M
            -
            1e-12
        )
        &
        (
            taus
            <=
            FIT_TAU_MAX_M
            +
            1e-12
        )
        &
        (
            errors
            >
            1e-9
        )
    )

    x = np.log(
        taus[
            mask
        ]
    )

    y = np.log(
        errors[
            mask
        ]
    )

    if len(
        x
    ) < 3:
        return (
            np.nan,
            np.nan,
            int(
                len(
                    x
                )
            ),
        )

    slope, intercept = np.polyfit(
        x,
        y,
        1,
    )

    yhat = (
        slope
        *
        x
        +
        intercept
    )

    ss_res = np.sum(
        (
            y
            -
            yhat
        )
        ** 2
    )

    ss_tot = np.sum(
        (
            y
            -
            np.mean(
                y
            )
        )
        ** 2
    )

    r2 = (
        1.0
        -
        ss_res
        /
        max(
            ss_tot,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    return (
        float(
            slope
        ),
        float(
            r2
        ),
        int(
            len(
                x
            )
        ),
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. RUN
# ==================================================================================================

checkpoint_rows_M = []
mechanism_rows_M = []
slope_rows_M = []

original_nu1_M = float(
    nu1_D
)

original_nu2_M = float(
    nu2_D
)

original_tau0_M = float(
    TAU0_D
)

test_start_M = time.perf_counter()


try:

    nu1_D = nu_M
    nu2_D = nu_M
    TAU0_D = TAU0_M

    for case_i, topology in enumerate(
        TOPOLOGIES_M,
        start=1,
    ):

        print()
        print("#" * 136)
        print(
            f"CASE {case_i}/4 | {topology}"
        )
        print("#" * 136)

        U10, U20 = topology_pair_L2(
            topology,
            ENV_M,
        )

        U1_truth = U10.clone()
        U2_truth = U20.clone()

        models, diagnostics, star_span_residual = build_models_M(
            U10,
            U20,
            ENV_M,
        )

        print()
        print(
            "Derivative residual diagnostics:"
        )

        for name in MODEL_NAMES_M:

            d = diagnostics[
                name
            ]

            print(
                f"  {name:5s}"
                f" | ||Rf||/||f||={d['Rf_ratio']:.3e}"
                f" | ||RAf||/||Af||={d['RAf_ratio']:.3e}"
            )

            mechanism_rows_M.append(
                {
                    "topology": topology,
                    "model": name,
                    "Rf_ratio": d[
                        "Rf_ratio"
                    ],
                    "RAf_ratio": d[
                        "RAf_ratio"
                    ],
                    "star2_in_old3_span_residual":
                        star_span_residual,
                }
            )

        print(
            "  STAR2 direction residual outside span(OLD q1, STAR3 q2):",
            f"{star_span_residual:.3e}",
        )

        # Store tau=0 state for completeness, but not slope fitting.
        for name in MODEL_NAMES_M:

            err = target_error_M(
                models[
                    name
                ],
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
            )

            checkpoint_rows_M.append(
                {
                    "topology": topology,
                    "model": name,
                    "tau": 0.0,
                    "target_error": err,
                }
            )

        next_cp_i = 0

        for step in range(
            1,
            MAX_STEPS_M + 1,
        ):

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Full Navier-Stokes truth
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            U1_truth = full_rk4_D(
                U1_truth,
                DT_PHYS_M,
                nu1_D,
                ENV_M,
            )

            U2_truth = full_rk4_D(
                U2_truth,
                DT_PHYS_M,
                nu2_D,
                ENV_M,
            )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Frozen reduced models
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            for name in MODEL_NAMES_M:

                rk4_M(
                    models[
                        name
                    ],
                    DTAU_M,
                    ENV_M,
                )

            tau = (
                step
                *
                DTAU_M
            )

            # --------------------------------------------------------------
            # Checkpoint
            # --------------------------------------------------------------

            if (
                next_cp_i
                <
                len(
                    CHECKPOINT_TAUS_M
                )
                and
                tau
                >=
                CHECKPOINT_TAUS_M[
                    next_cp_i
                ]
                -
                0.5
                *
                DTAU_M
            ):

                print(
                    f"  tau={tau:.5f}",
                    end="",
                )

                for name in MODEL_NAMES_M:

                    err = target_error_M(
                        models[
                            name
                        ],
                        U1_truth,
                        U2_truth,
                    )

                    checkpoint_rows_M.append(
                        {
                            "topology": topology,
                            "model": name,
                            "tau": tau,
                            "target_error": err,
                        }
                    )

                    print(
                        f" | {name}={100*err:.6f}%",
                        end="",
                    )

                print()

                next_cp_i += 1

            if tau >= FINAL_TAU_M:
                break

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Fit slopes for this topology
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        local_df = pd.DataFrame(
            checkpoint_rows_M
        )

        local_df = local_df[
            local_df[
                "topology"
            ]
            ==
            topology
        ]

        print()
        print(
            "FITTED LOCAL ORDERS:"
        )

        for name in MODEL_NAMES_M:

            sub = local_df[
                local_df[
                    "model"
                ]
                ==
                name
            ]

            slope, fit_r2, nfit = fit_slope_M(
                sub[
                    "tau"
                ].values,
                sub[
                    "target_error"
                ].values,
            )

            slope_rows_M.append(
                {
                    "topology": topology,
                    "model": name,
                    "slope": slope,
                    "fit_r2": fit_r2,
                    "fit_points": nfit,
                }
            )

            print(
                f"  {name:5s}"
                f" | slope={slope:8.4f}"
                f" | R²={fit_r2:8.5f}"
                f" | n={nfit}"
            )

        # Save after every topology.
        pd.DataFrame(
            checkpoint_rows_M
        ).to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_M_checkpoints_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        pd.DataFrame(
            mechanism_rows_M
        ).to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_M_mechanism_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        pd.DataFrame(
            slope_rows_M
        ).to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_M_slopes_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        print()
        print(
            "  💾 Drive checkpoint saved"
        )

        del U10
        del U20
        del U1_truth
        del U2_truth
        del models

        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()


finally:

    nu1_D = original_nu1_M
    nu2_D = original_nu2_M
    TAU0_D = original_tau0_M


torch.cuda.synchronize()

TOTAL_RUNTIME_M = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    test_start_M
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. FINAL TABLES
# ==================================================================================================

CHECKPOINTS_M = pd.DataFrame(
    checkpoint_rows_M
)

MECHANISM_M = pd.DataFrame(
    mechanism_rows_M
)

SLOPES_M = pd.DataFrame(
    slope_rows_M
)


CHECKPOINTS_M.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_M_local_order_checkpoints.csv",
    index=False,
)

MECHANISM_M.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_M_mechanism.csv",
    index=False,
)

SLOPES_M.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_M_local_order_slopes.csv",
    index=False,
)


SUMMARY_M = (
    SLOPES_M
    .groupby(
        "model",
        as_index=False,
    )
    .agg(
        median_slope=(
            "slope",
            "median",
        ),
        min_slope=(
            "slope",
            "min",
        ),
        max_slope=(
            "slope",
            "max",
        ),
        median_fit_r2=(
            "fit_r2",
            "median",
        ),
    )
)


expected_map_M = {
    "P": 2.0,
    "Q0": 3.0,
    "OLD2": 3.0,
    "STAR2": 4.0,
    "STAR3": 4.0,
}


SUMMARY_M[
    "expected_order"
] = SUMMARY_M[
    "model"
].map(
    expected_map_M
)


SUMMARY_M.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_M_order_summary.csv",
    index=False,
)


print()
print("=" * 136)
print("PER-TOPOLOGY LOCAL ORDER RESULTS")
print("=" * 136)
print()

print(
    SLOPES_M.to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.6g}",
    )
)


print()
print("=" * 136)
print("MODEL ORDER SUMMARY")
print("=" * 136)
print()

print(
    SUMMARY_M.to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.6g}",
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 10. PREDECLARED DIAGNOSTIC GATES
# ==================================================================================================

def median_slope_for_M(
    name,
):
    vals = SLOPES_M.loc[
        SLOPES_M[
            "model"
        ]
        ==
        name,
        "slope",
    ]

    return float(
        vals.median()
    )


p_P_M = median_slope_for_M(
    "P"
)

p_Q0_M = median_slope_for_M(
    "Q0"
)

p_OLD2_M = median_slope_for_M(
    "OLD2"
)

p_STAR2_M = median_slope_for_M(
    "STAR2"
)

p_STAR3_M = median_slope_for_M(
    "STAR3"
)


# Broad tolerances because:
# - complex64 arithmetic
# - finite RK4 timestep
# - fitted rather than asymptotic derivatives
#
# The IMPORTANT gate is the order jump, not hitting an exact integer.

GATE_P_M = bool(
    1.5
    <=
    p_P_M
    <=
    2.5
)

GATE_Q0_M = bool(
    2.4
    <=
    p_Q0_M
    <=
    3.6
)

GATE_OLD2_M = bool(
    2.4
    <=
    p_OLD2_M
    <=
    3.6
)

GATE_STAR2_M = bool(
    3.3
    <=
    p_STAR2_M
    <=
    4.7
)

GATE_STAR3_M = bool(
    3.3
    <=
    p_STAR3_M
    <=
    4.7
)

ORDER_LIFT_M = bool(
    p_STAR2_M
    >=
    p_OLD2_M
    +
    0.5
)

STAR2_STAR3_MATCH_M = bool(
    abs(
        p_STAR2_M
        -
        p_STAR3_M
    )
    <=
    0.5
)


# Mechanism gate:
#
# STAR2 should match Af essentially completely.
star2_RAf = MECHANISM_M.loc[
    MECHANISM_M[
        "model"
    ]
    ==
    "STAR2",
    "RAf_ratio",
]

star3_RAf = MECHANISM_M.loc[
    MECHANISM_M[
        "model"
    ]
    ==
    "STAR3",
    "RAf_ratio",
]


MECHANISM_STAR2_M = bool(
    float(
        star2_RAf.max()
    )
    <
    1e-4
)

MECHANISM_STAR3_M = bool(
    float(
        star3_RAf.max()
    )
    <
    1e-4
)


PRIMARY_M = bool(
    GATE_P_M
    and
    GATE_Q0_M
    and
    GATE_OLD2_M
    and
    GATE_STAR2_M
    and
    GATE_STAR3_M
    and
    ORDER_LIFT_M
    and
    STAR2_STAR3_MATCH_M
    and
    MECHANISM_STAR2_M
    and
    MECHANISM_STAR3_M
)


print()
print("=" * 136)
print("M3D TEST M — FINAL DIAGNOSIS")
print("=" * 136)
print()

print(
    f"P median order:      {p_P_M:.4f}"
)

print(
    f"Q0 median order:     {p_Q0_M:.4f}"
)

print(
    f"OLD2 median order:   {p_OLD2_M:.4f}"
)

print(
    f"STAR2 median order:  {p_STAR2_M:.4f}"
)

print(
    f"STAR3 median order:  {p_STAR3_M:.4f}"
)

print()

print(
    "P ~2 gate:",
    GATE_P_M,
)

print(
    "Q0 ~3 gate:",
    GATE_Q0_M,
)

print(
    "OLD2 ~3 gate:",
    GATE_OLD2_M,
)

print(
    "STAR2 ~4 gate:",
    GATE_STAR2_M,
)

print(
    "STAR3 ~4 gate:",
    GATE_STAR3_M,
)

print()

print(
    "STAR2 order lift over OLD2 >= 0.5:",
    ORDER_LIFT_M,
)

print(
    "STAR2 and STAR3 order match:",
    STAR2_STAR3_MATCH_M,
)

print()

print(
    "STAR2 acceleration-match mechanism:",
    MECHANISM_STAR2_M,
)

print(
    "STAR3 acceleration-match mechanism:",
    MECHANISM_STAR3_M,
)

print()

print(
    "PRIMARY TEST-M GATE:",
    PRIMARY_M,
)

print()

if PRIMARY_M:

    print(
        "🔥🔥🔥 LOCAL DERIVATIVE-MATCHING RESULT PASSED."
    )

    print(
        "STAR2 achieves the predicted fourth-order"
    )

    print(
        "resolved local accuracy using only two hidden directions."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "If STAR2 ~= STAR3, the third hidden coordinate is"
    )

    print(
        "not required for acceleration matching."
    )

elif (
    ORDER_LIFT_M
    and
    MECHANISM_STAR2_M
):

    print(
        "✅ DERIVATIVE MATCHING LIFTS THE OBSERVED ORDER,"
    )

    print(
        "but the fitted slopes do not cleanly hit every"
    )

    print(
        "predeclared integer-order window."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Inspect roundoff / fit-window behavior before changing theory."
    )

elif MECHANISM_STAR2_M:

    print(
        "🟡 STAR2 algebraically matches acceleration,"
    )

    print(
        "but the expected fourth-order trajectory slope was not observed."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "That would mean either numerical contamination or"
    )

    print(
        "our local-order derivation needs correction."
    )

else:

    print(
        "❌ STAR2 construction itself failed to match Af."
    )

    print(
        "Do not interpret the trajectory slopes until this is fixed."
    )


print()

print(
    "runtime:",
    f"{TOTAL_RUNTIME_M:.2f}s",
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. PERMANENT COMPLETION MARKER
# ==================================================================================================

with open(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_M_COMPLETE.txt",
    "w",
) as f:

    f.write(
        "M3D TEST M COMPLETE\n"
        f"runtime_seconds={TOTAL_RUNTIME_M}\n"
        f"P_order={p_P_M}\n"
        f"Q0_order={p_Q0_M}\n"
        f"OLD2_order={p_OLD2_M}\n"
        f"STAR2_order={p_STAR2_M}\n"
        f"STAR3_order={p_STAR3_M}\n"
        f"primary_gate={PRIMARY_M}\n"
    )


print()
print("=" * 136)
print("PERMANENT FILES")
print("=" * 136)

for name in [
    "m3d_M_local_order_checkpoints.csv",
    "m3d_M_mechanism.csv",
    "m3d_M_local_order_slopes.csv",
    "m3d_M_order_summary.csv",
    "m3d_M_COMPLETE.txt",
]:

    print(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        name
    )

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 10
# ==========================================================================
# ======================================================================================
# TEST M-A — ZERO-COST MOVING-WINDOW SLOPE AUDIT
# ======================================================================================

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

if "CHECKPOINTS_M" not in globals():
    CHECKPOINTS_M = pd.read_csv(
        SAVE_DIR / "m3d_M_local_order_checkpoints.csv"
    )

WINDOWS_MA = [
    (0.015, 0.060),
    (0.030, 0.120),
    (0.045, 0.120),
    (0.060, 0.120),
]

MODELS_MA = [
    "P",
    "Q0",
    "OLD2",
    "STAR2",
    "STAR3",
]

rows_MA = []

for topology in TOPOLOGIES_M:
    for model in MODELS_MA:

        sub = CHECKPOINTS_M[
            (CHECKPOINTS_M["topology"] == topology)
            &
            (CHECKPOINTS_M["model"] == model)
        ].copy()

        for lo, hi in WINDOWS_MA:

            fit = sub[
                (sub["tau"] >= lo - 1e-12)
                &
                (sub["tau"] <= hi + 1e-12)
                &
                (sub["target_error"] > 0)
            ]

            if len(fit) < 2:
                slope = np.nan
                r2 = np.nan
            else:
                x = np.log(fit["tau"].to_numpy(float))
                y = np.log(fit["target_error"].to_numpy(float))

                slope, intercept = np.polyfit(x, y, 1)
                yhat = slope*x + intercept

                ss_res = np.sum((y-yhat)**2)
                ss_tot = np.sum((y-y.mean())**2)

                r2 = 1 - ss_res/max(ss_tot, 1e-30)

            rows_MA.append({
                "topology": topology,
                "model": model,
                "tau_min": lo,
                "tau_max": hi,
                "points": len(fit),
                "slope": slope,
                "R2": r2,
            })

AUDIT_MA = pd.DataFrame(rows_MA)

print("="*110)
print("STAR2 MOVING-WINDOW SLOPES")
print("="*110)

print(
    AUDIT_MA[
        AUDIT_MA["model"] == "STAR2"
    ].to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x: f"{x:.5f}"
    )
)

print()
print("="*110)
print("STAR3 MOVING-WINDOW SLOPES")
print("="*110)

print(
    AUDIT_MA[
        AUDIT_MA["model"] == "STAR3"
    ].to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x: f"{x:.5f}"
    )
)

print()
print("="*110)
print("MEDIAN SLOPE BY WINDOW")
print("="*110)

SUMMARY_MA = (
    AUDIT_MA
    .groupby(["model", "tau_min", "tau_max"], as_index=False)
    .agg(
        median_slope=("slope", "median"),
        median_R2=("R2", "median"),
    )
)

print(
    SUMMARY_MA.to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x: f"{x:.5f}"
    )
)

AUDIT_MA.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR / "m3d_MA_window_audit.csv",
    index=False
)

print()
print("Saved:", SAVE_DIR / "m3d_MA_window_audit.csv")

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 11
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST M-B — PRECISION / TIMESTEP FALSIFICATION
#
# PURPOSE
# -------
# Test whether the anomalous ~t^2 STAR2 scaling for periodic_shear
# is caused by float32 / RK4 numerical contamination rather than
# failure of the local O(t^4) theory.
#
#
# FROZEN SCIENCE
# --------------
# topology:
#   periodic_shear     <- anomalous case
#   mixed_vortices     <- control case
#
# model:
#   OLD2
#   STAR2
#
# nu       = 0.0025
# tau0     = 40/3
# projector = mixed
# final tau = 0.12
# NO reanchoring
#
#
# nuMERICAL MATRIX
# ----------------
#
# A: float32 / complex64    dt = 0.0050
# B: float32 / complex64    dt = 0.0025
# C: float64 / complex128   dt = 0.0050
# D: float64 / complex128   dt = 0.0025
#
#
# PRIMARY DIAGNOSTICS
# -------------------
#
# 1. Local doubling order:
#
#        p(t1,t2) = log(E2/E1) / log(t2/t1)
#
# 2. Normalized coefficients:
#
#        OLD2:   K3(t) = E(t)/t^3
#        STAR2:  K4(t) = E(t)/t^4
#
# where E uses the FIXED initial resolved norm ||P U0||.
#
#
# EXPECTATION IF nuMERICAL-FLOOR HYPOTHESIS IS RIGHT
# --------------------------------------------------
#
# periodic_shear STAR2:
#
#       fp32  -> contaminated slope
#       fp64  -> slope approaches ~4
#
# mixed_vortices STAR2:
#
#       stays near ~4 as precision/timestep are refined.
#
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch


# ==================================================================================================
# 0. PREFLIGHT
# ==================================================================================================

REQUIRED_MB = [
    "make_env_D",
    "topology_pair_L2",
    "build_models_M",
    "reconstruct_M",
    "rk4_M",
    "full_rk4_D",
    "pack_modes_D",
    "pair_norm_D",

    "DEVICE_D",
    "REAL_DTYPE_D",
    "COMPLEX_DTYPE_D",

    "nu1_D",
    "nu2_D",
    "TAU0_D",

    "PROJECTOR_M",
]

missing_MB = [
    name
    for name in REQUIRED_MB
    if name not in globals()
]

if missing_MB:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Missing Test M-B dependencies:\n"
        + repr(missing_MB)
        + "\n\nRun the rebuilt baseline, L2 definitions, and Test M first."
    )

if not torch.cuda.is_available():
    raise RuntimeError("CUDA GPU required.")

if "SAVE_DIR" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError("SAVE_DIR missing.")

SAVE_DIR = Path(SAVE_DIR)
SAVE_DIR.mkdir(
    parents=True,
    exist_ok=True,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. FROZEN SCIENTIFIC SETTINGS
# ==================================================================================================

TOPOLOGIES_MB = [
    "periodic_shear",
    "mixed_vortices",
]

MODELS_MB = [
    "OLD2",
    "STAR2",
]

nu_MB = 2.5e-3

TAU0_MB = 40.0 / 3.0

FINAL_TAU_MB = 0.12

CHECKPOINT_TAUS_MB = [
    0.0150,
    0.0225,
    0.0300,
    0.0450,
    0.0600,
    0.0900,
    0.1200,
]


CONFIGS_MB = [
    {
        "label": "fp32_dt0050",
        "real_dtype": torch.float32,
        "complex_dtype": torch.complex64,
        "dt_phys": 0.0050,
    },
    {
        "label": "fp32_dt0025",
        "real_dtype": torch.float32,
        "complex_dtype": torch.complex64,
        "dt_phys": 0.0025,
    },
    {
        "label": "fp64_dt0050",
        "real_dtype": torch.float64,
        "complex_dtype": torch.complex128,
        "dt_phys": 0.0050,
    },
    {
        "label": "fp64_dt0025",
        "real_dtype": torch.float64,
        "complex_dtype": torch.complex128,
        "dt_phys": 0.0025,
    },
]


print("=" * 136)
print("M3D TEST M-B — PRECISION / TIMESTEP FALSIFICATION")
print("=" * 136)

print()
print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0))
print("topologies:", TOPOLOGIES_MB)
print("models:", MODELS_MB)
print("nu:", nu_MB)
print("tau0:", TAU0_MB)
print("final tau:", FINAL_TAU_MB)

print()
print("nuMERICAL MATRIX:")

for c in CONFIGS_MB:
    print(
        f"  {c['label']:14s}"
        f" | real={str(c['real_dtype']):15s}"
        f" | complex={str(c['complex_dtype']):17s}"
        f" | dt={c['dt_phys']}"
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. FIXED-REFERENCE TARGET ERROR
#
# E(t) = ||P(U_model - U_truth)|| / ||P U0||
#
# Using a fixed denominator makes K3/K4 interpretation cleaner.
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def target_error_fixed_MB(
    model,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
    U1_initial,
    U2_initial,
    env,
):
    U1_model, U2_model = reconstruct_M(
        model,
        env,
    )

    tids = model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    x_model = pack_modes_D(
        U1_model,
        U2_model,
        tids,
    )

    x_truth = pack_modes_D(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
    )

    x_initial = pack_modes_D(
        U1_initial,
        U2_initial,
        tids,
    )

    numerator = torch.linalg.vector_norm(
        x_model - x_truth
    )

    denominator = torch.linalg.vector_norm(
        x_initial
    )

    return float(
        (
            numerator
            /
            (
                denominator
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. PAIRWISE ORDER
#
# E ~ t^p  =>
#
# p = log(E2/E1) / log(t2/t1)
# ==================================================================================================

def pair_order_MB(
    t1,
    e1,
    t2,
    e2,
):
    if (
        not np.isfinite(e1)
        or
        not np.isfinite(e2)
        or
        e1 <= 0
        or
        e2 <= 0
    ):
        return np.nan

    return float(
        np.log(
            e2 / e1
        )
        /
        np.log(
            t2 / t1
        )
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. LATE-WINDOW POWER-LAW FIT
# ==================================================================================================

def fit_order_MB(
    df,
    lo=0.045,
    hi=0.12,
):
    sub = df[
        (df["tau"] >= lo - 1e-12)
        &
        (df["tau"] <= hi + 1e-12)
        &
        (df["error_fixed"] > 0)
    ]

    if len(sub) < 3:
        return np.nan, np.nan

    x = np.log(
        sub["tau"].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )
    )

    y = np.log(
        sub["error_fixed"].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )
    )

    slope, intercept = np.polyfit(
        x,
        y,
        1,
    )

    yhat = (
        slope
        *
        x
        +
        intercept
    )

    ss_res = np.sum(
        (
            y
            -
            yhat
        ) ** 2
    )

    ss_tot = np.sum(
        (
            y
            -
            np.mean(y)
        ) ** 2
    )

    r2 = (
        1.0
        -
        ss_res
        /
        max(
            ss_tot,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    return (
        float(slope),
        float(r2),
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. RUN ONE CONFIGURATION
#
# IMPORTANT:
#
# The baseline environment was originally constructed in float32.
#
# Therefore for every precision configuration we:
#
#   1. change the dtype globals,
#   2. rebuild the spectral environment,
#   3. regenerate the deterministic initial condition natively
#      in that precision.
#
# We DO NOT cast a float32 field to float64.
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def run_config_MB(
    config,
):
    global REAL_DTYPE_D
    global COMPLEX_DTYPE_D
    global nu1_D
    global nu2_D
    global TAU0_D

    label = config[
        "label"
    ]

    real_dtype = config[
        "real_dtype"
    ]

    complex_dtype = config[
        "complex_dtype"
    ]

    dt_phys = float(
        config[
            "dt_phys"
        ]
    )

    d_tau = (
        dt_phys
        /
        TAU0_MB
    )

    final_steps = int(
        round(
            FINAL_TAU_MB
            /
            d_tau
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Switch arithmetic BEFORE rebuilding environment.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    REAL_DTYPE_D = real_dtype
    COMPLEX_DTYPE_D = complex_dtype

    nu1_D = nu_MB
    nu2_D = nu_MB
    TAU0_D = TAU0_MB

    ENV_MB = make_env_D(
        40,
        L=8.0,
    )

    print()
    print("=" * 136)
    print(
        f"CONFIG {label}"
    )
    print("=" * 136)

    print(
        "real dtype:",
        REAL_DTYPE_D,
    )

    print(
        "complex dtype:",
        COMPLEX_DTYPE_D,
    )

    print(
        "env K dtype:",
        ENV_MB["KX"].dtype,
    )

    print(
        "dt physical:",
        dt_phys,
    )

    print(
        "d_tau:",
        d_tau,
    )

    print(
        "steps:",
        final_steps,
    )

    # Checkpoint step numbers.
    cp_step_to_tau = {}

    for tau in CHECKPOINT_TAUS_MB:

        step = int(
            round(
                tau
                /
                d_tau
            )
        )

        reconstructed_tau = (
            step
            *
            d_tau
        )

        if abs(
            reconstructed_tau
            -
            tau
        ) > 1e-10:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Checkpoint tau {tau} does not align with dt for {label}."
            )

        cp_step_to_tau[
            step
        ] = tau

    rows = []
    mechanism_rows = []

    for topology in TOPOLOGIES_MB:

        print()
        print("-" * 136)
        print(
            f"{label} | {topology}"
        )
        print("-" * 136)

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Generate state NATIVELY in current precision.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        U10, U20 = topology_pair_L2(
            topology,
            ENV_MB,
        )

        if U10.dtype != complex_dtype:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Generator dtype mismatch: {U10.dtype} != {complex_dtype}"
            )

        U1_truth = U10.clone()
        U2_truth = U20.clone()

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Build all Test-M bases using current precision.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        models_all, diagnostics, span_residual = build_models_M(
            U10,
            U20,
            ENV_MB,
        )

        # Keep only OLD2 and STAR2.
        models = {
            name: models_all[
                name
            ]
            for name in MODELS_MB
        }

        # Delete unneeded models immediately.
        for name in list(
            models_all.keys()
        ):
            if name not in MODELS_MB:
                del models_all[
                    name
                ]

        star2_RAf = diagnostics[
            "STAR2"
        ][
            "RAf_ratio"
        ]

        old2_RAf = diagnostics[
            "OLD2"
        ][
            "RAf_ratio"
        ]

        print(
            "OLD2  ||RAf||/||Af||:",
            f"{old2_RAf:.3e}",
        )

        print(
            "STAR2 ||RAf||/||Af||:",
            f"{star2_RAf:.3e}",
        )

        print(
            "STAR2 span residual:",
            f"{span_residual:.3e}",
        )

        mechanism_rows.append(
            {
                "config": label,
                "topology": topology,
                "real_dtype": str(real_dtype),
                "complex_dtype": str(complex_dtype),
                "dt_phys": dt_phys,
                "d_tau": d_tau,
                "old2_RAf_ratio": old2_RAf,
                "star2_RAf_ratio": star2_RAf,
                "star2_span_residual": span_residual,
            }
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Integrate.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        for step in range(
            1,
            final_steps + 1,
        ):

            U1_truth = full_rk4_D(
                U1_truth,
                dt_phys,
                nu1_D,
                ENV_MB,
            )

            U2_truth = full_rk4_D(
                U2_truth,
                dt_phys,
                nu2_D,
                ENV_MB,
            )

            for name in MODELS_MB:

                rk4_M(
                    models[
                        name
                    ],
                    d_tau,
                    ENV_MB,
                )

            if step in cp_step_to_tau:

                tau = cp_step_to_tau[
                    step
                ]

                print(
                    f"  tau={tau:7.4f}",
                    end="",
                )

                for name in MODELS_MB:

                    err = target_error_fixed_MB(
                        models[
                            name
                        ],
                        U1_truth,
                        U2_truth,
                        U10,
                        U20,
                        ENV_MB,
                    )

                    if name == "OLD2":

                        K3 = (
                            err
                            /
                            tau ** 3
                        )

                        K4 = np.nan

                    else:

                        K3 = np.nan

                        K4 = (
                            err
                            /
                            tau ** 4
                        )

                    rows.append(
                        {
                            "config": label,
                            "topology": topology,
                            "model": name,

                            "real_dtype": str(real_dtype),
                            "complex_dtype": str(complex_dtype),

                            "dt_phys": dt_phys,
                            "d_tau": d_tau,

                            "tau": tau,

                            "error_fixed": err,

                            "K3": K3,
                            "K4": K4,
                        }
                    )

                    if name == "OLD2":

                        print(
                            f" | OLD2={100*err:.8f}%",
                            end="",
                        )

                    else:

                        print(
                            f" | STAR2={100*err:.8f}%",
                            end="",
                        )

                print()

        del U10
        del U20
        del U1_truth
        del U2_truth
        del models
        del models_all

        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    return (
        pd.DataFrame(
            rows
        ),
        pd.DataFrame(
            mechanism_rows
        ),
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. RUN MATRIX
# ==================================================================================================

# Preserve original globals so the notebook is normal after M-B.
ORIGINAL_REAL_MB = REAL_DTYPE_D
ORIGINAL_COMPLEX_MB = COMPLEX_DTYPE_D

ORIGINAL_nu1_MB = float(
    nu1_D
)

ORIGINAL_nu2_MB = float(
    nu2_D
)

ORIGINAL_TAU0_MB = float(
    TAU0_D
)


all_rows_MB = []
all_mechanism_MB = []

start_MB = time.perf_counter()


try:

    for config in CONFIGS_MB:

        df_config, mech_config = run_config_MB(
            config
        )

        all_rows_MB.append(
            df_config
        )

        all_mechanism_MB.append(
            mech_config
        )

        # --------------------------------------------------------------
        # Permanent checkpoint after EVERY config.
        # --------------------------------------------------------------

        partial_results = pd.concat(
            all_rows_MB,
            ignore_index=True,
        )

        partial_mechanism = pd.concat(
            all_mechanism_MB,
            ignore_index=True,
        )

        partial_results.to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_MB_precision_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        partial_mechanism.to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_MB_mechanism_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        print()
        print(
            "💾 permanent Drive checkpoint:",
            config["label"],
        )


finally:

    # Restore original baseline arithmetic.
    REAL_DTYPE_D = ORIGINAL_REAL_MB
    COMPLEX_DTYPE_D = ORIGINAL_COMPLEX_MB

    nu1_D = ORIGINAL_nu1_MB
    nu2_D = ORIGINAL_nu2_MB
    TAU0_D = ORIGINAL_TAU0_MB


torch.cuda.synchronize()

TOTAL_RUNTIME_MB = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    start_MB
)


RESULTS_MB = pd.concat(
    all_rows_MB,
    ignore_index=True,
)

MECHANISM_MB = pd.concat(
    all_mechanism_MB,
    ignore_index=True,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. LOCAL-ORDER SUMMARY
# ==================================================================================================

summary_rows_MB = []


for (
    config,
    topology,
    model,
), sub in RESULTS_MB.groupby(
    [
        "config",
        "topology",
        "model",
    ]
):

    sub = sub.sort_values(
        "tau"
    )

    lookup = {
        float(row["tau"]):
        float(row["error_fixed"])
        for _, row
        in sub.iterrows()
    }

    def get_e(
        tau,
    ):
        return lookup.get(
            float(tau),
            np.nan,
        )

    p_003_006 = pair_order_MB(
        0.03,
        get_e(0.03),
        0.06,
        get_e(0.06),
    )

    p_006_012 = pair_order_MB(
        0.06,
        get_e(0.06),
        0.12,
        get_e(0.12),
    )

    slope_late, r2_late = fit_order_MB(
        sub,
        lo=0.045,
        hi=0.12,
    )

    row = {
        "config": config,
        "topology": topology,
        "model": model,

        "p_003_006": p_003_006,
        "p_006_012": p_006_012,

        "late_fit_slope": slope_late,
        "late_fit_R2": r2_late,

        "E_003": get_e(0.03),
        "E_006": get_e(0.06),
        "E_012": get_e(0.12),
    }

    if model == "STAR2":

        row["K4_003"] = (
            get_e(0.03)
            /
            0.03 ** 4
        )

        row["K4_006"] = (
            get_e(0.06)
            /
            0.06 ** 4
        )

        row["K4_012"] = (
            get_e(0.12)
            /
            0.12 ** 4
        )

        row[
            "K4_ratio_012_over_006"
        ] = (
            row["K4_012"]
            /
            max(
                row["K4_006"],
                1e-300,
            )
        )

    else:

        row["K3_003"] = (
            get_e(0.03)
            /
            0.03 ** 3
        )

        row["K3_006"] = (
            get_e(0.06)
            /
            0.06 ** 3
        )

        row["K3_012"] = (
            get_e(0.12)
            /
            0.12 ** 3
        )

        row[
            "K3_ratio_012_over_006"
        ] = (
            row["K3_012"]
            /
            max(
                row["K3_006"],
                1e-300,
            )
        )

    summary_rows_MB.append(
        row
    )


SUMMARY_MB = pd.DataFrame(
    summary_rows_MB
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. nuMERICAL REFINEMENT RATIOS
# ==================================================================================================

refinement_rows_MB = []


for topology in TOPOLOGIES_MB:

    for model in MODELS_MB:

        for tau in [
            0.03,
            0.06,
            0.12,
        ]:

            def error_at(
                label,
            ):
                q = RESULTS_MB[
                    (RESULTS_MB["config"] == label)
                    &
                    (RESULTS_MB["topology"] == topology)
                    &
                    (RESULTS_MB["model"] == model)
                    &
                    (
                        np.isclose(
                            RESULTS_MB["tau"],
                            tau,
                        )
                    )
                ]

                if len(q) != 1:
                    return np.nan

                return float(
                    q.iloc[0][
                        "error_fixed"
                    ]
                )

            e32_coarse = error_at(
                "fp32_dt0050"
            )

            e32_fine = error_at(
                "fp32_dt0025"
            )

            e64_coarse = error_at(
                "fp64_dt0050"
            )

            e64_fine = error_at(
                "fp64_dt0025"
            )

            refinement_rows_MB.append(
                {
                    "topology": topology,
                    "model": model,
                    "tau": tau,

                    "fp32_dt005_error":
                        e32_coarse,

                    "fp32_dt0025_error":
                        e32_fine,

                    "fp64_dt005_error":
                        e64_coarse,

                    "fp64_dt0025_error":
                        e64_fine,

                    # >1 means timestep refinement reduced measured error.
                    "fp32_dt_refinement_ratio":
                        e32_coarse
                        /
                        max(
                            e32_fine,
                            1e-300,
                        ),

                    "fp64_dt_refinement_ratio":
                        e64_coarse
                        /
                        max(
                            e64_fine,
                            1e-300,
                        ),

                    # >1 means double precision reduced measured error.
                    "precision_gain_at_dt0025":
                        e32_fine
                        /
                        max(
                            e64_fine,
                            1e-300,
                        ),
                }
            )


REFINEMENT_MB = pd.DataFrame(
    refinement_rows_MB
)


# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_MB.to_csv(

def get_star2_summary_MB(
    config,
    topology,
):
    q = STAR2_SUMMARY_MB[
        (STAR2_SUMMARY_MB["config"] == config)
        &
        (STAR2_SUMMARY_MB["topology"] == topology)
    ]

    if len(q) != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Missing STAR2 summary row."
        )

    return q.iloc[0]


ps64fine_MB = get_star2_summary_MB(
    "fp64_dt0025",
    "periodic_shear",
)

mix64fine_MB = get_star2_summary_MB(
    "fp64_dt0025",
    "mixed_vortices",
)


PERIODIC_ORDER_MB = float(
    ps64fine_MB[
        "p_006_012"
    ]
)

PERIODIC_LATE_FIT_MB = float(
    ps64fine_MB[
        "late_fit_slope"
    ]
)

PERIODIC_K4_RATIO_MB = float(
    ps64fine_MB[
        "K4_ratio_012_over_006"
    ]
)


MIXED_ORDER_MB = float(
    mix64fine_MB[
        "p_006_012"
    ]
)

MIXED_LATE_FIT_MB = float(
    mix64fine_MB[
        "late_fit_slope"
    ]
)

MIXED_K4_RATIO_MB = float(
    mix64fine_MB[
        "K4_ratio_012_over_006"
    ]
)


# Primary theory-compatible windows.

    3.5
    <=
    PERIODIC_ORDER_MB
    <=
    4.5
)

MIXED_FOURTH_ORDER_MB = bool(
    3.5
    <=
    MIXED_ORDER_MB
    <=
    4.5

    0.65
    <=

    <=
    1.55
)

    0.65
    <=
    MIXED_K4_RATIO_MB
    <=
    1.55
)


# Acceleration matching should be much cleaner in fp64.

    MECHANISM_MB[

    ==
    "fp64_dt0025"
]

)

    and
    PERIODIC_K4_STABLE_MB
    and
    MIXED_K4_STABLE_MB

)

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 12
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST M-B FIX — TRUE FP64 NAMESPACE SYNC + RESUME ONLY FP64 RUNS
#
# WHY THIS EXISTS
# ---------------
# The failed M-B run revealed:
#
#     requested REAL_DTYPE_D = float64
#     but make_env_D(...) still produced K tensors in float32
#
# and unpack_modes_D still internally created complex64 values.
#
# Cause:
# functions loaded from the rebuilt baseline retained a different Python
# globals dictionary than the notebook-level globals being changed by M-B.
#
# This patch synchronizes precision / viscosity / tau0 into EVERY relevant
# function namespace, verifies that fp64 is genuinely active, then runs only:
#
#     fp64_dt0050
#     fp64_dt0025
#
# Existing fp32 results are loaded from Drive and preserved.
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch


SAVE_DIR = Path(SAVE_DIR)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. COLLECT EVERY FUNCTION NAMESPACE THAT PARTICIPATES
# ==================================================================================================

FUNCTIONS_TO_SYNC_MB = []

for _name in [
    # rebuilt baseline
    "_fft_integer_modes_D",
    "make_env_D",
    "project_divfree_D",
    "dealias_D",
    "curl_hat_D",
    "velocity_rhs_D",
    "full_rk4_D",
    "_periodic_delta_D",
    "_make_mixed_velocity_D",
    "initial_flow_D",
    "make_projector_D",
    "pack_modes_D",
    "unpack_modes_D",
    "pair_inner_D",
    "pair_norm_D",
    "zero_target_modes_D",
    "_pair_rhs_D",
    "initialize_model_D",

    # L2 generators
    "finalize_velocity_L2",
    "grid_L2",
    "vortex_ring_single_L2",
    "periodic_shear_single_L2",
    "skew_tubes_single_L2",
    "topology_pair_L2",

    # Test M
    "pair_jvp_M",
    "complement_M",
    "build_models_M",
    "reconstruct_M",
    "rhs_M",
    "rk4_M",

    # Test M-B
    "target_error_fixed_MB",
    "run_config_MB",
]:

    obj = globals().get(_name)

    if callable(obj) and hasattr(obj, "__globals__"):
        FUNCTIONS_TO_SYNC_MB.append(obj)


# Deduplicate namespaces.
NAMESPACES_MB = []

_seen_ids_MB = set()

for fn in FUNCTIONS_TO_SYNC_MB:

    gd = fn.__globals__

    if id(gd) not in _seen_ids_MB:

        _seen_ids_MB.add(
            id(gd)
        )

        NAMESPACES_MB.append(
            gd
        )


print(
    "Distinct function namespaces found:",
    len(NAMESPACES_MB)
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. GLOBAL SYNCHRONIZER
# ==================================================================================================

def sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    real_dtype,
    complex_dtype,
    nu,
    tau0,
):
    # Current notebook namespace.
    globals()["REAL_DTYPE_D"] = real_dtype
    globals()["COMPLEX_DTYPE_D"] = complex_dtype

    globals()["nu1_D"] = float(nu)
    globals()["nu2_D"] = float(nu)
    globals()["TAU0_D"] = float(tau0)

    # Every function's own global dictionary.
    for gd in NAMESPACES_MB:

        gd["REAL_DTYPE_D"] = real_dtype
        gd["COMPLEX_DTYPE_D"] = complex_dtype

        gd["nu1_D"] = float(nu)
        gd["nu2_D"] = float(nu)
        gd["TAU0_D"] = float(tau0)


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. SWITCH TO TRUE FP64
# ==================================================================================================

sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float64,
    torch.complex128,
    nu_MB,
    TAU0_MB,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. HARD FP64 PREFLIGHT
#
# DO NOT RUN TRAJECTORIES unless every assertion below passes.
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 136)
print("TRUE FP64 PREFLIGHT")
print("=" * 136)


ENV_VERIFY_MB = make_env_D(
    40,
    L=8.0,
)


print(
    "notebook REAL_DTYPE_D:",
    REAL_DTYPE_D
)

print(
    "notebook COMPLEX_DTYPE_D:",
    COMPLEX_DTYPE_D
)

print(
    "environment KX dtype:",
    ENV_VERIFY_MB["KX"].dtype
)

print(
    "environment K2 dtype:",
    ENV_VERIFY_MB["K2"].dtype
)


assert REAL_DTYPE_D == torch.float64
assert COMPLEX_DTYPE_D == torch.complex128

assert ENV_VERIFY_MB["KX"].dtype == torch.float64, (
    "STOP: spectral environment is still not fp64."
)

assert ENV_VERIFY_MB["K2"].dtype == torch.float64, (
    "STOP: K2 is still not fp64."
)


# Generate periodic shear natively.
U1_verify_MB, U2_verify_MB = topology_pair_L2(
    "periodic_shear",
    ENV_VERIFY_MB,
)


print(
    "initial U1 dtype:",
    U1_verify_MB.dtype
)

print(
    "initial U2 dtype:",
    U2_verify_MB.dtype
)


assert U1_verify_MB.dtype == torch.complex128
assert U2_verify_MB.dtype == torch.complex128


# Build STAR2 natively.
models_verify_MB, diag_verify_MB, span_verify_MB = build_models_M(
    U1_verify_MB,
    U2_verify_MB,
    ENV_VERIFY_MB,
)


print(
    "STAR2 x dtype:",
    models_verify_MB["STAR2"]["x"].dtype
)

print(
    "STAR2 q0 dtype:",
    models_verify_MB["STAR2"]["basis"][0][0].dtype
)

print(
    "STAR2 q1* dtype:",
    models_verify_MB["STAR2"]["basis"][1][0].dtype
)

print(
    "STAR2 ||RAf||/||Af||:",
    f"{diag_verify_MB['STAR2']['RAf_ratio']:.3e}"
)

print(
    "STAR2 span residual:",
    f"{span_verify_MB:.3e}"
)


assert models_verify_MB["STAR2"]["x"].dtype == torch.float64

assert models_verify_MB["STAR2"]["basis"][0][0].dtype == torch.complex128

assert models_verify_MB["STAR2"]["basis"][1][0].dtype == torch.complex128


# This should collapse dramatically below the old ~1e-7 level.
assert diag_verify_MB["STAR2"]["RAf_ratio"] < 1e-10, (
    "\nSTOP: fp64 acceleration cancellation did not improve sufficiently.\n"
    f"Measured residual = {diag_verify_MB['STAR2']['RAf_ratio']:.3e}\n"
    "Do not run M-B trajectories until namespace precision is actually fixed."
)


# Critical unpack/reconstruct smoke test.
U1_recon_MB, U2_recon_MB = reconstruct_M(
    models_verify_MB["STAR2"],
    ENV_VERIFY_MB,
)


assert U1_recon_MB.dtype == torch.complex128
assert U2_recon_MB.dtype == torch.complex128


print()
print("✅ TRUE NATIVE FP64 PREFLIGHT PASSED.")
print("✅ Spectral operators are float64.")
print("✅ States/bases are complex128.")
print("✅ STAR2 cancellation is genuinely evaluated in fp64.")


del U1_verify_MB
del U2_verify_MB
del U1_recon_MB
del U2_recon_MB
del models_verify_MB

gc.collect()
torch.cuda.empty_cache()


# ==================================================================================================

FP32_RESULTS_PATH_MB = (
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_MB_precision_partial.csv"

    /

        "Missing saved fp32 partial results:\n"

# ==================================================================================================

FP64_CONFIGS_MB = [
    {
        "label": "fp64_dt0050",
        "real_dtype": torch.float64,
        "complex_dtype": torch.complex128,
        "dt_phys": 0.0050,
    },
    {
        "label": "fp64_dt0025",
        "real_dtype": torch.float64,
        "complex_dtype": torch.complex128,
        "dt_phys": 0.0025,
    },
]


new_results_MB = []
new_mechanism_MB = []

resume_start_MB = time.perf_counter()


try:

    for config in FP64_CONFIGS_MB:

        # IMPORTANT:
        # run_config_MB itself changes notebook globals, but now we ALSO
        # synchronize every participating function namespace first.
        sync_m3d_globals_MB(
            config[
                "real_dtype"
            ],
            config[
                "complex_dtype"
            ],
            nu_MB,
            TAU0_MB,
        )

        # Additional hard check immediately before run.
        env_check_MB = make_env_D(
            40,
            L=8.0,
        )

        if env_check_MB["KX"].dtype != torch.float64:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Precision sync disappeared before fp64 run."
            )

        df_config_MB, mech_config_MB = run_config_MB(
            config
        )

        new_results_MB.append(
            df_config_MB
        )

        new_mechanism_MB.append(
            mech_config_MB
        )

        # Save combined progress after each fp64 configuration.
        current_results_MB = pd.concat(
            [
                FP32_RESULTS_MB,
                *new_results_MB,
            ],
            ignore_index=True,
        )

        current_mechanism_MB = pd.concat(
            [
                FP32_MECHANISM_MB,
                *new_mechanism_MB,
            ],
            ignore_index=True,
        )

        current_results_MB.to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_MB_precision_resume_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        current_mechanism_MB.to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_MB_mechanism_resume_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        print()
        print(
            "💾 permanent resumed checkpoint:",
            config[
                "label"
            ]
        )


finally:

    # Return notebook/baseline to normal fp32 mode after experiment.
    sync_m3d_globals_MB(
        torch.float32,
        torch.complex64,
        nu_MB,
        TAU0_MB,
    )


torch.cuda.synchronize()


RESUME_RUNTIME_MB = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    resume_start_MB
)

# ==================================================================================================
# 8. RECOMPUTE ORDER SUMMARY
# ==================================================================================================

summary_rows_MB = []


for (
    config,
    topology,
    model,
), sub in RESULTS_MB.groupby(
    [
        "config",
        "topology",
        "model",
    ]
):

    sub = sub.sort_values(
        "tau"
    )

    lookup = {
        float(row["tau"]):
        float(row["error_fixed"])
        for _, row
        in sub.iterrows()
    }


    def get_e_MB(
        tau,
    ):
        return lookup.get(
            float(tau),
            np.nan,
        )


    p_003_006 = pair_order_MB(
        0.03,
        get_e_MB(0.03),
        0.06,
        get_e_MB(0.06),
    )

    p_006_012 = pair_order_MB(
        0.06,
        get_e_MB(0.06),
        0.12,
        get_e_MB(0.12),
    )

    slope_late, r2_late = fit_order_MB(
        sub,
        lo=0.045,
        hi=0.12,
    )


    row = {
        "config": config,
        "topology": topology,
        "model": model,

        "p_003_006": p_003_006,
        "p_006_012": p_006_012,

        "late_fit_slope": slope_late,
        "late_fit_R2": r2_late,

        "E_003": get_e_MB(
            0.03
        ),

        "E_006": get_e_MB(
            0.06
        ),

        "E_012": get_e_MB(
            0.12
        ),
    }


    if model == "STAR2":

        row["K4_003"] = (
            get_e_MB(0.03)
            /
            0.03 ** 4
        )

        row["K4_006"] = (
            get_e_MB(0.06)
            /
            0.06 ** 4
        )

        row["K4_012"] = (
            get_e_MB(0.12)
            /
            0.12 ** 4
        )

        row[
            "K4_ratio_012_over_006"
        ] = (
            row["K4_012"]
            /
            max(
                row["K4_006"],
                1e-300,
            )
        )


    else:

        row["K3_003"] = (
            get_e_MB(0.03)
            /
            0.03 ** 3
        )

        row["K3_006"] = (
            get_e_MB(0.06)
            /
            0.06 ** 3
        )

        row["K3_012"] = (
            get_e_MB(0.12)
            /
            0.12 ** 3
        )

        row[
            "K3_ratio_012_over_006"
        ] = (
            row["K3_012"]
            /
            max(
                row["K3_006"],
                1e-300,
            )
        )


    summary_rows_MB.append(
        row
    )


SUMMARY_MB = pd.DataFrame(
    summary_rows_MB
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. RECOMPUTE REFINEMENT TABLE
# ==================================================================================================

refinement_rows_MB = []


for topology in TOPOLOGIES_MB:

    for model in MODELS_MB:

        for tau in [
            0.03,
            0.06,
            0.12,
        ]:


            def error_at_MB(
                label,
            ):

                q = RESULTS_MB[
                    (RESULTS_MB["config"] == label)
                    &
                    (RESULTS_MB["topology"] == topology)
                    &
                    (RESULTS_MB["model"] == model)
                    &
                    np.isclose(
                        RESULTS_MB["tau"],
                        tau,
                    )
                ]

                if len(q) != 1:
                    return np.nan

                return float(
                    q.iloc[0][
                        "error_fixed"
                    ]
                )


            e32_coarse = error_at_MB(
                "fp32_dt0050"
            )

            e32_fine = error_at_MB(
                "fp32_dt0025"
            )

            e64_coarse = error_at_MB(
                "fp64_dt0050"
            )

            e64_fine = error_at_MB(
                "fp64_dt0025"
            )


            refinement_rows_MB.append(
                {
                    "topology": topology,
                    "model": model,
                    "tau": tau,

                    "fp32_dt005_error":
                        e32_coarse,

                    "fp32_dt0025_error":
                        e32_fine,

                    "fp64_dt005_error":
                        e64_coarse,

                    "fp64_dt0025_error":
                        e64_fine,

                    "fp32_dt_refinement_ratio":
                        e32_coarse
                        /
                        max(
                            e32_fine,
                            1e-300,
                        ),

                    "fp64_dt_refinement_ratio":
                        e64_coarse
                        /
                        max(
                            e64_fine,
                            1e-300,
                        ),

                    "precision_gain_at_dt0025":
                        e32_fine
                        /
                        max(
                            e64_fine,
                            1e-300,
                        ),
                }
            )


REFINEMENT_MB = pd.DataFrame(
    refinement_rows_MB
)

# 12. FINAL GATES — SAME SCIENTIFIC CRITERIA AS ORIGINAL M-B

def get_star2_summary_fixed_MB(
    config,
    topology,
):

    q = STAR2_SUMMARY_MB[
        (STAR2_SUMMARY_MB["config"] == config)
        &
        (STAR2_SUMMARY_MB["topology"] == topology)
    ]

    if len(q) != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Missing corrected STAR2 summary."
        )

    return q.iloc[0]


ps64fine_MB = get_star2_summary_fixed_MB(
    "fp64_dt0025",
    "periodic_shear",
)

mix64fine_MB = get_star2_summary_fixed_MB(
    "fp64_dt0025",
    "mixed_vortices",
)


PERIODIC_ORDER_MB = float(
    ps64fine_MB[
        "p_006_012"
    ]
)

PERIODIC_LATE_FIT_MB = float(
    ps64fine_MB[
        "late_fit_slope"
    ]
)

PERIODIC_K4_RATIO_MB = float(
    ps64fine_MB[
        "K4_ratio_012_over_006"
    ]
)


MIXED_ORDER_MB = float(
    mix64fine_MB[
        "p_006_012"
    ]
)

MIXED_LATE_FIT_MB = float(
    mix64fine_MB[
        "late_fit_slope"
    ]
)

MIXED_K4_RATIO_MB = float(
    mix64fine_MB[
        "K4_ratio_012_over_006"
    ]
)


PERIODIC_FOURTH_ORDER_MB = bool(
    3.5
    <=
    PERIODIC_ORDER_MB
    <=
    4.5
)

MIXED_FOURTH_ORDER_MB = bool(
    3.5
    <=
    MIXED_ORDER_MB
    <=
    4.5
)

    0.65
    <=
    PERIODIC_K4_RATIO_MB
    <=
    1.55
)

    0.65
    <=
    MIXED_K4_RATIO_MB
    <=
    1.55

    MECHANISM_MB[

    ==
    "fp64_dt0025"

    <

    and

# Distinct function namespaces found: 2
# 
# ========================================================================================================================================
# TRUE FP64 PREFLIGHT
# ========================================================================================================================================
# notebook REAL_DTYPE_D: torch.float64
# notebook COMPLEX_DTYPE_D: torch.complex128
# environment KX dtype: torch.float64
# environment K2 dtype: torch.float64
# initial U1 dtype: torch.complex128
# initial U2 dtype: torch.complex128
