# ==================================================================================================
# 3. STAR2 INITIALIZER
#
# This uses the SAME baseline initializer to establish:
#
#   P
#   q0
#   target coordinates
#   target scaling
#   hidden scaling
#
# It replaces ONLY old q1 with:
#
#       q1_star = normalized R1 A f
#
# where:
#
#       f  = F(U0)
#       Af = DF(U0) f
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def initialize_star2_N(
    U10,
    U20,
    env,
):
    base = initialize_model_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        PROJECTOR_N,
        env,
    )

    tids = base[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    q0 = (
        base["q0_U1"].clone(),
        base["q0_U2"].clone(),
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # f = F(U0)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    f1, f2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        env,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Af = DF(U0) f
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    Af1, Af2 = pair_jvp_M(
        U10,
        U20,
        f1,
        f2,
        env,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # q1* = R1 Af / ||R1 Af||
    # R1 = I - P - q0 q0*
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    r1, r2 = complement_M(
        Af1,
        Af2,
        tids,
        [
            q0,
        ],
    )

    rnorm = pair_norm_D(
        r1,
        r2,
    )

    rnorm_float = float(
        rnorm.detach().cpu()
    )

    if (
        not np.isfinite(
            rnorm_float
        )
        or
        rnorm_float < 1e-12
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "STAR2 q1* became numerically degenerate."
        )

    q1_star = (
        r1 / rnorm,
        r2 / rnorm,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Verify acceleration residual.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    RAf1, RAf2 = complement_M(
        Af1,
        Af2,
        tids,
        [
            q0,
            q1_star,
        ],
    )

    Af_norm = float(
        pair_norm_D(
            Af1,
            Af2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    RAf_ratio = float(
        (
            pair_norm_D(
                RAf1,
                RAf2,
            )
            /
            (
                Af_norm
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Generic Test-M model representation.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    model = {
        "name":
            "STAR2",

        "proj":
            base["proj"],

        "x_scale":
            base["x_scale"].clone(),

        "hidden_scale":
            base["hidden_scale"].clone(),

        "x":
            base["x"].clone(),

        "z":
            torch.zeros(
                2,
                device=DEVICE_D,
                dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
            ),

        "anchor_U1":
            U10.clone(),

        "anchor_U2":
            U20.clone(),

        "basis": [
            (
                q0[0].clone(),
                q0[1].clone(),
            ),
            (
                q1_star[0].clone(),
                q1_star[1].clone(),
            ),
        ],
    }

    diagnostics = {
        "RAf_ratio":
            RAf_ratio,

        "Af_norm":
            Af_norm,

        "q1star_raw_norm":
            rnorm_float,
    }

    return (
        model,
        diagnostics,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. STAR2 TEACHER-FREE REANCHOR
#
# CRITICAL:
#
# This reconstructs STAR2's OWN predicted state.
#
# Truth is never supplied.
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def reanchor_star2_N(
    model,
    env,
):
    before1, before2 = reconstruct_M(
        model,
        env,
    )

    t0 = time.perf_counter()

    new_model, diagnostics = initialize_star2_N(
        before1,
        before2,
        env,
    )

    build_time = (
        time.perf_counter()
        -
        t0
    )

    after1, after2 = reconstruct_M(
        new_model,
        env,
    )

    jump = float(
        (
            pair_norm_D(
                after1 - before1,
                after2 - before2,
            )
            /
            (
                pair_norm_D(
                    before1,
                    before2,
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    return (
        new_model,
        diagnostics,
        jump,
        build_time,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. OLD2 MECHANISM DIAGNOSTIC
#
# We do NOT alter OLD2.
#
# This simply measures:
#
#       ||R2 Af|| / ||Af||
#
# at its current anchor.
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def old2_accel_residual_N(
    model,
    env,
):
    U10 = model[
        "anchor_U1"
    ]

    U20 = model[
        "anchor_U2"
    ]

    tids = model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    q0 = (
        model["q0_U1"],
        model["q0_U2"],
    )

    q1 = (
        model["q1_U1"],
        model["q1_U2"],
    )

    f1, f2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        env,
    )

    Af1, Af2 = pair_jvp_M(
        U10,
        U20,
        f1,
        f2,
        env,
    )

    RAf1, RAf2 = complement_M(
        Af1,
        Af2,
        tids,
        [
            q0,
            q1,
        ],
    )

    return float(
        (
            pair_norm_D(
                RAf1,
                RAf2,
            )
            /
            (
                pair_norm_D(
                    Af1,
                    Af2,
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. OLD2 REANCHOR WRAPPER + CONTInuITY CHECK
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def reanchor_old2_N(
    model,
    env,
):
    before1, before2 = current_full_state_D(
        model,
        env,
    )

    t0 = time.perf_counter()

    new_model = reanchor_model_D(
        model,
        env,
    )

    build_time = (
        time.perf_counter()
        -
        t0
    )

    after1, after2 = current_full_state_D(
        new_model,
        env,
    )

    jump = float(
        (
            pair_norm_D(
                after1 - before1,
                after2 - before2,
            )
            /
            (
                pair_norm_D(
                    before1,
                    before2,
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    accel_residual = old2_accel_residual_N(
        new_model,
        env,
    )

    return (
        new_model,
        accel_residual,
        jump,
        build_time,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. COMMON SCORING
#
# STATE:
#
#   ||P U_model - P U_truth|| / ||P U_truth||
#
# RHS:
#
#   ||P F(U_model) - P F(U_truth)|| / ||P F(U_truth)||
#
# Truth is scoring-only.
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def score_pair_N(
    U1_model,
    U2_model,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
    tids,
    env,
):
    xm = pack_modes_D(
        U1_model,
        U2_model,
        tids,
    )

    xt = pack_modes_D(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
    )

    state_err = float(
        (
            torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                xm - xt
            )
            /
            (
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                    xt
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    Fm1, Fm2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1_model,
        U2_model,
        env,
    )

    Ft1, Ft2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        env,
    )

    fm = pack_modes_D(
        Fm1,
        Fm2,
        tids,
    )

    ft = pack_modes_D(
        Ft1,
        Ft2,
        tids,
    )

    rhs_err = float(
        (
            torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                fm - ft
            )
            /
            (
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                    ft
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    return (
        state_err,
        rhs_err,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def score_models_N(
    old2,
    star2,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
    env,
):
    old1, old2_state = current_full_state_D(
        old2,
        env,
    )

    star1, star2_state = reconstruct_M(
        star2,
        env,
    )

    tids = old2[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    old_state_err, old_rhs_err = score_pair_N(
        old1,
        old2_state,
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
        env,
    )

    star_state_err, star_rhs_err = score_pair_N(
        star1,
        star2_state,
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
        env,
    )

    return {
        "old2_state":
            old_state_err,

        "old2_rhs":
            old_rhs_err,

        "star2_state":
            star_state_err,

        "star2_rhs":
            star_rhs_err,
    }


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. OPTIONAL L2 REPRODUCIBILITY REFERENCE
#
# If the old L2 CSV is present, we compare OLD2 against it afterward.
# This is NOT needed to run Test N.
# ==================================================================================================

L2_REFERENCE_PATH_N = (
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_L2_12case_final.csv"
)

if L2_REFERENCE_PATH_N.exists():

    L2_REFERENCE_N = pd.read_csv(
        L2_REFERENCE_PATH_N
    )

    print()
    print(
        "✅ Found L2 reference for OLD2 reproducibility:"
    )

    print(
        L2_REFERENCE_PATH_N
    )

else:

    L2_REFERENCE_N = None

    print()
    print(
        "ℹ️ L2 reference CSV not found; reproducibility comparison will be skipped."
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. RUN 12 CASES
# ==================================================================================================

results_rows_N = []
checkpoint_rows_N = []
reanchor_rows_N = []

case_number_N = 0

total_start_N = time.perf_counter()


for topology in TOPOLOGIES_N:

    for nu_mult in nu_MULTS_N:

        case_number_N += 1

        nu = (
            BASE_nu_N
            *
            nu_mult
        )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Synchronize BOTH notebook + baseline namespaces.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        sync_m3d_globals_MB(
            torch.float32,
            torch.complex64,
            nu,
            TAU0_N,
        )

        print()
        print("#" * 140)
        print(
            f"CASE {case_number_N:02d}/12"
            f" | {topology:16s}"
            f" | nu x {nu_mult:.2f}"
        )
        print("#" * 140)

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # SAME deterministic initial condition for truth + both models.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        U10, U20 = topology_pair_L2(
            topology,
            ENV_N,
        )

        U1_truth = U10.clone()
        U2_truth = U20.clone()

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Build OLD2 — exact baseline implementation.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        t_build_old = time.perf_counter()

        old2 = initialize_model_D(
            U10,
            U20,
            PROJECTOR_N,
            ENV_N,
        )

        initial_build_old = (
            time.perf_counter()
            -
            t_build_old
        )

        old_initial_RAf = old2_accel_residual_N(
            old2,
            ENV_N,
        )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Build STAR2.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        t_build_star = time.perf_counter()

        star2, star_diag = initialize_star2_N(
            U10,
            U20,
            ENV_N,
        )

        initial_build_star = (
            time.perf_counter()
            -
            t_build_star
        )

        star_initial_RAf = star_diag[
            "RAf_ratio"
        ]

        print()
        print(
            "INITIAL MECHANISM:"
        )

        print(
            f"  OLD2  ||RAf||/||Af|| = {old_initial_RAf:.3e}"
        )

        print(
            f"  STAR2 ||RAf||/||Af|| = {star_initial_RAf:.3e}"
        )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Track reanchor stats.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        old_reanchors = 0
        star_reanchors = 0

        old_rebuild_total = 0.0
        star_rebuild_total = 0.0

        old_worst_jump = 0.0
        star_worst_jump = 0.0

        old_worst_RAf = old_initial_RAf
        star_worst_RAf = star_initial_RAf

        next_report_i = 0

        case_start = time.perf_counter()

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Integrate.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        for step in range(
            1,
            TOTAL_STEPS_N + 1,
        ):

            # ==============================================================
            # FULL NS TRUTH — scoring only
            # ==============================================================

            U1_truth = full_rk4_D(
                U1_truth,
                DT_PHYS_N,
                nu,
                ENV_N,
            )

            U2_truth = full_rk4_D(
                U2_truth,
                DT_PHYS_N,
                nu,
                ENV_N,
            )

            # ==============================================================
            # OLD2
            # ==============================================================

            m3d_rk4_D(
                old2,
                DTAU_N,
                old2[
                    "anchor_U1"
                ],
                old2[
                    "anchor_U2"
                ],
                ENV_N,
            )

            # ==============================================================
            # STAR2
            # ==============================================================

            rk4_M(
                star2,
                DTAU_N,
                ENV_N,
            )

            tau = (
                step
                *
                DTAU_N
            )

            # ==============================================================
            # TEACHER-FREE REANCHOR
            #
            # Exactly every 0.15 tau.
            # ==============================================================

            if (
                step
                %
                REANCHOR_STEPS_N
                ==
                0
                and
                tau
                <
                FINAL_TAU_N
            ):

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # OLD2 reanchor
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                (
                    old2,
                    old_RAf,
                    old_jump,
                    old_build_time,
                ) = reanchor_old2_N(
                    old2,
                    ENV_N,
                )

                old_reanchors += 1

                old_rebuild_total += (
                    old_build_time
                )

                old_worst_jump = max(
                    old_worst_jump,
                    old_jump,
                )

                old_worst_RAf = max(
                    old_worst_RAf,
                    old_RAf,
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------
                # STAR2 reanchor
                # ----------------------------------------------------------

                (
                    star2,
                    star_diag,
                    star_jump,
                    star_build_time,
                ) = reanchor_star2_N(
                    star2,
                    ENV_N,
                )

                star_RAf = star_diag[
                    "RAf_ratio"
                ]

                star_reanchors += 1

                star_rebuild_total += (
                    star_build_time
                )

                star_worst_jump = max(
                    star_worst_jump,
                    star_jump,
                )

                star_worst_RAf = max(
                    star_worst_RAf,
                    star_RAf,
                )

                reanchor_rows_N.append(
                    {
                        "case":
                            case_number_N,

                        "topology":
                            topology,

                        "nu_mult":
                            nu_mult,

                        "tau":
                            tau,

                        "old2_RAf_ratio":
                            old_RAf,

                        "star2_RAf_ratio":
                            star_RAf,

                        "old2_jump":
                            old_jump,

                        "star2_jump":
                            star_jump,

                        "old2_build_s":
                            old_build_time,

                        "star2_build_s":
                            star_build_time,
                    }
                )

            # ==============================================================
            # REPORT CHECKPOINT
            # ==============================================================

            if (
                next_report_i
                <
                len(
                    REPORT_TAUS_N
                )
                and
                tau
                >=
                REPORT_TAUS_N[
                    next_report_i
                ]
                -
                0.5
                *
                DTAU_N
            ):

                scores = score_models_N(
                    old2,
                    star2,
                    U1_truth,
                    U2_truth,
                    ENV_N,
                )

                state_gain = (
                    scores[
                        "old2_state"
                    ]
                    /
                    max(
                        scores[
                            "star2_state"
                        ],
                        1e-30,
                    )
                )

                rhs_gain = (
                    scores[
                        "old2_rhs"
                    ]
                    /
                    max(
                        scores[
                            "star2_rhs"
                        ],
                        1e-30,
                    )
                )

                checkpoint_rows_N.append(
                    {
                        "case":
                            case_number_N,

                        "topology":
                            topology,

                        "nu_mult":
                            nu_mult,

                        "tau":
                            tau,

                        "old2_state_error":
                            scores[
                                "old2_state"
                            ],

                        "star2_state_error":
                            scores[
                                "star2_state"
                            ],

                        "state_gain_old_over_star":
                            state_gain,

                        "old2_rhs_error":
                            scores[
                                "old2_rhs"
                            ],

                        "star2_rhs_error":
                            scores[
                                "star2_rhs"
                            ],

                        "rhs_gain_old_over_star":
                            rhs_gain,
                    }
                )

                print(
                    f"    tau={tau:6.3f}"
                    f" | OLD state={100*scores['old2_state']:8.3f}%"
                    f" | STAR state={100*scores['star2_state']:8.3f}%"
                    f" | OLD RHS={100*scores['old2_rhs']:8.3f}%"
                    f" | STAR RHS={100*scores['star2_rhs']:8.3f}%"
                    f" | state gain={state_gain:7.3f}x"
                )

                next_report_i += 1

            if tau >= FINAL_TAU_N:
                break

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # FINAL SCORE
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        final_tau = (
            step
            *
            DTAU_N
        )

        final_scores = score_models_N(
            old2,
            star2,
            U1_truth,
            U2_truth,
            ENV_N,
        )

        final_state_gain = (
            final_scores[
                "old2_state"
            ]
            /
            max(
                final_scores[
                    "star2_state"
                ],
                1e-30,
            )
        )

        final_rhs_gain = (
            final_scores[
                "old2_rhs"
            ]
            /
            max(
                final_scores[
                    "star2_rhs"
                ],
                1e-30,
            )
        )

        star_state_win = bool(
            final_scores[
                "star2_state"
            ]
            <
            final_scores[
                "old2_state"
            ]
        )

        star_rhs_win = bool(
            final_scores[
                "star2_rhs"
            ]
            <
            final_scores[
                "old2_rhs"
            ]
        )

        case_runtime = (
            time.perf_counter()
            -
            case_start
        )

        results_rows_N.append(
            {
                "case":
                    case_number_N,

                "topology":
                    topology,

                "nu_mult":
                    nu_mult,

                "nu":
                    nu,

                "final_tau":
                    final_tau,

                "old2_state_error":
                    final_scores[
                        "old2_state"
                    ],

                "star2_state_error":
                    final_scores[
                        "star2_state"
                    ],

                "state_gain_old_over_star":
                    final_state_gain,

                "old2_rhs_error":
                    final_scores[
                        "old2_rhs"
                    ],

                "star2_rhs_error":
                    final_scores[
                        "star2_rhs"
                    ],

                "rhs_gain_old_over_star":
                    final_rhs_gain,

                "star2_state_win":
                    star_state_win,

                "star2_rhs_win":
                    star_rhs_win,

                "old2_initial_RAf":
                    old_initial_RAf,

                "star2_initial_RAf":
                    star_initial_RAf,

                "old2_worst_RAf":
                    old_worst_RAf,

                "star2_worst_RAf":
                    star_worst_RAf,

                "old2_initial_build_s":
                    initial_build_old,

                "star2_initial_build_s":
                    initial_build_star,

                "old2_reanchors":
                    old_reanchors,

                "star2_reanchors":
                    star_reanchors,

                "old2_rebuild_total_s":
                    old_rebuild_total,

                "star2_rebuild_total_s":
                    star_rebuild_total,

                "old2_worst_jump":
                    old_worst_jump,

                "star2_worst_jump":
                    star_worst_jump,

                "case_runtime_s":
                    case_runtime,
            }
        )

        print()
        print(
            "FINAL"
            f" | OLD2 state={100*final_scores['old2_state']:.4f}%"
            f" | STAR2 state={100*final_scores['star2_state']:.4f}%"
            f" | gain={final_state_gain:.3f}x"
            f" | OLD2 RHS={100*final_scores['old2_rhs']:.4f}%"
            f" | STAR2 RHS={100*final_scores['star2_rhs']:.4f}%"
        )

        print(
            f"      STAR2 worst ||RAf||/||Af|| = {star_worst_RAf:.3e}"
        )

        print(
            f"      reanchors = OLD {old_reanchors} / STAR {star_reanchors}"
        )

        print(
            f"      worst jumps = OLD {100*old_worst_jump:.8f}%"
            f" / STAR {100*star_worst_jump:.8f}%"
        )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # SAVE AFTER EVERY CASE
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        pd.DataFrame(
            results_rows_N
        ).to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_N_results_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        pd.DataFrame(
            checkpoint_rows_N
        ).to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_N_checkpoints_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        pd.DataFrame(
            reanchor_rows_N
        ).to_csv(
            SAVE_DIR
            /
            "m3d_N_reanchors_partial.csv",
            index=False,
        )

        print(
            "      💾 permanent Drive checkpoint saved"
        )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # CLEAN GPU
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

        del U10
        del U20
        del U1_truth
        del U2_truth
        del old2
        del star2

        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()


torch.cuda.synchronize()

TOTAL_RUNTIME_N = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    total_start_N
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 10. FINAL DATAFRAMES
# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_N = pd.DataFrame(
    results_rows_N
)

CHECKPOINTS_N = pd.DataFrame(
    checkpoint_rows_N
)

REANCHORS_N = pd.DataFrame(
    reanchor_rows_N
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. L2 OLD2 REPRODUCIBILITY AUDIT
# ==================================================================================================

REPRO_N = None


if L2_REFERENCE_N is not None:

    needed_cols = {
        "topology",
        "nu_mult",
        "m3d2_state_error",
        "m3d2_rhs_error",
    }

    if needed_cols.issubset(
        L2_REFERENCE_N.columns
    ):

        repro = RESULTS_N.merge(
            L2_REFERENCE_N[
                [
                    "topology",
                    "nu_mult",
                    "m3d2_state_error",
                    "m3d2_rhs_error",
                ]
            ],
            on=[
                "topology",
                "nu_mult",
            ],
            how="left",
        )

        repro[
            "state_abs_difference"
        ] = np.abs(
            repro[
                "old2_state_error"
            ]
            -
            repro[
                "m3d2_state_error"
            ]
        )

        repro[
            "rhs_abs_difference"
        ] = np.abs(
            repro[
                "old2_rhs_error"
            ]
            -
            repro[
                "m3d2_rhs_error"
            ]
        )

        repro[
            "state_relative_difference"
        ] = (
            repro[
                "state_abs_difference"
            ]
            /
            np.maximum(
                np.abs(
                    repro[
                        "m3d2_state_error"
                    ]
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )

        repro[
            "rhs_relative_difference"
        ] = (
            repro[
                "rhs_abs_difference"
            ]
            /
            np.maximum(
                np.abs(
                    repro[
                        "m3d2_rhs_error"
                    ]
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )

        REPRO_N = repro[
            [
                "topology",
                "nu_mult",

                "old2_state_error",
                "m3d2_state_error",

                "state_abs_difference",
                "state_relative_difference",

                "old2_rhs_error",
                "m3d2_rhs_error",

                "rhs_abs_difference",
                "rhs_relative_difference",
            ]
        ]


# ==================================================================================================
# 12. SUMMARY TABLES
# ==================================================================================================

TOPOLOGY_SUMMARY_N = (
    RESULTS_N
    .groupby(
        "topology",
        as_index=False,
    )
    .agg(
        old2_state_median=(
            "old2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_state_median=(
            "star2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_state_worst=(
            "star2_state_error",
            "max",
        ),

        state_gain_median=(
            "state_gain_old_over_star",
            "median",
        ),

        old2_rhs_median=(
            "old2_rhs_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_rhs_median=(
            "star2_rhs_error",
            "median",
        ),

        rhs_gain_median=(
            "rhs_gain_old_over_star",
            "median",
        ),

        state_wins=(
            "star2_state_win",
            "sum",
        ),

        rhs_wins=(
            "star2_rhs_win",
            "sum",
        ),
    )
)


VISCOSITY_SUMMARY_N = (
    RESULTS_N
    .groupby(
        "nu_mult",
        as_index=False,
    )
    .agg(
        old2_state_median=(
            "old2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_state_median=(
            "star2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_state_worst=(
            "star2_state_error",
            "max",
        ),

        state_gain_median=(
            "state_gain_old_over_star",
            "median",
        ),

        old2_rhs_median=(

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 14
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST N — CORRECTED
# OLD2 vs STAR2 | 12-CASE TEACHER-FREE LONG-HORIZON GAUNTLET
# ==================================================================================================
#
# QUESTION
# --------
# STAR2 has already shown:
#
#     q1* ~ R1 DF(U0)F(U0)
#
# and genuine local resolved error:
#
#     P e(t) ~ O(t^4)
#
# while OLD2 behaves approximately:
#
#     P e(t) ~ O(t^3).
#
# Now test whether this local-order improvement matters over tau = 2
# with teacher-free reanchoring every 0.15.
#
#
# CASES
# -----
# 4 topologies x 3 viscosities = 12
#
# topologies:
#   vortex_ring
#   periodic_shear
#   skew_tubes
#   mixed_vortices
#
# viscosity multipliers:
#   0.25, 1.0, 4.0
#
#
# FROZEN
# ------
# N = 40
# L = 8
# projector = mixed
# base nu = 0.0025
# tau0 = 40/3
# physical dt = 0.01
# dimensionless dtau = 0.00075
# final tau = 2
# reanchor interval = 0.15
#
#
# IMPORTANT
# ---------
# - OLD2 is the original baseline M3D-2.
# - STAR2 differs ONLY in q1:
#
#       q1_star = normalized (I - P - q0 q0*) A f
#
# - Both models reanchor from THEIR OWN predicted states.
# - Truth is used ONLY for scoring.
# - Runs fp32/complex64, matching the long-horizon L2 regime.
#
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch


# ==================================================================================================
# 0. DEPENDENCY CHECK
# ==================================================================================================

REQUIRED_N = [
    "make_env_D",
    "initialize_model_D",
    "m3d_rk4_D",
    "reanchor_model_D",
    "current_full_state_D",
    "full_rk4_D",
    "_pair_rhs_D",

    "pack_modes_D",
    "pair_norm_D",

    "topology_pair_L2",

    "pair_jvp_M",
    "complement_M",
    "reconstruct_M",
    "rk4_M",

    "sync_m3d_globals_MB",

    "NU1_D",
    "NU2_D",
    "TAU0_D",
    "REAL_DTYPE_D",
    "COMPLEX_DTYPE_D",
    "DEVICE_D",
]

missing_N = [
    name for name in REQUIRED_N
    if name not in globals()
]

if missing_N:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Missing Test-N dependencies:\n"
        + repr(missing_N)
        + "\n\nRun baseline + L2 definitions + Test M + the M-B precision fix first."
    )

if not torch.cuda.is_available():
    raise RuntimeError("CUDA GPU required.")

if "SAVE_DIR" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError("SAVE_DIR missing.")

SAVE_DIR = Path(SAVE_DIR)
SAVE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. FROZEN SETTINGS
# ==================================================================================================

GRID_N = 40
DOMAIN_L_N = 8.0

TOPOLOGIES_N = [
    "vortex_ring",
    "periodic_shear",
    "skew_tubes",
    "mixed_vortices",
]

NU_MULTS_N = [
    0.25,
    1.0,
    4.0,
]

BASE_NU_N = 2.5e-3

TAU0_N = 40.0 / 3.0

DT_PHYS_N = 0.01

DTAU_N = DT_PHYS_N / TAU0_N

FINAL_TAU_N = 2.0

REANCHOR_INTERVAL_N = 0.15

REANCHOR_STEPS_N = int(
    round(REANCHOR_INTERVAL_N / DTAU_N)
)

TOTAL_STEPS_N = int(
    math.ceil(FINAL_TAU_N / DTAU_N)
)

REPORT_TAUS_N = [
    0.5,
    1.0,
    1.5,
    2.0,
]

PROJECTOR_N = "mixed"


# ==================================================================================================

# ==================================================================================================

sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float32,
    torch.complex64,
    BASE_NU_N,
    TAU0_N,
)

ENV_N = make_env_D(
    GRID_N,
    L=DOMAIN_L_N,
)


print("=" * 140)
print("M3D TEST N — CORRECTED OLD2 vs STAR2 12-CASE GAUNTLET")
print("=" * 140)

print()
print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0))

print("steps/reanchor:", REANCHOR_STEPS_N)
print("final tau:", FINAL_TAU_N)
print("steps/case:", TOTAL_STEPS_N)
print("total cases:", len(TOPOLOGIES_N) * len(NU_MULTS_N))


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. STAR2 INITIALIZER
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def initialize_star2_N(
    U10,
    U20,
    env,
):
    # Use baseline initialization so P, q0, coordinate packing,
    # x_scale and hidden_scale are identical to OLD2.

    base = initialize_model_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        PROJECTOR_N,
        env,
    )

    tids = base["proj"]["target_ids"]

    q0 = (
        base["q0_U1"].clone(),
        base["q0_U2"].clone(),
    )

    # f = F(U0)
    f1, f2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        env,
    )

    # Af = DF(U0) f
    Af1, Af2 = pair_jvp_M(
        U10,
        U20,
        f1,
        f2,
        env,
    )

    # q1_star = normalized R1 Af
    r1, r2 = complement_M(
        Af1,
        Af2,
        tids,
        [q0],
    )

    raw_norm = pair_norm_D(
        r1,
        r2,
    )

    raw_norm_value = float(
        raw_norm.detach().cpu()
    )

    if (
        not np.isfinite(raw_norm_value)
        or raw_norm_value < 1e-12
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "STAR2 q1* became numerically degenerate."
        )

    q1_star = (
        r1 / raw_norm,
        r2 / raw_norm,
    )

    # Check residual acceleration outside STAR2 span.
    RAf1, RAf2 = complement_M(
        Af1,
        Af2,
        tids,
        [
            q0,
            q1_star,
        ],
    )

    Af_norm = float(
        pair_norm_D(
            Af1,
            Af2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    RAf_ratio = float(
        (
            pair_norm_D(
                RAf1,
                RAf2,
            )
            /
            (
                Af_norm
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    model = {
        "name": "STAR2",

        "proj": base["proj"],

        "x_scale":
            base["x_scale"].clone(),

        "hidden_scale":
            base["hidden_scale"].clone(),

        "x":
            base["x"].clone(),

        "z":
            torch.zeros(
                2,
                device=DEVICE_D,
                dtype=REAL_DTYPE_D,
            ),

        "anchor_U1":
            U10.clone(),

        "anchor_U2":
            U20.clone(),

        "basis": [
            (
                q0[0].clone(),
                q0[1].clone(),
            ),
            (
                q1_star[0].clone(),
                q1_star[1].clone(),
            ),
        ],
    }

    diagnostics = {
        "RAf_ratio":
            RAf_ratio,

        "Af_norm":
            Af_norm,

        "q1star_raw_norm":
            raw_norm_value,
    }

    return model, diagnostics


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. STAR2 TEACHER-FREE REANCHOR
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def reanchor_star2_N(
    model,
    env,
):
    # STAR2's OWN current predicted state.
    before1, before2 = reconstruct_M(
        model,
        env,
    )

    t0 = time.perf_counter()

    new_model, diagnostics = initialize_star2_N(
        before1,
        before2,
        env,
    )

    build_time = (
        time.perf_counter()
        -
        t0
    )

    after1, after2 = reconstruct_M(
        new_model,
        env,
    )

    jump = float(
        (
            pair_norm_D(
                after1 - before1,
                after2 - before2,
            )
            /
            (
                pair_norm_D(
                    before1,
                    before2,
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    return (
        new_model,
        diagnostics,
        jump,
        build_time,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. OLD2 ACCELERATION-DEFECT DIAGNOSTIC
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def old2_accel_residual_N(
    model,
    env,
):
    U10 = model["anchor_U1"]
    U20 = model["anchor_U2"]

    tids = model["proj"]["target_ids"]

    q0 = (
        model["q0_U1"],
        model["q0_U2"],
    )

    q1 = (
        model["q1_U1"],
        model["q1_U2"],
    )

    f1, f2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U10,
        U20,
        env,
    )

    Af1, Af2 = pair_jvp_M(
        U10,
        U20,
        f1,
        f2,
        env,
    )

    RAf1, RAf2 = complement_M(
        Af1,
        Af2,
        tids,
        [
            q0,
            q1,
        ],
    )

    return float(
        (
            pair_norm_D(
                RAf1,
                RAf2,
            )
            /
            (
                pair_norm_D(
                    Af1,
                    Af2,
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. OLD2 TEACHER-FREE REANCHOR WRAPPER
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def reanchor_old2_N(
    model,
    env,
):
    before1, before2 = current_full_state_D(
        model,
        env,
    )

    t0 = time.perf_counter()

    new_model = reanchor_model_D(
        model,
        env,
    )

    build_time = (
        time.perf_counter()
        -
        t0
    )

    after1, after2 = current_full_state_D(
        new_model,
        env,
    )

    jump = float(
        (
            pair_norm_D(
                after1 - before1,
                after2 - before2,
            )
            /
            (
                pair_norm_D(
                    before1,
                    before2,
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    accel_residual = old2_accel_residual_N(
        new_model,
        env,
    )

    return (
        new_model,
        accel_residual,
        jump,
        build_time,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. SCORING
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def score_pair_N(
    U1_model,
    U2_model,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
    tids,
    env,
):
    x_model = pack_modes_D(
        U1_model,
        U2_model,
        tids,
    )

    x_truth = pack_modes_D(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
    )

    state_error = float(
        (
            torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                x_model - x_truth
            )
            /
            (
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                    x_truth
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    Fm1, Fm2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1_model,
        U2_model,
        env,
    )

    Ft1, Ft2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        env,
    )

    f_model = pack_modes_D(
        Fm1,
        Fm2,
        tids,
    )

    f_truth = pack_modes_D(
        Ft1,
        Ft2,
        tids,
    )

    rhs_error = float(
        (
            torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                f_model - f_truth
            )
            /
            (
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                    f_truth
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )

    return (
        state_error,
        rhs_error,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def score_models_N(
    old_model,
    star_model,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
    env,
):
    old_U1, old_U2 = current_full_state_D(
        old_model,
        env,
    )

    star_U1, star_U2 = reconstruct_M(
        star_model,
        env,
    )

    tids = old_model["proj"]["target_ids"]

    old_state, old_rhs = score_pair_N(
        old_U1,
        old_U2,
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
        env,
    )

    star_state, star_rhs = score_pair_N(
        star_U1,
        star_U2,
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
        env,
    )

    return {
        "old2_state": old_state,
        "old2_rhs": old_rhs,
        "star2_state": star_state,
        "star2_rhs": star_rhs,
    }


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. OPTIONAL L2 REPRODUCIBILITY REFERENCE
# ==================================================================================================

L2_REFERENCE_PATH_N = (
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_L2_12case_final.csv"
)

if L2_REFERENCE_PATH_N.exists():

    L2_REFERENCE_N = pd.read_csv(
        L2_REFERENCE_PATH_N
    )

    print()
    print(
        "✅ Found L2 OLD2 reference:",
        L2_REFERENCE_PATH_N,
    )

else:

    L2_REFERENCE_N = None

    print()
    print(
        "ℹ️ No L2 reference CSV found; reproducibility audit skipped."
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. RUN ALL 12 CASES
# ==================================================================================================

results_rows_N = []
checkpoint_rows_N = []
reanchor_rows_N = []

case_number_N = 0

total_start_N = time.perf_counter()


try:

    for topology in TOPOLOGIES_N:

        for nu_mult in NU_MULTS_N:

            case_number_N += 1

            # IMPORTANT:
            # This is the ONLY per-case viscosity variable.
            case_nu = (
                BASE_NU_N
                *
                float(nu_mult)
            )

            # Synchronize _pair_rhs_D etc. to THIS case viscosity.
            sync_m3d_globals_MB(
                torch.float32,
                torch.complex64,
                case_nu,
                TAU0_N,
            )

            print()
            print("#" * 140)

            print(
                f"CASE {case_number_N:02d}/12"
                f" | {topology:16s}"
                f" | nu x {nu_mult:.2f}"
                f" | nu={case_nu:.7g}"
            )

            print("#" * 140)

            # ======================================================================================
            # IDENTICAL INITIAL STATE
            # ======================================================================================

            U10, U20 = topology_pair_L2(
                topology,
                ENV_N,
            )

            U1_truth = U10.clone()
            U2_truth = U20.clone()

            # ======================================================================================
            # OLD2 BUILD
            # ======================================================================================

            t0 = time.perf_counter()

            old_model = initialize_model_D(
                U10,
                U20,
                PROJECTOR_N,
                ENV_N,
            )

            old_initial_build = (
                time.perf_counter()
                -
                t0
            )

            old_initial_RAf = old2_accel_residual_N(
                old_model,
                ENV_N,
            )

            # ======================================================================================
            # STAR2 BUILD
            # ======================================================================================

            t0 = time.perf_counter()

            star_model, star_diag = initialize_star2_N(
                U10,
                U20,
                ENV_N,
            )

            star_initial_build = (
                time.perf_counter()
                -
                t0
            )

            star_initial_RAf = float(
                star_diag["RAf_ratio"]
            )

            print()
            print("INITIAL MECHANISM:")

            print(
                f"  OLD2  ||RAf||/||Af|| = {old_initial_RAf:.3e}"
            )

            print(
                f"  STAR2 ||RAf||/||Af|| = {star_initial_RAf:.3e}"
            )

            # ======================================================================================
            # TRACKING
            # ======================================================================================

            old_reanchors = 0
            star_reanchors = 0

            old_rebuild_total = 0.0
            star_rebuild_total = 0.0

            old_worst_jump = 0.0
            star_worst_jump = 0.0

            old_worst_RAf = old_initial_RAf
            star_worst_RAf = star_initial_RAf

            next_report_i = 0

            case_start = time.perf_counter()

            # ======================================================================================
            # INTEGRATION
            # ======================================================================================

            for step in range(
                1,
                TOTAL_STEPS_N + 1,
            ):

                # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                # FULL NS TRUTH
                #
                # NOTE:
                # explicit viscosity is case_nu.
                # This fixes the previous NU NameError permanently.
                # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

                U1_truth = full_rk4_D(
                    U1_truth,
                    DT_PHYS_N,
                    case_nu,
                    ENV_N,
                )

                U2_truth = full_rk4_D(
                    U2_truth,
                    DT_PHYS_N,
                    case_nu,
                    ENV_N,
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                # OLD2
                # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

                m3d_rk4_D(
                    old_model,
                    DTAU_N,
                    old_model["anchor_U1"],
                    old_model["anchor_U2"],
                    ENV_N,
                )

                # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                # STAR2
                # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

                rk4_M(
                    star_model,
                    DTAU_N,
                    ENV_N,
                )

                tau = (
                    step
                    *
                    DTAU_N
                )

                # ==================================================================================
                # TEACHER-FREE REANCHOR
                # ==================================================================================

                if (
                    step % REANCHOR_STEPS_N == 0
                    and
                    tau < FINAL_TAU_N
                ):

                    # OLD2
                    (
                        old_model,
                        old_RAf,
                        old_jump,
                        old_build_time,
                    ) = reanchor_old2_N(
                        old_model,
                        ENV_N,
                    )

                    old_reanchors += 1
                    old_rebuild_total += old_build_time

                    old_worst_jump = max(
                        old_worst_jump,
                        old_jump,
                    )

                    old_worst_RAf = max(
                        old_worst_RAf,
                        old_RAf,
                    )

                    # STAR2
                    (
                        star_model,
                        star_diag,
                        star_jump,
                        star_build_time,
                    ) = reanchor_star2_N(
                        star_model,
                        ENV_N,
                    )

                    star_RAf = float(
                        star_diag["RAf_ratio"]
                    )

                    star_reanchors += 1
                    star_rebuild_total += star_build_time

                    star_worst_jump = max(
                        star_worst_jump,
                        star_jump,
                    )

                    star_worst_RAf = max(
                        star_worst_RAf,
                        star_RAf,
                    )

                    reanchor_rows_N.append(
                        {
                            "case": case_number_N,
                            "topology": topology,
                            "nu_mult": nu_mult,
                            "nu": case_nu,
                            "tau": tau,

                            "old2_RAf_ratio":
                                old_RAf,

                            "star2_RAf_ratio":
                                star_RAf,

                            "old2_jump":
                                old_jump,

                            "star2_jump":
                                star_jump,

                            "old2_build_s":
                                old_build_time,

                            "star2_build_s":
                                star_build_time,
                        }
                    )

                # ==================================================================================
                # CHECKPOINT REPORT
                # ==================================================================================

                if (
                    next_report_i
                    <
                    len(REPORT_TAUS_N)
                    and
                    tau
                    >=
                    REPORT_TAUS_N[next_report_i]
                    -
                    0.5 * DTAU_N
                ):

                    scores = score_models_N(
                        old_model,
                        star_model,
                        U1_truth,
                        U2_truth,
                        ENV_N,
                    )

                    state_gain = (
                        scores["old2_state"]
                        /
                        max(
                            scores["star2_state"],
                            1e-30,
                        )
                    )

                    rhs_gain = (
                        scores["old2_rhs"]
                        /
                        max(
                            scores["star2_rhs"],
                            1e-30,
                        )
                    )

                    checkpoint_rows_N.append(
                        {
                            "case":
                                case_number_N,

                            "topology":
                                topology,

                            "nu_mult":
                                nu_mult,

                            "nu":
                                case_nu,

                            "tau":
                                tau,

                            "old2_state_error":
                                scores["old2_state"],

                            "star2_state_error":
                                scores["star2_state"],

                            "state_gain_old_over_star":
                                state_gain,

                            "old2_rhs_error":
                                scores["old2_rhs"],

                            "star2_rhs_error":
                                scores["star2_rhs"],

                            "rhs_gain_old_over_star":
                                rhs_gain,
                        }
                    )

                    print(
                        f"    tau={tau:6.3f}"
                        f" | OLD state={100*scores['old2_state']:8.3f}%"
                        f" | STAR state={100*scores['star2_state']:8.3f}%"
                        f" | OLD RHS={100*scores['old2_rhs']:8.3f}%"
                        f" | STAR RHS={100*scores['star2_rhs']:8.3f}%"
                        f" | gain={state_gain:7.3f}x"
                    )

                    next_report_i += 1

                if tau >= FINAL_TAU_N:
                    break

            # ======================================================================================
            # FINAL CASE SCORE
            # ======================================================================================

            final_tau = (
                step
                *
                DTAU_N
            )

            final_scores = score_models_N(
                old_model,
                star_model,
                U1_truth,
                U2_truth,
                ENV_N,
            )

            final_state_gain = (
                final_scores["old2_state"]
                /
                max(
                    final_scores["star2_state"],
                    1e-30,
                )
            )

            final_rhs_gain = (
                final_scores["old2_rhs"]
                /
                max(
                    final_scores["star2_rhs"],
                    1e-30,
                )
            )

            star_state_win = bool(
                final_scores["star2_state"]
                <
                final_scores["old2_state"]
            )

            star_rhs_win = bool(
                final_scores["star2_rhs"]
                <
                final_scores["old2_rhs"]
            )

            case_runtime = (
                time.perf_counter()
                -
                case_start
            )

            results_rows_N.append(
                {
                    "case":
                        case_number_N,

                    "topology":
                        topology,

                    "nu_mult":
                        nu_mult,

                    "nu":
                        case_nu,

                    "final_tau":
                        final_tau,

                    "old2_state_error":
                        final_scores["old2_state"],

                    "star2_state_error":
                        final_scores["star2_state"],

                    "state_gain_old_over_star":
                        final_state_gain,

                    "old2_rhs_error":
                        final_scores["old2_rhs"],

                    "star2_rhs_error":
                        final_scores["star2_rhs"],

                    "rhs_gain_old_over_star":
                        final_rhs_gain,

                    "star2_state_win":
                        star_state_win,

                    "star2_rhs_win":
                        star_rhs_win,

                    "old2_initial_RAf":
                        old_initial_RAf,

                    "star2_initial_RAf":
                        star_initial_RAf,

                    "old2_worst_RAf":
                        old_worst_RAf,

                    "star2_worst_RAf":
                        star_worst_RAf,

                    "old2_initial_build_s":
                        old_initial_build,

                    "star2_initial_build_s":
                        star_initial_build,

                    "old2_reanchors":
                        old_reanchors,

                    "star2_reanchors":
                        star_reanchors,

                    "old2_rebuild_total_s":
                        old_rebuild_total,

                    "star2_rebuild_total_s":
                        star_rebuild_total,

                    "old2_worst_jump":
                        old_worst_jump,

                    "star2_worst_jump":
                        star_worst_jump,

                    "case_runtime_s":
                        case_runtime,
                }
            )

            print()
            print(
                "FINAL"
                f" | OLD2 state={100*final_scores['old2_state']:.4f}%"
                f" | STAR2 state={100*final_scores['star2_state']:.4f}%"
                f" | gain={final_state_gain:.3f}x"
            )

            print(
                f"      OLD2 RHS={100*final_scores['old2_rhs']:.4f}%"
                f" | STAR2 RHS={100*final_scores['star2_rhs']:.4f}%"
                f" | RHS gain={final_rhs_gain:.3f}x"
            )

            print(
                f"      STAR2 worst ||RAf||/||Af|| = {star_worst_RAf:.3e}"
            )

            print(
                f"      reanchors = OLD {old_reanchors}"
                f" / STAR {star_reanchors}"
            )

            print(
                f"      worst jumps = OLD {100*old_worst_jump:.8f}%"
                f" / STAR {100*star_worst_jump:.8f}%"
            )

            # ======================================================================================
            # SAVE AFTER EVERY COMPLETED CASE
            # ======================================================================================

            pd.DataFrame(
                results_rows_N
            ).to_csv(
                SAVE_DIR
                /
                "m3d_N_results_partial.csv",
                index=False,
            )

            pd.DataFrame(
                checkpoint_rows_N
            ).to_csv(
                SAVE_DIR
                /
                "m3d_N_checkpoints_partial.csv",
                index=False,
            )

            pd.DataFrame(
                reanchor_rows_N
            ).to_csv(
                SAVE_DIR
                /
                "m3d_N_reanchors_partial.csv",
                index=False,
            )

            print(
                "      💾 permanent Drive checkpoint saved"
            )

            del U10
            del U20
            del U1_truth
            del U2_truth
            del old_model
            del star_model

            gc.collect()
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()


finally:

    # Restore normal baseline regime no matter what happens.
    sync_m3d_globals_MB(
        torch.float32,
        torch.complex64,
        BASE_NU_N,
        TAU0_N,
    )


torch.cuda.synchronize()

TOTAL_RUNTIME_N = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    total_start_N
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 10. FINAL DATAFRAMES
# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_N = pd.DataFrame(
    results_rows_N
)

CHECKPOINTS_N = pd.DataFrame(
    checkpoint_rows_N
)

REANCHORS_N = pd.DataFrame(
    reanchor_rows_N
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. OLD2 vs PREVIOUS L2 REPRODUCIBILITY
# ==================================================================================================

REPRO_N = None

if L2_REFERENCE_N is not None:

    needed_cols = {
        "topology",
        "nu_mult",
        "m3d2_state_error",
        "m3d2_rhs_error",
    }

    if needed_cols.issubset(
        L2_REFERENCE_N.columns
    ):

        repro = RESULTS_N.merge(
            L2_REFERENCE_N[
                [
                    "topology",
                    "nu_mult",
                    "m3d2_state_error",
                    "m3d2_rhs_error",
                ]
            ],
            on=[
                "topology",
                "nu_mult",
            ],
            how="left",
        )

        repro["state_abs_difference"] = np.abs(
            repro["old2_state_error"]
            -
            repro["m3d2_state_error"]
        )

        repro["rhs_abs_difference"] = np.abs(
            repro["old2_rhs_error"]
            -
            repro["m3d2_rhs_error"]
        )

        repro["state_relative_difference"] = (
            repro["state_abs_difference"]
            /
            np.maximum(
                np.abs(
                    repro["m3d2_state_error"]
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )

        repro["rhs_relative_difference"] = (
            repro["rhs_abs_difference"]
            /
            np.maximum(
                np.abs(
                    repro["m3d2_rhs_error"]
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )

        REPRO_N = repro[
            [
                "topology",
                "nu_mult",

                "old2_state_error",
                "m3d2_state_error",

                "state_abs_difference",
                "state_relative_difference",

                "old2_rhs_error",
                "m3d2_rhs_error",

                "rhs_abs_difference",
                "rhs_relative_difference",
            ]
        ]


# ==================================================================================================
# 12. SUMMARY TABLES
# ==================================================================================================

TOPOLOGY_SUMMARY_N = (
    RESULTS_N
    .groupby(
        "topology",
        as_index=False,
    )
    .agg(
        old2_state_median=(
            "old2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_state_median=(
            "star2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_state_worst=(
            "star2_state_error",
            "max",
        ),

        state_gain_median=(
            "state_gain_old_over_star",
            "median",
        ),

        old2_rhs_median=(
            "old2_rhs_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_rhs_median=(
            "star2_rhs_error",
            "median",
        ),

        rhs_gain_median=(
            "rhs_gain_old_over_star",
            "median",
        ),

        state_wins=(
            "star2_state_win",
            "sum",
        ),

        rhs_wins=(
            "star2_rhs_win",
            "sum",
        ),
    )
)


VISCOSITY_SUMMARY_N = (
    RESULTS_N
    .groupby(
        "nu_mult",
        as_index=False,
    )
    .agg(
        old2_state_median=(
            "old2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_state_median=(
            "star2_state_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_state_worst=(
            "star2_state_error",
            "max",
        ),

        state_gain_median=(
            "state_gain_old_over_star",
            "median",
        ),

        old2_rhs_median=(
            "old2_rhs_error",
            "median",
        ),

        star2_rhs_median=(
            "star2_rhs_error",
            "median",
        ),

        rhs_gain_median=(
            "rhs_gain_old_over_star",
            "median",
        ),

        state_wins=(
            "star2_state_win",
            "sum",
        ),

        rhs_wins=(
            "star2_rhs_win",
            "sum",
        ),
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 13. SAVE FINAL FILES
# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_N.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_N_12case_final.csv",
    index=False,
)

CHECKPOINTS_N.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_N_checkpoints.csv",
    index=False,
)

REANCHORS_N.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_N_reanchors.csv",
    index=False,
)

TOPOLOGY_SUMMARY_N.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_N_topology_summary.csv",
    index=False,
)

VISCOSITY_SUMMARY_N.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_N_viscosity_summary.csv",
    index=False,
)

if REPRO_N is not None:

    REPRO_N.to_csv(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_N_old2_L2_reproducibility.csv",
        index=False,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 14. PRINT FINAL 12-CASE TABLE
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 140)
print("TEST N — FINAL 12-CASE RESULTS")
print("=" * 140)
print()

display_cols_N = [
    "case",
    "topology",
    "nu_mult",

    "old2_state_error",
    "star2_state_error",
    "state_gain_old_over_star",

    "old2_rhs_error",
    "star2_rhs_error",
    "rhs_gain_old_over_star",

    "star2_state_win",
    "star2_rhs_win",

    "star2_worst_RAf",
]

print(
    RESULTS_N[
        display_cols_N
    ].to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.8g}",
    )
)


print()
print("=" * 140)
print("TOPOLOGY SUMMARY")
print("=" * 140)
print()

print(
    TOPOLOGY_SUMMARY_N.to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.8g}",
    )
)


print()
print("=" * 140)
print("VISCOSITY SUMMARY")
print("=" * 140)
print()

print(
    VISCOSITY_SUMMARY_N.to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.8g}",
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 15. L2 REPRODUCIBILITY AUDIT
# ==================================================================================================

if REPRO_N is not None:

    print()
    print("=" * 140)
    print("OLD2 vs PREVIOUS L2 REPRODUCIBILITY")
    print("=" * 140)
    print()

    print(
        REPRO_N.to_string(
            index=False,
            float_format=lambda x:
            f"{x:.8g}",
        )
    )

    print()

    print(
        "max OLD2 state relative difference:",
        f"{100*REPRO_N['state_relative_difference'].max():.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        "max OLD2 RHS relative difference:",
        f"{100*REPRO_N['rhs_relative_difference'].max():.8f}%"
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 16. GLOBAL METRICS
# ==================================================================================================

MEDIAN_OLD_STATE_N = float(
    RESULTS_N["old2_state_error"].median()
)

MEDIAN_STAR_STATE_N = float(
    RESULTS_N["star2_state_error"].median()
)

MEDIAN_OLD_RHS_N = float(
    RESULTS_N["old2_rhs_error"].median()
)

MEDIAN_STAR_RHS_N = float(
    RESULTS_N["star2_rhs_error"].median()
)


MEDIAN_STATE_GAIN_N = float(
    RESULTS_N[
        "state_gain_old_over_star"
    ].median()
)

MEDIAN_RHS_GAIN_N = float(
    RESULTS_N[
        "rhs_gain_old_over_star"
    ].median()
)


STATE_WINS_N = int(
    RESULTS_N[
        "star2_state_win"
    ].sum()
)

RHS_WINS_N = int(
    RESULTS_N[
        "star2_rhs_win"
    ].sum()
)


WORST_STAR_STATE_N = float(
    RESULTS_N[
        "star2_state_error"
    ].max()
)

WORST_STAR_RHS_N = float(
    RESULTS_N[
        "star2_rhs_error"
    ].max()
)

WORST_STAR_RAf_N = float(
    RESULTS_N[
        "star2_worst_RAf"
    ].max()
)

WORST_STAR_JUMP_N = float(
    RESULTS_N[
        "star2_worst_jump"
    ].max()
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 17. PREDECLARED GATES
# ==================================================================================================

MECHANISM_GATE_N = bool(
    WORST_STAR_RAf_N
    <
    1e-4
)

STATE_GAIN_GATE_N = bool(
    MEDIAN_STATE_GAIN_N
    >=
    1.20
)

STATE_WIN_GATE_N = bool(
    STATE_WINS_N
    >=
    8
)

RHS_GAIN_GATE_N = bool(
    MEDIAN_RHS_GAIN_N
    >=
    1.10
)

CONTINUITY_GATE_N = bool(
    WORST_STAR_JUMP_N
    <
    1e-4
)


PRIMARY_N = bool(
    MECHANISM_GATE_N
    and
    STATE_GAIN_GATE_N
    and
    STATE_WIN_GATE_N
    and
    RHS_GAIN_GATE_N
    and
    CONTINUITY_GATE_N
)

# ==================================================================================================

    print(

#     tau= 1.500 | OLD state=   0.148% | STAR state=   0.111% | OLD RHS=   1.110% | STAR RHS=   0.771% | gain=  1.336x
#     tau= 2.000 | OLD state=   0.190% | STAR state=   0.150% | OLD RHS=   0.773% | STAR RHS=   0.635% | gain=  1.268x
# 
# FINAL | OLD2 state=0.1902% | STAR2 state=0.1501% | gain=1.268x
#       OLD2 RHS=0.7728% | STAR2 RHS=0.6351% | RHS gain=1.217x
#       STAR2 worst ||RAf||/||Af|| = 1.945e-07
#       reanchors = OLD 13 / STAR 13
#       worst jumps = OLD 0.00000286% / STAR 0.00000270%
#       💾 permanent Drive checkpoint saved

# higher local order contributes,

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 15
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST O — ERROR-SOURCE DECOMPOSITION
#
# periodic_shear | nu x 1.0
#
# PURPOSE
# -------
# Explain why STAR2 can be dramatically better at medium horizon
# but lose much of that advantage by tau ~ 2.
#
#
# TWO STAR2 TRAJECTORIES PER SEGMENT
# ----------------------------------
#
# GLOBAL STAR2
#   - teacher-free
#   - carries all accumulated state error
#   - reanchors from its OWN predicted state every 0.15 tau
#
# ORACLE-LOCAL STAR2
#   - diagnostic only
#   - rebuilt from TRUE state at the beginning of every segment
#   - runs exactly one segment
#   - discarded afterward
#
#
# EXACT TARGET-SPACE DECOMPOSITION
# --------------------------------
#
# Let:
#
#   xG = global STAR2 target state
#   xO = oracle-local STAR2 target state
#   xT = truth target state
#
# Then:
#
#   xG - xT = (xG - xO) + (xO - xT)
#
#              HISTORY          LOCAL
#
# where:
#
# HISTORY includes accumulated state error + consequences of anchoring
# the local basis at the predicted rather than true state.
#
# LOCAL is the error of a fresh truth-anchored STAR2 segment.
#
#
# INTERPRETATION
# --------------
#
# If LOCAL stays tiny while GLOBAL grows:
#
#     accumulated anchor/history error dominates.
#
# If fresh LOCAL itself grows strongly later in the trajectory:
#
#     local geometry / manifold difficulty is worsening.
#
#
# FROZEN
# ------
#
# topology = periodic_shear
# nu = 0.0025
# N = 40
# L = 8
# tau0 = 40/3
# dt physical = 0.01
# dtau = 0.00075
# segment/reanchor = 0.15 tau
# final tau ~ 2
# fp32 / complex64
#
# NO MODEL MODIFICATIONS.
# NO TUNING.
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch


# ==================================================================================================
# 0. PREFLIGHT
# ==================================================================================================
