    "make_env_D",
    "topology_pair_L2",

    "initialize_star2_N",
    "reanchor_star2_N",

    "reconstruct_M",
    "rk4_M",

    "full_rk4_D",
    "_pair_rhs_D",
    "pack_modes_D",
    "pair_norm_D",

    "sync_m3d_globals_MB",

    "REAL_DTYPE_D",
    "COMPLEX_DTYPE_D",
]

missing_O = [
    name
    for name in REQUIRED_O
    if name not in globals()
]

if missing_O:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Missing Test-O dependencies:\n"
        + repr(missing_O)
        + "\n\nRun the corrected Test N definitions first."
    )

if not torch.cuda.is_available():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "CUDA GPU required."
    )

if "SAVE_DIR" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "SAVE_DIR missing."
    )

SAVE_DIR = Path(
    SAVE_DIR
)

SAVE_DIR.mkdir(
    parents=True,
    exist_ok=True,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. FROZEN SETTINGS
# ==================================================================================================

TOPOLOGY_O = "periodic_shear"

NU_O = 2.5e-3

TAU0_O = 40.0 / 3.0

DT_PHYS_O = 0.01

DTAU_O = (
    DT_PHYS_O
    /
    TAU0_O
)

FINAL_TAU_O = 2.0

SEGMENT_TAU_O = 0.15

SEGMENT_STEPS_O = int(
    round(
        SEGMENT_TAU_O
        /
        DTAU_O
    )
)

TOTAL_STEPS_O = int(
    math.ceil(
        FINAL_TAU_O
        /
        DTAU_O
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. FORCE SAME NUMERICAL REGIME AS TEST N
# ==================================================================================================

sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float32,
    torch.complex64,
    NU_O,
    TAU0_O,
)

ENV_O = make_env_D(
    40,
    L=8.0,
)


assert ENV_O["KX"].dtype == torch.float32
assert REAL_DTYPE_D == torch.float32
assert COMPLEX_DTYPE_D == torch.complex64


print("=" * 140)
print("M3D TEST O — ERROR-SOURCE DECOMPOSITION")
print("=" * 140)

print()
print(
    "GPU:",
    torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
)

print(
    "topology:",
    TOPOLOGY_O,
)

print(
    "nu:",
    NU_O,
)

print(
    "tau0:",
    TAU0_O,
)

print(
    "physical dt:",
    DT_PHYS_O,
)

print(
    "dimensionless dtau:",
    DTAU_O,
)

print(
    "segment tau:",
    SEGMENT_TAU_O,
)

print(
    "segment steps:",
    SEGMENT_STEPS_O,
)

print(
    "total steps:",
    TOTAL_STEPS_O,
)

print(
    "final numerical tau:",
    TOTAL_STEPS_O * DTAU_O,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. TARGET STATE + RHS VECTORS
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def target_vectors_O(
    U1,
    U2,
    tids,
    env,
):
    x = pack_modes_D(
        U1,
        U2,
        tids,
    )

    F1, F2 = _pair_rhs_D(
        U1,
        U2,
        env,
    )

    fx = pack_modes_D(
        F1,
        F2,
        tids,
    )

    return (
        x,
        fx,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. SAFE VECTOR METRICS
# ==================================================================================================

def norm_float_O(
    v,
):
    return float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            v
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def cosine_O(
    a,
    b,
):
    na = norm_float_O(a)
    nb = norm_float_O(b)

    if (
        na < 1e-30
        or
        nb < 1e-30
    ):
        return np.nan

    # packed target vectors are real in this implementation,
    # but this also safely handles complex tensors.
    inner = torch.sum(
        torch.conj(a)
        *
        b
    ).real

    return float(
        (
            inner
            /
            (
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(a)
                *
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(b)
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. EXACT END-OF-SEGMENT DECOMPOSITION
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def decomposition_O(
    global_model,
    oracle_model,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
    env,
):
    Ug1, Ug2 = reconstruct_M(
        global_model,
        env,
    )

    Uo1, Uo2 = reconstruct_M(
        oracle_model,
        env,
    )

    tids = global_model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    xG, fG = target_vectors_O(
        Ug1,
        Ug2,
        tids,
        env,
    )

    xO, fO = target_vectors_O(
        Uo1,
        Uo2,
        tids,
        env,
    )

    xT, fT = target_vectors_O(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
        env,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # EXACT STATE DECOMPOSITION
    #
    # e_global = e_history + e_local
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    e_global = (
        xG
        -
        xT
    )

    e_history = (
        xG
        -
        xO
    )

    e_local = (
        xO
        -
        xT
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # EXACT RHS DECOMPOSITION
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    r_global = (
        fG
        -
        fT
    )

    r_history = (
        fG
        -
        fO
    )

    r_local = (
        fO
        -
        fT
    )

    xT_norm = (
        norm_float_O(
            xT
        )
        +
        1e-30
    )

    fT_norm = (
        norm_float_O(
            fT
        )
        +
        1e-30
    )

    # Exact closure diagnostic.
    state_closure = norm_float_O(
        e_global
        -
        (
            e_history
            +
            e_local
        )
    )

    rhs_closure = norm_float_O(
        r_global
        -
        (
            r_history
            +
            r_local
        )
    )

    global_state = (
        norm_float_O(
            e_global
        )
        /
        xT_norm
    )

    history_state = (
        norm_float_O(
            e_history
        )
        /
        xT_norm
    )

    local_state = (
        norm_float_O(
            e_local
        )
        /
        xT_norm
    )

    global_rhs = (
        norm_float_O(
            r_global
        )
        /
        fT_norm
    )

    history_rhs = (
        norm_float_O(
            r_history
        )
        /
        fT_norm
    )

    local_rhs = (
        norm_float_O(
            r_local
        )
        /
        fT_norm
    )

    return {
        "global_state":
            global_state,

        "history_state":
            history_state,

        "local_state":
            local_state,

        "global_rhs":
            global_rhs,

        "history_rhs":
            history_rhs,

        "local_rhs":
            local_rhs,

        "history_over_global_state":
            history_state
            /
            max(
                global_state,
                1e-30,
            ),

        "local_over_global_state":
            local_state
            /
            max(
                global_state,
                1e-30,
            ),

        "history_over_global_rhs":
            history_rhs
            /
            max(
                global_rhs,
                1e-30,
            ),

        "local_over_global_rhs":
            local_rhs
            /
            max(
                global_rhs,
                1e-30,
            ),

        "history_local_cosine_state":
            cosine_O(
                e_history,
                e_local,
            ),

        "history_local_cosine_rhs":
            cosine_O(
                r_history,
                r_local,
            ),

        "state_decomposition_residual":
            state_closure
            /
            (
                norm_float_O(
                    e_global
                )
                +
                1e-30
            ),

        "rhs_decomposition_residual":
            rhs_closure
            /
            (
                norm_float_O(
                    r_global
                )
                +
                1e-30
            ),
    }


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. GLOBAL START ERROR
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def global_start_error_O(
    global_model,
    U1_truth,
    U2_truth,
    env,
):
    Ug1, Ug2 = reconstruct_M(
        global_model,
        env,
    )

    tids = global_model[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    xG = pack_modes_D(
        Ug1,
        Ug2,
        tids,
    )

    xT = pack_modes_D(
        U1_truth,
        U2_truth,
        tids,
    )

    return float(
        (
            torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                xG - xT
            )
            /
            (
                torch.linalg.vector_norm(
                    xT
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. INITIAL CONDITION
# ==================================================================================================

U10_O, U20_O = topology_pair_L2(
    TOPOLOGY_O,
    ENV_O,
)

U1_truth_O = U10_O.clone()
U2_truth_O = U20_O.clone()


# Teacher-free trajectory.
global_star_O, global_diag_O = initialize_star2_N(
    U10_O,
    U20_O,
    ENV_O,
)


print()
print(
    "initial GLOBAL STAR2 ||RAf||/||Af||:",
    f"{global_diag_O['RAf_ratio']:.3e}",
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. SEGMENT LOOP
# ==================================================================================================

rows_O = []

global_reanchor_rows_O = []

current_step_O = 0

segment_index_O = 0

test_start_O = time.perf_counter()


while current_step_O < TOTAL_STEPS_O:

    segment_index_O += 1

    segment_start_step = (
        current_step_O
    )

    remaining_steps = (
        TOTAL_STEPS_O
        -
        current_step_O
    )

    this_segment_steps = min(
        SEGMENT_STEPS_O,
        remaining_steps,
    )

    tau_start = (
        segment_start_step
        *
        DTAU_O
    )

    tau_end = (
        (
            segment_start_step
            +
            this_segment_steps
        )
        *
        DTAU_O
    )

    segment_length_tau = (
        this_segment_steps
        *
        DTAU_O
    )

    is_full_segment = bool(
        this_segment_steps
        ==
        SEGMENT_STEPS_O
    )

    print()
    print("#" * 140)

    print(
        f"SEGMENT {segment_index_O:02d}"
        f" | tau {tau_start:.5f} -> {tau_end:.5f}"
        f" | Δtau={segment_length_tau:.5f}"
        f" | full={is_full_segment}"
    )

    print("#" * 140)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # GLOBAL start error.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    global_start_state_error = global_start_error_O(
        global_star_O,
        U1_truth_O,
        U2_truth_O,
        ENV_O,
    )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # ORACLE-LOCAL shadow:
    #
    # Fresh STAR2 basis from TRUE state at segment start.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    oracle_star_O, oracle_diag_O = initialize_star2_N(
        U1_truth_O,
        U2_truth_O,
        ENV_O,
    )

    print(
        f"  global start error      = {100*global_start_state_error:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  oracle ||RAf||/||Af||   = {oracle_diag_O['RAf_ratio']:.3e}"
    )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Integrate truth + global + oracle over SAME segment.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    for _ in range(
        this_segment_steps
    ):

        U1_truth_O = full_rk4_D(
            U1_truth_O,
            DT_PHYS_O,
            NU_O,
            ENV_O,
        )

        U2_truth_O = full_rk4_D(
            U2_truth_O,
            DT_PHYS_O,
            NU_O,
            ENV_O,
        )

        rk4_M(
            global_star_O,
            DTAU_O,
            ENV_O,
        )

        rk4_M(
            oracle_star_O,
            DTAU_O,
            ENV_O,
        )

    current_step_O += (
        this_segment_steps
    )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Exact decomposition at segment end.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    D = decomposition_O(
        global_star_O,
        oracle_star_O,
        U1_truth_O,
        U2_truth_O,
        ENV_O,
    )

    local_K4 = (
        D[
            "local_state"
        ]
        /
        max(
            segment_length_tau ** 4,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    history_amp_vs_start = (
        D[
            "history_state"
        ]
        /
        max(
            global_start_state_error,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    row = {
        "segment":
            segment_index_O,

        "tau_start":
            tau_start,

        "tau_end":
            tau_end,

        "segment_tau":
            segment_length_tau,

        "full_segment":
            is_full_segment,

        "global_start_state_error":
            global_start_state_error,

        "global_end_state_error":
            D[
                "global_state"
            ],

        "oracle_local_state_error":
            D[
                "local_state"
            ],

        "history_state_error":
            D[
                "history_state"
            ],

        "local_over_global_state":
            D[
                "local_over_global_state"
            ],

        "history_over_global_state":
            D[
                "history_over_global_state"
            ],

        "history_amp_vs_start":
            history_amp_vs_start,

        "local_K4":
            local_K4,

        "history_local_cosine_state":
            D[
                "history_local_cosine_state"
            ],

        "global_end_rhs_error":
            D[
                "global_rhs"
            ],

        "oracle_local_rhs_error":
            D[
                "local_rhs"
            ],

        "history_rhs_error":
            D[
                "history_rhs"
            ],

        "local_over_global_rhs":
            D[
                "local_over_global_rhs"
            ],

        "history_over_global_rhs":
            D[
                "history_over_global_rhs"
            ],

        "history_local_cosine_rhs":
            D[
                "history_local_cosine_rhs"
            ],

        "oracle_RAf_ratio":
            oracle_diag_O[
                "RAf_ratio"
            ],

        "state_decomposition_residual":
            D[
                "state_decomposition_residual"
            ],

        "rhs_decomposition_residual":
            D[
                "rhs_decomposition_residual"
            ],
    }

    rows_O.append(
        row
    )

    print()
    print(
        f"  GLOBAL end state        = {100*D['global_state']:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  ORACLE fresh-local      = {100*D['local_state']:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  HISTORY contribution    = {100*D['history_state']:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  local/global            = {D['local_over_global_state']:.5f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  history/global          = {D['history_over_global_state']:.5f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  history amp vs start    = {history_amp_vs_start:.5f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  local K4                = {local_K4:.6e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  history/local cosine    = {D['history_local_cosine_state']:.5f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  GLOBAL end RHS          = {100*D['global_rhs']:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  ORACLE local RHS        = {100*D['local_rhs']:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  HISTORY RHS             = {100*D['history_rhs']:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  state closure residual  = {D['state_decomposition_residual']:.3e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  RHS closure residual    = {D['rhs_decomposition_residual']:.3e}"
    )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Save after every segment.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    pd.DataFrame(
        rows_O
    ).to_csv(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_O_segments_partial.csv",
        index=False,
    )

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Teacher-free global reanchor for NEXT segment.
    #
    # Do NOT reanchor after final segment.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    if current_step_O < TOTAL_STEPS_O:

        (
            global_star_O,
            global_diag_after_O,
            global_jump_O,
            global_build_time_O,
        ) = reanchor_star2_N(
            global_star_O,
            ENV_O,
        )

        global_reanchor_rows_O.append(
            {
                "after_segment":
                    segment_index_O,

                "tau":
                    tau_end,

                "global_RAf_ratio":
                    global_diag_after_O[
                        "RAf_ratio"
                    ],

                "jump":
                    global_jump_O,

                "build_time_s":
                    global_build_time_O,
            }
        )

        print()

        print(
            f"  teacher-free reanchor   | RAf={global_diag_after_O['RAf_ratio']:.3e}"
            f" | jump={100*global_jump_O:.10f}%"
        )

    # Oracle is thrown away.
    del oracle_star_O

    gc.collect()
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()


torch.cuda.synchronize()

TOTAL_RUNTIME_O = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    test_start_O
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. RESULTS
# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_O = pd.DataFrame(
    rows_O
)

REANCHORS_O = pd.DataFrame(
    global_reanchor_rows_O
)


RESULTS_O.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_O_segment_decomposition.csv",
    index=False,
)

REANCHORS_O.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_O_global_reanchors.csv",
    index=False,
)


print()
print("=" * 140)
print("TEST O — SEGMENT DECOMPOSITION")
print("=" * 140)
print()

display_cols_O = [
    "segment",
    "tau_start",
    "tau_end",

    "global_start_state_error",
    "global_end_state_error",

    "oracle_local_state_error",
    "history_state_error",

    "local_over_global_state",
    "history_over_global_state",

    "history_amp_vs_start",
    "local_K4",

    "history_local_cosine_state",
]

print(
    RESULTS_O[
        display_cols_O
    ].to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.8g}",
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 10. FULL-SEGMENT AGING ANALYSIS
# ==================================================================================================

FULL_O = RESULTS_O[
    RESULTS_O[
        "full_segment"
    ]
].copy()


if len(FULL_O) < 8:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Too few full segments for aging analysis."
    )


n_full_O = len(
    FULL_O
)

quarter_O = max(
    3,
    n_full_O // 3,
)


EARLY_O = FULL_O.iloc[
    :quarter_O
]

LATE_O = FULL_O.iloc[
    -quarter_O:
]


EARLY_LOCAL_MEDIAN_O = float(
    EARLY_O[
        "oracle_local_state_error"
    ].median()
)

LATE_LOCAL_MEDIAN_O = float(
    LATE_O[
        "oracle_local_state_error"
    ].median()
)


LOCAL_AGING_FACTOR_O = (
    LATE_LOCAL_MEDIAN_O
    /
    max(
        EARLY_LOCAL_MEDIAN_O,
        1e-30,
    )
)


LATE_GLOBAL_MEDIAN_O = float(
    LATE_O[
        "global_end_state_error"
    ].median()
)

LATE_HISTORY_MEDIAN_O = float(
    LATE_O[
        "history_state_error"
    ].median()
)

LATE_LOCAL_FRACTION_O = float(
    LATE_O[
        "local_over_global_state"
    ].median()
)

LATE_HISTORY_FRACTION_O = float(
    LATE_O[
        "history_over_global_state"
    ].median()
)


K4_EARLY_MEDIAN_O = float(
    EARLY_O[
        "local_K4"
    ].median()
)

K4_LATE_MEDIAN_O = float(
    LATE_O[
        "local_K4"
    ].median()
)

K4_AGING_FACTOR_O = (
    K4_LATE_MEDIAN_O
    /
    max(
        K4_EARLY_MEDIAN_O,
        1e-30,
    )
)


MAX_DECOMP_RESIDUAL_O = float(
    RESULTS_O[
        "state_decomposition_residual"
    ].max()
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. DIAGNOSTIC CLASSIFICATION
#
# These thresholds are interpretation aids, NOT mathematical theorems.
# ==================================================================================================

DECOMP_GATE_O = bool(
    MAX_DECOMP_RESIDUAL_O
    <
    1e-5
)


STRONG_HISTORY_DOMINANCE_O = bool(
    LATE_LOCAL_FRACTION_O
    <
    0.25
    and
    LATE_HISTORY_FRACTION_O
    >
    0.75
)


STRONG_LOCAL_AGING_O = bool(
    LOCAL_AGING_FACTOR_O
    >
    5.0
    or
    K4_AGING_FACTOR_O
    >
    5.0
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 12. FINAL DIAGNOSIS
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 140)
print("M3D TEST O — FINAL DIAGNOSIS")
print("=" * 140)
print()

print(
    "full segments:",
    n_full_O,
)

print()

print(
    "early oracle-local median state error:",
    f"{100*EARLY_LOCAL_MEDIAN_O:.8f}%"
)

print(
    "late oracle-local median state error:",
    f"{100*LATE_LOCAL_MEDIAN_O:.8f}%"
)

print(
    "oracle local-error aging factor:",
    f"{LOCAL_AGING_FACTOR_O:.4f}x"
)

print()

print(
    "early median local K4:",
    f"{K4_EARLY_MEDIAN_O:.8e}"
)

print(
    "late median local K4:",
    f"{K4_LATE_MEDIAN_O:.8e}"
)

print(
    "K4 aging factor:",
    f"{K4_AGING_FACTOR_O:.4f}x"
)

print()

print(
    "late global median state error:",
    f"{100*LATE_GLOBAL_MEDIAN_O:.8f}%"
)

print(
    "late history median state contribution:",
    f"{100*LATE_HISTORY_MEDIAN_O:.8f}%"
)

print(
    "late median LOCAL/GLOBAL norm ratio:",
    f"{LATE_LOCAL_FRACTION_O:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late median HISTORY/GLOBAL norm ratio:",
    f"{LATE_HISTORY_FRACTION_O:.6f}"
)

print()

print(
    "max exact decomposition residual:",
    f"{MAX_DECOMP_RESIDUAL_O:.3e}"
)

print()

print(
    "exact-decomposition gate:",
    DECOMP_GATE_O
)

print(
    "strong history-dominance diagnostic:",
    STRONG_HISTORY_DOMINANCE_O
)

print(
    "strong local-aging diagnostic:",
    STRONG_LOCAL_AGING_O
)

print()


if (
    DECOMP_GATE_O
    and
    STRONG_HISTORY_DOMINANCE_O
    and
    not STRONG_LOCAL_AGING_O
):

    print(
        "🔥 HISTORY / ERROR PROPAGATION DOMINATES."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Fresh truth-anchored STAR2 segments remain accurate,"
    )

    print(
        "while the teacher-free trajectory accumulates much larger error."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "This means the next target is NOT more hidden modes."
    )

    print(
        "It is controlling accumulated anchor-state error / propagation."
    )


elif (
    DECOMP_GATE_O
    and
    STRONG_LOCAL_AGING_O
):

    print(
        "🔥 LOCAL MANIFOLD AGING IS REAL."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Even fresh truth-anchored STAR2 segments become materially"
    )

    print(
        "harder later in the trajectory."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "The next target should be curvature-aware basis enrichment"
    )

    print(
        "or adaptive segment length."
    )


elif DECOMP_GATE_O:

    print(
        "🟡 MIXED ERROR MECHANISM."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Both inherited/history error and fresh local-model error"
    )

    print(
        "matter materially."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Use the segment table to identify where dominance switches."
    )


else:

    print(
        "❌ DECOMPOSITION NUMERICS FAILED."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Do not interpret the mechanism until the vector identity"
    )

    print(
        "closes numerically."
    )


print()

print(
    "runtime:",
    f"{TOTAL_RUNTIME_O:.2f}s"
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 13. COMPLETION FILE
# ==================================================================================================

with open(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_O_COMPLETE.txt",
    "w",
) as f:

    f.write(
        "M3D TEST O COMPLETE\n"
        f"runtime_seconds={TOTAL_RUNTIME_O}\n"
        f"early_local_median={EARLY_LOCAL_MEDIAN_O}\n"
        f"late_local_median={LATE_LOCAL_MEDIAN_O}\n"
        f"local_aging_factor={LOCAL_AGING_FACTOR_O}\n"
        f"K4_aging_factor={K4_AGING_FACTOR_O}\n"
        f"late_local_fraction={LATE_LOCAL_FRACTION_O}\n"
        f"late_history_fraction={LATE_HISTORY_FRACTION_O}\n"
        f"max_decomposition_residual={MAX_DECOMP_RESIDUAL_O}\n"
        f"history_dominance={STRONG_HISTORY_DOMINANCE_O}\n"
        f"local_aging={STRONG_LOCAL_AGING_O}\n"
    )


print()
print("=" * 140)
print("PERMANENT FILES")
print("=" * 140)

for filename in [
    "m3d_O_segment_decomposition.csv",
    "m3d_O_global_reanchors.csv",
    "m3d_O_COMPLETE.txt",
]:

    print(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        filename
    )

#   ORACLE local RHS        = 0.08474831%
#   HISTORY RHS             = 14.53961713%
#   state closure residual  = 1.212e-08
#   RHS closure residual    = 4.818e-09
# 
#   teacher-free reanchor   | RAf=1.025e-07 | jump=0.0000004248%
# 
# ############################################################################################################################################
# SEGMENT 14 | tau 1.95000 -> 2.00025 | Δtau=0.05025 | full=False
# ############################################################################################################################################
#   global start error      = 2.07752101%
#   oracle ||RAf||/||Af||   = 2.288e-07
# 
#   GLOBAL end state        = 2.34695250%
#   ORACLE fresh-local      = 0.00002054%
#   HISTORY contribution    = 2.34694693%
#   local/global            = 0.00001
#   history/global          = 1.00000
#   history amp vs start    = 1.12969
#   local K4                = 3.221996e-02
#   history/local cosine    = 0.26984
# 
#   GLOBAL end RHS          = 14.60178424%
#   ORACLE local RHS        = 0.00319040%
#   HISTORY RHS             = 14.60095761%
#   state closure residual  = 1.267e-08
#   RHS closure residual    = 1.223e-08

# ============================================================================================================================================
# TEST O — SEGMENT DECOMPOSITION
# ============================================================================================================================================
# 
#  segment  tau_start  tau_end  global_start_state_error  global_end_state_error  oracle_local_state_error  history_state_error  local_over_global_state
#        1          0     0.15             1.5668897e-16           8.5990168e-06             8.5990168e-06                    0                        1

# ============================================================================================================================================
# M3D TEST O — FINAL DIAGNOSIS
# ============================================================================================================================================
# 
# full segments: 13
# 
# early oracle-local median state error: 0.00080350%
# late oracle-local median state error: 0.00103449%
# oracle local-error aging factor: 1.2875x
# 
# early median local K4: 1.58716191e-02
# late median local K4: 2.04342943e-02
# K4 aging factor: 1.2875x
# 
# late global median state error: 1.07094645%
# late history median state contribution: 1.07063756%
# late median LOCAL/GLOBAL norm ratio: 0.001010
# late median HISTORY/GLOBAL norm ratio: 0.999694
# 
# max exact decomposition residual: 1.678e-08
# 
# exact-decomposition gate: True
# strong history-dominance diagnostic: True
# strong local-aging diagnostic: False
# 
# 🔥 HISTORY / ERROR PROPAGATION DOMINATES.
# 
# Fresh truth-anchored STAR2 segments remain accurate,
# while the teacher-free trajectory accumulates much larger error.
# 
# This means the next target is NOT more hidden modes.
# It is controlling accumulated anchor-state error / propagation.
# 
# runtime: 138.54s
# 
# ============================================================================================================================================
# PERMANENT FILES
# ============================================================================================================================================
# /content/drive/MyDrive/M3D/m3d_O_segment_decomposition.csv
# /content/drive/MyDrive/M3D/m3d_O_global_reanchors.csv
# /content/drive/MyDrive/M3D/m3d_O_COMPLETE.txt
# 

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 16
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST P — PHYSICAL vs ROM-INDUCED ERROR AMPLIFICATION
#
# CASE
# ----
# periodic_shear | nu x 1.0
#
#
# PURPOSE
# -------
# Test O showed:
#
#     fresh local STAR2 error stays tiny
#     accumulated history error dominates long horizon
#
# Test P asks:
#
#     Is that history growth simply physical sensitivity of Navier-Stokes
#     to the slightly wrong state?
#
#     OR
#
#     Does the STAR2 adaptive reduced map amplify that error MORE than
#     the full Navier-Stokes dynamics would?
#
#
# AT EACH SEGMENT START
# ---------------------
#
# Truth state:
#
#     U_k
#
# Teacher-free global STAR2 state:
#
#     U~_k
#
# Start TWO shadows from EXACTLY the same perturbed state U~_k:
#
#     FOM shadow:
#         U~_k --full NS--> U_F
#
#     STAR2 shadow:
#         U~_k --STAR2--> U_S
#
# Truth evolves:
#
#         U_k --full NS--> U_T
#
#
# TARGET-SPACE DECOMPOSITION
# --------------------------
#
#     e_STAR = P(U_S - U_T)
#
#     e_FOM  = P(U_F - U_T)
#
#     e_ROM  = P(U_S - U_F)
#
# Therefore EXACTLY:
#
#     e_STAR = e_FOM + e_ROM
#
#
# AMPLIFICATION
# -------------
#
#     A_FOM  = ||e_FOM||  / ||P(U~_k - U_k)||
#
#     A_STAR = ||e_STAR|| / ||P(U~_k - U_k)||
#
#     Gamma  = A_STAR / A_FOM
#
#
# INTERPRETATION
# --------------
#
# Gamma ~ 1:
#     history growth is mostly physical flow sensitivity.
#
# Gamma >> 1:
#     STAR2 introduces additional amplification beyond full NS.
#
# Gamma << 1:
#     STAR2 actually damps perturbations relative to full NS.
#
#
# IMPORTANT
# ---------
# This measures FINITE-ERROR amplification along the actual teacher-free
# trajectory. It is not automatically identical to an infinitesimal
# Jacobian / Lyapunov exponent once errors become large.
#
#
# FROZEN
# ------
#
# topology = periodic_shear
# nu = 0.0025
#
# N = 40
# L = 8
#
# tau0 = 40/3
# physical dt = 0.01
# dtau = 0.00075
#
# segment tau = 0.15
# final tau = 2
#
# fp32 / complex64
#
# NO tuning.
# NO basis changes.
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch


# ==================================================================================================
# 0. PREFLIGHT
# ==================================================================================================

REQUIRED_P = [
    "make_env_D",
    "topology_pair_L2",

    "initialize_star2_N",
    "reanchor_star2_N",

    "reconstruct_M",
    "rk4_M",

    "full_rk4_D",
    "pack_modes_D",
    "pair_norm_D",

    "sync_m3d_globals_MB",

    "REAL_DTYPE_D",
    "COMPLEX_DTYPE_D",
]

missing_P = [
    name
    for name in REQUIRED_P
    if name not in globals()
]

if missing_P:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Missing Test-P dependencies:\n"
        + repr(missing_P)
        + "\n\nRun corrected Test N definitions first."
    )

if not torch.cuda.is_available():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "CUDA GPU required."
    )

if "SAVE_DIR" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "SAVE_DIR missing."
    )

SAVE_DIR = Path(SAVE_DIR)
SAVE_DIR.mkdir(
    parents=True,
    exist_ok=True,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. FROZEN SETTINGS
# ==================================================================================================

TOPOLOGY_P = "periodic_shear"

NU_P = 2.5e-3

TAU0_P = 40.0 / 3.0

DT_PHYS_P = 0.01

DTAU_P = (
    DT_PHYS_P
    /
    TAU0_P
)

SEGMENT_TAU_P = 0.15

SEGMENT_STEPS_P = int(
    round(
        SEGMENT_TAU_P
        /
        DTAU_P
    )
)

FINAL_TAU_P = 2.0

TOTAL_STEPS_P = int(
    math.ceil(
        FINAL_TAU_P
        /
        DTAU_P
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. NUMERICAL REGIME
# ==================================================================================================

sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float32,
    torch.complex64,
    NU_P,
    TAU0_P,
)

ENV_P = make_env_D(
    40,
    L=8.0,
)

assert ENV_P["KX"].dtype == torch.float32
assert REAL_DTYPE_D == torch.float32
assert COMPLEX_DTYPE_D == torch.complex64


print("=" * 144)
print("M3D TEST P — PHYSICAL vs ROM-INDUCED ERROR AMPLIFICATION")
print("=" * 144)

print()
print(
    "GPU:",
    torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
)

print(
    "topology:",
    TOPOLOGY_P,
)

print(
    "nu:",
    NU_P,
)

print(
    "tau0:",
    TAU0_P,
)

print(
    "physical dt:",
    DT_PHYS_P,
)

print(
    "dtau:",
    DTAU_P,
)

print(
    "segment tau:",
    SEGMENT_TAU_P,
)

print(
    "segment steps:",
    SEGMENT_STEPS_P,
)

print(
    "final tau:",
    FINAL_TAU_P,
)

print(
    "total steps:",
    TOTAL_STEPS_P,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. HELPERS
# ==================================================================================================

def vecnorm_P(
    x,
):
    return float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            x
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def pairnorm_P(
    U1,
    U2,
):
    return float(
        pair_norm_D(
            U1,
            U2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def cosine_P(
    a,
    b,
):
    na = vecnorm_P(a)
    nb = vecnorm_P(b)

    if (
        na < 1e-30
        or
        nb < 1e-30
    ):
        return np.nan

    numerator = torch.sum(
        torch.conj(a) * b
    ).real

    denominator = (
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(a)
        *
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(b)
    )

    return float(
        (
            numerator
            /
            denominator
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def target_state_P(
    U1,
    U2,
    tids,
):
    return pack_modes_D(
        U1,
        U2,
        tids,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. INITIAL CONDITION
# ==================================================================================================

U10_P, U20_P = topology_pair_L2(
    TOPOLOGY_P,
    ENV_P,
)

U1_truth_P = U10_P.clone()
U2_truth_P = U20_P.clone()


global_star_P, global_diag_P = initialize_star2_N(
    U10_P,
    U20_P,
    ENV_P,
)


print()
print(
    "initial STAR2 ||RAf||/||Af||:",
    f"{global_diag_P['RAf_ratio']:.3e}",
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. SEGMENT LOOP
# ==================================================================================================

rows_P = []
reanchor_rows_P = []

current_step_P = 0
segment_index_P = 0

start_time_P = time.perf_counter()


while current_step_P < TOTAL_STEPS_P:

    segment_index_P += 1

    remaining_steps = (
        TOTAL_STEPS_P
        -
        current_step_P
    )

    this_steps = min(
        SEGMENT_STEPS_P,
        remaining_steps,
    )

    tau_start = (
        current_step_P
        *
        DTAU_P
    )

    tau_end = (
        (
            current_step_P
            +
            this_steps
        )
        *
        DTAU_P
    )

    segment_tau = (
        this_steps
        *
        DTAU_P
    )

    full_segment = bool(
        this_steps
        ==
        SEGMENT_STEPS_P
    )


    print()
    print("#" * 144)

    print(
        f"SEGMENT {segment_index_P:02d}"
        f" | tau {tau_start:.5f} -> {tau_end:.5f}"
        f" | Δtau={segment_tau:.5f}"
        f" | full={full_segment}"
    )

    print("#" * 144)


    # ==============================================================================================
    # STATES AT SEGMENT START
    # ==============================================================================================

    Ug1_start, Ug2_start = reconstruct_M(
        global_star_P,
        ENV_P,
    )

    Ut1_start = U1_truth_P.clone()
    Ut2_start = U2_truth_P.clone()

    tids = global_star_P[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]

    xG_start = target_state_P(
        Ug1_start,
        Ug2_start,
        tids,
    )

    xT_start = target_state_P(
        Ut1_start,
        Ut2_start,
        tids,
    )


    # Target perturbation at start.
    delta_target_start = (
        xG_start
        -
        xT_start
    )

    target_start_abs = vecnorm_P(
        delta_target_start
    )

    target_truth_start_norm = (
        vecnorm_P(
            xT_start
        )
        +
        1e-30
    )

    target_start_rel = (
        target_start_abs
        /
        target_truth_start_norm
    )


    # Full-state perturbation at start.
    full_start_abs = pairnorm_P(
        Ug1_start - Ut1_start,
        Ug2_start - Ut2_start,
    )

    full_truth_start_norm = (
        pairnorm_P(
            Ut1_start,
            Ut2_start,
        )
        +
        1e-30
    )

    full_start_rel = (
        full_start_abs
        /
        full_truth_start_norm
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # FOM SHADOW
    #
    # Start full Navier-Stokes from EXACT GLOBAL ERRONEOUS STATE.
    # ==============================================================================================

    U1_fom_P = Ug1_start.clone()
    U2_fom_P = Ug2_start.clone()


    # ==============================================================================================
    # STAR2 SHADOW
    #
    # Rebuild STAR2 from EXACT SAME GLOBAL ERRONEOUS STATE.
    # ==============================================================================================

    star_shadow_P, star_shadow_diag_P = initialize_star2_N(
        Ug1_start,
        Ug2_start,
        ENV_P,
    )


    print(
        f"  start target error       = {100*target_start_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  start full-state error   = {100*full_start_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  STAR shadow RAf          = {star_shadow_diag_P['RAf_ratio']:.3e}"
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # INTEGRATE SAME SEGMENT
    # ==============================================================================================

    for _ in range(
        this_steps
    ):

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Truth
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        U1_truth_P = full_rk4_D(
            U1_truth_P,
            DT_PHYS_P,
            NU_P,
            ENV_P,
        )

        U2_truth_P = full_rk4_D(
            U2_truth_P,
            DT_PHYS_P,
            NU_P,
            ENV_P,
        )


        # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        # FOM perturbation shadow
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        U1_fom_P = full_rk4_D(
            U1_fom_P,
            DT_PHYS_P,
            NU_P,
            ENV_P,
        )

        U2_fom_P = full_rk4_D(
            U2_fom_P,
            DT_PHYS_P,
            NU_P,
            ENV_P,
        )


        # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Teacher-free GLOBAL STAR2
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        rk4_M(
            global_star_P,
            DTAU_P,
            ENV_P,
        )


        # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        # STAR2 perturbation shadow
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        rk4_M(
            star_shadow_P,
            DTAU_P,
            ENV_P,
        )


    current_step_P += (
        this_steps
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # END STATES
    # ==============================================================================================

    Ug1_end, Ug2_end = reconstruct_M(
        global_star_P,
        ENV_P,
    )

    Us1_end, Us2_end = reconstruct_M(
        star_shadow_P,
        ENV_P,
    )

    Ut1_end = U1_truth_P
    Ut2_end = U2_truth_P

    Uf1_end = U1_fom_P
    Uf2_end = U2_fom_P


    # ==============================================================================================
    # TARGET VECTORS
    # ==============================================================================================

    xT_end = target_state_P(
        Ut1_end,
        Ut2_end,
        tids,
    )

    xF_end = target_state_P(
        Uf1_end,
        Uf2_end,
        tids,
    )

    xS_end = target_state_P(
        Us1_end,
        Us2_end,
        tids,
    )

    xG_end = target_state_P(
        Ug1_end,
        Ug2_end,
        tids,
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # EXACT VECTOR DECOMPOSITION
    #
    # e_STAR = e_FOM + e_ROM
    # ==============================================================================================

    e_fom = (
        xF_end
        -
        xT_end
    )

    e_star = (
        xS_end
        -
        xT_end
    )

    e_rom = (
        xS_end
        -
        xF_end
    )

    e_global = (
        xG_end
        -
        xT_end
    )


    end_truth_norm = (
        vecnorm_P(
            xT_end
        )
        +
        1e-30
    )


    fom_end_abs = vecnorm_P(
        e_fom
    )

    star_end_abs = vecnorm_P(
        e_star
    )

    rom_excess_abs = vecnorm_P(
        e_rom
    )

    global_end_abs = vecnorm_P(
        e_global
    )


    fom_end_rel = (
        fom_end_abs
        /
        end_truth_norm
    )

    star_end_rel = (
        star_end_abs
        /
        end_truth_norm
    )

    rom_excess_rel = (
        rom_excess_abs
        /
        end_truth_norm
    )

    global_end_rel = (
        global_end_abs
        /
        end_truth_norm
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # TARGET AMPLIFICATION
    # ==============================================================================================

    if target_start_abs > 1e-20:

        A_FOM_target = (
            fom_end_abs
            /
            target_start_abs
        )

        A_STAR_target = (
            star_end_abs
            /
            target_start_abs
        )

        Gamma_target = (
            A_STAR_target
            /
            max(
                A_FOM_target,
                1e-30,
            )
        )

    else:

        A_FOM_target = np.nan
        A_STAR_target = np.nan
        Gamma_target = np.nan


    # ==============================================================================================
    # FULL-STATE AMPLIFICATION
    # ==============================================================================================

    full_fom_end_abs = pairnorm_P(
        Uf1_end - Ut1_end,
        Uf2_end - Ut2_end,
    )

    full_star_end_abs = pairnorm_P(
        Us1_end - Ut1_end,
        Us2_end - Ut2_end,
    )

    if full_start_abs > 1e-20:

        A_FOM_full = (
            full_fom_end_abs
            /
            full_start_abs
        )

        A_STAR_full = (
            full_star_end_abs
            /
            full_start_abs
        )

        Gamma_full = (
            A_STAR_full
            /
            max(
                A_FOM_full,
                1e-30,
            )
        )

    else:

        A_FOM_full = np.nan
        A_STAR_full = np.nan
        Gamma_full = np.nan


    # ==============================================================================================
    # ROM-EXCESS GEOMETRY
    # ==============================================================================================

    rom_over_fom = (
        rom_excess_abs
        /
        max(
            fom_end_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    rom_over_star = (
        rom_excess_abs
        /
        max(
            star_end_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    physical_rom_cosine = cosine_P(
        e_fom,
        e_rom,
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # EXACT DECOMPOSITION CLOSURE
    # ==============================================================================================

    closure = vecnorm_P(
        e_star
        -
        (
            e_fom
            +
            e_rom
        )
    )

    closure_relative = (
        closure
        /
        max(
            star_end_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # GLOBAL vs INDEPENDENT STAR SHADOW CONSISTENCY
    #
    # They start from same state and use same STAR2 construction.
    # They should be nearly identical.
    # ==============================================================================================

    global_shadow_difference = vecnorm_P(
        xG_end
        -
        xS_end
    )

    global_shadow_relative = (
        global_shadow_difference
        /
        end_truth_norm
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # ROW
    # ==============================================================================================

    row = {
        "segment":
            segment_index_P,

        "tau_start":
            tau_start,

        "tau_end":
            tau_end,

        "segment_tau":
            segment_tau,

        "full_segment":
            full_segment,

        # Start perturbation
        "target_start_abs":
            target_start_abs,

        "target_start_rel":
            target_start_rel,

        "full_start_abs":
            full_start_abs,

        "full_start_rel":
            full_start_rel,

        # End relative errors
        "fom_end_target_rel":
            fom_end_rel,

        "star_end_target_rel":
            star_end_rel,

        "global_end_target_rel":
            global_end_rel,

        "rom_excess_target_rel":
            rom_excess_rel,

        # Amplification
        "A_FOM_target":
            A_FOM_target,

        "A_STAR_target":
            A_STAR_target,

        "Gamma_target":
            Gamma_target,

        "A_FOM_full":
            A_FOM_full,

        "A_STAR_full":
            A_STAR_full,

        "Gamma_full":
            Gamma_full,

        # ROM-vs-physical geometry
        "rom_over_fom":
            rom_over_fom,

        "rom_over_star":
            rom_over_star,

        "physical_rom_cosine":
            physical_rom_cosine,

        # Mechanism
        "star_shadow_RAf":
            star_shadow_diag_P[
                "RAf_ratio"
            ],

        # Numerical checks
        "decomposition_residual":
            closure_relative,

        "global_shadow_relative_difference":
            global_shadow_relative,
    }


    rows_P.append(
        row
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # PRINT
    # ==============================================================================================

    print()

    print(
        f"  FOM end target error     = {100*fom_end_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  STAR end target error    = {100*star_end_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  ROM excess ||S-F||       = {100*rom_excess_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print()


    if np.isfinite(
        A_FOM_target
    ):

        print(
            f"  A_FOM target             = {A_FOM_target:.6f}"
        )

        print(
            f"  A_STAR target            = {A_STAR_target:.6f}"
        )

        print(
            f"  Gamma target             = {Gamma_target:.6f}"
        )

        print()

        print(
            f"  A_FOM full               = {A_FOM_full:.6f}"
        )

        print(
            f"  A_STAR full              = {A_STAR_full:.6f}"
        )

        print(
            f"  Gamma full               = {Gamma_full:.6f}"
        )

    else:

        print(
            "  amplification undefined on segment 1"
        )

        print(
            "  because start perturbation is ~zero."
        )


    print()

    print(
        f"  ROM/FOM error norm       = {rom_over_fom:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  ROM/STAR error norm      = {rom_over_star:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  cos(FOM error, ROM error)= {physical_rom_cosine:.6f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  exact closure residual   = {closure_relative:.3e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  global-vs-shadow diff    = {100*global_shadow_relative:.10f}%"
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # SAVE AFTER EVERY SEGMENT
    # ==============================================================================================

    pd.DataFrame(
        rows_P
    ).to_csv(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_P_segments_partial.csv",
        index=False,
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # GLOBAL TEACHER-FREE REANCHOR
    # ==============================================================================================

    if current_step_P < TOTAL_STEPS_P:

        (
            global_star_P,
            global_diag_after_P,
            global_jump_P,
            global_build_time_P,
        ) = reanchor_star2_N(
            global_star_P,
            ENV_P,
        )

        reanchor_rows_P.append(
            {
                "after_segment":
                    segment_index_P,

                "tau":
                    tau_end,

                "RAf_ratio":
                    global_diag_after_P[
                        "RAf_ratio"
                    ],

                "jump":
                    global_jump_P,

                "build_time_s":
                    global_build_time_P,
            }
        )

        print()

        print(
            f"  GLOBAL reanchor"
            f" | RAf={global_diag_after_P['RAf_ratio']:.3e}"
            f" | jump={100*global_jump_P:.10f}%"
        )


    del star_shadow_P
    del U1_fom_P
    del U2_fom_P

    gc.collect()
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()


torch.cuda.synchronize()

TOTAL_RUNTIME_P = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    start_time_P
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. FINAL DATAFRAMES
# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_P = pd.DataFrame(
    rows_P
)

REANCHORS_P = pd.DataFrame(
    reanchor_rows_P
)


RESULTS_P.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_P_physical_vs_rom.csv",
    index=False,
)

REANCHORS_P.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_P_reanchors.csv",
    index=False,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. PRINT FULL TABLE
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 144)
print("TEST P — PHYSICAL vs ROM AMPLIFICATION")
print("=" * 144)
print()

display_cols_P = [
    "segment",
    "tau_start",
    "tau_end",

    "target_start_rel",

    "fom_end_target_rel",
    "star_end_target_rel",

    "A_FOM_target",
    "A_STAR_target",
    "Gamma_target",

    "rom_over_fom",
    "physical_rom_cosine",

    "A_FOM_full",
    "A_STAR_full",
    "Gamma_full",
]

print(
    RESULTS_P[
        display_cols_P
    ].to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.8g}",
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. VALID SEGMENTS FOR AMPLIFICATION ANALYSIS
#
# Segment 1 begins with ~zero error, so amplification is undefined.
# Exclude final short segment from primary comparison.
# ==================================================================================================

VALID_P = RESULTS_P[
    RESULTS_P[
        "full_segment"
    ]
    &
    np.isfinite(
        RESULTS_P[
            "Gamma_target"
        ]
    )
].copy()


if len(VALID_P) < 8:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Too few valid full segments for Test P analysis."
    )


# Split early/late.
n_valid_P = len(
    VALID_P
)

chunk_P = max(
    3,
    n_valid_P // 3,
)

EARLY_P = VALID_P.iloc[
    :chunk_P
]

LATE_P = VALID_P.iloc[
    -chunk_P:
]


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. SUMMARY METRICS
# ==================================================================================================

MEDIAN_A_FOM_P = float(
    VALID_P[
        "A_FOM_target"
    ].median()
)

MEDIAN_A_STAR_P = float(
    VALID_P[
        "A_STAR_target"
    ].median()
)

MEDIAN_GAMMA_P = float(
    VALID_P[
        "Gamma_target"
    ].median()
)


LATE_A_FOM_P = float(
    LATE_P[
        "A_FOM_target"
    ].median()
)

LATE_A_STAR_P = float(
    LATE_P[
        "A_STAR_target"
    ].median()
)

LATE_GAMMA_P = float(
    LATE_P[
        "Gamma_target"
    ].median()
)


EARLY_GAMMA_P = float(
    EARLY_P[
        "Gamma_target"
    ].median()
)


LATE_ROM_OVER_FOM_P = float(
    LATE_P[
        "rom_over_fom"
    ].median()
)

LATE_ROM_OVER_STAR_P = float(
    LATE_P[
        "rom_over_star"
    ].median()
)

LATE_COSINE_P = float(
    LATE_P[
        "physical_rom_cosine"
    ].median()
)


LATE_GAMMA_FULL_P = float(
    LATE_P[
        "Gamma_full"
    ].median()
)


MAX_CLOSURE_P = float(
    RESULTS_P[
        "decomposition_residual"
    ].max()
)

MAX_GLOBAL_SHADOW_DIFF_P = float(
    RESULTS_P[
        "global_shadow_relative_difference"
    ].max()
)

MAX_STAR_RAf_P = float(
    RESULTS_P[
        "star_shadow_RAf"
    ].max()
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 10. DIAGNOSTIC CLASSIFICATION
#
# These are diagnostic bands, not theorems.
# ==================================================================================================

NUMERICS_GATE_P = bool(
    MAX_CLOSURE_P
    <
    1e-5
    and
    MAX_GLOBAL_SHADOW_DIFF_P
    <
    1e-5
)


MECHANISM_GATE_P = bool(
    MAX_STAR_RAf_P
    <
    1e-4
)


PHYSICAL_LIKE_P = bool(
    0.80
    <=
    LATE_GAMMA_P
    <=
    1.25
    and
    LATE_ROM_OVER_FOM_P
    <
    0.25
)


ROM_EXTRA_AMPLIFICATION_P = bool(
    LATE_GAMMA_P
    >
    1.25
    and
    LATE_ROM_OVER_FOM_P
    >
    0.25
)


ROM_DAMPING_P = bool(
    LATE_GAMMA_P
    <
    0.80
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. FINAL DIAGNOSIS
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 144)
print("M3D TEST P — FINAL DIAGNOSIS")
print("=" * 144)
print()

print(
    "valid full segments:",
    len(VALID_P)
)

print()

print(
    "median A_FOM target:",
    f"{MEDIAN_A_FOM_P:.6f}"
)

print(
    "median A_STAR target:",
    f"{MEDIAN_A_STAR_P:.6f}"
)

print(
    "median Gamma:",
    f"{MEDIAN_GAMMA_P:.6f}"
)

print()

print(
    "early median Gamma:",
    f"{EARLY_GAMMA_P:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late median A_FOM:",
    f"{LATE_A_FOM_P:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late median A_STAR:",
    f"{LATE_A_STAR_P:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late median Gamma:",
    f"{LATE_GAMMA_P:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late median FULL-state Gamma:",
    f"{LATE_GAMMA_FULL_P:.6f}"
)

print()

print(
    "late median ||ROM excess|| / ||FOM error||:",
    f"{LATE_ROM_OVER_FOM_P:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late median ||ROM excess|| / ||STAR error||:",
    f"{LATE_ROM_OVER_STAR_P:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late median cos(FOM, ROM):",
    f"{LATE_COSINE_P:.6f}"
)

print()

print(
    "max exact decomposition residual:",
    f"{MAX_CLOSURE_P:.3e}"
)

print(
    "max global-vs-independent-STAR shadow difference:",
    f"{MAX_GLOBAL_SHADOW_DIFF_P:.3e}"
)

print(
    "max STAR2 acceleration residual:",
    f"{MAX_STAR_RAf_P:.3e}"
)

print()

print(
    "numerics gate:",
    NUMERICS_GATE_P
)

print(
    "STAR2 mechanism gate:",
    MECHANISM_GATE_P
)

print()

print(
    "physical-like amplification diagnostic:",
    PHYSICAL_LIKE_P
)

print(
    "ROM-extra-amplification diagnostic:",
    ROM_EXTRA_AMPLIFICATION_P
)

print(
    "ROM-damping diagnostic:",
    ROM_DAMPING_P
)

print()


if (
    NUMERICS_GATE_P
    and
    MECHANISM_GATE_P
    and
    PHYSICAL_LIKE_P
):

    print(
        "🔥 HISTORY GROWTH IS MOSTLY PHYSICAL."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Starting full Navier-Stokes and STAR2 from the SAME"
    )

    print(
        "perturbed state produces similar one-segment amplification."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "The long-horizon drift is therefore primarily inherited"
    )

    print(
        "flow sensitivity, not instability uniquely created by STAR2."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Next target: self-correction / uncertainty control,"
    )

    print(
        "because merely rebuilding the local model cannot remove"
    )

    print(
        "physically amplified state uncertainty."
    )


elif (
    NUMERICS_GATE_P
    and
    MECHANISM_GATE_P
    and
    ROM_EXTRA_AMPLIFICATION_P
):

    print(
        "🔥 THE REDUCED MAP ADDS EXTRA ERROR AMPLIFICATION."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "STAR2 separates from truth materially faster than full"
    )

    print(
        "Navier-Stokes does when both start from the same wrong state."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Next target: stability-aware / contraction-aware basis design"
    )

    print(
        "or propagation-regularized reanchoring."
    )


elif (
    NUMERICS_GATE_P
    and
    MECHANISM_GATE_P
    and
    ROM_DAMPING_P
):

    print(
        "🔥 STAR2 DAMPENS PERTURBATIONS RELATIVE TO THE FULL FLOW."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "The dominant global error growth is therefore not caused"
    )

    print(
        "by excess reduced-map instability."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "We should analyze physical sensitivity and accumulated"
    )

    print(
        "unobservable-state error next."
    )


elif (
    NUMERICS_GATE_P
    and
    MECHANISM_GATE_P
):

    print(
        "🟡 MIXED PHYSICAL / ROM-INDUCED AMPLIFICATION."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Neither a pure physical-sensitivity explanation nor a"
    )

    print(
        "pure ROM-instability explanation fits all late segments."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Inspect the per-segment Gamma and error-vector cosine"
    )

    print(
        "to identify where the mechanism switches."
    )


else:

    print(
        "❌ TEST P NUMERICAL / MECHANISM CHECK FAILED."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Do not interpret amplification until the consistency"
    )

    print(
        "or STAR2 mechanism issue is fixed."
    )


print()

print(
    "runtime:",
    f"{TOTAL_RUNTIME_P:.2f}s"
)


# ==================================================================================================

# ==================================================================================================

    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_P_COMPLETE.txt",
    "w",
) as f:

    f.write(
        "M3D TEST P COMPLETE\n"
        f"runtime_seconds={TOTAL_RUNTIME_P}\n"
        f"median_A_FOM={MEDIAN_A_FOM_P}\n"
        f"median_A_STAR={MEDIAN_A_STAR_P}\n"
        f"median_gamma={MEDIAN_GAMMA_P}\n"
        f"late_A_FOM={LATE_A_FOM_P}\n"
        f"late_A_STAR={LATE_A_STAR_P}\n"
        f"late_gamma={LATE_GAMMA_P}\n"
        f"late_gamma_full={LATE_GAMMA_FULL_P}\n"
        f"late_rom_over_fom={LATE_ROM_OVER_FOM_P}\n"
        f"late_cosine={LATE_COSINE_P}\n"
        f"physical_like={PHYSICAL_LIKE_P}\n"
        f"rom_extra_amplification={ROM_EXTRA_AMPLIFICATION_P}\n"
        f"rom_damping={ROM_DAMPING_P}\n"
    )


print()
print("=" * 144)
print("PERMANENT FILES")
print("=" * 144)

for filename in [
    "m3d_P_physical_vs_rom.csv",
    "m3d_P_reanchors.csv",
    "m3d_P_COMPLETE.txt",
]:

    print(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        filename
    )

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 17
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST Q — CAUSAL P/Q ERROR-TRANSFER DECOMPOSITION
#
# QUESTION
# --------
# Test O:
#   fresh STAR2 local error stays tiny;
#   inherited history dominates long-horizon error.
#
# Test P:
#   resolved history amplification is mostly physical Navier-Stokes sensitivity;
#   meanwhile a much larger full-state error reservoir grows outside the target P-space.
#
# Test Q asks:
#
#   DOES THAT HIDDEN Q-ERROR CAUSALLY LEAK BACK INTO THE OBSERVABLE P-SPACE?
#
#
# ACTUAL ERROR AT EACH SEGMENT START
# ----------------------------------
#
#       delta = U_global_STAR2 - U_truth
#
# Split exactly:
#
#       delta = P delta + Q delta
#
# where P is the prescribed observable Fourier-mode projector.
#
#
# FOUR FULL-NS TRAJECTORIES DURING EACH SEGMENT
# ---------------------------------------------
#
# Truth:
#
#       U
#
# P-only perturbation:
#
#       U + P delta
#
# Q-only perturbation:
#
#       U + Q delta
#
# Full actual perturbation:
#
#       U + delta
#
#
# At segment end measure ONLY observable P-error:
#
#       e_P    = P[Phi(U + Pdelta) - Phi(U)]
#
#       e_Q    = P[Phi(U + Qdelta) - Phi(U)]
#
#       e_full = P[Phi(U + delta)  - Phi(U)]
#
#
# IMPORTANT:
#
# Q-only begins with:
#
#       P(Q delta) = 0
#
# Therefore any later e_Q != 0 is direct Q -> P error transfer.
#
#
# NONLINEAR INTERACTION
# ---------------------
#
# Because Navier-Stokes is nonlinear:
#
#       e_full != e_P + e_Q
#
# in general.
#
# Define:
#
#       e_int = e_full - e_P - e_Q
#
# and a symmetric two-component interaction split:
#
#       phi_P = e_P + 0.5 e_int
#
#       phi_Q = e_Q + 0.5 e_int
#
# so exactly:
#
#       e_full = phi_P + phi_Q.
#
# This is the two-component Shapley-style decomposition of the nonlinear
# perturbation response relative to the truth baseline.
#
#
# FROZEN
# ------
#
# topology = periodic_shear
# nu = 0.0025
#
# N = 40
# L = 8
#
# tau0 = 40/3
# physical dt = 0.01
# dtau = 0.00075
#
# segment tau = 0.15
# final tau = 2
#
# fp32 / complex64
#
# The teacher-free STAR2 trajectory is NOT modified.
# P/Q shadows are SCORING-ONLY FOM experiments.
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch


# ==================================================================================================
# 0. PREFLIGHT
# ==================================================================================================

REQUIRED_Q = [
    "make_env_D",
    "topology_pair_L2",

    "initialize_star2_N",
    "reanchor_star2_N",

    "reconstruct_M",
    "rk4_M",

    "full_rk4_D",
    "pack_modes_D",
    "pair_norm_D",

    "sync_m3d_globals_MB",

    "REAL_DTYPE_D",
    "COMPLEX_DTYPE_D",
]

missing_Q = [
    name
    for name in REQUIRED_Q
    if name not in globals()
]

if missing_Q:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Missing Test-Q dependencies:\n"
        + repr(missing_Q)
        + "\n\nRun corrected Test N definitions first."
    )

if not torch.cuda.is_available():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "CUDA GPU required."
    )

if "SAVE_DIR" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "SAVE_DIR missing."
    )

SAVE_DIR = Path(SAVE_DIR)
SAVE_DIR.mkdir(
    parents=True,
    exist_ok=True,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. FROZEN SETTINGS
# ==================================================================================================

TOPOLOGY_Q = "periodic_shear"

NU_Q = 2.5e-3

TAU0_Q = 40.0 / 3.0

DT_PHYS_Q = 0.01

DTAU_Q = (
    DT_PHYS_Q
    /
    TAU0_Q
)

SEGMENT_TAU_Q = 0.15

SEGMENT_STEPS_Q = int(
    round(
        SEGMENT_TAU_Q
        /
        DTAU_Q
    )
)

FINAL_TAU_Q = 2.0

TOTAL_STEPS_Q = int(
    math.ceil(
        FINAL_TAU_Q
        /
        DTAU_Q
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. FORCE CORRECT FP32 LONG-HORIZON REGIME
# ==================================================================================================

sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float32,
    torch.complex64,
    NU_Q,
    TAU0_Q,
)

ENV_Q = make_env_D(
    40,
    L=8.0,
)

assert ENV_Q["KX"].dtype == torch.float32
assert REAL_DTYPE_D == torch.float32
assert COMPLEX_DTYPE_D == torch.complex64


print("=" * 148)
print("M3D TEST Q — CAUSAL P/Q ERROR-TRANSFER DECOMPOSITION")
print("=" * 148)

print()
print(
    "GPU:",
    torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
)

print(
    "topology:",
    TOPOLOGY_Q,
)

    "nu:",
    NU_Q,
)

print(
    "tau0:",
    TAU0_Q,
)

print(
    "physical dt:",
    DT_PHYS_Q,
)
