    "dtau:",
    DTAU_Q,
)

print(
    "segment tau:",
    SEGMENT_TAU_Q,
)

print(
    "segment steps:",
    SEGMENT_STEPS_Q,
)

print(
    "final tau:",
    FINAL_TAU_Q,
)

print(
    "total steps:",
    TOTAL_STEPS_Q,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. BASIC NORM HELPERS
# ==================================================================================================

def vecnorm_Q(
    x,
):
    return float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            x
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def pairnorm_Q(
    U1,
    U2,
):
    return float(
        pair_norm_D(
            U1,
            U2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def inner_real_Q(
    a,
    b,
):
    return float(
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(a)
            *
            b
        ).real.detach().cpu()
    )


def cosine_Q(
    a,
    b,
):
    na = vecnorm_Q(a)
    nb = vecnorm_Q(b)

    if (
        na < 1e-30
        or
        nb < 1e-30
    ):
        return np.nan

    return (
        inner_real_Q(
            a,
            b,
        )
        /
        (
            na
            *
            nb
        )
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. EXPLICIT P/Q SPLITTER
#
# Avoid relying on any unknown helper signature.
#
# target_ids are flattened spatial Fourier indices.
# We retain those entries for P and define Q = I - P.
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def split_PQ_Q(
    D1,
    D2,
    target_ids,
):
    P1 = torch.zeros_like(
        D1
    )

    P2 = torch.zeros_like(
        D2
    )

    D1f = D1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    D2f = D2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f = P1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P2f = P2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    P2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    Q1 = (
        D1
        -
        P1
    )

    Q2 = (
        D2
        -
        P2
    )

    return (
        P1,
        P2,
        Q1,
        Q2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. TARGET VECTOR
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def target_vector_Q(
    U1,
    U2,
    target_ids,
):
    return pack_modes_D(
        U1,
        U2,
        target_ids,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. SIGNED CONTRIBUTION ALONG FINAL ERROR
#
# If:
#
#       e_full = a + b
#
# define signed alignment contribution:
#
#       s_a = <a,e_full> / ||e_full||^2
#
# Then:
#
#       s_a + s_b = 1
#
# up to numerical precision.
# ==================================================================================================

def signed_share_Q(
    component,
    full_error,
):
    denom = (
        vecnorm_Q(
            full_error
        ) ** 2
    )

    if denom < 1e-30:
        return np.nan

    return (
        inner_real_Q(
            component,
            full_error,
        )
        /
        denom
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. INITIAL CONDITION
# ==================================================================================================

U10_Q, U20_Q = topology_pair_L2(
    TOPOLOGY_Q,
    ENV_Q,
)

U1_truth_Q = U10_Q.clone()
U2_truth_Q = U20_Q.clone()


global_star_Q, global_diag_Q = initialize_star2_N(
    U10_Q,
    U20_Q,
    ENV_Q,
)


TARGET_IDS_Q = global_star_Q[
    "proj"
][
    "target_ids"
]


print()
print(
    "initial STAR2 ||RAf||/||Af||:",
    f"{global_diag_Q['RAf_ratio']:.3e}",
)

print(
    "target Fourier indices:",
    len(TARGET_IDS_Q),
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. SEGMENT LOOP
# ==================================================================================================

rows_Q = []
reanchor_rows_Q = []

current_step_Q = 0
segment_index_Q = 0

start_time_Q = time.perf_counter()


while current_step_Q < TOTAL_STEPS_Q:

    segment_index_Q += 1

    remaining_steps = (
        TOTAL_STEPS_Q
        -
        current_step_Q
    )

    this_steps = min(
        SEGMENT_STEPS_Q,
        remaining_steps,
    )

    tau_start = (
        current_step_Q
        *
        DTAU_Q
    )

    tau_end = (
        (
            current_step_Q
            +
            this_steps
        )
        *
        DTAU_Q
    )

    segment_tau = (
        this_steps
        *
        DTAU_Q
    )

    full_segment = bool(
        this_steps
        ==
        SEGMENT_STEPS_Q
    )


    print()
    print("#" * 148)

    print(
        f"SEGMENT {segment_index_Q:02d}"
        f" | tau {tau_start:.5f} -> {tau_end:.5f}"
        f" | Δtau={segment_tau:.5f}"
        f" | full={full_segment}"
    )

    print("#" * 148)


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8A. ACTUAL TEACHER-FREE GLOBAL ERROR AT SEGMENT START
    # ==============================================================================================

    Ug1_start, Ug2_start = reconstruct_M(
        global_star_Q,
        ENV_Q,
    )

    Ut1_start = U1_truth_Q.clone()
    Ut2_start = U2_truth_Q.clone()


    D1 = (
        Ug1_start
        -
        Ut1_start
    )

    D2 = (
        Ug2_start
        -
        Ut2_start
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8B. SPLIT ACTUAL ERROR INTO P AND Q
    # ==============================================================================================

    (
        PD1,
        PD2,
        QD1,
        QD2,
    ) = split_PQ_Q(
        D1,
        D2,
        TARGET_IDS_Q,
    )


    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Verify exact reconstruction.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    split_residual_abs = pairnorm_Q(
        (
            PD1
            +
            QD1
            -
            D1
        ),
        (
            PD2
            +
            QD2
            -
            D2
        ),
    )

    delta_norm = (
        pairnorm_Q(
            D1,
            D2,
        )
        +
        1e-30
    )

    split_residual_rel = (
        split_residual_abs
        /
        delta_norm
    )


    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Norms of the two reservoirs.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    P_start_abs = pairnorm_Q(
        PD1,
        PD2,
    )

    Q_start_abs = pairnorm_Q(
        QD1,
        QD2,
    )

    FULL_start_abs = pairnorm_Q(
        D1,
        D2,
    )


    reservoir_Q_over_P = (
        Q_start_abs
        /
        max(
            P_start_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Q component MUST contain no target error initially.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    q_target_start = target_vector_Q(
        QD1,
        QD2,
        TARGET_IDS_Q,
    )

    p_target_start = target_vector_Q(
        PD1,
        PD2,
        TARGET_IDS_Q,
    )

    full_target_start = target_vector_Q(
        D1,
        D2,
        TARGET_IDS_Q,
    )

    q_target_leak_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        q_target_start
    )

    p_target_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        p_target_start
    )

    full_target_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        full_target_start
    )


    q_start_leak_ratio = (
        q_target_leak_abs
        /
        max(
            full_target_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    print(
        f"  actual full-state |delta| = {FULL_start_abs:.8e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  P reservoir norm          = {P_start_abs:.8e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Q reservoir norm          = {Q_start_abs:.8e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Q/P reservoir ratio       = {reservoir_Q_over_P:.6f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  observable start error    = {full_target_abs:.8e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Q observable leakage      = {q_target_leak_abs:.8e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Q-start leak ratio        = {q_start_leak_ratio:.3e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  P/Q split residual        = {split_residual_rel:.3e}"
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8C. CREATE THREE CAUSAL FOM SHADOWS
    #
    # P-only:
    #
    #       U_truth + P delta
    #
    # Q-only:
    #
    #       U_truth + Q delta
    #
    # FULL:
    #
    #       U_truth + delta = actual STAR2 predicted state
    # ==============================================================================================

    U1_Pshadow = (
        Ut1_start
        +
        PD1
    )

    U2_Pshadow = (
        Ut2_start
        +
        PD2
    )


    U1_Qshadow = (
        Ut1_start
        +
        QD1
    )

    U2_Qshadow = (
        Ut2_start
        +
        QD2
    )


    U1_FULLshadow = (
        Ut1_start
        +
        D1
    )

    U2_FULLshadow = (
        Ut2_start
        +
        D2
    )


    # Verify FULL shadow is literally the STAR2 global start state.
    full_start_match = (
        pairnorm_Q(
            U1_FULLshadow
            -
            Ug1_start,

            U2_FULLshadow
            -
            Ug2_start,
        )
        /
        (
            pairnorm_Q(
                Ug1_start,
                Ug2_start,
            )
            +
            1e-30
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8D. ADVANCE:
    #
    # truth
    # P-only FOM
    # Q-only FOM
    # full-error FOM
    # global STAR2
    # ==============================================================================================

    for _ in range(
        this_steps
    ):

        # Truth
        U1_truth_Q = full_rk4_D(
            U1_truth_Q,
            DT_PHYS_Q,
            NU_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )

        U2_truth_Q = full_rk4_D(
            U2_truth_Q,
            DT_PHYS_Q,
            NU_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )


        # P-only FOM shadow
        U1_Pshadow = full_rk4_D(
            U1_Pshadow,
            DT_PHYS_Q,
            NU_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )

        U2_Pshadow = full_rk4_D(
            U2_Pshadow,
            DT_PHYS_Q,
            NU_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )


        # Q-only FOM shadow
        U1_Qshadow = full_rk4_D(
            U1_Qshadow,
            DT_PHYS_Q,
            NU_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )

        U2_Qshadow = full_rk4_D(
            U2_Qshadow,
            DT_PHYS_Q,
            NU_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )


        # Full actual-error FOM shadow
        U1_FULLshadow = full_rk4_D(
            U1_FULLshadow,
            DT_PHYS_Q,
            NU_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )

        U2_FULLshadow = full_rk4_D(
            U2_FULLshadow,
            DT_PHYS_Q,
            NU_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )


        # Actual teacher-free STAR2 trajectory
        rk4_M(
            global_star_Q,
            DTAU_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )


    current_step_Q += (
        this_steps
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8E. END-OF-SEGMENT TARGET VECTORS
    # ==============================================================================================

    xTruth_end = target_vector_Q(
        U1_truth_Q,
        U2_truth_Q,
        TARGET_IDS_Q,
    )

    xP_end = target_vector_Q(
        U1_Pshadow,
        U2_Pshadow,
        TARGET_IDS_Q,
    )

    xQ_end = target_vector_Q(
        U1_Qshadow,
        U2_Qshadow,
        TARGET_IDS_Q,
    )

    xFULL_end = target_vector_Q(
        U1_FULLshadow,
        U2_FULLshadow,
        TARGET_IDS_Q,
    )


    Ug1_end, Ug2_end = reconstruct_M(
        global_star_Q,
        ENV_Q,
    )

    xGLOBAL_end = target_vector_Q(
        Ug1_end,
        Ug2_end,
        TARGET_IDS_Q,
    )


    truth_target_norm = (
        vecnorm_Q(
            xTruth_end
        )
        +
        1e-30
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8F. CAUSAL RESPONSES
    # ==============================================================================================

    eP = (
        xP_end
        -
        xTruth_end
    )

    eQ = (
        xQ_end
        -
        xTruth_end
    )

    eFULL = (
        xFULL_end
        -
        xTruth_end
    )

    eGLOBAL = (
        xGLOBAL_end
        -
        xTruth_end
    )


    # Nonlinear P/Q interaction.
    eINT = (
        eFULL
        -
        eP
        -
        eQ
    )


    # Symmetric two-component nonlinear attribution.
    phiP = (
        eP
        +
        0.5
        *
        eINT
    )

    phiQ = (
        eQ
        +
        0.5
        *
        eINT
    )


    # Verify:
    #
    # eFULL = phiP + phiQ
    shapley_closure = (
        vecnorm_Q(
            eFULL
            -
            phiP
            -
            phiQ
        )
        /
        (
            vecnorm_Q(
                eFULL
            )
            +
            1e-30
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8G. RESPONSE NORMS
    # ==============================================================================================

    P_response_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        eP
    )

    Q_response_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        eQ
    )

    FULL_response_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        eFULL
    )

    GLOBAL_response_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        eGLOBAL
    )

    INT_response_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        eINT
    )

    PHIP_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        phiP
    )

    PHIQ_abs = vecnorm_Q(
        phiQ
    )


    P_response_rel = (
        P_response_abs
        /
        truth_target_norm
    )

    Q_response_rel = (
        Q_response_abs
        /
        truth_target_norm
    )

    FULL_response_rel = (
        FULL_response_abs
        /
        truth_target_norm
    )

    GLOBAL_response_rel = (
        GLOBAL_response_abs
        /
        truth_target_norm
    )

    INT_response_rel = (
        INT_response_abs
        /
        truth_target_norm
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8H. NORM RATIOS RELATIVE TO ACTUAL FOM ERROR
    # ==============================================================================================

    P_over_FULL = (
        P_response_abs
        /
        max(
            FULL_response_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    Q_over_FULL = (
        Q_response_abs
        /
        max(
            FULL_response_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    INT_over_FULL = (
        INT_response_abs
        /
        max(
            FULL_response_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    PHIP_over_FULL = (
        PHIP_abs
        /
        max(
            FULL_response_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )

    PHIQ_over_FULL = (
        PHIQ_abs
        /
        max(
            FULL_response_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8I. SIGNED CONTRIBUTIONS ALONG ACTUAL FINAL ERROR
    #
    # phiP + phiQ = eFULL
    #
    # therefore signed shares should sum to 1.
    # ==============================================================================================

    P_signed_share = signed_share_Q(
        phiP,
        eFULL,
    )

    Q_signed_share = signed_share_Q(
        phiQ,
        eFULL,
    )

    signed_share_sum = (
        P_signed_share
        +
        Q_signed_share
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8J. RAW VECTOR GEOMETRY
    # ==============================================================================================

    P_FULL_cosine = cosine_Q(
        eP,
        eFULL,
    )

    Q_FULL_cosine = cosine_Q(
        eQ,
        eFULL,
    )

    P_Q_cosine = cosine_Q(
        eP,
        eQ,
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8K. TRANSFER EFFICIENCY PER UNIT STARTING RESERVOIR
    #
    # Same output norm / same full-state input norm convention for P and Q.
    #
    # This lets us distinguish:
    #
    #   "Q dominates because there is tons of Q error"
    #
    # from:
    #
    #   "Q is intrinsically more efficient at producing future P error."
    # ==============================================================================================

    if P_start_abs > 1e-20:

        P_to_P_eff = (
            P_response_abs
            /
            P_start_abs
        )

    else:

        P_to_P_eff = np.nan


    if Q_start_abs > 1e-20:

        Q_to_P_eff = (
            Q_response_abs
            /
            Q_start_abs
        )

    else:

        Q_to_P_eff = np.nan


    if (
        np.isfinite(P_to_P_eff)
        and
        np.isfinite(Q_to_P_eff)
        and
        P_to_P_eff > 1e-30
    ):

        Q_vs_P_efficiency = (
            Q_to_P_eff
            /
            P_to_P_eff
        )

    else:

        Q_vs_P_efficiency = np.nan


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8L. GLOBAL STAR vs FULL-NS FROM SAME ERRONEOUS START
    #
    # This should reproduce the Test-P distinction:
    #
    # full shadow = physical propagation of actual error
    # global STAR = physical propagation + fresh ROM error
    # ==============================================================================================

    STAR_minus_FOM = (
        xGLOBAL_end
        -
        xFULL_end
    )

    star_rom_excess_rel = (
        vecnorm_Q(
            STAR_minus_FOM
        )
        /
        truth_target_norm
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8M. STORE
    # ==============================================================================================

    row = {
        "segment":
            segment_index_Q,

        "tau_start":
            tau_start,

        "tau_end":
            tau_end,

        "segment_tau":
            segment_tau,

        "full_segment":
            full_segment,

        # Start split
        "full_start_norm":
            FULL_start_abs,

        "P_start_norm":
            P_start_abs,

        "Q_start_norm":
            Q_start_abs,

        "Q_over_P_reservoir":
            reservoir_Q_over_P,

        "Q_target_start_leak":
            q_target_leak_abs,

        "Q_start_leak_ratio":
            q_start_leak_ratio,

        "split_residual":
            split_residual_rel,

        "full_start_match":
            full_start_match,

        # Raw causal end errors
        "P_only_end_target_error":
            P_response_rel,

        "Q_only_end_target_error":
            Q_response_rel,

        "FULL_end_target_error":
            FULL_response_rel,

        "GLOBAL_STAR_end_target_error":
            GLOBAL_response_rel,

        "interaction_target_error":
            INT_response_rel,

        # Ratios
        "P_over_FULL":
            P_over_FULL,

        "Q_over_FULL":
            Q_over_FULL,

        "interaction_over_FULL":
            INT_over_FULL,

        # Symmetric nonlinear attribution
        "phiP_over_FULL":
            PHIP_over_FULL,

        "phiQ_over_FULL":
            PHIQ_over_FULL,

        "P_signed_share":
            P_signed_share,

        "Q_signed_share":
            Q_signed_share,

        "signed_share_sum":
            signed_share_sum,

        # Geometry
        "P_FULL_cosine":
            P_FULL_cosine,

        "Q_FULL_cosine":
            Q_FULL_cosine,

        "P_Q_cosine":
            P_Q_cosine,

        # Transfer efficiency
        "P_to_P_eff":
            P_to_P_eff,

        "Q_to_P_eff":
            Q_to_P_eff,

        "Q_vs_P_efficiency":
            Q_vs_P_efficiency,

        # STAR-vs-FOM residual
        "STAR_minus_FOM_target_error":
            star_rom_excess_rel,

        # exact nonlinear attribution closure
        "shapley_closure":
            shapley_closure,
    }


    rows_Q.append(
        row
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8N. PRINT SEGMENT
    # ==============================================================================================

    print()
    print(
        f"  P-only -> P error         = {100*P_response_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  Q-only -> P error         = {100*Q_response_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  FULL actual FOM error     = {100*FULL_response_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  nonlinear interaction     = {100*INT_response_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  ||P response||/||FULL||   = {P_over_FULL:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  ||Q response||/||FULL||   = {Q_over_FULL:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  ||interaction||/||FULL||  = {INT_over_FULL:.6f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  symmetric P share         = {P_signed_share:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  symmetric Q share         = {Q_signed_share:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  share sum                 = {signed_share_sum:.9f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  cos(P response, FULL)     = {P_FULL_cosine:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  cos(Q response, FULL)     = {Q_FULL_cosine:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  cos(P response, Q resp.)  = {P_Q_cosine:.6f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  P->P efficiency           = {P_to_P_eff:.6e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Q->P efficiency           = {Q_to_P_eff:.6e}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Q/P efficiency ratio      = {Q_vs_P_efficiency:.6f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  STAR - physical FOM error = {100*star_rom_excess_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  nonlinear closure         = {shapley_closure:.3e}"
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8O. CHECKPOINT AFTER EVERY SEGMENT
    # ==============================================================================================

    pd.DataFrame(
        rows_Q
    ).to_csv(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_Q_segments_partial.csv",
        index=False,
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8P. TEACHER-FREE STAR2 REANCHOR FOR NEXT SEGMENT
    # ==============================================================================================

    if current_step_Q < TOTAL_STEPS_Q:

        (
            global_star_Q,
            global_diag_after_Q,
            global_jump_Q,
            global_build_Q,
        ) = reanchor_star2_N(
            global_star_Q,
            ENV_Q,
        )

        reanchor_rows_Q.append(
            {
                "after_segment":
                    segment_index_Q,

                "tau":
                    tau_end,

                "RAf_ratio":
                    global_diag_after_Q[
                        "RAf_ratio"
                    ],

                "jump":
                    global_jump_Q,

                "build_time_s":
                    global_build_Q,
            }
        )

        print()

        print(
            f"  GLOBAL STAR reanchor"
            f" | RAf={global_diag_after_Q['RAf_ratio']:.3e}"
            f" | jump={100*global_jump_Q:.10f}%"
        )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # CLEAN
    # ==============================================================================================

    del U1_Pshadow
    del U2_Pshadow
    del U1_Qshadow
    del U2_Qshadow
    del U1_FULLshadow
    del U2_FULLshadow

    gc.collect()
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()


torch.cuda.synchronize()

TOTAL_RUNTIME_Q = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    start_time_Q
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. FINAL TABLES
# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_Q = pd.DataFrame(
    rows_Q
)

REANCHORS_Q = pd.DataFrame(
    reanchor_rows_Q
)


RESULTS_Q.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_Q_PQ_transfer.csv",
    index=False,
)

REANCHORS_Q.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_Q_reanchors.csv",
    index=False,
)


print()
print("=" * 148)
print("TEST Q — P/Q CAUSAL TRANSFER TABLE")
print("=" * 148)
print()

display_cols_Q = [
    "segment",
    "tau_start",
    "tau_end",

    "Q_over_P_reservoir",

    "P_only_end_target_error",
    "Q_only_end_target_error",
    "FULL_end_target_error",

    "P_over_FULL",
    "Q_over_FULL",
    "interaction_over_FULL",

    "P_signed_share",
    "Q_signed_share",

    "P_to_P_eff",
    "Q_to_P_eff",
    "Q_vs_P_efficiency",
]

print(
    RESULTS_Q[
        display_cols_Q
    ].to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.8g}",
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 10. PRIMARY ANALYSIS SET
#
# EXCLUDE:
#
# - segment 1: actual error starts essentially at zero
# - final short segment: not a 0.15-tau comparison
# ==================================================================================================

VALID_Q = RESULTS_Q[
    RESULTS_Q[
        "full_segment"
    ]
    &
    (
        RESULTS_Q[
            "segment"
        ]
        >
        1
    )
].copy()


if len(VALID_Q) < 8:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Too few valid segments for Test Q."
    )


n_valid_Q = len(
    VALID_Q
)

chunk_Q = max(
    3,
    n_valid_Q // 3,
)

EARLY_Q = VALID_Q.iloc[
    :chunk_Q
]

LATE_Q = VALID_Q.iloc[
    -chunk_Q:
]


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. SUMMARY METRICS
# ==================================================================================================

MEDIAN_RESERVOIR_Q = float(
    VALID_Q[
        "Q_over_P_reservoir"
    ].median()
)

EARLY_RESERVOIR_Q = float(
    EARLY_Q[
        "Q_over_P_reservoir"
    ].median()
)

LATE_RESERVOIR_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "Q_over_P_reservoir"
    ].median()
)


MEDIAN_Q_OVER_FULL_Q = float(
    VALID_Q[
        "Q_over_FULL"
    ].median()
)

LATE_Q_OVER_FULL_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "Q_over_FULL"
    ].median()
)

LATE_P_OVER_FULL_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "P_over_FULL"
    ].median()
)

LATE_INTERACTION_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "interaction_over_FULL"
    ].median()
)


LATE_P_SHARE_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "P_signed_share"
    ].median()
)

LATE_Q_SHARE_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "Q_signed_share"
    ].median()
)


MEDIAN_EFF_RATIO_Q = float(
    VALID_Q[
        "Q_vs_P_efficiency"
    ]
    .replace(
        [np.inf, -np.inf],
        np.nan,
    )
    .dropna()
    .median()
)

LATE_EFF_RATIO_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "Q_vs_P_efficiency"
    ]
    .replace(
        [np.inf, -np.inf],
        np.nan,
    )
    .dropna()
    .median()
)


MAX_Q_START_LEAK_Q = float(
    RESULTS_Q[
        "Q_start_leak_ratio"
    ].replace(
        [np.inf, -np.inf],
        np.nan,
    ).dropna().max()
)

MAX_SPLIT_RESIDUAL_Q = float(
    RESULTS_Q[
        "split_residual"
    ].max()
)

MAX_FULL_START_MATCH_Q = float(
    RESULTS_Q[
        "full_start_match"
    ].max()
)

MAX_SHAPLEY_CLOSURE_Q = float(
    RESULTS_Q[
        "shapley_closure"
    ].max()
)


# ==============================================================================================
# Absolute Q-only leakage relative to truth, late.
# ==============================================================================================

LATE_Q_ONLY_ERROR_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "Q_only_end_target_error"
    ].median()
)

LATE_P_ONLY_ERROR_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "P_only_end_target_error"
    ].median()
)

LATE_FULL_ERROR_Q = float(
    LATE_Q[
        "FULL_end_target_error"
    ].median()
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 12. DIAGNOSTIC GATES
# ==================================================================================================

NUMERICS_GATE_Q = bool(
    MAX_SPLIT_RESIDUAL_Q
    <
    1e-6
    and
    MAX_FULL_START_MATCH_Q
    <
    1e-6
    and
    MAX_SHAPLEY_CLOSURE_Q
    <
    1e-6
)


Q_ZERO_START_GATE_Q = bool(
    MAX_Q_START_LEAK_Q
    <
    1e-5
)


# Causal classification.
#
# These are diagnostic thresholds, not mathematical theorems.

Q_DOMINANT_Q = bool(
    LATE_Q_SHARE_Q
    >
    0.60
)

P_DOMINANT_Q = bool(
    LATE_P_SHARE_Q
    >
    0.60
)

MIXED_Q = bool(
    not Q_DOMINANT_Q
    and
    not P_DOMINANT_Q
)


STRONG_NONLINEAR_INTERACTION_Q = bool(
    LATE_INTERACTION_Q
    >
    0.25
)


LARGE_Q_RESERVOIR_Q = bool(
    LATE_RESERVOIR_Q
    >
    5.0
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 13. FINAL DIAGNOSIS
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 148)
print("M3D TEST Q — FINAL DIAGNOSIS")
print("=" * 148)
print()

print(
    "valid full segments:",
    len(VALID_Q)
)

print()

print(
    "median Q/P starting reservoir:",
    f"{MEDIAN_RESERVOIR_Q:.6f}x"
)

print(
    "early median Q/P reservoir:",
    f"{EARLY_RESERVOIR_Q:.6f}x"
)

print(
    "late median Q/P reservoir:",
    f"{LATE_RESERVOIR_Q:.6f}x"
)

print()

print(
    "late median P-only -> P error:",
    f"{100*LATE_P_ONLY_ERROR_Q:.8f}%"
)

print(
    "late median Q-only -> P error:",
    f"{100*LATE_Q_ONLY_ERROR_Q:.8f}%"
)

print(
    "late median FULL perturbation -> P error:",
    f"{100*LATE_FULL_ERROR_Q:.8f}%"
)

print()

print(
    "late ||P-only response|| / ||FULL response||:",
    f"{LATE_P_OVER_FULL_Q:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late ||Q-only response|| / ||FULL response||:",
    f"{LATE_Q_OVER_FULL_Q:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late nonlinear interaction / FULL:",
    f"{LATE_INTERACTION_Q:.6f}"
)

print()

print(
    "late symmetric P signed share:",
    f"{LATE_P_SHARE_Q:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late symmetric Q signed share:",
    f"{LATE_Q_SHARE_Q:.6f}"
)

print(
    "P+Q signed share:",
    f"{LATE_P_SHARE_Q + LATE_Q_SHARE_Q:.6f}"
)

print()

print(
    "median Q/P transfer-efficiency ratio:",
    f"{MEDIAN_EFF_RATIO_Q:.6f}"
)

print(
    "late Q/P transfer-efficiency ratio:",
    f"{LATE_EFF_RATIO_Q:.6f}"
)

print()

print(
    "max Q target leakage at segment start:",
    f"{MAX_Q_START_LEAK_Q:.3e}"
)

print(
    "max P/Q split residual:",
    f"{MAX_SPLIT_RESIDUAL_Q:.3e}"
)

print(
    "max FULL-start reconstruction mismatch:",
    f"{MAX_FULL_START_MATCH_Q:.3e}"
)

print(
    "max symmetric-decomposition closure:",
    f"{MAX_SHAPLEY_CLOSURE_Q:.3e}"
)

print()

print(
    "numerics gate:",
    NUMERICS_GATE_Q
)

print(
    "Q starts observably zero gate:",
    Q_ZERO_START_GATE_Q
)

print(
    "large hidden Q reservoir:",
    LARGE_Q_RESERVOIR_Q
)

print()

print(
    "Q-dominant causal attribution:",
    Q_DOMINANT_Q
)

print(
    "P-dominant causal attribution:",
    P_DOMINANT_Q
)

print(
    "mixed P/Q attribution:",
    MIXED_Q
)

print(
    "strong nonlinear interaction:",
    STRONG_NONLINEAR_INTERACTION_Q
)

print()


if (
    NUMERICS_GATE_Q
    and
    Q_ZERO_START_GATE_Q
    and
    Q_DOMINANT_Q
):

    print(
        "🔥🔥🔥 Q -> P LEAKAGE IS THE DOMINANT SOURCE OF FUTURE OBSERVABLE ERROR."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "The Q-only perturbation begins with essentially ZERO target error,"
    )

    print(
        "yet it generates most of the later resolved error."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "This identifies the hidden-state error reservoir as a causal"
    )

    print(
        "driver of long-horizon observable drift."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Next target:"
    )

    print(
        "design an error-aware hidden-state correction / retention criterion"
    )

    print(
        "that suppresses dangerous Q directions before they leak into P."
    )


elif (
    NUMERICS_GATE_Q
    and
    Q_ZERO_START_GATE_Q
    and
    P_DOMINANT_Q
):

    print(
        "🔥 OBSERVABLE P-ERROR MOSTLY AMPLIFIES ITSELF."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "A large Q reservoir may exist, but causal Q -> P transfer"
    )

    print(
        "is not the dominant source of future observable error."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Next target:"
    )

    print(
        "resolved-state uncertainty propagation / correction,"
    )

    print(
        "not additional hidden-state modeling."
    )


elif (
    NUMERICS_GATE_Q
    and
    Q_ZERO_START_GATE_Q
):

    print(
        "🟡 MIXED P / Q ERROR-TRANSFER MECHANISM."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Both existing observable error and hidden-state error"
    )

    print(
        "materially contribute to future target drift."
    )

    if STRONG_NONLINEAR_INTERACTION_Q:

        print()

        print(
            "Nonlinear P-Q interaction is also large,"
        )

        print(
            "so independent linear leakage coefficients will be insufficient."
        )

    print()

    print(
        "Next target:"
    )

    print(
        "identify the specific Q directions with largest future P influence."
    )


else:

    print(
        "❌ TEST Q NUMERICAL CAUSAL SPLIT FAILED."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Do not interpret P/Q attribution until the split and"
    )

    print(
        "zero-observable-start checks pass."
    )


print()

print(
    "runtime:",
    f"{TOTAL_RUNTIME_Q:.2f}s"
)

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 18
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST R — LEAKAGE-ACTIVE HIDDEN SUBSPACE
#
# EMPIRICAL Q-ERROR POD + FINITE-TIME LEAKAGE SVD + CAUSAL TOP-k VALIDATION
#
#
# PRIOR RESULTS
# -------------
#
# M-B:
#     STAR2 local resolved error is genuinely O(tau^4).
#
# O:
#     long-horizon error is overwhelmingly inherited/history error.
#
# P:
#     resolved amplification is mostly physical rather than ROM-instability.
#
# Q:
#     both P-error and Q-error causally generate future observable P-error.
#
#     At tau = 1.35 -> 1.50:
#
#         P-only -> P = ~0.123%
#         Q-only -> P = ~0.175%
#
#
# TEST R QUESTION
# ---------------
#
# Is the ACTUALLY OCCUPIED hidden-error space low-dimensional,
# and does its future Q -> P leakage concentrate into only a few directions?
#
#
# OPERATOR OF INTEREST
# --------------------
#
#       T(U,h) = P D Phi_h(U) Q
#
# where Phi_h is the full Navier-Stokes flow map.
#
#
# IMPORTANT LIMITATION
# --------------------
#
# We do NOT claim to compute T over the entire enormous Q-space.
#
# Instead:
#
#   1. collect the actual Q-errors generated by teacher-free STAR2
#   2. POD their span
#   3. restrict T to that empirical Q-error subspace
#   4. compute its SVD accurately using full-NS finite differences
#
#
# This answers the practically relevant question:
#
#     "Among hidden directions M3D actually accumulates,
#      are only a few responsible for future observable leakage?"
#
#
# FROZEN
# ------
#
# topology = periodic_shear
# nu = 0.0025
#
# N = 40
# L = 8
#
# tau0 = 40/3
#
# teacher-free STAR2:
#     physical dt = 0.01
#     reanchor = 0.15 tau
#
# leakage horizon:
#     h = 0.15 tau = 2 physical time units
#
# critical base state:
#     tau* = 1.35
#
# Snapshot times:
#     0.15, 0.30, ..., 1.35
#
#
# TRAJECTORY GENERATION:
#     fp32 / complex64
#
# LEAKAGE LINEARIZATION:
#     genuine fp64 / complex128
#
# finite-difference relative perturbation:
#     1e-5 of ||U(tau*)||
#
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch


# ==================================================================================================
# 0. PREFLIGHT
# ==================================================================================================

REQUIRED_R = [
    "make_env_D",
    "topology_pair_L2",

    "initialize_star2_N",
    "reanchor_star2_N",

    "reconstruct_M",
    "rk4_M",

    "full_rk4_D",
    "pack_modes_D",
    "pair_norm_D",

    "sync_m3d_globals_MB",

    "REAL_DTYPE_D",
    "COMPLEX_DTYPE_D",
]

missing_R = [
    name
    for name in REQUIRED_R
    if name not in globals()
]

if missing_R:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Missing Test-R dependencies:\n"
        + repr(missing_R)
        + "\n\nRun corrected Test N definitions first."
    )

if not torch.cuda.is_available():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "CUDA GPU required."
    )

if "SAVE_DIR" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "SAVE_DIR missing."
    )

SAVE_DIR = Path(SAVE_DIR)

SAVE_DIR.mkdir(
    parents=True,
    exist_ok=True,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. FROZEN SETTINGS
# ==================================================================================================

TOPOLOGY_R = "periodic_shear"

NU_R = 2.5e-3

TAU0_R = 40.0 / 3.0

DT_PHYS_R = 0.01

DTAU_R = (
    DT_PHYS_R
    /
    TAU0_R
)

SEGMENT_TAU_R = 0.15

SEGMENT_STEPS_R = int(
    round(
        SEGMENT_TAU_R
        /
        DTAU_R
    )
)

CRITICAL_TAU_R = 1.35

CRITICAL_SEGMENTS_R = int(
    round(
        CRITICAL_TAU_R
        /
        SEGMENT_TAU_R
    )
)

SNAPSHOT_TAUS_R = [
    SEGMENT_TAU_R * i
    for i in range(
        1,
        CRITICAL_SEGMENTS_R + 1,
    )
]

EPS_REL_R = 1e-5

POD_EPS_R = 1e-12

TOPK_TESTS_R = [
    1,
    2,
    3,
    5,
]


print("=" * 148)
print("M3D TEST R — LEAKAGE-ACTIVE HIDDEN SUBSPACE")
print("=" * 148)

print()
print(
    "GPU:",
    torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
)

print(
    "topology:",
    TOPOLOGY_R,
)

print(
    "nu:",
    NU_R,
)

print(
    "critical tau:",
    CRITICAL_TAU_R,
)

print(
    "Q snapshots:",
    SNAPSHOT_TAUS_R,
)

print(
    "leakage horizon:",
    SEGMENT_TAU_R,
)

print(
    "physical leakage horizon:",
    SEGMENT_TAU_R * TAU0_R,
)

print(
    "fp64 finite-difference eps_rel:",
    EPS_REL_R,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. BASIC HELPERS
# ==================================================================================================

def pair_inner_real_R(
    A1,
    A2,
    B1,
    B2,
):
    value = (
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A1)
            *
            B1
        )
        +
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A2)
            *
            B2
        )
    ).real

    return value


def pair_norm_R(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return torch.sqrt(
        torch.clamp(
            pair_inner_real_R(
                A1,
                A2,
                A1,
                A2,
            ),
            min=0.0,
        )
    )


def pair_norm_float_R(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return float(
        pair_norm_R(
            A1,
            A2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_norm_float_R(
    x,
):
    return float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            x
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_cosine_R(
    a,
    b,
):
    na = torch.linalg.vector_norm(a)
    nb = torch.linalg.vector_norm(b)

    if (
        float(na.detach().cpu()) < 1e-30
        or
        float(nb.detach().cpu()) < 1e-30
    ):
        return np.nan

    val = (
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(a)
            *
            b
        ).real
        /
        (
            na
            *
            nb
        )
    )

    return float(
        val.detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. EXACT P / Q SPLIT
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def split_PQ_R(
    D1,
    D2,
    target_ids,
):
    P1 = torch.zeros_like(
        D1
    )

    P2 = torch.zeros_like(
        D2
    )

    D1f = D1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    D2f = D2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f = P1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P2f = P2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    P2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    Q1 = (
        D1
        -
        P1
    )

    Q2 = (
        D2
        -
        P2
    )

    return (
        P1,
        P2,
        Q1,
        Q2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. FOM SEGMENT PROPAGATOR
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def propagate_fom_segment_R(
    U1,
    U2,
    env,
    steps=SEGMENT_STEPS_R,
):
    A1 = U1.clone()
    A2 = U2.clone()

    for _ in range(
        steps
    ):
        A1 = full_rk4_D(
            A1,
            DT_PHYS_R,
            NU_R,
            env,
        )

        A2 = full_rk4_D(
            A2,
            DT_PHYS_R,
            NU_R,
            env,
        )

    return (
        A1,
        A2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. STAGE A — REBUILD THE ACTUAL TEACHER-FREE TRAJECTORY TO tau*=1.35
#
# fp32 is intentional:
# this reproduces the exact long-horizon Test-Q operating regime.
# ==================================================================================================

sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float32,
    torch.complex64,
    NU_R,
    TAU0_R,
)

ENV32_R = make_env_D(
    40,
    L=8.0,
)

assert ENV32_R["KX"].dtype == torch.float32


U10_R, U20_R = topology_pair_L2(
    TOPOLOGY_R,
    ENV32_R,
)

U1_truth_R = U10_R.clone()
U2_truth_R = U20_R.clone()


global_star_R, initial_diag_R = initialize_star2_N(
    U10_R,
    U20_R,
    ENV32_R,
)


TARGET_IDS_R = global_star_R[
    "proj"
][
    "target_ids"
]


Q_SNAPSHOTS_R = []

snapshot_rows_R = []


print()
print("=" * 148)
print("STAGE A — COLLECT ACTUAL Q-ERROR SNAPSHOTS")
print("=" * 148)

print(
    "initial STAR2 RAf:",
    f"{initial_diag_R['RAf_ratio']:.3e}",
)


for segment in range(
    1,
    CRITICAL_SEGMENTS_R + 1,
):

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Advance truth + global STAR2 one full segment.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    for _ in range(
        SEGMENT_STEPS_R
    ):

        U1_truth_R = full_rk4_D(
            U1_truth_R,
            DT_PHYS_R,
            NU_R,
            ENV32_R,
        )

        U2_truth_R = full_rk4_D(
            U2_truth_R,
            DT_PHYS_R,
            NU_R,
            ENV32_R,
        )

        rk4_M(
            global_star_R,
            DTAU_R,
            ENV32_R,
        )


    tau = (
        segment
        *
        SEGMENT_TAU_R
    )


    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Reanchor global STAR2 exactly as in Test Q.
    #
    # Reanchor is continuity-preserving, so state itself should not move.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    (
        global_star_R,
        global_diag_R,
        global_jump_R,
        global_build_R,
    ) = reanchor_star2_N(
        global_star_R,
        ENV32_R,
    )


    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Actual predicted state and actual error.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Ug1_R, Ug2_R = reconstruct_M(
        global_star_R,
        ENV32_R,
    )

    D1_R = (
        Ug1_R
        -
        U1_truth_R
    )

    D2_R = (
        Ug2_R
        -
        U2_truth_R
    )


    (
        PD1_R,
        PD2_R,
        QD1_R,
        QD2_R,
    ) = split_PQ_R(
        D1_R,
        D2_R,
        TARGET_IDS_R,
    )


    p_norm = pair_norm_float_R(
        PD1_R,
        PD2_R,
    )

    q_norm = pair_norm_float_R(
        QD1_R,
        QD2_R,
    )


    # Q observable leakage MUST be zero.
    q_target = pack_modes_D(
        QD1_R,
        QD2_R,
        TARGET_IDS_R,
    )

    q_target_leak = vec_norm_float_R(
        q_target
    )


    # Keep the actual Q-error snapshot.
    Q_SNAPSHOTS_R.append(
        (
            QD1_R.clone(),
            QD2_R.clone(),
        )
    )


    snapshot_rows_R.append(
        {
            "tau":
                tau,

            "P_error_norm":
                p_norm,

            "Q_error_norm":
                q_norm,

            "Q_over_P":
                q_norm
                /
                max(
                    p_norm,
                    1e-30,
                ),

            "Q_target_leak":
                q_target_leak,

            "STAR2_RAf":
                global_diag_R[
                    "RAf_ratio"
                ],

            "reanchor_jump":
                global_jump_R,
        }
    )


    print(
        f"  tau={tau:5.2f}"
        f" | ||Pδ||={p_norm:12.5e}"
        f" | ||Qδ||={q_norm:12.5e}"
        f" | Q/P={q_norm/max(p_norm,1e-30):9.3f}"
        f" | Q->P start leak={q_target_leak:.3e}"
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. FREEZE CRITICAL STATE
# ==================================================================================================

U1_BASE32_R = U1_truth_R.clone()
U2_BASE32_R = U2_truth_R.clone()

Ug1_CRIT32_R, Ug2_CRIT32_R = reconstruct_M(
    global_star_R,
    ENV32_R,
)

D1_CRIT32_R = (
    Ug1_CRIT32_R
    -
    U1_BASE32_R
)

D2_CRIT32_R = (
    Ug2_CRIT32_R
    -
    U2_BASE32_R
)

(
    _PD1,
    _PD2,
    Q1_ACTUAL32_R,
    Q2_ACTUAL32_R,
) = split_PQ_R(
    D1_CRIT32_R,
    D2_CRIT32_R,
    TARGET_IDS_R,
)


SNAPSHOTS_R = pd.DataFrame(
    snapshot_rows_R
)

SNAPSHOTS_R.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_R_Q_snapshots.csv",
    index=False,
)


print()
print(
    "Critical Q norm:",
    f"{pair_norm_float_R(Q1_ACTUAL32_R,Q2_ACTUAL32_R):.8e}"
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. STAGE B — SWITCH LEAKAGE ANALYSIS TO TRUE FP64
# ==================================================================================================

sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float64,
    torch.complex128,
    NU_R,
    TAU0_R,
)

ENV64_R = make_env_D(
    40,
    L=8.0,
)

assert ENV64_R["KX"].dtype == torch.float64


U1_BASE_R = U1_BASE32_R.to(
    torch.complex128
)

U2_BASE_R = U2_BASE32_R.to(
    torch.complex128
)


Q_SNAPSHOTS64_R = [
    (
        q1.to(
            torch.complex128
        ),
        q2.to(
            torch.complex128
        ),
    )
    for q1, q2
    in Q_SNAPSHOTS_R
]


Q1_ACTUAL_R = Q1_ACTUAL32_R.to(
    torch.complex128
)

Q2_ACTUAL_R = Q2_ACTUAL32_R.to(
    torch.complex128
)


print()
print("=" * 148)
print("STAGE B — EMPIRICAL Q-ERROR POD")
print("=" * 148)

print(
    "environment dtype:",
    ENV64_R["KX"].dtype,
)

print(
    "base state dtype:",
    U1_BASE_R.dtype,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. Q-SNAPSHOT GRAM MATRIX
#
# S = [q_1 ... q_m]
#
# G = S^T S
#
# POD modes:
#
#     psi_j = S v_j / sqrt(lambda_j)
#
# This avoids constructing a giant dense state-by-snapshot matrix.
# ==================================================================================================

M_R = len(
    Q_SNAPSHOTS64_R
)

G_R = torch.zeros(
    (
        M_R,
        M_R,
    ),
    device=U1_BASE_R.device,
    dtype=torch.float64,
)


for i in range(
    M_R
):
    for j in range(
        i,
        M_R,
    ):

        value = pair_inner_real_R(
            Q_SNAPSHOTS64_R[i][0],
            Q_SNAPSHOTS64_R[i][1],
            Q_SNAPSHOTS64_R[j][0],
            Q_SNAPSHOTS64_R[j][1],
        )

        G_R[
            i,
            j
        ] = value

        G_R[
            j,
            i
        ] = value


evals_R, evecs_R = torch.linalg.eigh(
    G_R
)

order_R = torch.argsort(
    evals_R,
    descending=True,
)

evals_R = evals_R[
    order_R
]

evecs_R = evecs_R[
    :,
    order_R
]


max_eval_R = float(
    torch.clamp(
        evals_R[0],
        min=0.0,
    ).detach().cpu()
)


valid_mask_R = (
    evals_R
    >
    POD_EPS_R
    *
    max(
        max_eval_R,
        1e-300,
    )
)


POD_RANK_R = int(
    valid_mask_R.sum().detach().cpu()
)


evals_valid_R = torch.clamp(
    evals_R[
        :POD_RANK_R
    ],
    min=0.0,
)

evecs_valid_R = evecs_R[
    :,
    :POD_RANK_R
]


total_energy_R = float(
    torch.sum(
        evals_valid_R
    ).detach().cpu()
)


energy_frac_R = (
    torch.cumsum(
        evals_valid_R,
        dim=0,
    )
    /
    torch.sum(
        evals_valid_R
    )
)


def rank_for_energy_R(
    target,
):
    idx = torch.nonzero(
        energy_frac_R
        >=
        target,
        as_tuple=False,
    )

    if len(idx) == 0:
        return POD_RANK_R

    return int(
        idx[0].item()
        +
        1
    )


POD_RANK_99_R = rank_for_energy_R(
    0.99
)

POD_RANK_999_R = rank_for_energy_R(
    0.999
)


print(
    "snapshot count:",
    M_R,
)

print(
    "numerical POD rank:",
    POD_RANK_R,
)

print(
    "rank for 99% Q-error energy:",
    POD_RANK_99_R,
)

print(
    "rank for 99.9% Q-error energy:",
    POD_RANK_999_R,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. BUILD ORTHONORMAL Q-POD MODES
# ==================================================================================================

POD_MODES_R = []


for mode_i in range(
    POD_RANK_R
):

    lam = evals_valid_R[
        mode_i
    ]

    coeff = (
        evecs_valid_R[
            :,
            mode_i
        ]
        /
        torch.sqrt(
            lam
        )
    )


    mode1 = torch.zeros_like(
        U1_BASE_R
    )

    mode2 = torch.zeros_like(
        U2_BASE_R
    )


    for snap_i in range(
        M_R
    ):

        mode1 = (
            mode1
            +
            coeff[
                snap_i
            ]
            *
            Q_SNAPSHOTS64_R[
                snap_i
            ][
                0
            ]
        )

        mode2 = (
            mode2
            +
            coeff[
                snap_i
            ]
            *
            Q_SNAPSHOTS64_R[
                snap_i
            ][
                1
            ]
        )


    norm_mode = pair_norm_R(
        mode1,
        mode2,
    )

    mode1 = (
        mode1
        /
        norm_mode
    )

    mode2 = (
        mode2
        /
        norm_mode
    )


    POD_MODES_R.append(
        (
            mode1,
            mode2,
        )
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 10. POD ORTHONORMALITY CHECK
# ==================================================================================================

POD_GRAM_CHECK_R = torch.zeros(
    (
        POD_RANK_R,
        POD_RANK_R,
    ),
    device=U1_BASE_R.device,
    dtype=torch.float64,
)


for i in range(
    POD_RANK_R
):
    for j in range(
        POD_RANK_R
    ):

        POD_GRAM_CHECK_R[
            i,
            j
        ] = pair_inner_real_R(
            POD_MODES_R[i][0],
            POD_MODES_R[i][1],
            POD_MODES_R[j][0],
            POD_MODES_R[j][1],
        )


POD_ORTH_ERROR_R = float(
    torch.max(
        torch.abs(
            POD_GRAM_CHECK_R
            -
            torch.eye(
                POD_RANK_R,
                device=U1_BASE_R.device,
                dtype=torch.float64,
            )
        )
    ).detach().cpu()
)


print(
    "POD orthogonality error:",
    f"{POD_ORTH_ERROR_R:.3e}",
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. ACTUAL CRITICAL Q-ERROR COORDINATES IN POD BASIS
# ==================================================================================================

ACTUAL_COEFF_R = torch.zeros(
    POD_RANK_R,
    device=U1_BASE_R.device,
    dtype=torch.float64,
)


for i in range(
    POD_RANK_R
):

    ACTUAL_COEFF_R[
        i
    ] = pair_inner_real_R(
        POD_MODES_R[i][0],
        POD_MODES_R[i][1],
        Q1_ACTUAL_R,
        Q2_ACTUAL_R,
    )


Q_ACTUAL_NORM_R = pair_norm_float_R(
    Q1_ACTUAL_R,
    Q2_ACTUAL_R,
)


Q1_POD_RECON_R = torch.zeros_like(
    Q1_ACTUAL_R
)

Q2_POD_RECON_R = torch.zeros_like(
    Q2_ACTUAL_R
)


for i in range(
    POD_RANK_R
):

    Q1_POD_RECON_R = (
        Q1_POD_RECON_R
        +
        ACTUAL_COEFF_R[
            i
        ]
        *
        POD_MODES_R[
            i
        ][
            0
        ]
    )

    Q2_POD_RECON_R = (
        Q2_POD_RECON_R
        +
        ACTUAL_COEFF_R[
            i
        ]
        *
        POD_MODES_R[
            i
        ][
            1
        ]
    )


ACTUAL_POD_RESIDUAL_R = (
    pair_norm_float_R(
        Q1_ACTUAL_R
        -
        Q1_POD_RECON_R,

        Q2_ACTUAL_R
        -
        Q2_POD_RECON_R,
    )
    /
    max(
        Q_ACTUAL_NORM_R,
        1e-30,
    )
)


print(
    "critical Q error represented by POD residual:",
    f"{ACTUAL_POD_RESIDUAL_R:.3e}",
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 12. LEAKAGE FINITE-DIFFERENCE SCALE
#
# POD modes have unit full-state norm.
#
# epsilon_abs therefore gives:
#
#       ||epsilon * q|| / ||U|| = EPS_REL_R
# ==================================================================================================

BASE_NORM_R = pair_norm_float_R(
    U1_BASE_R,
    U2_BASE_R,
)

EPS_ABS_R = (
    EPS_REL_R
    *
    BASE_NORM_R
)


print()
print(
    "base full-state norm:",
    f"{BASE_NORM_R:.8e}",
)

print(
    "finite-difference epsilon:",
    f"{EPS_ABS_R:.8e}",
)

print(
    "relative perturbation:",
    f"{EPS_ABS_R / BASE_NORM_R:.3e}",
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 13. REFERENCE TRUTH END STATE
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 148)
print("STAGE C — BUILD RESTRICTED FINITE-TIME LEAKAGE OPERATOR")
print("=" * 148)


U1_TRUTH_END_R, U2_TRUTH_END_R = propagate_fom_segment_R(
    U1_BASE_R,
    U2_BASE_R,
    ENV64_R,
)


X_TRUTH_END_R = pack_modes_D(
    U1_TRUTH_END_R,
    U2_TRUTH_END_R,
    TARGET_IDS_R,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 14. CENTRAL FINITE-DIFFERENCE LEAKAGE RESPONSE
#
# For unit q:
#
#     T q ≈
#
#     P Phi(U + eps q) - P Phi(U - eps q)
#     ------------------------------------
#                       2 eps
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def leakage_response_R(
    q1,
    q2,
    eps_abs,
):
    U1_plus = (
        U1_BASE_R
        +
        eps_abs
        *
        q1
    )

    U2_plus = (
        U2_BASE_R
        +
        eps_abs
        *
        q2
    )


    U1_minus = (
        U1_BASE_R
        -
        eps_abs
        *
        q1
    )

    U2_minus = (
        U2_BASE_R
        -
        eps_abs
        *
        q2
    )


    U1_plus_end, U2_plus_end = propagate_fom_segment_R(
        U1_plus,
        U2_plus,
        ENV64_R,
    )

    U1_minus_end, U2_minus_end = propagate_fom_segment_R(
        U1_minus,
        U2_minus,
        ENV64_R,
    )


    x_plus = pack_modes_D(
        U1_plus_end,
        U2_plus_end,
        TARGET_IDS_R,
    )

    x_minus = pack_modes_D(
        U1_minus_end,
        U2_minus_end,
        TARGET_IDS_R,
    )


    response = (
        x_plus
        -
        x_minus
    ) / (
        2.0
        *
        eps_abs
    )


    return response


# ==================================================================================================
# 15. APPLY T TO EACH EMPIRICAL Q-POD MODE
# ==================================================================================================

LEAKAGE_COLUMNS_R = []

leakage_mode_rows_R = []

operator_start_R = time.perf_counter()


for mode_i in range(
    POD_RANK_R
):

    print(
        f"  leakage column {mode_i+1:02d}/{POD_RANK_R}",
        end="",
    )

    t0 = time.perf_counter()

    response = leakage_response_R(
        POD_MODES_R[
            mode_i
        ][
            0
        ],

        POD_MODES_R[
            mode_i
        ][
            1
        ],

        EPS_ABS_R,
    )


    elapsed = (
        time.perf_counter()
        -
        t0
    )


    response_norm = vec_norm_float_R(
        response
    )


    LEAKAGE_COLUMNS_R.append(
        response
    )


    leakage_mode_rows_R.append(
        {
            "pod_mode":
                mode_i + 1,

            "Q_snapshot_energy_fraction":
                float(
                    evals_valid_R[
                        mode_i
                    ].detach().cpu()
                    /
                    torch.sum(
                        evals_valid_R
                    ).detach().cpu()
                ),

            "Tq_norm":
                response_norm,

            "runtime_s":
                elapsed,
        }
    )


    print(
        f" | ||Tq||={response_norm:.8e}"
        f" | {elapsed:.2f}s"
    )


    # Permanent checkpoint.
    Y_partial = torch.stack(
        LEAKAGE_COLUMNS_R,
        dim=1,
    )

    torch.save(
        {
            "Y_partial":
                Y_partial.detach().cpu(),

            "columns_complete":
                len(
                    LEAKAGE_COLUMNS_R
                ),

            "eps_abs":
                EPS_ABS_R,

            "eps_rel":
                EPS_REL_R,
        },
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_R_operator_partial.pt",
    )


OPERATOR_RUNTIME_R = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    operator_start_R
)


Y_R = torch.stack(
    LEAKAGE_COLUMNS_R,
    dim=1,
)


LEAKAGE_MODES_R = pd.DataFrame(
    leakage_mode_rows_R
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 16. SVD OF RESTRICTED LEAKAGE OPERATOR
#
# Y maps POD coordinates -> future target coordinates:
#
#       y = Y a
#
# because the POD modes are orthonormal.
# ==================================================================================================

U_LEAK_R, S_LEAK_R, VH_LEAK_R = torch.linalg.svd(
    Y_R,
    full_matrices=False,
)


LEAKAGE_ENERGY_R = (
    S_LEAK_R ** 2
)

LEAKAGE_ENERGY_FRAC_R = (
    LEAKAGE_ENERGY_R
    /
    torch.sum(
        LEAKAGE_ENERGY_R
    )
)

LEAKAGE_CUM_FRAC_R = torch.cumsum(
    LEAKAGE_ENERGY_FRAC_R,
    dim=0,
)


def leakage_top_fraction_R(
    k,
):
    k = min(
        k,
        len(
            S_LEAK_R
        ),
    )

    return float(
        LEAKAGE_CUM_FRAC_R[
            k - 1
        ].detach().cpu()
    )


TOP1_OPERATOR_FRAC_R = leakage_top_fraction_R(
    1
)

TOP2_OPERATOR_FRAC_R = leakage_top_fraction_R(
    2
)

TOP3_OPERATOR_FRAC_R = leakage_top_fraction_R(
    3
)

TOP5_OPERATOR_FRAC_R = leakage_top_fraction_R(
    min(
        5,
        POD_RANK_R,
    )
)


STABLE_RANK_R = float(
    (
        torch.sum(
            S_LEAK_R ** 2
        )
        /
        (
            S_LEAK_R[0] ** 2
            +
            1e-300
        )
    ).detach().cpu()
)


prob_R = (
    LEAKAGE_ENERGY_FRAC_R
    /
    torch.sum(
        LEAKAGE_ENERGY_FRAC_R
    )
)

ENTROPY_R = -torch.sum(
    prob_R
    *
    torch.log(
        torch.clamp(
            prob_R,
            min=1e-300,
        )
    )
)

EFFECTIVE_RANK_R = float(
    torch.exp(
        ENTROPY_R
    ).detach().cpu()
)


print()
print("=" * 148)
print("RESTRICTED LEAKAGE SPECTRUM")
print("=" * 148)

for i in range(
    len(
        S_LEAK_R
    )
):

    print(
        f"  sigma[{i+1:02d}]"
        f" = {float(S_LEAK_R[i].detach().cpu()):.8e}"
        f" | energy={100*float(LEAKAGE_ENERGY_FRAC_R[i].detach().cpu()):8.4f}%"
        f" | cumulative={100*float(LEAKAGE_CUM_FRAC_R[i].detach().cpu()):8.4f}%"
    )


print()

print(
    "top-1 operator energy:",
    f"{100*TOP1_OPERATOR_FRAC_R:.4f}%"
)

print(
    "top-2 operator energy:",
    f"{100*TOP2_OPERATOR_FRAC_R:.4f}%"
)

print(
    "top-3 operator energy:",
    f"{100*TOP3_OPERATOR_FRAC_R:.4f}%"
)

print(
    "top-5 operator energy:",
    f"{100*TOP5_OPERATOR_FRAC_R:.4f}%"
)

print()

print(
    "stable rank:",
    f"{STABLE_RANK_R:.6f}"
)

print(
    "entropy effective rank:",
    f"{EFFECTIVE_RANK_R:.6f}"
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 17. CONSTRUCT PHYSICAL INPUT LEAKAGE SINGULAR DIRECTIONS
#
# Vh[j,i] gives coefficients of input singular direction j
# in empirical POD mode i.
# ==================================================================================================

LEAKAGE_INPUT_MODES_R = []


for j in range(
    POD_RANK_R
):

    v_coeff = VH_LEAK_R[
        j,
        :
    ]


    v1 = torch.zeros_like(
        U1_BASE_R
    )

    v2 = torch.zeros_like(
        U2_BASE_R
    )


    for i in range(
        POD_RANK_R
    ):

        v1 = (
            v1
            +
            v_coeff[
                i
            ]
            *
            POD_MODES_R[
                i
            ][
                0
            ]
        )

        v2 = (
            v2
            +
            v_coeff[
                i
            ]
            *
            POD_MODES_R[
                i
            ][
                1
            ]
        )


    vn = pair_norm_R(
        v1,
        v2,
    )

    v1 = (
        v1
        /
        vn
    )

    v2 = (
        v2
        /
        vn
    )


    LEAKAGE_INPUT_MODES_R.append(
        (
            v1,
            v2,
        )
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 18. FINITE-DIFFERENCE LINEARITY FALSIFICATION
#
# Re-evaluate the strongest leakage direction at eps/2.
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 148)
print("FINITE-DIFFERENCE LINEARITY CHECK")
print("=" * 148)


TOP1_RESPONSE_FROM_SVD_R = (
    U_LEAK_R[
        :,
        0
    ]
    *
    S_LEAK_R[
        0
    ]
)


TOP1_RESPONSE_HALF_R = leakage_response_R(
    LEAKAGE_INPUT_MODES_R[
        0
    ][
        0
    ],

    LEAKAGE_INPUT_MODES_R[
        0
    ][
        1
    ],

    EPS_ABS_R
    /
    2.0,
)


FD_COSINE_R = vec_cosine_R(
    TOP1_RESPONSE_FROM_SVD_R,
    TOP1_RESPONSE_HALF_R,
)


FD_NORM_RATIO_R = (
    vec_norm_float_R(
        TOP1_RESPONSE_HALF_R
    )
    /
    max(
        vec_norm_float_R(
            TOP1_RESPONSE_FROM_SVD_R
        ),
        1e-30,
    )
)


FD_REL_DIFF_R = (
    vec_norm_float_R(
        TOP1_RESPONSE_HALF_R
        -
        TOP1_RESPONSE_FROM_SVD_R
    )
    /
    max(
        vec_norm_float_R(
            TOP1_RESPONSE_FROM_SVD_R
        ),
        1e-30,
    )
)


print(
    "top-direction eps vs eps/2 cosine:",
    f"{FD_COSINE_R:.10f}"
)

print(
    "top-direction norm ratio:",
    f"{FD_NORM_RATIO_R:.10f}"
)

print(
    "relative derivative difference:",
    f"{FD_REL_DIFF_R:.6e}"
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 19. ACTUAL CRITICAL Q-ERROR IN LEAKAGE-SINGULAR COORDINATES
#
# POD coordinates:
#
#       a
#
# singular input coordinates:
#
#       b = Vh a
# ==================================================================================================

A_R = ACTUAL_COEFF_R

B_R = (
    VH_LEAK_R
    @
    A_R
)


LINEAR_PREDICTED_RESPONSE_R = (
    Y_R
    @
    A_R
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 20. ACTUAL NONLINEAR Q-ONLY CAUSAL RESPONSE
#
# Truth:
#
#       U
#
# Q-only:
#
#       U + delta_Q_actual
#
# run FULL NS for one segment.
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 148)
print("STAGE D — CAUSAL TOP-k LEAKAGE-SUBSPACE VALIDATION")
print("=" * 148)


U1_QACT_END_R, U2_QACT_END_R = propagate_fom_segment_R(
    U1_BASE_R
    +
    Q1_ACTUAL_R,

    U2_BASE_R
    +
    Q2_ACTUAL_R,

    ENV64_R,
)


X_QACT_END_R = pack_modes_D(
    U1_QACT_END_R,
    U2_QACT_END_R,
    TARGET_IDS_R,
)


E_QACT_R = (
    X_QACT_END_R
    -
    X_TRUTH_END_R
)


E_QACT_NORM_R = vec_norm_float_R(
    E_QACT_R
)


TRUTH_TARGET_END_NORM_R = (
    vec_norm_float_R(
        X_TRUTH_END_R
    )
    +
    1e-30
)


ACTUAL_Q_ONLY_REL_R = (
    E_QACT_NORM_R
    /
    TRUTH_TARGET_END_NORM_R
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 21. DOES THE SMALL-PERTURBATION LINEAR OPERATOR PREDICT THE ACTUAL
#     LARGE Q-ONLY RESPONSE?
# ==================================================================================================

LINEAR_RESPONSE_NORM_R = vec_norm_float_R(
    LINEAR_PREDICTED_RESPONSE_R
)


LINEAR_VS_ACTUAL_COS_R = vec_cosine_R(
    LINEAR_PREDICTED_RESPONSE_R,
    E_QACT_R,
)


LINEAR_VS_ACTUAL_NORM_RATIO_R = (
    LINEAR_RESPONSE_NORM_R
    /
    max(
        E_QACT_NORM_R,
        1e-30,
    )
)


LINEAR_VS_ACTUAL_RESID_R = (
    vec_norm_float_R(
        E_QACT_R
        -
        LINEAR_PREDICTED_RESPONSE_R
    )
    /
    max(
        E_QACT_NORM_R,
        1e-30,
    )
)


print(
    "actual Q-only -> P error:",
    f"{100*ACTUAL_Q_ONLY_REL_R:.8f}%"
)

print(
    "linear prediction cosine:",
    f"{LINEAR_VS_ACTUAL_COS_R:.8f}"
)

print(
    "linear prediction norm/actual:",
    f"{LINEAR_VS_ACTUAL_NORM_RATIO_R:.8f}"
)

print(
    "linear prediction residual:",
    f"{LINEAR_VS_ACTUAL_RESID_R:.8f}"
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 22. CAUSAL TOP-k TESTS
#
# Project the ACTUAL Q-error onto only the first k leakage singular directions.
#
# This is the key compression test.
# ==================================================================================================

topk_rows_R = []


valid_topks_R = sorted(
    set(
        min(
            k,
            POD_RANK_R,
        )
        for k in TOPK_TESTS_R
        if min(
            k,
            POD_RANK_R,
        ) >= 1
    )
)


for k in valid_topks_R:

    print()
    print(
        f"  causal top-{k} test..."
    )


    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Projection coefficients in POD basis.
    #
    # V = Vh^T
    #
    # coeff_topk = V_k V_k^T a
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    V_R = VH_LEAK_R.T

    Vk_R = V_R[
        :,
        :k
    ]


    coeff_topk_R = (
        Vk_R
        @
        (
            Vk_R.T
            @
            A_R
        )
    )


    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Build actual-amplitude top-k Q error.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Q1_TOPK_R = torch.zeros_like(
        Q1_ACTUAL_R
    )

    Q2_TOPK_R = torch.zeros_like(
        Q2_ACTUAL_R
    )


    for i in range(
        POD_RANK_R
    ):

        Q1_TOPK_R = (
            Q1_TOPK_R
            +
            coeff_topk_R[
                i
            ]
            *
            POD_MODES_R[
                i
            ][
                0
            ]
        )

        Q2_TOPK_R = (
            Q2_TOPK_R
            +
            coeff_topk_R[
                i
            ]
            *
            POD_MODES_R[
                i
            ][
                1
            ]
        )


    TOPK_INPUT_NORM_R = pair_norm_float_R(
        Q1_TOPK_R,
        Q2_TOPK_R,
    )


    INPUT_ENERGY_FRACTION_R = (
        TOPK_INPUT_NORM_R ** 2
        /
        max(
            Q_ACTUAL_NORM_R ** 2,
            1e-300,
        )
    )


    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Full NS causal test.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    U1_TOPK_END_R, U2_TOPK_END_R = propagate_fom_segment_R(
        U1_BASE_R
        +
        Q1_TOPK_R,

        U2_BASE_R
        +
        Q2_TOPK_R,

        ENV64_R,
    )


    X_TOPK_END_R = pack_modes_D(
        U1_TOPK_END_R,
        U2_TOPK_END_R,
        TARGET_IDS_R,
    )


    E_TOPK_R = (
        X_TOPK_END_R
        -
        X_TRUTH_END_R
    )


    TOPK_OUTPUT_NORM_R = vec_norm_float_R(
        E_TOPK_R
    )


    OUTPUT_NORM_RATIO_R = (
        TOPK_OUTPUT_NORM_R
        /
        max(
            E_QACT_NORM_R,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    OUTPUT_COSINE_R = vec_cosine_R(
        E_TOPK_R,
        E_QACT_R,
    )


    OUTPUT_RESIDUAL_R = (
        vec_norm_float_R(
            E_QACT_R
            -
            E_TOPK_R
        )
        /
        max(
            E_QACT_NORM_R,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # Linear SVD prediction capture.
    b_top = B_R[
        :k
    ]

    linear_topk_output = (
        U_LEAK_R[
            :,
            :k
        ]
        @
        (
            S_LEAK_R[
                :k
            ]
            *
            b_top
        )
    )


    LINEAR_ACTUAL_OUTPUT_CAPTURE_R = (
        vec_norm_float_R(
            linear_topk_output
        )
        /
        max(
            LINEAR_RESPONSE_NORM_R,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    topk_rows_R.append(
        {
            "k":
                k,

            "input_Q_energy_fraction":
                INPUT_ENERGY_FRACTION_R,

            "operator_energy_fraction":
                leakage_top_fraction_R(
                    k
                ),

            "linear_actual_output_norm_fraction":
                LINEAR_ACTUAL_OUTPUT_CAPTURE_R,

            "causal_output_norm_ratio":
                OUTPUT_NORM_RATIO_R,

            "causal_output_cosine":
                OUTPUT_COSINE_R,

            "causal_output_residual":
                OUTPUT_RESIDUAL_R,
        }
    )


    print(
        f"    Q input energy retained = {100*INPUT_ENERGY_FRACTION_R:.3f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"    operator energy retained= {100*leakage_top_fraction_R(k):.3f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"    causal output norm/full = {OUTPUT_NORM_RATIO_R:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"    causal output cosine    = {OUTPUT_COSINE_R:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"    causal output residual  = {OUTPUT_RESIDUAL_R:.6f}"
    )


TOPK_RESULTS_R = pd.DataFrame(
    topk_rows_R
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 23. SINGULAR-VALUE TABLE
# ==================================================================================================

spectrum_rows_R = []


for i in range(
    len(
        S_LEAK_R
    )
):

    spectrum_rows_R.append(
        {
            "mode":
                i + 1,

            "sigma":
                float(
                    S_LEAK_R[
                        i
                    ].detach().cpu()
                ),

            "energy_fraction":
                float(
                    LEAKAGE_ENERGY_FRAC_R[
                        i
                    ].detach().cpu()
                ),

            "cumulative_energy_fraction":
                float(
                    LEAKAGE_CUM_FRAC_R[
                        i
                    ].detach().cpu()
                ),

            "actual_Q_coordinate":
                float(
                    B_R[
                        i
                    ].detach().cpu()
                ),

            "actual_Q_coordinate_energy_fraction":
                float(
                    (
                        B_R[
                            i
                        ] ** 2
                    ).detach().cpu()
                    /
                    max(
                        Q_ACTUAL_NORM_R ** 2,
                        1e-300,
                    )
                ),
        }
    )


SPECTRUM_R = pd.DataFrame(
    spectrum_rows_R
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 24. SAVE TOP PHYSICAL LEAKAGE DIRECTIONS
# ==================================================================================================

TOP_SAVE_R = min(
    5,
    len(
        LEAKAGE_INPUT_MODES_R
    ),
)


torch.save(
    {
        "tau_star":
            CRITICAL_TAU_R,

        "h_tau":
            SEGMENT_TAU_R,

        "singular_values":
            S_LEAK_R.detach().cpu(),

        "Vh_pod_coordinates":
            VH_LEAK_R.detach().cpu(),

        "top_input_modes":
            [
                {
                    "U1":
                        LEAKAGE_INPUT_MODES_R[i][0]
                        .detach()
                        .cpu(),

                    "U2":
                        LEAKAGE_INPUT_MODES_R[i][1]
                        .detach()
                        .cpu(),
                }
                for i in range(
                    TOP_SAVE_R
                )
            ],
    },
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_R_top_leakage_directions.pt",
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 25. SAVE TABLES
# ==================================================================================================

LEAKAGE_MODES_R.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_R_pod_mode_leakage.csv",
    index=False,
)

SPECTRUM_R.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_R_leakage_spectrum.csv",
    index=False,
)

TOPK_RESULTS_R.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_R_topk_causal_validation.csv",
    index=False,
)


torch.save(
    {
        "Y":
            Y_R.detach().cpu(),

        "U":
            U_LEAK_R.detach().cpu(),

        "S":
            S_LEAK_R.detach().cpu(),

        "Vh":
            VH_LEAK_R.detach().cpu(),

        "actual_pod_coeff":
            A_R.detach().cpu(),

        "actual_singular_coeff":
            B_R.detach().cpu(),

        "eps_abs":
            EPS_ABS_R,

        "eps_rel":
            EPS_REL_R,

        "tau_star":
            CRITICAL_TAU_R,

        "h_tau":
            SEGMENT_TAU_R,
    },
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_R_restricted_operator.pt",
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 26. DIAGNOSTIC GATES
#
# IMPORTANT:
# these are diagnostic thresholds, NOT mathematical theorems.
# ==================================================================================================

NUMERICS_GATE_R = bool(
    POD_ORTH_ERROR_R
    <
    1e-8
    and
    ACTUAL_POD_RESIDUAL_R
    <
    1e-8
    and
    FD_COSINE_R
    >
    0.999
    and
    FD_REL_DIFF_R
    <
    0.02
)


LOW_RANK_OPERATOR_R = bool(
    TOP3_OPERATOR_FRAC_R
    >=
    0.80
)


# Get top-3 causal row if available.
top3_query_R = TOPK_RESULTS_R[
    TOPK_RESULTS_R[
        "k"
    ]
    ==
    min(
        3,
        POD_RANK_R,
    )
]


if len(
    top3_query_R
) == 1:

    TOP3_INPUT_ENERGY_R = float(
        top3_query_R.iloc[
            0
        ][
            "input_Q_energy_fraction"
        ]
    )

    TOP3_CAUSAL_RESID_R = float(
        top3_query_R.iloc[
            0
        ][
            "causal_output_residual"
        ]
    )

    TOP3_CAUSAL_COS_R = float(
        top3_query_R.iloc[
            0
        ][
            "causal_output_cosine"
        ]
    )

else:

    TOP3_INPUT_ENERGY_R = np.nan
    TOP3_CAUSAL_RESID_R = np.nan
    TOP3_CAUSAL_COS_R = np.nan


TOP3_CAUSAL_CAPTURE_R = bool(
    np.isfinite(
        TOP3_CAUSAL_RESID_R
    )
    and
    TOP3_CAUSAL_RESID_R
    <=
    0.25
    and
    TOP3_CAUSAL_COS_R
    >=
    0.95
)


TOP3_COMPRESSIVE_R = bool(
    np.isfinite(
        TOP3_INPUT_ENERGY_R
    )
    and
    TOP3_INPUT_ENERGY_R
    <=
    0.50
)


STRONG_LOW_RANK_LEAKAGE_R = bool(
    NUMERICS_GATE_R
    and
    LOW_RANK_OPERATOR_R
    and
    TOP3_CAUSAL_CAPTURE_R
    and
    TOP3_COMPRESSIVE_R
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 27. FINAL REPORT
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 148)
print("TEST R — TOP-k CAUSAL VALIDATION")
print("=" * 148)
print()

print(
    TOPK_RESULTS_R.to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.8g}",
    )
)


print()
print("=" * 148)
print("M3D TEST R — FINAL DIAGNOSIS")
print("=" * 148)
print()

print(
    "Q snapshot count:",
    M_R
)

print(
    "Q snapshot numerical POD rank:",
    POD_RANK_R
)

print(
    "rank for 99% Q-error energy:",
    POD_RANK_99_R
)

print(
    "rank for 99.9% Q-error energy:",
    POD_RANK_999_R
)

print()

print(
    "POD orthogonality error:",
    f"{POD_ORTH_ERROR_R:.3e}"
)

print(
    "critical Q POD reconstruction residual:",
    f"{ACTUAL_POD_RESIDUAL_R:.3e}"
)

print()

print(
    "sigma_1:",
    f"{float(S_LEAK_R[0].detach().cpu()):.8e}"
)

print(
    "top-1 leakage-operator energy:",
    f"{100*TOP1_OPERATOR_FRAC_R:.4f}%"
)

print(

    f"{100*TOP2_OPERATOR_FRAC_R:.4f}%"
)
