    "top-3 leakage-operator energy:",
    f"{100*TOP3_OPERATOR_FRAC_R:.4f}%"
)

print(
    "top-5 leakage-operator energy:",
    f"{100*TOP5_OPERATOR_FRAC_R:.4f}%"
)

print()

print(
    "stable rank:",
    f"{STABLE_RANK_R:.6f}"
)

print(
    "entropy effective rank:",
    f"{EFFECTIVE_RANK_R:.6f}"
)

print()

print(
    "finite-difference eps/eps2 cosine:",
    f"{FD_COSINE_R:.10f}"
)

print(
    "finite-difference relative difference:",
    f"{FD_REL_DIFF_R:.6e}"
)

print()

print(
    "actual Q-only causal target error:",
    f"{100*ACTUAL_Q_ONLY_REL_R:.8f}%"
)

print(
    "linearized operator vs actual cosine:",
    f"{LINEAR_VS_ACTUAL_COS_R:.8f}"
)

print(
    "linearized operator norm / actual:",
    f"{LINEAR_VS_ACTUAL_NORM_RATIO_R:.8f}"
)

print(
    "linearized operator residual:",
    f"{LINEAR_VS_ACTUAL_RESID_R:.8f}"
)

print()

print(
    "top-3 actual Q input energy retained:",
    (
        f"{100*TOP3_INPUT_ENERGY_R:.4f}%"
        if np.isfinite(TOP3_INPUT_ENERGY_R)
        else
        "N/A"
    )
)

print(
    "top-3 causal output cosine:",
    (
        f"{TOP3_CAUSAL_COS_R:.8f}"
        if np.isfinite(TOP3_CAUSAL_COS_R)
        else
        "N/A"
    )
)

print(
    "top-3 causal output residual:",
    (

# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST S — TEMPORAL REALIZED-LEAKAGE TRACKER
#
# CAUSAL-IN-TIME EMPIRICAL Q SUBSPACE
# + LOCAL FINITE-TIME LEAKAGE SVD
# + REALIZED CHANNEL RISK
# + NEXT-SEGMENT CAUSAL VALIDATION
#
#
# CORE OBJECT
# -----------
#
# At time tau_k:
#
#       T_k = P D Phi_h(U_k) Q_emp,k
#
# where Q_emp,k is constructed ONLY from Q-error snapshots available
# at or before tau_k.
#
# If:
#
#       T_k = U_k Sigma_k V_k^*
#
# and the actual hidden error is:
#
#       deltaQ_k = sum_i b_{k,i} v_{k,i}
#
# then the realized future observable contribution of channel i is:
#
#       c_{k,i} = sigma_{k,i} b_{k,i}
#
#
# IMPORTANT DISTINCTION
# ---------------------
#
# sigma_i:
#       worst-case leakage sensitivity
#
# b_i:
#       how much actual hidden error occupies that direction
#
# |sigma_i b_i|:
#       REALIZED leakage danger
#
#
# TEST S QUESTIONS
# ----------------
#
# 1. Is the leakage map low-rank THROUGH TIME, not just at tau=1.35?
#
# 2. Does one/few realized channels dominate:
#
#       sum_i |c_i|^2 ?
#
# 3. Does
#
#       ||T_k deltaQ_k||
#
# accurately predict the actual next-segment Q-only -> P error?
#
# 4. Does the dominant realized channel persist across adjacent anchors?
#
# 5. Does leakage risk rise BEFORE the large observable-error burst?
#
#
# FROZEN
# ------
#
# topology = periodic_shear
# nu = 0.0025
#
# N = 40
# L = 8
#
# tau0 = 40/3
# physical dt = 0.01
#
# reanchor / prediction horizon:
#
#       h = 0.15 tau
#
# Analyze segment starts:
#
#       tau = 0.15, 0.30, ..., 1.35
#
# Collect teacher-free trajectory through:
#
#       tau = 1.50
#
# so each analyzed start has a next global endpoint.
#
#
# STAGE A:
#       exact Test-Q style teacher-free fp32 trajectory
#
# STAGE B:
#       true fp64 leakage analysis
#
# Finite-difference:
#
#       central difference
#       eps_rel = 1e-5
#
# NO future Q snapshots are used at any tau_k.
# ==================================================================================================

# ==================================================================================================
# 2. HELPERS
# ==================================================================================================

def pair_inner_S(
    A1,
    A2,
    B1,
    B2,
):
    return (
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A1)
            *
            B1
        )
        +
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A2)
            *
            B2
        )
    ).real


def pair_norm_tensor_S(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return torch.sqrt(
        torch.clamp(
            pair_inner_S(
                A1,
                A2,
                A1,
                A2,
            ),
            min=0.0,
        )
    )


def pair_norm_float_S(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return float(
        pair_norm_tensor_S(
            A1,
            A2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_norm_S(
    x,
):
    return float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            x
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_cos_S(
    a,
    b,
):
    na = torch.linalg.vector_norm(
        a
    )

    nb = torch.linalg.vector_norm(
        b
    )

    if (
        float(
            na.detach().cpu()
        )
        <
        1e-30
        or
        float(
            nb.detach().cpu()
        )
        <
        1e-30
    ):
        return np.nan

    val = (
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(a)
            *
            b
        ).real
        /
        (
            na
            *
            nb
        )
    )

    return float(
        val.detach().cpu()
    )


def pair_abs_cos_S(
    A1,
    A2,
    B1,
    B2,
):
    na = pair_norm_tensor_S(
        A1,
        A2,
    )

    nb = pair_norm_tensor_S(
        B1,
        B2,
    )

    if (
        float(
            na.detach().cpu()
        )
        <
        1e-30
        or
        float(
            nb.detach().cpu()
        )
        <
        1e-30
    ):
        return np.nan

    val = (
        pair_inner_S(
            A1,
            A2,
            B1,
            B2,
        )
        /
        (
            na
            *
            nb
        )
    )

    return float(
        torch.abs(
            val
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. EXACT P / Q SPLIT
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def split_PQ_S(
    D1,
    D2,
    target_ids,
):
    P1 = torch.zeros_like(
        D1
    )

    P2 = torch.zeros_like(
        D2
    )

    D1f = D1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    D2f = D2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f = P1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P2f = P2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    P2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    Q1 = (
        D1
        -
        P1
    )

    Q2 = (
        D2
        -
        P2
    )

    return (
        P1,
        P2,
        Q1,
        Q2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. FULL-NS SEGMENT PROPAGATOR
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def propagate_segment_S(
    U1,
    U2,
    env,
):
    A1 = U1.clone()
    A2 = U2.clone()

    for _ in range(
        SEGMENT_STEPS_S
    ):

        A1 = full_rk4_D(
            A1,
            DT_PHYS_S,
            NU_S,
            env,
        )

        A2 = full_rk4_D(
            A2,
            DT_PHYS_S,
            NU_S,
            env,
        )

    return (
        A1,
        A2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. STAGE A — COLLECT ACTUAL TEACHER-FREE TRAJECTORY
#
# IMPORTANT:
# We collect one extra endpoint at tau=1.50 so every analyzed
# start through tau=1.35 has its next global state available.
# ==================================================================================================

sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float32,
    torch.complex64,
    NU_S,
    TAU0_S,
)

ENV32_S = make_env_D(
    40,
    L=8.0,
)


U10_S, U20_S = topology_pair_L2(
    TOPOLOGY_S,
    ENV32_S,
)

U1_truth_S = U10_S.clone()
U2_truth_S = U20_S.clone()


global_star_S, init_diag_S = initialize_star2_N(
    U10_S,
    U20_S,
    ENV32_S,
)


TARGET_IDS_S = global_star_S[
    "proj"
][
    "target_ids"
]


TRAJ_S = []


print()
print("=" * 152)
print("STAGE A — TEACHER-FREE TRAJECTORY COLLECTION")
print("=" * 152)

print(
    "initial STAR2 RAf:",
    f"{init_diag_S['RAf_ratio']:.3e}",
)


for seg in range(
    1,
    N_COLLECTION_S + 1,
):

    for _ in range(
        SEGMENT_STEPS_S
    ):

        U1_truth_S = full_rk4_D(
            U1_truth_S,
            DT_PHYS_S,
            NU_S,
            ENV32_S,
        )

        U2_truth_S = full_rk4_D(
            U2_truth_S,
            DT_PHYS_S,
            NU_S,
            ENV32_S,
        )

        rk4_M(
            global_star_S,
            DTAU_S,
            ENV32_S,
        )


    tau = (
        seg
        *
        SEGMENT_TAU_S
    )


    # Reanchor exactly like Tests N/O/P/Q.
    (
        global_star_S,
        diag_after_S,
        jump_S,
        build_S,
    ) = reanchor_star2_N(
        global_star_S,
        ENV32_S,
    )


    Ug1_S, Ug2_S = reconstruct_M(
        global_star_S,
        ENV32_S,
    )


    D1_S = (
        Ug1_S
        -
        U1_truth_S
    )

    D2_S = (
        Ug2_S
        -
        U2_truth_S
    )


    (
        PD1_S,
        PD2_S,
        QD1_S,
        QD2_S,
    ) = split_PQ_S(
        D1_S,
        D2_S,
        TARGET_IDS_S,
    )


    x_truth_S = pack_modes_D(
        U1_truth_S,
        U2_truth_S,
        TARGET_IDS_S,
    )

    x_global_S = pack_modes_D(
        Ug1_S,
        Ug2_S,
        TARGET_IDS_S,
    )


    target_rel_S = (
        vec_norm_S(
            x_global_S
            -
            x_truth_S
        )
        /
        (
            vec_norm_S(
                x_truth_S
            )
            +
            1e-30
        )
    )


    pnorm_S = pair_norm_float_S(
        PD1_S,
        PD2_S,
    )

    qnorm_S = pair_norm_float_S(
        QD1_S,
        QD2_S,
    )


    q_target_leak_S = vec_norm_S(
        pack_modes_D(
            QD1_S,
            QD2_S,
            TARGET_IDS_S,
        )
    )


    TRAJ_S.append(
        {
            "tau":
                tau,

            "truth1":
                U1_truth_S.clone(),

            "truth2":
                U2_truth_S.clone(),

            "global1":
                Ug1_S.clone(),

            "global2":
                Ug2_S.clone(),

            "P1":
                PD1_S.clone(),

            "P2":
                PD2_S.clone(),

            "Q1":
                QD1_S.clone(),

            "Q2":
                QD2_S.clone(),

            "P_norm":
                pnorm_S,

            "Q_norm":
                qnorm_S,

            "global_target_rel":
                target_rel_S,

            "RAf":
                diag_after_S[
                    "RAf_ratio"
                ],

            "jump":
                jump_S,
        }
    )


    print(
        f"  tau={tau:5.2f}"
        f" | P error={100*target_rel_S:10.6f}%"
        f" | Q/P={qnorm_S/max(pnorm_S,1e-30):9.3f}"
        f" | Q target leak={q_target_leak_S:.2e}"
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. STAGE B — TRUE FP64 TEMPORAL LEAKAGE ANALYSIS
# ==================================================================================================

sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float64,
    torch.complex128,
    NU_S,
    TAU0_S,
)

ENV64_S = make_env_D(
    40,
    L=8.0,
)


print()
print("=" * 152)
print("STAGE B — CAUSAL TEMPORAL LEAKAGE OPERATORS")
print("=" * 152)

print(
    "environment dtype:",
    ENV64_S[
        "KX"
    ].dtype,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. CAUSAL POD BUILDER
#
# CRITICAL:
#
# At analysis index k we use ONLY snapshots:
#
#       Q_1, ..., Q_k
#
# No future Q-error information.
# ==================================================================================================

def causal_pod_S(
    q_snapshots,
):
    m = len(
        q_snapshots
    )

    G = torch.zeros(
        (
            m,
            m,
        ),
        device=q_snapshots[0][0].device,
        dtype=torch.float64,
    )


    for i in range(
        m
    ):
        for j in range(
            i,
            m
        ):

            value = pair_inner_S(
                q_snapshots[i][0],
                q_snapshots[i][1],
                q_snapshots[j][0],
                q_snapshots[j][1],
            )

            G[
                i,
                j
            ] = value

            G[
                j,
                i
            ] = value


    evals, evecs = torch.linalg.eigh(
        G
    )

    order = torch.argsort(
        evals,
        descending=True,
    )

    evals = evals[
        order
    ]

    evecs = evecs[
        :,
        order
    ]


    max_eval = float(
        torch.clamp(
            evals[0],
            min=0.0,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


    valid = (
        evals
        >
        POD_EPS_S
        *
        max(
            max_eval,
            1e-300,
        )
    )


    rank = int(
        valid.sum().detach().cpu()
    )


    evals = torch.clamp(
        evals[
            :rank
        ],
        min=0.0,
    )

    evecs = evecs[
        :,
        :rank
    ]


    modes = []


    for mode_i in range(
        rank
    ):

        coeff = (
            evecs[
                :,
                mode_i
            ]
            /
            torch.sqrt(
                evals[
                    mode_i
                ]
            )
        )


        q1 = torch.zeros_like(
            q_snapshots[0][0]
        )

        q2 = torch.zeros_like(
            q_snapshots[0][1]
        )


        for snap_i in range(
            m
        ):

            q1 = (
                q1
                +
                coeff[
                    snap_i
                ]
                *
                q_snapshots[
                    snap_i
                ][
                    0
                ]
            )

            q2 = (
                q2
                +
                coeff[
                    snap_i
                ]
                *
                q_snapshots[
                    snap_i
                ][
                    1
                ]
            )


        norm_q = pair_norm_tensor_S(
            q1,
            q2,
        )

        q1 = (
            q1
            /
            norm_q
        )

        q2 = (
            q2
            /
            norm_q
        )


        modes.append(
            (
                q1,
                q2,
            )
        )


    # Gram check.
    max_orth_err = 0.0

    for i in range(
        rank
    ):
        for j in range(
            rank
        ):

            gij = float(
                pair_inner_S(
                    modes[i][0],
                    modes[i][1],
                    modes[j][0],
                    modes[j][1],
                ).detach().cpu()
            )

            expected = (
                1.0
                if i == j
                else 0.0
            )

            max_orth_err = max(
                max_orth_err,
                abs(
                    gij
                    -
                    expected
                ),
            )


    energy = (
        evals
        /
        torch.sum(
            evals
        )
    )

    cumulative = torch.cumsum(
        energy,
        dim=0,
    )


    def rank_for_energy(
        threshold,
    ):
        where = torch.nonzero(
            cumulative
            >=
            threshold,
            as_tuple=False,
        )

        if len(where) == 0:
            return rank

        return int(
            where[0].item()
            +
            1
        )


    return {
        "rank":
            rank,

        "modes":
            modes,

        "evals":
            evals,

        "energy":
            energy,

        "rank99":
            rank_for_energy(
                0.99
            ),

        "rank999":
            rank_for_energy(
                0.999
            ),

        "orth_error":
            max_orth_err,
    }


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. FINITE-TIME LEAKAGE RESPONSE
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def leakage_response_S(
    U1_base,
    U2_base,
    q1,
    q2,
    eps_abs,
):
    U1_plus = (
        U1_base
        +
        eps_abs
        *
        q1
    )

    U2_plus = (
        U2_base
        +
        eps_abs
        *
        q2
    )


    U1_minus = (
        U1_base
        -
        eps_abs
        *
        q1
    )

    U2_minus = (
        U2_base
        -
        eps_abs
        *
        q2
    )


    U1p_end, U2p_end = propagate_segment_S(
        U1_plus,
        U2_plus,
        ENV64_S,
    )

    U1m_end, U2m_end = propagate_segment_S(
        U1_minus,
        U2_minus,
        ENV64_S,
    )


    xp = pack_modes_D(
        U1p_end,
        U2p_end,
        TARGET_IDS_S,
    )

    xm = pack_modes_D(
        U1m_end,
        U2m_end,
        TARGET_IDS_S,
    )


    return (
        xp
        -
        xm
    ) / (
        2.0
        *
        eps_abs
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. MAIN TEMPORAL LOOP
# ==================================================================================================

ROWS_S = []

CHANNEL_ROWS_S = []

previous_dom_mode_S = None

all_q_snapshots64_S = []

test_start_S = time.perf_counter()


for idx in range(
    N_ANALYSIS_S
):

    point = TRAJ_S[
        idx
    ]

    next_point = TRAJ_S[
        idx
        +
        1
    ]


    tau = float(
        point[
            "tau"
        ]
    )


    print()
    print("#" * 152)
    print(
        f"ANALYSIS tau={tau:.2f}"
        f" -> {tau+SEGMENT_TAU_S:.2f}"
        f" | causal snapshots={idx+1}"
    )
    print("#" * 152)


    # ==============================================================================================
    # Current truth + actual Q error in fp64
    # ==============================================================================================

    U1_base = point[
        "truth1"
    ].to(
        torch.complex128
    )

    U2_base = point[
        "truth2"
    ].to(
        torch.complex128
    )


    Q1_actual = point[
        "Q1"
    ].to(
        torch.complex128
    )

    Q2_actual = point[
        "Q2"
    ].to(
        torch.complex128
    )


    all_q_snapshots64_S.append(
        (
            Q1_actual.clone(),
            Q2_actual.clone(),
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # CAUSAL POD — only present and past snapshots
    # ==============================================================================================

    POD = causal_pod_S(
        all_q_snapshots64_S
    )

    rank = POD[
        "rank"
    ]

    modes = POD[
        "modes"
    ]


    print(
        f"  causal POD rank          = {rank}"
    )

    print(
        f"  rank99 / rank99.9        = {POD['rank99']} / {POD['rank999']}"
    )

    print(
        f"  POD orth error           = {POD['orth_error']:.3e}"
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # Coordinates of ACTUAL Q error in causal POD
    # ==============================================================================================

    a = torch.zeros(
        rank,
        device=U1_base.device,
        dtype=torch.float64,
    )


    for j in range(
        rank
    ):

        a[
            j
        ] = pair_inner_S(
            modes[j][0],
            modes[j][1],
            Q1_actual,
            Q2_actual,
        )


    Q1_recon = torch.zeros_like(
        Q1_actual
    )

    Q2_recon = torch.zeros_like(
        Q2_actual
    )


    for j in range(
        rank
    ):

        Q1_recon = (
            Q1_recon
            +
            a[
                j
            ]
            *
            modes[j][0]
        )

        Q2_recon = (
            Q2_recon
            +
            a[
                j
            ]
            *
            modes[j][1]
        )


    actual_q_norm = pair_norm_float_S(
        Q1_actual,
        Q2_actual,
    )


    pod_recon_resid = (
        pair_norm_float_S(
            Q1_actual
            -
            Q1_recon,

            Q2_actual
            -
            Q2_recon,
        )
        /
        max(
            actual_q_norm,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # Truth endpoint
    # ==============================================================================================

    U1_truth_end, U2_truth_end = propagate_segment_S(
        U1_base,
        U2_base,
        ENV64_S,
    )


    x_truth_end = pack_modes_D(
        U1_truth_end,
        U2_truth_end,
        TARGET_IDS_S,
    )


    truth_target_norm = (
        vec_norm_S(
            x_truth_end
        )
        +
        1e-30
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # Build finite-time leakage operator
    # ==============================================================================================

    base_norm = pair_norm_float_S(
        U1_base,
        U2_base,
    )

    eps_abs = (
        EPS_REL_S
        *
        base_norm
    )


    columns = []


    for j in range(
        rank
    ):

        response = leakage_response_S(
            U1_base,
            U2_base,
            modes[j][0],
            modes[j][1],
            eps_abs,
        )

        columns.append(
            response
        )


    Y = torch.stack(
        columns,
        dim=1,
    )


    U_leak, S_leak, VH_leak = torch.linalg.svd(
        Y,
        full_matrices=False,
    )


    operator_energy = (
        S_leak ** 2
    )

    operator_energy = (
        operator_energy
        /
        torch.sum(
            operator_energy
        )
    )

    operator_cum = torch.cumsum(
        operator_energy,
        dim=0,
    )


    op_top1 = float(
        operator_cum[
            0
        ].detach().cpu()
    )

    op_top3 = float(
        operator_cum[
            min(
                3,
                rank,
            )
            -
            1
        ].detach().cpu()
    )


    stable_rank = float(
        (
            torch.sum(
                S_leak ** 2
            )
            /
            (
                S_leak[
                    0
                ] ** 2
                +
                1e-300
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # REALIZED coordinates
    #
    # b = Vh a
    #
    # channel output amplitude:
    #
    # c_i = sigma_i b_i
    # ==============================================================================================

    b = (
        VH_leak
        @
        a
    )


    c = (
        S_leak
        *
        b
    )


    realized_energy = (
        c ** 2
    )


    realized_energy_total = float(
        torch.sum(
            realized_energy
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


    if realized_energy_total > 1e-300:

        realized_fraction = (
            realized_energy
            /
            torch.sum(
                realized_energy
            )
        )

    else:

        realized_fraction = torch.zeros_like(
            realized_energy
        )


    risk_order = torch.argsort(
        realized_fraction,
        descending=True,
    )


    dom_i = int(
        risk_order[
            0
        ].item()
    )


    top1_realized = float(
        realized_fraction[
            dom_i
        ].detach().cpu()
    )


    n_top3 = min(
        3,
        rank,
    )

    top3_realized = float(
        torch.sum(
            realized_fraction[
                risk_order[
                    :n_top3
                ]
            ]
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


    # Full predicted leakage vector.
    y_pred = (
        Y
        @
        a
    )


    predicted_abs = vec_norm_S(
        y_pred
    )

    predicted_rel = (
        predicted_abs
        /
        truth_target_norm
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # Construct physical singular input modes
    # ==============================================================================================

    singular_modes = []


    for sidx in range(
        rank
    ):

        coeff = VH_leak[
            sidx,
            :
        ]


        v1 = torch.zeros_like(
            U1_base
        )

        v2 = torch.zeros_like(
            U2_base
        )


        for j in range(
            rank
        ):

            v1 = (
                v1
                +
                coeff[
                    j
                ]
                *
                modes[
                    j
                ][
                    0
                ]
            )

            v2 = (
                v2
                +
                coeff[
                    j
                ]
                *
                modes[
                    j
                ][
                    1
                ]
            )


        vn = pair_norm_tensor_S(
            v1,
            v2,
        )

        v1 = (
            v1
            /
            vn
        )

        v2 = (
            v2
            /
            vn
        )


        singular_modes.append(
            (
                v1,
                v2,
            )
        )


    dom_mode = singular_modes[
        dom_i
    ]


    if previous_dom_mode_S is None:

        dom_persistence = np.nan

    else:

        dom_persistence = pair_abs_cos_S(
            dom_mode[0],
            dom_mode[1],
            previous_dom_mode_S[0],
            previous_dom_mode_S[1],
        )


    previous_dom_mode_S = (
        dom_mode[0].clone(),
        dom_mode[1].clone(),
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # FD refinement on REALIZED-DOMINANT channel
    # ==============================================================================================

    dom_response_eps2 = leakage_response_S(
        U1_base,
        U2_base,
        dom_mode[0],
        dom_mode[1],
        eps_abs
        /
        2.0,
    )


    dom_response_reference = (
        U_leak[
            :,
            dom_i
        ]
        *
        S_leak[
            dom_i
        ]
    )


    fd_cos = vec_cos_S(
        dom_response_reference,
        dom_response_eps2,
    )


    fd_rel_diff = (
        vec_norm_S(
            dom_response_reference
            -
            dom_response_eps2
        )
        /
        max(
            vec_norm_S(
                dom_response_reference
            ),
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # ACTUAL nonlinear Q-only causal response
    # ==============================================================================================

    U1_q_end, U2_q_end = propagate_segment_S(
        U1_base
        +
        Q1_actual,

        U2_base
        +
        Q2_actual,

        ENV64_S,
    )


    x_q_end = pack_modes_D(
        U1_q_end,
        U2_q_end,
        TARGET_IDS_S,
    )


    e_q_actual = (
        x_q_end
        -
        x_truth_end
    )


    actual_q_output_abs = vec_norm_S(
        e_q_actual
    )

    actual_q_output_rel = (
        actual_q_output_abs
        /
        truth_target_norm
    )


    pred_actual_cos = vec_cos_S(
        y_pred,
        e_q_actual,
    )


    pred_actual_norm_ratio = (
        predicted_abs
        /
        max(
            actual_q_output_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    pred_actual_resid = (
        vec_norm_S(
            y_pred
            -
            e_q_actual
        )
        /
        max(
            actual_q_output_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # DIRECT CAUSAL TOP-1 REALIZED CHANNEL
    #
    # q_top1 = b_dom * v_dom
    # ==============================================================================================

    Q1_top1 = (
        b[
            dom_i
        ]
        *
        singular_modes[
            dom_i
        ][
            0
        ]
    )

    Q2_top1 = (
        b[
            dom_i
        ]
        *
        singular_modes[
            dom_i
        ][
            1
        ]
    )


    U1_top1_end, U2_top1_end = propagate_segment_S(
        U1_base
        +
        Q1_top1,

        U2_base
        +
        Q2_top1,

        ENV64_S,
    )


    e_top1 = (
        pack_modes_D(
            U1_top1_end,
            U2_top1_end,
            TARGET_IDS_S,
        )
        -
        x_truth_end
    )


    top1_causal_norm_ratio = (
        vec_norm_S(
            e_top1
        )
        /
        max(
            actual_q_output_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    top1_causal_cos = vec_cos_S(
        e_top1,
        e_q_actual,
    )


    top1_causal_resid = (
        vec_norm_S(
            e_top1
            -
            e_q_actual
        )
        /
        max(
            actual_q_output_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # DIRECT CAUSAL TOP-3 REALIZED-RISK CHANNELS
    #
    # NOTE:
    # These are NOT automatically singular modes 1,2,3.
    #
    # They are the three largest actual:
    #
    #       |sigma_i b_i|
    # ==============================================================================================

    top_risk_indices = risk_order[
        :n_top3
    ]


    Q1_top3 = torch.zeros_like(
        Q1_actual
    )

    Q2_top3 = torch.zeros_like(
        Q2_actual
    )


    for ridx_tensor in top_risk_indices:

        ridx = int(
            ridx_tensor.item()
        )

        Q1_top3 = (
            Q1_top3
            +
            b[
                ridx
            ]
            *
            singular_modes[
                ridx
            ][
                0
            ]
        )

        Q2_top3 = (
            Q2_top3
            +
            b[
                ridx
            ]
            *
            singular_modes[
                ridx
            ][
                1
            ]
        )


    U1_top3_end, U2_top3_end = propagate_segment_S(
        U1_base
        +
        Q1_top3,

        U2_base
        +
        Q2_top3,

        ENV64_S,
    )


    e_top3 = (
        pack_modes_D(
            U1_top3_end,
            U2_top3_end,
            TARGET_IDS_S,
        )
        -
        x_truth_end
    )


    top3_causal_norm_ratio = (
        vec_norm_S(
            e_top3
        )
        /
        max(
            actual_q_output_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    top3_causal_cos = vec_cos_S(
        e_top3,
        e_q_actual,
    )


    top3_causal_resid = (
        vec_norm_S(
            e_top3
            -
            e_q_actual
        )
        /
        max(
            actual_q_output_abs,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # CURRENT / NEXT GLOBAL OBSERVABLE ERRORS
    # ==============================================================================================

    current_global_rel = float(
        point[
            "global_target_rel"
        ]
    )

    next_global_rel = float(
        next_point[
            "global_target_rel"
        ]
    )


    next_increment = (
        next_global_rel
        -
        current_global_rel
    )


    global_growth_factor = (
        next_global_rel
        /
        max(
            current_global_rel,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    leakage_warning_ratio = (
        predicted_rel
        /
        max(
            current_global_rel,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # CHANNEL TABLE
    # ==============================================================================================

    for sidx in range(
        rank
    ):

        CHANNEL_ROWS_S.append(
            {
                "tau":
                    tau,

                "causal_pod_rank":
                    rank,

                "singular_index":
                    sidx + 1,

                "sigma":
                    float(
                        S_leak[
                            sidx
                        ].detach().cpu()
                    ),

                "occupancy_b":
                    float(
                        b[
                            sidx
                        ].detach().cpu()
                    ),

                "realized_c":
                    float(
                        c[
                            sidx
                        ].detach().cpu()
                    ),

                "abs_realized_c":
                    abs(
                        float(
                            c[
                                sidx
                            ].detach().cpu()
                        )
                    ),

                "operator_energy_fraction":
                    float(
                        operator_energy[
                            sidx
                        ].detach().cpu()
                    ),

                "realized_output_energy_fraction":
                    float(
                        realized_fraction[
                            sidx
                        ].detach().cpu()
                    ),

                "is_realized_dominant":
                    bool(
                        sidx
                        ==
                        dom_i
                    ),
            }
        )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # SUMMARY ROW
    # ==============================================================================================

    row = {
        "tau":
            tau,

        "tau_end":
            tau
            +
            SEGMENT_TAU_S,

        "causal_snapshot_count":
            idx
            +
            1,

        "causal_pod_rank":
            rank,

        "rank99":
            POD[
                "rank99"
            ],

        "rank999":
            POD[
                "rank999"
            ],

        "pod_orth_error":
            POD[
                "orth_error"
            ],

        "pod_reconstruction_residual":
            pod_recon_resid,

        "operator_top1_energy":
            op_top1,

        "operator_top3_energy":
            op_top3,

        "operator_stable_rank":
            stable_rank,

        "dominant_realized_singular_index":
            dom_i
            +
            1,

        "dominant_sigma":
            float(
                S_leak[
                    dom_i
                ].detach().cpu()
            ),

        "dominant_abs_occupancy":
            abs(
                float(
                    b[
                        dom_i
                    ].detach().cpu()
                )
            ),

        "dominant_abs_realized_c":
            abs(
                float(
                    c[
                        dom_i
                    ].detach().cpu()
                )
            ),

        "realized_top1_energy":
            top1_realized,

        "realized_top3_energy":
            top3_realized,

        "dominant_input_persistence":
            dom_persistence,

        "fd_dominant_cosine":
            fd_cos,

        "fd_dominant_relative_difference":
            fd_rel_diff,

        "predicted_Q_to_P_rel":
            predicted_rel,

        "actual_Q_to_P_rel":
            actual_q_output_rel,

        "prediction_cosine":
            pred_actual_cos,

        "prediction_norm_ratio":
            pred_actual_norm_ratio,

        "prediction_residual":
            pred_actual_resid,

        "top1_causal_norm_ratio":
            top1_causal_norm_ratio,

        "top1_causal_cosine":
            top1_causal_cos,

        "top1_causal_residual":
            top1_causal_resid,

        "top3_causal_norm_ratio":
            top3_causal_norm_ratio,

        "top3_causal_cosine":
            top3_causal_cos,

        "top3_causal_residual":
            top3_causal_resid,

        "current_global_target_error":
            current_global_rel,

        "next_global_target_error":
            next_global_rel,

        "next_global_error_increment":
            next_increment,

        "next_global_growth_factor":
            global_growth_factor,

        "leakage_warning_ratio":
            leakage_warning_ratio,

        "Q_norm":
            actual_q_norm,

        "Q_over_P":
            float(
                point[
                    "Q_norm"
                ]
                /
                max(
                    point[
                        "P_norm"
                    ],
                    1e-30,
                )
            ),
    }


    ROWS_S.append(
        row
    )


    print()
    print(
        f"  operator top-1 / top-3   = "
        f"{100*op_top1:7.3f}% / {100*op_top3:7.3f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  stable rank              = {stable_rank:.4f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  REALIZED dominant index  = v_{dom_i+1}"
    )

    print(
        f"  realized top-1 energy    = {100*top1_realized:7.3f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  realized top-3 energy    = {100*top3_realized:7.3f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  dominant persistence     = {dom_persistence}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  predicted Q->P           = {100*predicted_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  actual Q->P              = {100*actual_q_output_rel:.8f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  prediction cosine        = {pred_actual_cos:.8f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  prediction norm ratio    = {pred_actual_norm_ratio:.8f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  prediction residual      = {pred_actual_resid:.8f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  top-1 causal output      = {top1_causal_norm_ratio:.5f}x"
        f" | cos={top1_causal_cos:.5f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  top-3 causal output      = {top3_causal_norm_ratio:.5f}x"
        f" | cos={top3_causal_cos:.5f}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"  current global P error   = {100*current_global_rel:.6f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  next global P error      = {100*next_global_rel:.6f}%"
    )

    print(
        f"  leakage risk / current P = {leakage_warning_ratio:.4f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  FD relative diff         = {fd_rel_diff:.3e}"
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # PERMANENT PARTIAL SAVE AFTER EVERY TIME
    # ==============================================================================================

    pd.DataFrame(
        ROWS_S
    ).to_csv(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_S_temporal_partial.csv",
        index=False,
    )

    pd.DataFrame(
        CHANNEL_ROWS_S
    ).to_csv(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_S_channels_partial.csv",
        index=False,
    )


    gc.collect()
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()


torch.cuda.synchronize()

TOTAL_RUNTIME_S = (
    time.perf_counter()
    -
    test_start_S
)

# ==================================================================================================
# 11. SPEARMAN HELPER WITHOUT SCIPY DEPENDENCY
# ==================================================================================================

def spearman_S(
    x,
    y,
):
    x = pd.Series(
        np.asarray(
            x,
            dtype=float,
        )
    )

    y = pd.Series(
        np.asarray(
            y,
            dtype=float,
        )
    )

    mask = (
        np.isfinite(
            x
        )
        &
        np.isfinite(
            y
        )
    )

    x = x[
        mask
    ].rank(
        method="average"
    )

    y = y[
        mask
    ].rank(
        method="average"
    )

    if len(
        x
    ) < 3:
        return np.nan

    return float(
        np.corrcoef(
            x.to_numpy(),
            y.to_numpy(),
        )[
            0,
            1
        ]
    )

# M3D TEST T — 12-CASE CROSS-FLOW FALSIFICATION OF REALIZED LEAKAGE RISK
#
#
# OBJECTIVE
# ---------
#
# Tests S/R established in periodic shear:
#
#   1. finite-time Q -> P leakage is low-rank
#   2. realized leakage is concentrated in ~1-3 channels
#   3. T deltaQ predicts the nonlinear Q-only response extremely accurately
#   4. leakage risk rises near the later global target-error burst
#
#
# TEST T asks:
#
#   DOES THIS GENERALIZE?
#
#
# 12 CASES
# --------
#
#   4 flow topologies
#       vortex_ring
#       periodic_shear
#       skew_tubes
#       mixed_vortices
#
#   x
#
#   3 viscosities
#       0.25x
#       1.00x
#       4.00x
#
#
# IMPORTANT
# ---------
#
# Uses corrected viscosity namespace synchronization from Test N / M-B.
#
# Every case explicitly calls:
#
#       sync_m3d_globals_MB(...)
#
# with that CASE'S viscosity before trajectory generation AND before
# fp64 leakage analysis.
#
#
# ANALYSIS HORIZON
# ----------------
#
# full teacher-free trajectory:
#
#       tau = 0 -> 1.95
#
# analyzed anchors:
#
#       tau = 0.30, 0.45, ..., 1.80
#
# future leakage horizon:
#
#       h = 0.15 tau
#
#
# WHY START AT tau=.30?
# ---------------------
#
# Avoids the essentially-zero initial error regime that contaminated
# naive amplification ratios in Test P.
#
#
# OPERATOR
# --------
#
#       T_k = P D Phi_h(U_k) Q_emp,k
#
# Q_emp,k uses ONLY Q-error snapshots from times <= tau_k.
#
# For computational tractability across 12 cases:
#
#   - retain causal POD modes through 99.9% Q-error energy
#   - hard cap at 6 modes
#   - build T with fp64 FORWARD finite differences
#   - perform a CENTRAL-DIFFERENCE falsification at the maximum-risk
#     anchor of every case
#
#
# PRIMARY MECHANISM METRIC
# ------------------------
#
#       R_k = ||T_k deltaQ_k|| / ||P Phi_h(U_k)||
#
#
# PRIMARY WARNING METRIC
# ----------------------
#
#       W_k = R_k / E_P,k
#
# where E_P,k is current global resolved error.
#
#
# BASELINES
# ---------
#
#       B1 = current P error
#       B2 = Q-error norm / full truth norm
#       B3 = full error norm / full truth norm
#       B4 = Q/P error ratio
#       B5 = tau
#
#
# FUTURE TARGETS
# --------------
#
# Absolute next-segment increase:
#
#       G_abs = E_P,k+1 - E_P,k
#
# Relative/log growth:
#
#       G_log = log(E_P,k+1 / E_P,k)
#
#
# CROSS-CASE EVALUATION
# ---------------------
#
# A. per-case Spearman correlation
# B. within-case rank-pooled correlation
# C. leave-one-case-out single-feature prediction of G_log
#
#
# OUTCOMES
# --------
#
# GENERAL_LEAKAGE_MECHANISM_T:
#       Is low-rank realized leakage a general mechanism?
#
# WARNING_SUPERIORITY_T:
#       Does leakage warning outperform conservative dumb baselines?
#
# These are intentionally SEPARATE claims.
#
#

print(
    "collection through tau:",
    COLLECTION_LAST_TAU_T,
)

print(
    "analysis tau range:",
    MIN_ANALYSIS_TAU_T,
    "to",
    MAX_ANALYSIS_TAU_T,
)

print(
    "segment tau:",
    SEGMENT_TAU_T,
)

print(
    "POD energy target:",
    POD_ENERGY_TARGET_T,
)

print(
    "max POD rank:",
    MAX_POD_RANK_T,
)

print(
    "forward FD eps_rel:",
    FD_EPS_REL_T,
)

print(
    "resume:",
    RESUME_T,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. NUMERICAL HELPERS
# ==================================================================================================

def pair_inner_T(
    A1,
    A2,
    B1,
    B2,
):
    return (
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A1)
            *
            B1
        )
        +
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A2)
            *
            B2
        )
    ).real


def pair_norm_tensor_T(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return torch.sqrt(
        torch.clamp(
            pair_inner_T(
                A1,
                A2,
                A1,
                A2,
            ),
            min=0.0,
        )
    )


def pair_norm_T(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return float(
        pair_norm_tensor_T(
            A1,
            A2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_norm_T(
    x,
):
    return float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            x
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_cos_T(
    a,
    b,
):
    na = torch.linalg.vector_norm(
        a
    )

    nb = torch.linalg.vector_norm(
        b
    )

    naf = float(
        na.detach().cpu()
    )

    nbf = float(
        nb.detach().cpu()
    )

    if (
        naf < 1e-30
        or
        nbf < 1e-30
    ):
        return np.nan

    return float(
        (
            torch.sum(
                torch.conj(a)
                *
                b
            ).real
            /
            (
                na
                *
                nb
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. EXACT P/Q SPLITTER
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def split_PQ_T(
    D1,
    D2,
    target_ids,
):
    P1 = torch.zeros_like(
        D1
    )

    P2 = torch.zeros_like(
        D2
    )

    D1f = D1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    D2f = D2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f = P1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P2f = P2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    P2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    return (
        P1,
        P2,
        D1 - P1,
        D2 - P2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. FOM SEGMENT
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def fom_segment_T(
    U1,
    U2,
    env,
    nu,
):
    A1 = U1.clone()
    A2 = U2.clone()

    for _ in range(
        SEGMENT_STEPS_T
    ):

        A1 = full_rk4_D(
            A1,
            DT_PHYS_T,
            nu,
            env,
        )

        A2 = full_rk4_D(
            A2,
            DT_PHYS_T,
            nu,
            env,
        )

    return (
        A1,
        A2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. CAUSAL TRUNCATED POD
#
# Uses only snapshots <= current time.
#
# Retain enough modes for 99.9% snapshot energy,
# but cap at MAX_POD_RANK_T.
# ==================================================================================================

def causal_pod_T(
    snapshots,
):
    m = len(
        snapshots
    )

    device = snapshots[
        0
    ][
        0
    ].device


    G = torch.zeros(
        (
            m,
            m,
        ),
        device=device,
        dtype=torch.float64,
    )


    for i in range(
        m
    ):
        for j in range(
            i,
            m
        ):

            gij = pair_inner_T(
                snapshots[i][0],
                snapshots[i][1],
                snapshots[j][0],
                snapshots[j][1],
            )

            G[
                i,
                j
            ] = gij

            G[
                j,
                i
            ] = gij


    evals, evecs = torch.linalg.eigh(
        G
    )

    order = torch.argsort(
        evals,
        descending=True,
    )

    evals = evals[
        order
    ]

    evecs = evecs[
        :,
        order
    ]


    max_eval = float(
        torch.clamp(
            evals[
                0
            ],
            min=0.0,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


    numerical_rank = int(
        (
            evals
            >
            POD_EPS_T
            *
            max(
                max_eval,
                1e-300,
            )
        ).sum().detach().cpu()
    )


    evals_valid = torch.clamp(
        evals[
            :numerical_rank
        ],
        min=0.0,
    )

    evecs_valid = evecs[
        :,
        :numerical_rank
    ]


    energy = (
        evals_valid
        /
        torch.sum(
            evals_valid
        )
    )

    cumulative = torch.cumsum(
        energy,
        dim=0,
    )


    hit = torch.nonzero(
        cumulative
        >=
        POD_ENERGY_TARGET_T,
        as_tuple=False,
    )


    if len(
        hit
    ) == 0:

        required_rank = numerical_rank

    else:

        required_rank = int(
            hit[
                0
            ].item()
            +
            1
        )


    retained_rank = min(
        required_rank,
        MAX_POD_RANK_T,
        numerical_rank,
    )


    modes = []


    for mode_i in range(
        retained_rank
    ):

        coeff = (
            evecs_valid[
                :,
                mode_i
            ]
            /
            torch.sqrt(
                evals_valid[
                    mode_i
                ]
            )
        )


        q1 = torch.zeros_like(
            snapshots[
                0
            ][
                0
            ]
        )

        q2 = torch.zeros_like(
            snapshots[
                0
            ][
                1
            ]
        )


        for snap_i in range(
            m
        ):

            q1 = (
                q1
                +
                coeff[
                    snap_i
                ]
                *
                snapshots[
                    snap_i
                ][
                    0
                ]
            )

            q2 = (
                q2
                +
                coeff[
                    snap_i
                ]
                *
                snapshots[
                    snap_i
                ][
                    1
                ]
            )


        qn = pair_norm_tensor_T(
            q1,
            q2,
        )

        q1 = (
            q1
            /
            qn
        )

        q2 = (
            q2
            /
            qn
        )


        modes.append(
            (
                q1,
                q2,
            )
        )


    max_orth_error = 0.0


    for i in range(
        retained_rank
    ):
        for j in range(
            retained_rank
        ):

            actual = float(
                pair_inner_T(
                    modes[i][0],
                    modes[i][1],
                    modes[j][0],
                    modes[j][1],
                ).detach().cpu()
            )

            expected = (
                1.0
                if i == j
                else 0.0
            )

            max_orth_error = max(
                max_orth_error,
                abs(
                    actual
                    -
                    expected
                ),
            )


    retained_snapshot_energy = float(
        cumulative[
            retained_rank
            -
            1
        ].detach().cpu()
    )


    return {
        "numerical_rank":
            numerical_rank,

        "required_rank":
            required_rank,

        "rank":
            retained_rank,

        "modes":
            modes,

        "orth_error":
            max_orth_error,

        "snapshot_energy":
            retained_snapshot_energy,

        "cap_hit":
            bool(
                required_rank
                >
                MAX_POD_RANK_T
            ),
    }


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. FORWARD FINITE-TIME LEAKAGE COLUMN
#
# truth endpoint x0_end is reused.
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def forward_leakage_column_T(
    U1_base,
    U2_base,
    x0_end,
    q1,
    q2,
    eps_abs,
    target_ids,
    env,
    nu,
):
    U1p, U2p = fom_segment_T(
        U1_base
        +
        eps_abs
        *
        q1,

        U2_base
        +
        eps_abs
        *
        q2,

        env,
        nu,
    )


    xp = pack_modes_D(
        U1p,
        U2p,
        target_ids,
    )


    return (
        xp
        -
        x0_end
    ) / eps_abs


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. CENTRAL FD CHECK
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def central_leakage_T(
    U1_base,
    U2_base,
    q1,
    q2,
    eps_abs,
    target_ids,
    env,
    nu,
):
    U1p, U2p = fom_segment_T(
        U1_base
        +
        eps_abs
        *
        q1,

        U2_base
        +
        eps_abs
        *
        q2,

        env,
        nu,
    )


    U1m, U2m = fom_segment_T(
        U1_base
        -
        eps_abs
        *
        q1,

        U2_base
        -
        eps_abs
        *
        q2,

        env,
        nu,
    )


    xp = pack_modes_D(
        U1p,
        U2p,
        target_ids,
    )

    xm = pack_modes_D(
        U1m,
        U2m,
        target_ids,
    )


    return (
        xp
        -
        xm
    ) / (
        2.0
        *
        eps_abs
    )
