if (
    RESUME_T
    and
    POINTS_FILE_T.exists()
):

    POINT_ROWS_T = pd.read_csv(
        POINTS_FILE_T
    ).to_dict(
        "records"
    )

else:

    POINT_ROWS_T = []


if (
    RESUME_T
    and
    CHANNELS_FILE_T.exists()
):

    CHANNEL_ROWS_T = pd.read_csv(
        CHANNELS_FILE_T
    ).to_dict(
        "records"
    )

else:

    CHANNEL_ROWS_T = []


if (
    RESUME_T
    and
    FD_FILE_T.exists()
):

    FD_ROWS_T = pd.read_csv(
        FD_FILE_T
    ).to_dict(
        "records"
    )

else:

    FD_ROWS_T = []


COMPLETED_CASES_T = set(
    str(
        row[
            "case"
        ]
    )
    for row in FD_ROWS_T
)


print()
print(
    "completed cases recovered:",
    sorted(
        COMPLETED_CASES_T
    ),
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. MAIN 12-CASE LOOP
# ==================================================================================================

RUN_START_T = time.perf_counter()


for topology in TOPOLOGIES_T:

    for nu_mult in NU_MULTS_T:

        case_nu = (
            BASE_NU_T
            *
            nu_mult
        )

        case_name = (
            f"{topology}"
            f"__nu{nu_mult:g}"
        )


        if case_name in COMPLETED_CASES_T:

            print()
            print(
                "SKIP completed:",
                case_name,
            )

            continue


        print()
        print("=" * 156)
        print(
            "CASE:",
            case_name
        )
        print("=" * 156)


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 9A. CORRECT FP32 TEACHER-FREE TRAJECTORY
        # ==========================================================================================

        sync_m3d_globals_MB(
            torch.float32,
            torch.complex64,
            case_nu,
            TAU0_T,
        )


        ENV32_T = make_env_D(
            40,
            L=8.0,
        )


        U10_T, U20_T = topology_pair_L2(
            topology,
            ENV32_T,
        )


        U1_truth_T = U10_T.clone()
        U2_truth_T = U20_T.clone()


        global_star_T, initial_diag_T = initialize_star2_N(
            U10_T,
            U20_T,
            ENV32_T,
        )


        target_ids_T = global_star_T[
            "proj"
        ][
            "target_ids"
        ]


        trajectory_T = []


        print(
            "  initial RAf:",
            f"{initial_diag_T['RAf_ratio']:.3e}"
        )


        for seg in range(
            1,
            N_COLLECTION_T + 1,
        ):

            for _ in range(
                SEGMENT_STEPS_T
            ):

                U1_truth_T = full_rk4_D(
                    U1_truth_T,
                    DT_PHYS_T,
                    case_nu,
                    ENV32_T,
                )

                U2_truth_T = full_rk4_D(
                    U2_truth_T,
                    DT_PHYS_T,
                    case_nu,
                    ENV32_T,
                )


                rk4_M(
                    global_star_T,
                    DTAU_T,
                    ENV32_T,
                )


            tau = (
                seg
                *
                SEGMENT_TAU_T
            )


            if seg < N_COLLECTION_T:

                (
                    global_star_T,
                    diag_T,
                    jump_T,
                    build_T,
                ) = reanchor_star2_N(
                    global_star_T,
                    ENV32_T,
                )

            else:

                diag_T = {
                    "RAf_ratio":
                        np.nan
                }

                jump_T = np.nan


            Ug1_T, Ug2_T = reconstruct_M(
                global_star_T,
                ENV32_T,
            )


            D1_T = (
                Ug1_T
                -
                U1_truth_T
            )

            D2_T = (
                Ug2_T
                -
                U2_truth_T
            )


            (
                PD1_T,
                PD2_T,
                QD1_T,
                QD2_T,
            ) = split_PQ_T(
                D1_T,
                D2_T,
                target_ids_T,
            )


            x_truth_T = pack_modes_D(
                U1_truth_T,
                U2_truth_T,
                target_ids_T,
            )

            x_global_T = pack_modes_D(
                Ug1_T,
                Ug2_T,
                target_ids_T,
            )


            P_rel_T = (
                vec_norm_T(
                    x_global_T
                    -
                    x_truth_T
                )
                /
                (
                    vec_norm_T(
                        x_truth_T
                    )
                    +
                    1e-30
                )
            )


            truth_full_norm_T = (
                pair_norm_T(
                    U1_truth_T,
                    U2_truth_T,
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )


            p_abs_T = pair_norm_T(
                PD1_T,
                PD2_T,
            )

            q_abs_T = pair_norm_T(
                QD1_T,
                QD2_T,
            )

            full_abs_T = pair_norm_T(
                D1_T,
                D2_T,
            )


            trajectory_T.append(
                {
                    "tau":
                        tau,

                    "truth1":
                        U1_truth_T.clone(),

                    "truth2":
                        U2_truth_T.clone(),

                    "Q1":
                        QD1_T.clone(),

                    "Q2":
                        QD2_T.clone(),

                    "P_abs":
                        p_abs_T,

                    "Q_abs":
                        q_abs_T,

                    "full_abs":
                        full_abs_T,

                    "truth_full_norm":
                        truth_full_norm_T,

                    "P_rel":
                        P_rel_T,

                    "Q_rel":
                        q_abs_T
                        /
                        truth_full_norm_T,

                    "full_rel":
                        full_abs_T
                        /
                        truth_full_norm_T,

                    "Q_over_P":
                        q_abs_T
                        /
                        max(
                            p_abs_T,
                            1e-30,
                        ),

                    "RAf":
                        diag_T[
                            "RAf_ratio"
                        ],

                    "jump":
                        jump_T,
                }
            )


            print(
                f"  collect tau={tau:4.2f}"
                f" | P={100*P_rel_T:9.5f}%"
                f" | Q/P={q_abs_T/max(p_abs_T,1e-30):9.3f}"
            )


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 9B. SWITCH THIS CASE TO TRUE FP64
        # ==========================================================================================

        sync_m3d_globals_MB(
            torch.float64,
            torch.complex128,
            case_nu,
            TAU0_T,
        )


        ENV64_T = make_env_D(
            40,
            L=8.0,
        )


        q_snapshots64_T = [
            (
                point[
                    "Q1"
                ].to(
                    torch.complex128
                ),

                point[
                    "Q2"
                ].to(
                    torch.complex128
                ),
            )
            for point
            in trajectory_T
        ]


        max_risk_record_T = None


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 9C. ANALYZE FULL 0.15 GRID
        #
        # seg indices:
        #
        # trajectory[0] = tau .15
        # trajectory[1] = tau .30
        # ...
        #
        # need next point, so max start = tau1.80
        # ==========================================================================================

        for idx in range(
            len(
                trajectory_T
            )
            -
            1
        ):

            point = trajectory_T[
                idx
            ]

            next_point = trajectory_T[
                idx
                +
                1
            ]


            tau = float(
                point[
                    "tau"
                ]
            )


            if (
                tau
                <
                MIN_ANALYSIS_TAU_T
                -
                1e-12
            ):
                continue


            if (
                tau
                >
                MAX_ANALYSIS_TAU_T
                +
                1e-12
            ):
                continue


            print()
            print(
                f"  ANALYZE tau={tau:.2f}"
                f" -> {tau+SEGMENT_TAU_T:.2f}"
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Base truth + actual Q.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            U1_base_T = point[
                "truth1"
            ].to(
                torch.complex128
            )

            U2_base_T = point[
                "truth2"
            ].to(
                torch.complex128
            )


            Q1_actual_T = q_snapshots64_T[
                idx
            ][
                0
            ]

            Q2_actual_T = q_snapshots64_T[
                idx
            ][
                1
            ]


            Q_actual_norm_T = pair_norm_T(
                Q1_actual_T,
                Q2_actual_T,
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Causal POD using all Q snapshots <= current tau.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            POD_T = causal_pod_T(
                q_snapshots64_T[
                    :idx
                    +
                    1
                ]
            )


            rank_T = POD_T[
                "rank"
            ]

            modes_T = POD_T[
                "modes"
            ]


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Actual Q coordinates in retained causal POD.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            a_T = torch.zeros(
                rank_T,
                device=U1_base_T.device,
                dtype=torch.float64,
            )


            for j in range(
                rank_T
            ):

                a_T[
                    j
                ] = pair_inner_T(
                    modes_T[
                        j
                    ][
                        0
                    ],

                    modes_T[
                        j
                    ][
                        1
                    ],

                    Q1_actual_T,
                    Q2_actual_T,
                )


            q_projected_energy_T = float(
                torch.sum(
                    a_T ** 2
                ).detach().cpu()
                /
                max(
                    Q_actual_norm_T ** 2,
                    1e-300,
                )
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # fp64 truth endpoint.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            U1_truth_end_T, U2_truth_end_T = fom_segment_T(
                U1_base_T,
                U2_base_T,
                ENV64_T,
                case_nu,
            )


            x_truth_end_T = pack_modes_D(
                U1_truth_end_T,
                U2_truth_end_T,
                target_ids_T,
            )


            target_truth_end_norm_T = (
                vec_norm_T(
                    x_truth_end_T
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Operator finite difference scale.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            base_norm_T = pair_norm_T(
                U1_base_T,
                U2_base_T,
            )


            eps_abs_T = (
                FD_EPS_REL_T
                *
                base_norm_T
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Build restricted forward-FD leakage operator.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            columns_T = []


            for j in range(
                rank_T
            ):

                response_T = forward_leakage_column_T(
                    U1_base_T,
                    U2_base_T,
                    x_truth_end_T,

                    modes_T[
                        j
                    ][
                        0
                    ],

                    modes_T[
                        j
                    ][
                        1
                    ],

                    eps_abs_T,
                    target_ids_T,
                    ENV64_T,
                    case_nu,
                )


                columns_T.append(
                    response_T
                )


            Y_T = torch.stack(
                columns_T,
                dim=1,
            )


            Uleak_T, Sleak_T, Vhleak_T = torch.linalg.svd(
                Y_T,
                full_matrices=False,
            )


            operator_energy_T = (
                Sleak_T ** 2
            )

            operator_energy_T = (
                operator_energy_T
                /
                torch.sum(
                    operator_energy_T
                )
            )

            op_cum_T = torch.cumsum(
                operator_energy_T,
                dim=0,
            )


            top3_operator_T = float(
                op_cum_T[
                    min(
                        3,
                        rank_T,
                    )
                    -
                    1
                ].detach().cpu()
            )


            stable_rank_T = float(
                (
                    torch.sum(
                        Sleak_T ** 2
                    )
                    /
                    (
                        Sleak_T[
                            0
                        ] ** 2
                        +
                        1e-300
                    )
                ).detach().cpu()
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Realized leakage channels.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            b_T = (
                Vhleak_T
                @
                a_T
            )


            c_T = (
                Sleak_T
                *
                b_T
            )


            realized_energy_T = (
                c_T ** 2
            )


            realized_total_T = float(
                torch.sum(
                    realized_energy_T
                ).detach().cpu()
            )


            if realized_total_T > 1e-300:

                realized_frac_T = (
                    realized_energy_T
                    /
                    torch.sum(
                        realized_energy_T
                    )
                )

            else:

                realized_frac_T = torch.zeros_like(
                    realized_energy_T
                )


            risk_order_T = torch.argsort(
                realized_frac_T,
                descending=True,
            )


            realized_top1_T = float(
                realized_frac_T[
                    risk_order_T[
                        0
                    ]
                ].detach().cpu()
            )


            realized_top3_T = float(
                torch.sum(
                    realized_frac_T[
                        risk_order_T[
                            :min(
                                3,
                                rank_T,
                            )
                        ]
                    ]
                ).detach().cpu()
            )


            dominant_index_T = int(
                risk_order_T[
                    0
                ].item()
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Predicted realized Q -> P output.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            y_pred_T = (
                Y_T
                @
                a_T
            )


            risk_abs_T = vec_norm_T(
                y_pred_T
            )


            risk_rel_T = (
                risk_abs_T
                /
                target_truth_end_norm_T
            )


            current_P_rel_T = float(
                point[
                    "P_rel"
                ]
            )


            warning_ratio_T = (
                risk_rel_T
                /
                max(
                    current_P_rel_T,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Actual nonlinear Q-only causal response.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            U1_qend_T, U2_qend_T = fom_segment_T(
                U1_base_T
                +
                Q1_actual_T,

                U2_base_T
                +
                Q2_actual_T,

                ENV64_T,
                case_nu,
            )


            x_qend_T = pack_modes_D(
                U1_qend_T,
                U2_qend_T,
                target_ids_T,
            )


            e_q_actual_T = (
                x_qend_T
                -
                x_truth_end_T
            )


            actual_q_to_p_abs_T = vec_norm_T(
                e_q_actual_T
            )


            actual_q_to_p_rel_T = (
                actual_q_to_p_abs_T
                /
                target_truth_end_norm_T
            )


            prediction_cos_T = vec_cos_T(
                y_pred_T,
                e_q_actual_T,
            )


            prediction_norm_ratio_T = (
                risk_abs_T
                /
                max(
                    actual_q_to_p_abs_T,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            prediction_residual_T = (
                vec_norm_T(
                    y_pred_T
                    -
                    e_q_actual_T
                )
                /
                max(
                    actual_q_to_p_abs_T,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Future global targets.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            next_P_rel_T = float(
                next_point[
                    "P_rel"
                ]
            )


            next_increment_T = (
                next_P_rel_T
                -
                current_P_rel_T
            )


            log_growth_T = math.log(
                max(
                    next_P_rel_T,
                    1e-30,
                )
                /
                max(
                    current_P_rel_T,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            growth_factor_T = (
                next_P_rel_T
                /
                max(
                    current_P_rel_T,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Full singular physical mode for max-risk FD check.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            dom_coeff_T = Vhleak_T[
                dominant_index_T,
                :
            ]


            dom1_T = torch.zeros_like(
                U1_base_T
            )

            dom2_T = torch.zeros_like(
                U2_base_T
            )


            for j in range(
                rank_T
            ):

                dom1_T = (
                    dom1_T
                    +
                    dom_coeff_T[
                        j
                    ]
                    *
                    modes_T[
                        j
                    ][
                        0
                    ]
                )

                dom2_T = (
                    dom2_T
                    +
                    dom_coeff_T[
                        j
                    ]
                    *
                    modes_T[
                        j
                    ][
                        1
                    ]
                )


            dn_T = pair_norm_tensor_T(
                dom1_T,
                dom2_T,
            )


            dom1_T = (
                dom1_T
                /
                dn_T
            )

            dom2_T = (
                dom2_T
                /
                dn_T
            )


            dom_forward_derivative_T = (
                Uleak_T[
                    :,
                    dominant_index_T
                ]
                *
                Sleak_T[
                    dominant_index_T
                ]
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Save max-risk anchor for central FD falsification.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            if (
                max_risk_record_T is None
                or
                risk_rel_T
                >
                max_risk_record_T[
                    "risk_rel"
                ]
            ):

                max_risk_record_T = {
                    "tau":
                        tau,

                    "risk_rel":
                        risk_rel_T,

                    "U1":
                        U1_base_T.clone(),

                    "U2":
                        U2_base_T.clone(),

                    "dom1":
                        dom1_T.clone(),

                    "dom2":
                        dom2_T.clone(),

                    "eps":
                        eps_abs_T,

                    "forward":
                        dom_forward_derivative_T.clone(),
                }


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Main point row.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            POINT_ROWS_T.append(
                {
                    "case":
                        case_name,

                    "topology":
                        topology,

                    "nu_mult":
                        nu_mult,

                    "nu":
                        case_nu,

                    "tau":
                        tau,

                    "tau_end":
                        tau
                        +
                        SEGMENT_TAU_T,

                    "causal_snapshot_count":
                        idx
                        +
                        1,

                    "pod_numerical_rank":
                        POD_T[
                            "numerical_rank"
                        ],

                    "pod_required_rank":
                        POD_T[
                            "required_rank"
                        ],

                    "pod_retained_rank":
                        rank_T,

                    "pod_cap_hit":
                        POD_T[
                            "cap_hit"
                        ],

                    "pod_snapshot_energy":
                        POD_T[
                            "snapshot_energy"
                        ],

                    "actual_Q_projected_energy":
                        q_projected_energy_T,

                    "pod_orth_error":
                        POD_T[
                            "orth_error"
                        ],

                    "operator_top3_energy":
                        top3_operator_T,

                    "operator_stable_rank":
                        stable_rank_T,

                    "realized_top1_energy":
                        realized_top1_T,

                    "realized_top3_energy":
                        realized_top3_T,

                    "dominant_realized_index":
                        dominant_index_T
                        +
                        1,

                    # Main leakage quantities
                    "risk_rel":
                        risk_rel_T,

                    "risk_over_P":
                        warning_ratio_T,

                    "actual_Q_to_P_rel":
                        actual_q_to_p_rel_T,

                    "prediction_cosine":
                        prediction_cos_T,

                    "prediction_norm_ratio":
                        prediction_norm_ratio_T,

                    "prediction_residual":
                        prediction_residual_T,

                    # Baselines
                    "current_P_rel":
                        current_P_rel_T,

                    "Q_rel":
                        float(
                            point[
                                "Q_rel"
                            ]
                        ),

                    "full_rel":
                        float(
                            point[
                                "full_rel"
                            ]
                        ),

                    "Q_over_P":
                        float(
                            point[
                                "Q_over_P"
                            ]
                        ),

                    # Targets
                    "next_P_rel":
                        next_P_rel_T,

                    "next_increment":
                        next_increment_T,

                    "growth_factor":
                        growth_factor_T,

                    "log_growth":
                        log_growth_T,
                }
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Channel rows.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            for sidx in range(
                rank_T
            ):

                CHANNEL_ROWS_T.append(
                    {
                        "case":
                            case_name,

                        "topology":
                            topology,

                        "nu_mult":
                            nu_mult,

                        "tau":
                            tau,

                        "singular_index":
                            sidx
                            +
                            1,

                        "sigma":
                            float(
                                Sleak_T[
                                    sidx
                                ].detach().cpu()
                            ),

                        "occupancy_b":
                            float(
                                b_T[
                                    sidx
                                ].detach().cpu()
                            ),

                        "realized_c":
                            float(
                                c_T[
                                    sidx
                                ].detach().cpu()
                            ),

                        "operator_energy_fraction":
                            float(
                                operator_energy_T[
                                    sidx
                                ].detach().cpu()
                            ),

                        "realized_energy_fraction":
                            float(
                                realized_frac_T[
                                    sidx
                                ].detach().cpu()
                            ),
                    }
                )


            print(
                f"    rank={rank_T}"
                f" | POD-Q={100*q_projected_energy_T:7.3f}%"
                f" | op3={100*top3_operator_T:7.3f}%"
                f" | real3={100*realized_top3_T:7.3f}%"
            )

            print(
                f"    risk={100*risk_rel_T:.7f}%"
                f" | actualQ->P={100*actual_q_to_p_rel_T:.7f}%"
                f" | cos={prediction_cos_T:.6f}"
                f" | resid={prediction_residual_T:.4f}"
            )

            print(
                f"    P now={100*current_P_rel_T:.5f}%"
                f" -> next={100*next_P_rel_T:.5f}%"
                f" | W={warning_ratio_T:.4f}"
            )


            gc.collect()
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 9D. CENTRAL FD FALSIFICATION AT MAX-RISK ANCHOR
        # ==========================================================================================

        assert max_risk_record_T is not None


        print()
        print(
            "  CENTRAL FD CHECK AT MAX-RISK tau=",
            max_risk_record_T[
                "tau"
            ],
        )


        central_T = central_leakage_T(
            max_risk_record_T[
                "U1"
            ],

            max_risk_record_T[
                "U2"
            ],

            max_risk_record_T[
                "dom1"
            ],

            max_risk_record_T[
                "dom2"
            ],

            max_risk_record_T[
                "eps"
            ],

            target_ids_T,
            ENV64_T,
            case_nu,
        )


        fd_cos_T = vec_cos_T(
            max_risk_record_T[
                "forward"
            ],
            central_T,
        )


        fd_norm_ratio_T = (
            vec_norm_T(
                max_risk_record_T[
                    "forward"
                ]
            )
            /
            max(
                vec_norm_T(
                    central_T
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        fd_residual_T = (
            vec_norm_T(
                max_risk_record_T[
                    "forward"
                ]
                -
                central_T
            )
            /
            max(
                vec_norm_T(
                    central_T
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        FD_ROWS_T.append(
            {
                "case":
                    case_name,

                "topology":
                    topology,

                "nu_mult":
                    nu_mult,

                "nu":
                    case_nu,

                "max_risk_tau":
                    max_risk_record_T[
                        "tau"
                    ],

                "max_risk_rel":
                    max_risk_record_T[
                        "risk_rel"
                    ],

                "fd_forward_central_cosine":
                    fd_cos_T,

                "fd_forward_central_norm_ratio":
                    fd_norm_ratio_T,

                "fd_forward_central_residual":
                    fd_residual_T,
            }
        )


        print(
            f"    FD cosine={fd_cos_T:.10f}"
        )

        print(
            f"    FD norm ratio={fd_norm_ratio_T:.10f}"
        )

        print(
            f"    FD residual={fd_residual_T:.3e}"
        )


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 9E. CASE CHECKPOINT
        # ==========================================================================================

        pd.DataFrame(
            POINT_ROWS_T
        ).to_csv(
            POINTS_FILE_T,
            index=False,
        )


        pd.DataFrame(
            CHANNEL_ROWS_T
        ).to_csv(
            CHANNELS_FILE_T,
            index=False,
        )


        pd.DataFrame(
            FD_ROWS_T
        ).to_csv(
            FD_FILE_T,
            index=False,
        )


        COMPLETED_CASES_T.add(
            case_name
        )


        # Restore memory.
        del trajectory_T
        del q_snapshots64_T
        del global_star_T

        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()


# ==================================================================================================

# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_T = pd.DataFrame(

)

    )
)

    "case"
].nunique() != EXPECTED_CASES_T:

    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Test T incomplete: found "
        f"{RESULTS_T['case'].nunique()} / {EXPECTED_CASES_T} cases."
    )


RESULTS_T.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_T_crosscase_points.csv",
    index=False,
)

    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_T_crosscase_channels.csv",
    index=False,
)

    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_T_crosscase_fd.csv",
    index=False,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. PERIODIC-SHEAR ν=1 SENTINEL
#
# Test-S reference around tau=1.35:
#
# predicted Q -> P ~= 0.0017479507
# next global P      ~= 0.0035751044
#
# Forward FD should reproduce this closely.
# ==================================================================================================

SENTINEL_T = RESULTS_T[
    (
        RESULTS_T[
            "topology"
        ]
        ==
        "periodic_shear"
    )
    &
    (
        np.isclose(
            RESULTS_T[
                "nu_mult"
            ],
            1.0,
        )
    )
    &
    (
        np.isclose(
            RESULTS_T[
                "tau"
            ],
            1.35,
        )
    )
]


if len(
    SENTINEL_T
) != 1:

    raise RuntimeError(
        "Periodic-shear nu=1 tau=1.35 sentinel missing."
    )


sentinel_row_T = SENTINEL_T.iloc[
    0
]


S_REF_RISK_T = 0.0017479507

S_REF_NEXT_T = 0.0035751044


SENTINEL_RISK_RELERR_T = abs(
    float(
        sentinel_row_T[
            "risk_rel"
        ]
    )
    -
    S_REF_RISK_T
) / S_REF_RISK_T


SENTINEL_NEXT_RELERR_T = abs(
    float(
        sentinel_row_T[
            "next_P_rel"
        ]
    )
    -
    S_REF_NEXT_T
) / S_REF_NEXT_T


SENTINEL_GATE_T = bool(
    SENTINEL_RISK_RELERR_T
    <
    0.03
    and
    SENTINEL_NEXT_RELERR_T
    <
    0.01
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 12. SPEARMAN
# ==================================================================================================

def spearman_T(
    x,
    y,
):
    x = pd.Series(
        np.asarray(
            x,
            dtype=float,
        )
    )

    y = pd.Series(
        np.asarray(
            y,
            dtype=float,
        )
    )

    mask = (
        np.isfinite(
            x
        )
        &
        np.isfinite(
            y
        )
    )

    x = x[
        mask
    ].rank(
        method="average"
    )

    y = y[
        mask
    ].rank(
        method="average"
    )


    if len(
        x
    ) < 3:

        return np.nan


    sx = float(
        np.std(
            x.to_numpy()
        )
    )

    sy = float(
        np.std(
            y.to_numpy()
        )
    )


    if (
        sx < 1e-30
        or
        sy < 1e-30
    ):

        return np.nan


    return float(
        np.corrcoef(
            x.to_numpy(),
            y.to_numpy(),
        )[
            0,
            1
        ]
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 13. METRICS / TARGETS
# ==================================================================================================

METRICS_T = {
    "LEAKAGE_RISK":
        "risk_rel",

    "LEAKAGE_WARNING":
        "risk_over_P",

    "CURRENT_P":
        "current_P_rel",

    "Q_NORM":
        "Q_rel",

    "FULL_ERROR":
        "full_rel",

    "Q_OVER_P":
        "Q_over_P",

    "TAU":
        "tau",
}


BASELINE_METRICS_T = [
    "CURRENT_P",
    "Q_NORM",
    "FULL_ERROR",
    "Q_OVER_P",
    "TAU",
]


TARGETS_T = {
    "NEXT_INCREMENT":
        "next_increment",

    "LOG_GROWTH":
        "log_growth",
}


# ==================================================================================================
# 14. PER-CASE SPEARMAN
# ==================================================================================================

CORR_ROWS_T = []


for case_name, case_df in RESULTS_T.groupby(
    "case"
):

    for metric_name, metric_col in METRICS_T.items():

        for target_name, target_col in TARGETS_T.items():

            rho = spearman_T(
                case_df[
                    metric_col
                ],
                case_df[
                    target_col
                ],
            )


            CORR_ROWS_T.append(
                {
                    "case":
                        case_name,

                    "metric":
                        metric_name,

                    "target":
                        target_name,

                    "spearman":
                        rho,
                }
            )


CORRS_T = pd.DataFrame(
    CORR_ROWS_T
)


CORRS_T.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_T_percase_correlations.csv",
    index=False,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 15. CORRELATION SUMMARY
# ==================================================================================================

CORR_SUMMARY_T = (
    CORRS_T
    .groupby(
        [
            "metric",
            "target",
        ]
    )[
        "spearman"
    ]
    .agg(
        [
            "median",
            "mean",
            "min",
            "max",
        ]
    )
    .reset_index()
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 16. WITHIN-CASE RANK-POOLED CORRELATION
#
# Prevents a globally "hard" case from dominating merely because
# all of its errors are larger.
# ==================================================================================================

RANK_POOL_ROWS_T = []


for metric_name, metric_col in METRICS_T.items():

    for target_name, target_col in TARGETS_T.items():

        ranked_parts = []


        for case_name, case_df in RESULTS_T.groupby(
            "case"
        ):

            temp = pd.DataFrame(
                {
                    "mx":
                        case_df[
                            metric_col
                        ].rank(
                            pct=True,
                            method="average",
                        ),

                    "ty":
                        case_df[
                            target_col
                        ].rank(
                            pct=True,
                            method="average",
                        ),
                }
            )


            ranked_parts.append(
                temp
            )


        pooled = pd.concat(
            ranked_parts,
            ignore_index=True,
        )


        rho = spearman_T(
            pooled[
                "mx"
            ],
            pooled[
                "ty"
            ],
        )


        RANK_POOL_ROWS_T.append(
            {
                "metric":
                    metric_name,

                "target":
                    target_name,

                "within_case_pooled_spearman":
                    rho,
            }
        )


RANK_POOL_T = pd.DataFrame(
    RANK_POOL_ROWS_T
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 17. LEAVE-ONE-CASE-OUT SINGLE-FEATURE PREDICTION
#
# Predict:
#
#       y = LOG_GROWTH
#
# One feature at a time.
#
# Positive magnitude features are log transformed.
# tau is left linear.
#
# ALL preprocessing / regression is fit on the other 11 cases.
# ==================================================================================================

def transform_feature_T(
    values,
    metric_name,
):
    x = np.asarray(
        values,
        dtype=float,
    )


    if metric_name == "TAU":

        return x


    floor = 1e-16


    return np.log(
        np.maximum(
            x,
            floor,
        )
    )


LOCO_PRED_ROWS_T = []


unique_cases_T = sorted(
    RESULTS_T[
        "case"
    ].unique()
)


for metric_name, metric_col in METRICS_T.items():

    for heldout in unique_cases_T:

        train = RESULTS_T[
            RESULTS_T[
                "case"
            ]
            !=
            heldout
        ].copy()


        test = RESULTS_T[
            RESULTS_T[
                "case"
            ]
            ==
            heldout
        ].copy()


        x_train = transform_feature_T(
            train[
                metric_col
            ],
            metric_name,
        )

        x_test = transform_feature_T(
            test[
                metric_col
            ],
            metric_name,
        )


        y_train = train[
            "log_growth"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )

        y_test = test[
            "log_growth"
        ].to_numpy(
            dtype=float
        )


        mu = float(
            np.mean(
                x_train
            )
        )

        sd = float(
            np.std(
                x_train
            )
        )


        if sd < 1e-14:
            sd = 1.0


        z_train = (
            x_train
            -
            mu
        ) / sd

        z_test = (
            x_test
            -
            mu
        ) / sd


        X_train = np.column_stack(
            [
                np.ones_like(
                    z_train
                ),
                z_train,
            ]
        )


        beta, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(
            X_train,
            y_train,
            rcond=None,
        )


        y_pred = (
            beta[
                0
            ]
            +
            beta[
                1
            ]
            *
            z_test
        )


        for tau, truth, pred in zip(
            test[
                "tau"
            ].to_numpy(),
            y_test,
            y_pred,
        ):

            LOCO_PRED_ROWS_T.append(
                {
                    "metric":
                        metric_name,

                    "heldout_case":
                        heldout,

                    "tau":
                        tau,

                    "true_log_growth":
                        truth,

                    "pred_log_growth":
                        pred,
                }
            )


LOCO_PREDS_T = pd.DataFrame(
    LOCO_PRED_ROWS_T
)


LOCO_SUMMARY_ROWS_T = []


for metric_name, temp in LOCO_PREDS_T.groupby(
    "metric"
):

    y = temp[
        "true_log_growth"
    ].to_numpy(
        dtype=float
    )

    yp = temp[
        "pred_log_growth"
    ].to_numpy(
        dtype=float
    )


    rmse = float(
        np.sqrt(
            np.mean(
                (
                    y
                    -
                    yp
                ) ** 2
            )
        )
    )

    mae = float(
        np.mean(
            np.abs(
                y
                -
                yp
            )
        )
    )


    ss_res = float(
        np.sum(
            (
                y
                -
                yp
            ) ** 2
        )
    )

    ss_tot = float(
        np.sum(
            (
                y
                -
                np.mean(
                    y
                )
            ) ** 2
        )
    )


    r2 = (
        1.0
        -
        ss_res
        /
        max(
            ss_tot,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    LOCO_SUMMARY_ROWS_T.append(
        {
            "metric":
                metric_name,

            "LOCO_RMSE":
                rmse,

            "LOCO_MAE":
                mae,

            "LOCO_R2":
                r2,
        }
    )


LOCO_SUMMARY_T = pd.DataFrame(
    LOCO_SUMMARY_ROWS_T
)


LOCO_PREDS_T.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_T_LOCO_predictions.csv",
    index=False,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 18. MECHANISM GENERALIZATION SUMMARY
# ==================================================================================================

MEDIAN_Q_CAPTURE_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "actual_Q_projected_energy"
    ].median()
)

P10_Q_CAPTURE_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "actual_Q_projected_energy"
    ].quantile(
        0.10
    )
)


MEDIAN_OPERATOR_TOP3_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "operator_top3_energy"
    ].median()
)

P10_OPERATOR_TOP3_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "operator_top3_energy"
    ].quantile(
        0.10
    )
)


MEDIAN_REALIZED_TOP1_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "realized_top1_energy"
    ].median()
)

MEDIAN_REALIZED_TOP3_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "realized_top3_energy"
    ].median()
)

P10_REALIZED_TOP3_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "realized_top3_energy"
    ].quantile(
        0.10
    )
)


MEDIAN_PRED_COS_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "prediction_cosine"
    ].median()
)

P10_PRED_COS_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "prediction_cosine"
    ].quantile(
        0.10
    )
)


MEDIAN_PRED_NORM_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "prediction_norm_ratio"
    ].median()
)

MEDIAN_PRED_RESID_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "prediction_residual"
    ].median()
)

P90_PRED_RESID_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "prediction_residual"
    ].quantile(
        0.90
    )
)


MAX_POD_ORTH_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "pod_orth_error"
    ].max()
)


CAP_HIT_RATE_T = float(
    RESULTS_T[
        "pod_cap_hit"
    ].astype(
        float
    ).mean()
)


MIN_FD_COS_T = float(
    FD_RESULTS_T[
        "fd_forward_central_cosine"
    ].min()
)


MAX_FD_RESID_T = float(
    FD_RESULTS_T[
        "fd_forward_central_residual"
    ].max()
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 19. PRIMARY WARNING COMPARISON
#
# Primary warning feature:
#
#       LEAKAGE_WARNING = R / current-P-error
#
# Primary target:
#
#       LOG_GROWTH
# ==================================================================================================

def summary_lookup_T(
    metric,
    target,
    column,
):
    row = CORR_SUMMARY_T[
        (
            CORR_SUMMARY_T[
                "metric"
            ]
            ==
            metric
        )
        &
        (
            CORR_SUMMARY_T[
                "target"
            ]
            ==
            target
        )
    ]

    return float(
        row.iloc[
            0
        ][
            column
        ]
    )


def pooled_lookup_T(
    metric,
    target,
):
    row = RANK_POOL_T[
        (
            RANK_POOL_T[
                "metric"
            ]
            ==
            metric
        )
        &
        (
            RANK_POOL_T[
                "target"
            ]
            ==
            target
        )
    ]

    return float(
        row.iloc[
            0
        ][
            "within_case_pooled_spearman"
        ]
    )


def loco_lookup_T(
    metric,
    column,
):
    row = LOCO_SUMMARY_T[
        LOCO_SUMMARY_T[
            "metric"
        ]
        ==
        metric
    ]

    return float(
        row.iloc[
            0
        ][
            column
        ]
    )


WARNING_MEDIAN_RHO_T = summary_lookup_T(
    "LEAKAGE_WARNING",
    "LOG_GROWTH",
    "median",
)

WARNING_POOLED_RHO_T = pooled_lookup_T(
    "LEAKAGE_WARNING",
    "LOG_GROWTH",
)

WARNING_LOCO_RMSE_T = loco_lookup_T(
    "LEAKAGE_WARNING",
    "LOCO_RMSE",
)


BASELINE_MEDIAN_RHOS_T = {
    metric:
        summary_lookup_T(
            metric,
            "LOG_GROWTH",
            "median",
        )
    for metric
    in BASELINE_METRICS_T
}


BASELINE_POOLED_RHOS_T = {
    metric:
        pooled_lookup_T(
            metric,
            "LOG_GROWTH",
        )
    for metric
    in BASELINE_METRICS_T
}


BASELINE_LOCO_RMSES_T = {
    metric:
        loco_lookup_T(
            metric,
            "LOCO_RMSE",
        )
    for metric
    in BASELINE_METRICS_T
}


BEST_BASELINE_MEDIAN_NAME_T = max(
    BASELINE_MEDIAN_RHOS_T,
    key=BASELINE_MEDIAN_RHOS_T.get,
)

BEST_BASELINE_MEDIAN_RHO_T = (
    BASELINE_MEDIAN_RHOS_T[
        BEST_BASELINE_MEDIAN_NAME_T
    ]
)


BEST_BASELINE_POOLED_NAME_T = max(
    BASELINE_POOLED_RHOS_T,
    key=BASELINE_POOLED_RHOS_T.get,
)

BEST_BASELINE_POOLED_RHO_T = (
    BASELINE_POOLED_RHOS_T[
        BEST_BASELINE_POOLED_NAME_T
    ]
)


BEST_BASELINE_LOCO_NAME_T = min(
    BASELINE_LOCO_RMSES_T,
    key=BASELINE_LOCO_RMSES_T.get,
)

BEST_BASELINE_LOCO_RMSE_T = (
    BASELINE_LOCO_RMSES_T[
        BEST_BASELINE_LOCO_NAME_T
    ]
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 20. GATES
#
# These are diagnostic / preregistered engineering thresholds,
# not mathematical theorems.
# ==================================================================================================

NUMERICS_GATE_T = bool(
    MAX_POD_ORTH_T
    <
    1e-8
    and
    MIN_FD_COS_T
    >
    0.999
    and
    MAX_FD_RESID_T
    <
    0.02
    and
    SENTINEL_GATE_T
)


Q_SUBSPACE_GATE_T = bool(
    MEDIAN_Q_CAPTURE_T
    >=
    0.99
    and
    P10_Q_CAPTURE_T
    >=
    0.95
)


LINEAR_PREDICTION_GATE_T = bool(
    MEDIAN_PRED_COS_T
    >=
    0.98
    and
    0.90
    <=
    MEDIAN_PRED_NORM_T
    <=
    1.10
    and
    P90_PRED_RESID_T
    <=
    0.20
)


OPERATOR_LOW_RANK_GATE_T = bool(
    MEDIAN_OPERATOR_TOP3_T
    >=
    0.80
)


REALIZED_LOW_RANK_GATE_T = bool(
    MEDIAN_REALIZED_TOP3_T
    >=
    0.90
)


GENERAL_LEAKAGE_MECHANISM_T = bool(
    NUMERICS_GATE_T
    and
    Q_SUBSPACE_GATE_T
    and
    LINEAR_PREDICTION_GATE_T
    and
    OPERATOR_LOW_RANK_GATE_T
    and
    REALIZED_LOW_RANK_GATE_T
)


# Warning superiority must beat the BEST dumb baseline,
# not merely a hand-picked weak one.

WARNING_CORRELATION_SUPERIORITY_T = bool(
    WARNING_MEDIAN_RHO_T
    >=
    BEST_BASELINE_MEDIAN_RHO_T
    +
    0.10
    and
    WARNING_POOLED_RHO_T
    >=
    BEST_BASELINE_POOLED_RHO_T
    +
    0.05
)


WARNING_LOCO_SUPERIORITY_T = bool(
    WARNING_LOCO_RMSE_T
    <=
    0.95
    *
    BEST_BASELINE_LOCO_RMSE_T
)


WARNING_SUPERIORITY_T = bool(
    WARNING_CORRELATION_SUPERIORITY_T
    and
    WARNING_LOCO_SUPERIORITY_T
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 21. CASE-LEVEL MECHANISM SUMMARY
# ==================================================================================================

CASE_SUMMARY_T = (
    RESULTS_T
    .groupby(
        [
            "case",
            "topology",
            "nu_mult",
        ]
    )
    .agg(
        n_points=(
            "tau",
            "count",
        ),

        median_Q_capture=(
            "actual_Q_projected_energy",
            "median",
        ),

        median_operator_top3=(
            "operator_top3_energy",
            "median",
        ),

        median_realized_top3=(
            "realized_top3_energy",
            "median",
        ),

        median_prediction_cosine=(
            "prediction_cosine",
            "median",
        ),

        median_prediction_residual=(
            "prediction_residual",
            "median",
        ),

        max_risk=(
            "risk_rel",
            "max",
        ),

        max_warning_ratio=(
            "risk_over_P",
            "max",
        ),
    )
    .reset_index()
)


CASE_SUMMARY_T.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_T_case_summary.csv",
    index=False,
)

    )

    )

    )

    print()

    )

    print(

    )

    print()

    print(

    )

    print(
        "derive a truth-free estimator of dangerous hidden-error occupancy,"
    )

    print(
        "then test whether it can drive online adaptive correction."
    )


elif GENERAL_LEAKAGE_MECHANISM_T:

    print(
        "🔥 THE GENERAL LEAKAGE MECHANISM SURVIVED,"
    )

    )

    print()

    print(
        "That is still a scientifically useful result:"
    )

    print(

    )

    print(
        "but ||T deltaQ|| / current-P-error is not yet justified"
    )

    print(
        "as a superior predictive alarm."
    )

    print()

    print(
        "NEXT:"
    )

    print(
        "study better normalized or uncertainty-weighted leakage quantities"
    )

    print(
        "WITHOUT changing the underlying mechanism claim."
    )


elif WARNING_SUPERIORITY_T:

    print(
        "🟡 THE WARNING SIGNAL PERFORMED WELL,"
    )

    print(
        "but the low-rank mechanism itself was not sufficiently universal."
    )

    )

    )


else:

    print(

    )

    )

    print()

    )

# CENTRAL FIRST + SECOND DIRECTIONAL DERIVATIVES
# + AMPLITUDE SWEEP
# + HARD-CASE / CLEAN-CONTROL FALSIFICATION
#
#
# TEST-T RESULT
# -------------
#
# Hidden-to-observable leakage remained strongly low-rank across 12 cases,
# but the FIRST-ORDER finite-time tangent prediction lost quantitative
# accuracy in some high-error, low-viscosity regimes.
#
#
# HYPOTHESIS
# ----------
#
# The hidden DIRECTIONS are still basically right.
#
# The missing physics is finite-amplitude curvature:
#
#       E(alpha)
#       =
#       P[Phi_h(U + alpha d) - Phi_h(U)]
#
# where:
#
#       d = actual delta_Q
#
#
# Taylor expansion with respect to alpha:
#
#       E(alpha)
#       =
#       alpha g1
#       +
#       alpha^2/2 g2
#       +
#       O(alpha^3)
#
# where:
#
#       g1 = d/dalpha E(alpha)|0
#
#       g2 = d^2/dalpha^2 E(alpha)|0
#
#
# TEST
# ----
#
# Compare:
#
#       E1(alpha) = alpha g1
#
# versus:
#
#       E2(alpha) = alpha g1 + alpha^2/2 g2
#
#
# AMPLITUDES
# ----------
#
#       alpha = .125, .25, .50, .75, 1.00
#
#
# CASES
# -----
#
# Three known hard finite-amplitude Test-T regimes:
#
#       vortex_ring      nu=.25x, tau=1.80
#       skew_tubes       nu=.25x, tau=1.80
#       mixed_vortices   nu=.25x, tau=1.80
#
# Two controls:
#
#       periodic_shear   nu=1x,   tau=1.80
#       vortex_ring      nu=4x,   tau=1.80
#
#
# IMPORTANT
# ---------
#
# Test U is NOT a new cross-case generalization test.
#
# Hard cases are intentionally selected because Test T already showed
# first-order finite-amplitude failure there.
#
# Test U asks WHY.
#
#
# NUMERICS
# --------
#
# trajectory generation:
#       fp32 / complex64, exactly as Test T
#
# derivative + causal evaluations:
#       fp64 / complex128
#
#
# derivative alpha step is chosen so:
#
#       ||eps_alpha * deltaQ|| / ||U|| = 1e-5
#
#
# We independently repeat derivatives at eps/2.
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch

    )

if not torch.cuda.is_available():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "CUDA GPU required."
    )

if "SAVE_DIR" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError(
        "SAVE_DIR missing."
    )

    SAVE_DIR
)

    parents=True,
    exist_ok=True,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. FROZEN SETTINGS
# ==================================================================================================

BASE_NU_U = 2.5e-3

TAU0_U = 40.0 / 3.0

DT_PHYS_U = 0.01

DTAU_U = (
    DT_PHYS_U
    /
    TAU0_U
)

SEGMENT_TAU_U = 0.15

SEGMENT_STEPS_U = int(
    round(
        SEGMENT_TAU_U
        /
        DTAU_U
    )
)

ANCHOR_TAU_U = 1.80

ANCHOR_SEGMENTS_U = int(
    round(
        ANCHOR_TAU_U
        /
        SEGMENT_TAU_U
    )
)

AMPLITUDES_U = np.array(
    [
        0.125,
        0.25,
        0.50,
        0.75,
        1.00,
    ],
    dtype=float,
)

DERIVATIVE_REL_PERT_U = 1e-5


CASES_U = [
    {
        "name":
            "vortex_ring__nu0.25",

        "topology":
            "vortex_ring",

        "nu_mult":
            0.25,

        "kind":
            "hard",
    },

    {
        "name":
            "skew_tubes__nu0.25",

        "topology":
            "skew_tubes",

        "nu_mult":
            0.25,

        "kind":
            "hard",
    },

    {
        "name":
            "mixed_vortices__nu0.25",

        "topology":
            "mixed_vortices",

        "nu_mult":
            0.25,

        "kind":
            "hard",
    },

    {
        "name":
            "periodic_shear__nu1",

        "topology":
            "periodic_shear",

        "nu_mult":
            1.0,

        "kind":
            "control",
    },

    {
        "name":
            "vortex_ring__nu4",

        "topology":
            "vortex_ring",

        "nu_mult":
            4.0,

        "kind":
            "control",
    },
]


print("=" * 152)
print("M3D TEST U — FINITE-AMPLITUDE LEAKAGE LAW")
print("=" * 152)

print()
print(
    "GPU:",
    torch.cuda.get_device_name(0),
)

print(
    "anchor tau:",
    ANCHOR_TAU_U,
)

print(
    "future horizon:",
    SEGMENT_TAU_U,
)

    AMPLITUDES_U.tolist(),
)
