    "derivative relative perturbation:",
    DERIVATIVE_REL_PERT_U,
)

print(
    "cases:",
    [
        x["name"]
        for x in CASES_U
    ],
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. BASIC HELPERS
# ==================================================================================================

def pair_inner_U(
    A1,
    A2,
    B1,
    B2,
):
    return (
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A1)
            *
            B1
        )
        +
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A2)
            *
            B2
        )
    ).real


def pair_norm_tensor_U(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return torch.sqrt(
        torch.clamp(
            pair_inner_U(
                A1,
                A2,
                A1,
                A2,
            ),
            min=0.0,
        )
    )


def pair_norm_U(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return float(
        pair_norm_tensor_U(
            A1,
            A2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_norm_U(
    x,
):
    return float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            x
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_cos_U(
    a,
    b,
):
    na = torch.linalg.vector_norm(
        a
    )

    nb = torch.linalg.vector_norm(
        b
    )

    naf = float(
        na.detach().cpu()
    )

    nbf = float(
        nb.detach().cpu()
    )


    if (
        naf < 1e-30
        or
        nbf < 1e-30
    ):
        return np.nan


    return float(
        (
            torch.sum(
                torch.conj(a)
                *
                b
            ).real
            /
            (
                na
                *
                nb
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. EXACT P/Q SPLIT
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def split_PQ_U(
    D1,
    D2,
    target_ids,
):
    P1 = torch.zeros_like(
        D1
    )

    P2 = torch.zeros_like(
        D2
    )

    D1f = D1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    D2f = D2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f = P1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P2f = P2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )


    P1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    P2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]


    return (
        P1,
        P2,
        D1
        -
        P1,
        D2
        -
        P2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. FULL-NS SEGMENT PROPAGATOR
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def fom_segment_U(
    U1,
    U2,
    env,
    nu,
):
    A1 = U1.clone()
    A2 = U2.clone()


    for _ in range(
        SEGMENT_STEPS_U
    ):

        A1 = full_rk4_D(
            A1,
            DT_PHYS_U,
            nu,
            env,
        )

        A2 = full_rk4_D(
            A2,
            DT_PHYS_U,
            nu,
            env,
        )


    return (
        A1,
        A2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. PROPAGATED TARGET RESPONSE FOR ARBITRARY alpha
#
#       E(alpha)
#       =
#       P[Phi(U + alpha*d) - Phi(U)]
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def actual_response_U(
    U1_base,
    U2_base,

    d1,
    d2,

    alpha,

    X0_END,

    target_ids,

    env,
    nu,
):
    A1, A2 = fom_segment_U(
        U1_base
        +
        alpha
        *
        d1,

        U2_base
        +
        alpha
        *
        d2,

        env,
        nu,
    )


    X = pack_modes_D(
        A1,
        A2,
        target_ids,
    )


    return (
        X
        -
        X0_END
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. LOG-LOG SLOPE
# ==================================================================================================

def loglog_slope_U(
    x,
    y,
):
    x = np.asarray(
        x,
        dtype=float,
    )

    y = np.asarray(
        y,
        dtype=float,
    )


    mask = (
        np.isfinite(
            x
        )
        &
        np.isfinite(
            y
        )
        &
        (
            x > 0
        )
        &
        (
            y > 0
        )
    )


    if np.sum(
        mask
    ) < 3:
        return np.nan


    lx = np.log(
        x[
            mask
        ]
    )

    ly = np.log(
        y[
            mask
        ]
    )


    slope = np.polyfit(
        lx,
        ly,
        1,
    )[
        0
    ]


    return float(
        slope
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. OUTPUT STORAGE
# ==================================================================================================

ROWS_U = []

CASE_ROWS_U = []

DERIVATIVE_ROWS_U = []


RUN_START_U = time.perf_counter()


# ==================================================================================================
# 8. CASE LOOP
# ==================================================================================================

for case in CASES_U:

    case_name_U = case[
        "name"
    ]

    topology_U = case[
        "topology"
    ]

    nu_mult_U = case[
        "nu_mult"
    ]

    kind_U = case[
        "kind"
    ]


    nu_U = (
        BASE_NU_U
        *
        nu_mult_U
    )


    print()
    print("=" * 152)
    print(
        "CASE:",
        case_name_U,
        "|",
        kind_U.upper(),
    )
    print("=" * 152)


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8A. REPRODUCE TEACHER-FREE STAR2 TO tau=1.80 IN FP32
    # ==============================================================================================

    sync_m3d_globals_MB(
        torch.float32,
        torch.complex64,
        nu_U,
        TAU0_U,
    )


    ENV32_U = make_env_D(
        40,
        L=8.0,
    )


    U10_U, U20_U = topology_pair_L2(
        topology_U,
        ENV32_U,
    )


    U1_truth_U = U10_U.clone()
    U2_truth_U = U20_U.clone()


    global_star_U, init_diag_U = initialize_star2_N(
        U10_U,
        U20_U,
        ENV32_U,
    )


    TARGET_IDS_U = global_star_U[
        "proj"
    ][
        "target_ids"
    ]


    for segment_U in range(
        1,
        ANCHOR_SEGMENTS_U
        +
        1,
    ):

        for _ in range(
            SEGMENT_STEPS_U
        ):

            U1_truth_U = full_rk4_D(
                U1_truth_U,
                DT_PHYS_U,
                nu_U,
                ENV32_U,
            )

            U2_truth_U = full_rk4_D(
                U2_truth_U,
                DT_PHYS_U,
                nu_U,
                ENV32_U,
            )


            rk4_M(
                global_star_U,
                DTAU_U,
                ENV32_U,
            )


        # Reanchor at the end of every segment through the anchor.
        (
            global_star_U,
            diag_U,
            jump_U,
            build_U,
        ) = reanchor_star2_N(
            global_star_U,
            ENV32_U,
        )


    Ug1_U, Ug2_U = reconstruct_M(
        global_star_U,
        ENV32_U,
    )


    D1_U = (
        Ug1_U
        -
        U1_truth_U
    )

    D2_U = (
        Ug2_U
        -
        U2_truth_U
    )


    (
        P1_U,
        P2_U,
        Q1_U,
        Q2_U,
    ) = split_PQ_U(
        D1_U,
        D2_U,
        TARGET_IDS_U,
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8B. MOVE BASE + ACTUAL Q ERROR TO TRUE FP64
    # ==============================================================================================

    sync_m3d_globals_MB(
        torch.float64,
        torch.complex128,
        nu_U,
        TAU0_U,
    )


    ENV64_U = make_env_D(
        40,
        L=8.0,
    )


    U1_BASE_U = U1_truth_U.to(
        torch.complex128
    )

    U2_BASE_U = U2_truth_U.to(
        torch.complex128
    )


    d1_U = Q1_U.to(
        torch.complex128
    )

    d2_U = Q2_U.to(
        torch.complex128
    )


    base_norm_U = pair_norm_U(
        U1_BASE_U,
        U2_BASE_U,
    )

    d_norm_U = pair_norm_U(
        d1_U,
        d2_U,
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8C. UNPERTURBED TRUTH ENDPOINT
    # ==============================================================================================

    U1_END_U, U2_END_U = fom_segment_U(
        U1_BASE_U,
        U2_BASE_U,
        ENV64_U,
        nu_U,
    )


    X0_END_U = pack_modes_D(
        U1_END_U,
        U2_END_U,
        TARGET_IDS_U,
    )


    truth_target_norm_U = (
        vec_norm_U(
            X0_END_U
        )
        +
        1e-30
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8D. CHOOSE DIMENSIONLESS alpha DERIVATIVE STEP
    #
    # Want:
    #
    #       ||eps_alpha*d|| / ||U|| = 1e-5
    # ==============================================================================================

    eps_alpha_U = (
        DERIVATIVE_REL_PERT_U
        *
        base_norm_U
        /
        max(
            d_norm_U,
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    # Avoid pathological values only.
    eps_alpha_U = float(
        np.clip(
            eps_alpha_U,
            1e-6,
            5e-2,
        )
    )


    actual_rel_state_pert_U = (
        eps_alpha_U
        *
        d_norm_U
        /
        base_norm_U
    )


    print(
        "  ||U||:",
        f"{base_norm_U:.8e}",
    )

    print(
        "  ||deltaQ||:",
        f"{d_norm_U:.8e}",
    )

    print(
        "  derivative eps_alpha:",
        f"{eps_alpha_U:.8e}",
    )

    print(
        "  derivative relative state perturbation:",
        f"{actual_rel_state_pert_U:.3e}",
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8E. CENTRAL FIRST + SECOND DIRECTIONAL DERIVATIVES AT eps
    # ==============================================================================================

    E_plus_U = actual_response_U(
        U1_BASE_U,
        U2_BASE_U,

        d1_U,
        d2_U,

        +
        eps_alpha_U,

        X0_END_U,
        TARGET_IDS_U,
        ENV64_U,
        nu_U,
    )


    E_minus_U = actual_response_U(
        U1_BASE_U,
        U2_BASE_U,

        d1_U,
        d2_U,

        -
        eps_alpha_U,

        X0_END_U,
        TARGET_IDS_U,
        ENV64_U,
        nu_U,
    )


    g1_U = (
        E_plus_U
        -
        E_minus_U
    ) / (
        2.0
        *
        eps_alpha_U
    )


    g2_U = (
        E_plus_U
        +
        E_minus_U
    ) / (
        eps_alpha_U ** 2
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8F. REPEAT AT eps/2
    # ==============================================================================================

    eps2_U = (
        eps_alpha_U
        /
        2.0
    )


    E_plus2_U = actual_response_U(
        U1_BASE_U,
        U2_BASE_U,

        d1_U,
        d2_U,

        +
        eps2_U,

        X0_END_U,
        TARGET_IDS_U,
        ENV64_U,
        nu_U,
    )


    E_minus2_U = actual_response_U(
        U1_BASE_U,
        U2_BASE_U,

        d1_U,
        d2_U,

        -
        eps2_U,

        X0_END_U,
        TARGET_IDS_U,
        ENV64_U,
        nu_U,
    )


    g1_half_U = (
        E_plus2_U
        -
        E_minus2_U
    ) / (
        2.0
        *
        eps2_U
    )


    g2_half_U = (
        E_plus2_U
        +
        E_minus2_U
    ) / (
        eps2_U ** 2
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8G. DERIVATIVE CONVERGENCE
    # ==============================================================================================

    g1_cos_U = vec_cos_U(
        g1_U,
        g1_half_U,
    )


    g1_rel_diff_U = (
        vec_norm_U(
            g1_U
            -
            g1_half_U
        )
        /
        max(
            vec_norm_U(
                g1_half_U
            ),
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    g2_cos_U = vec_cos_U(
        g2_U,
        g2_half_U,
    )


    g2_rel_diff_U = (
        vec_norm_U(
            g2_U
            -
            g2_half_U
        )
        /
        max(
            vec_norm_U(
                g2_half_U
            ),
            1e-30,
        )
    )


    DERIVATIVE_ROWS_U.append(
        {
            "case":
                case_name_U,

            "kind":
                kind_U,

            "eps_alpha":
                eps_alpha_U,

            "relative_state_perturbation":
                actual_rel_state_pert_U,

            "g1_cos_eps_vs_half":
                g1_cos_U,

            "g1_relative_difference":
                g1_rel_diff_U,

            "g2_cos_eps_vs_half":
                g2_cos_U,

            "g2_relative_difference":
                g2_rel_diff_U,

            "g1_norm":
                vec_norm_U(
                    g1_half_U
                ),

            "g2_norm":
                vec_norm_U(
                    g2_half_U
                ),
        }
    )


    print()

    print(
        "  g1 eps/eps2 cosine:",
        f"{g1_cos_U:.10f}",
    )

    print(
        "  g1 relative diff:",
        f"{g1_rel_diff_U:.3e}",
    )

    print(
        "  g2 eps/eps2 cosine:",
        f"{g2_cos_U:.10f}",
    )

    print(
        "  g2 relative diff:",
        f"{g2_rel_diff_U:.3e}",
    )


    # Use eps/2 derivatives as the higher-resolution estimates.
    G1_U = g1_half_U
    G2_U = g2_half_U


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8H. FINITE-AMPLITUDE SWEEP
    # ==============================================================================================

    case_alpha_rows_U = []


    print()
    print(
        "  AMPLITUDE SWEEP"
    )


    for alpha_U in AMPLITUDES_U:

        E_actual_U = actual_response_U(
            U1_BASE_U,
            U2_BASE_U,

            d1_U,
            d2_U,

            float(
                alpha_U
            ),

            X0_END_U,
            TARGET_IDS_U,
            ENV64_U,
            nu_U,
        )


        E1_U = (
            float(
                alpha_U
            )
            *
            G1_U
        )


        E2_U = (
            float(
                alpha_U
            )
            *
            G1_U
            +
            0.5
            *
            (
                float(
                    alpha_U
                ) ** 2
            )
            *
            G2_U
        )


        actual_norm_U = vec_norm_U(
            E_actual_U
        )


        first_resid_U = (
            vec_norm_U(
                E_actual_U
                -
                E1_U
            )
            /
            max(
                actual_norm_U,
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        second_resid_U = (
            vec_norm_U(
                E_actual_U
                -
                E2_U
            )
            /
            max(
                actual_norm_U,
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        first_cos_U = vec_cos_U(
            E1_U,
            E_actual_U,
        )


        second_cos_U = vec_cos_U(
            E2_U,
            E_actual_U,
        )


        first_norm_ratio_U = (
            vec_norm_U(
                E1_U
            )
            /
            max(
                actual_norm_U,
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        second_norm_ratio_U = (
            vec_norm_U(
                E2_U
            )
            /
            max(
                actual_norm_U,
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        improvement_U = (
            first_resid_U
            /
            max(
                second_resid_U,
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        row_U = {
            "case":
                case_name_U,

            "topology":
                topology_U,

            "nu_mult":
                nu_mult_U,

            "kind":
                kind_U,

            "tau":
                ANCHOR_TAU_U,

            "alpha":
                float(
                    alpha_U
                ),

            "actual_response_rel":
                actual_norm_U
                /
                truth_target_norm_U,

            "first_order_residual":
                first_resid_U,

            "second_order_residual":
                second_resid_U,

            "first_order_cosine":
                first_cos_U,

            "second_order_cosine":
                second_cos_U,

            "first_order_norm_ratio":
                first_norm_ratio_U,

            "second_order_norm_ratio":
                second_norm_ratio_U,

            "quadratic_improvement_factor":
                improvement_U,
        }


        ROWS_U.append(
            row_U
        )

        case_alpha_rows_U.append(
            row_U
        )


        print(
            f"    alpha={alpha_U:5.3f}"
            f" | E={100*actual_norm_U/truth_target_norm_U:9.5f}%"
            f" | r1={first_resid_U:8.5f}"
            f" | r2={second_resid_U:8.5f}"
            f" | gain={improvement_U:7.3f}x"
        )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # 8I. CASE-LEVEL SCALING TEST
    #
    # If quadratic term dominates first-order residual:
    #
    #       relative first-order residual ~ alpha^1
    #
    # If cubic term dominates second-order residual:
    #
    #       relative second-order residual ~ alpha^2
    #
    # because denominator itself is O(alpha).
    # ==============================================================================================

    case_df_U = pd.DataFrame(
        case_alpha_rows_U
    )


    slope_r1_U = loglog_slope_U(
        case_df_U[
            "alpha"
        ],

        case_df_U[
            "first_order_residual"
        ],
    )


    slope_r2_U = loglog_slope_U(
        case_df_U[
            "alpha"
        ],

        case_df_U[
            "second_order_residual"
        ],
    )


    alpha1_U = case_df_U[
        np.isclose(
            case_df_U[
                "alpha"
            ],
            1.0,
        )
    ].iloc[
        0
    ]


    CASE_ROWS_U.append(
        {
            "case":
                case_name_U,

            "topology":
                topology_U,

            "nu_mult":
                nu_mult_U,

            "kind":
                kind_U,

            "first_residual_alpha1":
                float(
                    alpha1_U[
                        "first_order_residual"
                    ]
                ),

            "second_residual_alpha1":
                float(
                    alpha1_U[
                        "second_order_residual"
                    ]
                ),

            "quadratic_gain_alpha1":
                float(
                    alpha1_U[
                        "quadratic_improvement_factor"
                    ]
                ),

            "first_cos_alpha1":
                float(
                    alpha1_U[
                        "first_order_cosine"
                    ]
                ),

            "second_cos_alpha1":
                float(
                    alpha1_U[
                        "second_order_cosine"
                    ]
                ),

            "first_norm_ratio_alpha1":
                float(
                    alpha1_U[
                        "first_order_norm_ratio"
                    ]
                ),

            "second_norm_ratio_alpha1":
                float(
                    alpha1_U[
                        "second_order_norm_ratio"
                    ]
                ),

            "first_residual_scaling_slope":
                slope_r1_U,

            "second_residual_scaling_slope":
                slope_r2_U,

            "g1_convergence_cosine":
                g1_cos_U,

            "g1_convergence_residual":
                g1_rel_diff_U,

            "g2_convergence_cosine":
                g2_cos_U,

            "g2_convergence_residual":
                g2_rel_diff_U,
        }
    )


    print()

    print(
        "  first-order residual scaling slope:",
        f"{slope_r1_U:.5f}",
    )

    print(
        "  second-order residual scaling slope:",
        f"{slope_r2_U:.5f}",
    )

    print(
        "  alpha=1 first residual:",
        f"{float(alpha1_U['first_order_residual']):.6f}",
    )

    print(
        "  alpha=1 second residual:",
        f"{float(alpha1_U['second_order_residual']):.6f}",
    )

    print(
        "  alpha=1 quadratic improvement:",
        f"{float(alpha1_U['quadratic_improvement_factor']):.4f}x",
    )


    # ==============================================================================================
    # CHECKPOINT AFTER EACH CASE
    # ==============================================================================================

    pd.DataFrame(
        ROWS_U
    ).to_csv(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_U_amplitude_partial.csv",
        index=False,
    )


    pd.DataFrame(
        CASE_ROWS_U
    ).to_csv(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_U_cases_partial.csv",
        index=False,
    )


    pd.DataFrame(
        DERIVATIVE_ROWS_U
    ).to_csv(
        SAVE_DIR
        /
        "m3d_U_derivatives_partial.csv",
        index=False,
    )


    gc.collect()
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. FINAL DATAFRAMES
# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_U = pd.DataFrame(
    ROWS_U
)

CASES_RESULT_U = pd.DataFrame(
    CASE_ROWS_U
)

DERIVATIVES_U = pd.DataFrame(
    DERIVATIVE_ROWS_U
)


RESULTS_U.to_csv(

    /
    "m3d_U_amplitude_sweep.csv",

    /

    index=False,
)

    CASES_RESULT_U[
        "kind"
    ]
    ==
    "hard"
].copy()


CONTROL_U = CASES_RESULT_U[
    CASES_RESULT_U[
        "kind"
    ]
    ==
    "control"
].copy()


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. SUMMARY METRICS
# ==================================================================================================

HARD_MEDIAN_R1_U = float(
    HARD_U[
        "first_residual_alpha1"
    ].median()
)


HARD_MEDIAN_R2_U = float(
    HARD_U[
        "second_residual_alpha1"
    ].median()
)


HARD_MEDIAN_GAIN_U = float(
    HARD_U[
        "quadratic_gain_alpha1"
    ].median()
)


HARD_MIN_GAIN_U = float(
    HARD_U[
        "quadratic_gain_alpha1"
    ].min()
)


HARD_MEDIAN_R1_SLOPE_U = float(
    HARD_U[
        "first_residual_scaling_slope"
    ].median()
)


HARD_MEDIAN_R2_SLOPE_U = float(
    HARD_U[
        "second_residual_scaling_slope"
    ].median()
)


CONTROL_MEDIAN_R1_U = float(
    CONTROL_U[
        "first_residual_alpha1"
    ].median()
)


CONTROL_MEDIAN_R2_U = float(
    CONTROL_U[
        "second_residual_alpha1"
    ].median()
)


MAX_G1_DIFF_U = float(
    DERIVATIVES_U[
        "g1_relative_difference"
    ].max()
)


MIN_G1_COS_U = float(
    DERIVATIVES_U[
        "g1_cos_eps_vs_half"
    ].min()
)


MAX_G2_DIFF_U = float(
    DERIVATIVES_U[
        "g2_relative_difference"
    ].max()
)


MIN_G2_COS_U = float(
    DERIVATIVES_U[
        "g2_cos_eps_vs_half"
    ].min()
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 12. GATES
#
# Diagnostic preregistered thresholds.
#
# We are testing a mechanism, not optimizing a benchmark.
# ==================================================================================================

FIRST_DERIVATIVE_GATE_U = bool(
    MIN_G1_COS_U
    >
    0.999
    and
    MAX_G1_DIFF_U
    <
    0.02
)


SECOND_DERIVATIVE_GATE_U = bool(
    MIN_G2_COS_U
    >
    0.99
    and
    MAX_G2_DIFF_U
    <
    0.10
)


NUMERICS_GATE_U = bool(
    FIRST_DERIVATIVE_GATE_U
    and
    SECOND_DERIVATIVE_GATE_U
)


# Expected if omitted term is primarily quadratic:
#
# relative first-order residual ~ alpha^1

QUADRATIC_SCALING_GATE_U = bool(
    0.60
    <=
    HARD_MEDIAN_R1_SLOPE_U
    <=
    1.40
)


# Major practical question:
#
# Does second-order term materially repair hard cases?

SECOND_ORDER_REPAIR_GATE_U = bool(
    HARD_MEDIAN_GAIN_U
    >=
    2.0
    and
    HARD_MIN_GAIN_U
    >=
    1.25
    and
    HARD_MEDIAN_R2_U
    <=
    0.15
)


# Controls should not be broken by quadratic correction.

CONTROL_SAFETY_GATE_U = bool(
    CONTROL_MEDIAN_R2_U
    <=
    CONTROL_MEDIAN_R1_U
    +
    0.02
)


FINITE_AMPLITUDE_QUADRATIC_LAW_U = bool(
    NUMERICS_GATE_U
    and
    QUADRATIC_SCALING_GATE_U
    and
    SECOND_ORDER_REPAIR_GATE_U
    and
    CONTROL_SAFETY_GATE_U
)

if FINITE_AMPLITUDE_QUADRATIC_LAW_U:

    print(
        "🔥🔥🔥 TEST-U HYPOTHESIS SURVIVED."
    )

    print()

    print(

    print(

    )

    print()

    print(

    )

    print(

    print(

    )

    print()

    print(

    )

    print(
        "Cross-case falsification of the second-order leakage law."
    )


elif NUMERICS_GATE_U:

    print(

    )

    print(

    print()

    )

    print(
        "or direction rotation dominate."
    )


else:

    print(

    print()

#    periodic_shear__nu1 control             0.17714254            0.0030438259              58.197328        0.98726888         0.99999542

# quadratic residual-scaling gate: True

# control safety gate: True

# FINITE-AMPLITUDE QUADRATIC LAW: True

# 🔥🔥🔥 TEST-U HYPOTHESIS SURVIVED.
# 
# The difficult Test-T regimes retain the same low-dimensional
# hidden leakage structure, but require a SECOND-ORDER
# finite-amplitude transfer law.

# The first-order error scales consistently with an omitted

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 22
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST V — 12-CASE CROSS-FLOW FALSIFICATION OF THE QUADRATIC LEAKAGE LAW
#
# 4 topologies x 3 viscosities x 11 anchors = 132 full-amplitude tests
#
#
# TEST-U HYPOTHESIS TO FALSIFY
# ----------------------------
#
# For actual hidden error d = delta_Q:
#
#       E(d)
#       =
#       P[Phi_h(U+d) - Phi_h(U)]
#
# is approximated by
#
#       E1 = g1
#
# and, more accurately,
#
#       E2 = g1 + 1/2 g2
#
# where
#
#       g1 = D(P Phi_h)[d]
#
#       g2 = D^2(P Phi_h)[d,d].
#
#
# PRIMARY QUESTION
# ----------------
#
# Does the large improvement seen in Test U survive:
#
#       - all 4 topologies
#       - nu multipliers .25 / 1 / 4
#       - every tau=.30,...,1.80 anchor
#
#
# TRAJECTORY:
#       fp32 / complex64
#
# LEAKAGE LAW:
#       fp64 / complex128
#
# derivative perturbation:
#       ||eps*d|| / ||U|| = 1e-5
#
#
# NUMERICAL FALSIFICATION
# -----------------------
#
# At the WORST first-order anchor from every case,
# recompute g1/g2 using eps/2.
#
#
# CHECKPOINTING
# -------------
#
# Saves after every complete case.
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch


# ==================================================================================================

# ==================================================================================================

    "make_env_D",
    "topology_pair_L2",

    "reanchor_star2_N",
    "reconstruct_M",
    "rk4_M",

    "pack_modes_D",
    "sync_m3d_globals_MB",

    x
    for x in REQUIRED_V
    if x not in globals()
]

if missing_V:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Missing Test-V dependencies:\n"
        + repr(missing_V)
        + "\nRun corrected Test N definitions first."
    )

if not torch.cuda.is_available():
    raise RuntimeError("CUDA GPU required.")

if "SAVE_DIR" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError("SAVE_DIR missing.")

SAVE_DIR = Path(SAVE_DIR)
SAVE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


# ==================================================================================================
# 1. FROZEN SETTINGS
# ==================================================================================================

TOPOLOGIES_V = [
    "vortex_ring",
    "periodic_shear",
    "skew_tubes",
    "mixed_vortices",
]

NU_MULTS_V = [
    0.25,
    1.0,
    4.0,
]

BASE_NU_V = 2.5e-3

TAU0_V = 40.0 / 3.0

DT_PHYS_V = 0.01

DTAU_V = DT_PHYS_V / TAU0_V

SEGMENT_TAU_V = 0.15

SEGMENT_STEPS_V = int(
    round(
        SEGMENT_TAU_V
        /
        DTAU_V
    )
)

COLLECTION_LAST_TAU_V = 1.95

N_COLLECTION_V = int(
    round(
        COLLECTION_LAST_TAU_V
        /
        SEGMENT_TAU_V
    )
)

MIN_ANALYSIS_TAU_V = 0.30
MAX_ANALYSIS_TAU_V = 1.80

DERIV_REL_V = 1e-5

RESUME_V = True


POINTS_PARTIAL_V = (
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_V_points_partial.csv"
)

CHECKS_PARTIAL_V = (
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_V_derivative_checks_partial.csv"
)


print("=" * 156)
print("M3D TEST V — CROSS-FLOW QUADRATIC LEAKAGE LAW")
print("=" * 156)

print()
print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0))
print("cases:", len(TOPOLOGIES_V) * len(NU_MULTS_V))
print("anchors per case: 11")
print("expected tests: 132")
print("finite-time horizon:", SEGMENT_TAU_V)
print("derivative state perturbation:", DERIV_REL_V)
print("resume:", RESUME_V)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. HELPERS
# ==================================================================================================

def pair_inner_V(A1, A2, B1, B2):

    return (
        torch.sum(torch.conj(A1) * B1)
        +
        torch.sum(torch.conj(A2) * B2)
    ).real


def pair_norm_tensor_V(A1, A2):

    return torch.sqrt(
        torch.clamp(
            pair_inner_V(
                A1,
                A2,
                A1,
                A2,
            ),
            min=0.0,
        )
    )


def pair_norm_V(A1, A2):

    return float(
        pair_norm_tensor_V(
            A1,
            A2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_norm_V(x):

    return float(
        torch.linalg.vector_norm(
            x
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def vec_cos_V(a, b):

    na = torch.linalg.vector_norm(a)
    nb = torch.linalg.vector_norm(b)

    naf = float(na.detach().cpu())
    nbf = float(nb.detach().cpu())

    if naf < 1e-30 or nbf < 1e-30:
        return np.nan

    return float(
        (
            torch.sum(
                torch.conj(a)
                *
                b
            ).real
            /
            (
                na
                *
                nb
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. P/Q SPLIT
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def split_PQ_V(
    D1,
    D2,
    target_ids,
):

    P1 = torch.zeros_like(D1)
    P2 = torch.zeros_like(D2)

    D1f = D1.reshape(3, -1)
    D2f = D2.reshape(3, -1)

    P1f = P1.reshape(3, -1)
    P2f = P2.reshape(3, -1)

    P1f[:, target_ids] = D1f[:, target_ids]
    P2f[:, target_ids] = D2f[:, target_ids]

    return (
        P1,
        P2,
        D1 - P1,
        D2 - P2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. FULL-ORDER SEGMENT
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def segment_V(
    U1,
    U2,
    env,
    nu,
):

    A1 = U1.clone()
    A2 = U2.clone()

    for _ in range(SEGMENT_STEPS_V):

        A1 = full_rk4_D(
            A1,
            DT_PHYS_V,
            nu,
            env,
        )

        A2 = full_rk4_D(
            A2,
            DT_PHYS_V,
            nu,
            env,
        )

    return A1, A2


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. RESPONSE E(alpha)
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def response_V(
    U1,
    U2,

    d1,
    d2,

    alpha,

    x0_end,
    target_ids,

    env,
    nu,
):

    A1, A2 = segment_V(
        U1
        +
        alpha
        *
        d1,

        U2
        +
        alpha
        *
        d2,

        env,
        nu,
    )

    x = pack_modes_D(
        A1,
        A2,
        target_ids,
    )

    return (
        x
        -
        x0_end
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. CHECKPOINT RECOVERY
# ==================================================================================================

if RESUME_V and POINTS_PARTIAL_V.exists():

    ROWS_V = (
        pd.read_csv(
            POINTS_PARTIAL_V
        )
        .to_dict(
            "records"
        )
    )

else:

    ROWS_V = []


if RESUME_V and CHECKS_PARTIAL_V.exists():

    CHECK_ROWS_V = (
        pd.read_csv(
            CHECKS_PARTIAL_V
        )
        .to_dict(
            "records"
        )
    )

else:

    CHECK_ROWS_V = []


COMPLETED_V = set(
    str(x["case"])
    for x in CHECK_ROWS_V
)


print()
print(
    "recovered complete cases:",
    sorted(COMPLETED_V),
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 7. CASE LOOP
# ==================================================================================================

RUN_START_V = time.perf_counter()


for topology_V in TOPOLOGIES_V:

    for nu_mult_V in NU_MULTS_V:

        nu_V = (
            BASE_NU_V
            *
            nu_mult_V
        )

        case_V = (
            f"{topology_V}"
            f"__nu{nu_mult_V:g}"
        )


        if case_V in COMPLETED_V:

            print()
            print(
                "SKIP:",
                case_V,
            )

            continue


        print()
        print("=" * 156)
        print("CASE:", case_V)
        print("=" * 156)


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 7A. FP32 TEACHER-FREE TRAJECTORY
        # ==========================================================================================

        sync_m3d_globals_MB(
            torch.float32,
            torch.complex64,
            nu_V,
            TAU0_V,
        )


        ENV32_V = make_env_D(
            40,
            L=8.0,
        )


        U10_V, U20_V = topology_pair_L2(
            topology_V,
            ENV32_V,
        )


        U1_truth_V = U10_V.clone()
        U2_truth_V = U20_V.clone()


        model_V, init_diag_V = initialize_star2_N(
            U10_V,
            U20_V,
            ENV32_V,
        )


        target_ids_V = model_V[
            "proj"
        ][
            "target_ids"
        ]


        TRAJ_V = []


        print(
            "  initial RAf:",
            f"{init_diag_V['RAf_ratio']:.3e}",
        )


        for seg_V in range(
            1,
            N_COLLECTION_V + 1,
        ):

            for _ in range(
                SEGMENT_STEPS_V
            ):

                U1_truth_V = full_rk4_D(
                    U1_truth_V,
                    DT_PHYS_V,
                    nu_V,
                    ENV32_V,
                )

                U2_truth_V = full_rk4_D(
                    U2_truth_V,
                    DT_PHYS_V,
                    nu_V,
                    ENV32_V,
                )


                rk4_M(
                    model_V,
                    DTAU_V,
                    ENV32_V,
                )


            tau_V = (
                seg_V
                *
                SEGMENT_TAU_V
            )


            if seg_V < N_COLLECTION_V:

                (
                    model_V,
                    diag_V,
                    jump_V,
                    build_V,
                ) = reanchor_star2_N(
                    model_V,
                    ENV32_V,
                )


            Ug1_V, Ug2_V = reconstruct_M(
                model_V,
                ENV32_V,
            )


            D1_V = (
                Ug1_V
                -
                U1_truth_V
            )

            D2_V = (
                Ug2_V
                -
                U2_truth_V
            )


            (
                PD1_V,
                PD2_V,
                QD1_V,
                QD2_V,
            ) = split_PQ_V(
                D1_V,
                D2_V,
                target_ids_V,
            )


            x_truth_V = pack_modes_D(
                U1_truth_V,
                U2_truth_V,
                target_ids_V,
            )


            x_global_V = pack_modes_D(
                Ug1_V,
                Ug2_V,
                target_ids_V,
            )


            P_rel_V = (
                vec_norm_V(
                    x_global_V
                    -
                    x_truth_V
                )
                /
                (
                    vec_norm_V(
                        x_truth_V
                    )
                    +
                    1e-30
                )
            )


            TRAJ_V.append(
                {
                    "tau":
                        tau_V,

                    "truth1":
                        U1_truth_V.clone(),

                    "truth2":
                        U2_truth_V.clone(),

                    "Q1":
                        QD1_V.clone(),

                    "Q2":
                        QD2_V.clone(),

                    "P_rel":
                        P_rel_V,
                }
            )


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 7B. SWITCH TO TRUE FP64
        # ==========================================================================================

        sync_m3d_globals_MB(
            torch.float64,
            torch.complex128,
            nu_V,
            TAU0_V,
        )


        ENV64_V = make_env_D(
            40,
            L=8.0,
        )


        worst_record_V = None


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 7C. EVERY ANALYZED ANCHOR
        # ==========================================================================================

        for idx_V in range(
            len(TRAJ_V)
            -
            1
        ):

            point_V = TRAJ_V[
                idx_V
            ]

            next_V = TRAJ_V[
                idx_V
                +
                1
            ]


            tau_V = float(
                point_V[
                    "tau"
                ]
            )


            if tau_V < MIN_ANALYSIS_TAU_V - 1e-12:
                continue

            if tau_V > MAX_ANALYSIS_TAU_V + 1e-12:
                continue


            U1_V = point_V[
                "truth1"
            ].to(
                torch.complex128
            )

            U2_V = point_V[
                "truth2"
            ].to(
                torch.complex128
            )


            d1_V = point_V[
                "Q1"
            ].to(
                torch.complex128
            )

            d2_V = point_V[
                "Q2"
            ].to(
                torch.complex128
            )


            base_norm_V = pair_norm_V(
                U1_V,
                U2_V,
            )

            d_norm_V = pair_norm_V(
                d1_V,
                d2_V,
            )


            eps_V = (
                DERIV_REL_V
                *
                base_norm_V
                /
                max(
                    d_norm_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            eps_V = float(
                np.clip(
                    eps_V,
                    1e-6,
                    5e-2,
                )
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Baseline endpoint.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            U1_end_V, U2_end_V = segment_V(
                U1_V,
                U2_V,
                ENV64_V,
                nu_V,
            )


            x0_end_V = pack_modes_D(
                U1_end_V,
                U2_end_V,
                target_ids_V,
            )


            target_norm_V = (
                vec_norm_V(
                    x0_end_V
                )
                +
                1e-30
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Central derivatives.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            Ep_V = response_V(
                U1_V,
                U2_V,
                d1_V,
                d2_V,
                +eps_V,
                x0_end_V,
                target_ids_V,
                ENV64_V,
                nu_V,
            )


            Em_V = response_V(
                U1_V,
                U2_V,
                d1_V,
                d2_V,
                -eps_V,
                x0_end_V,
                target_ids_V,
                ENV64_V,
                nu_V,
            )


            g1_V = (
                Ep_V
                -
                Em_V
            ) / (
                2.0
                *
                eps_V
            )


            g2_V = (
                Ep_V
                +
                Em_V
            ) / (
                eps_V ** 2
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Actual alpha=1 nonlinear Q-only response.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            Eactual_V = response_V(
                U1_V,
                U2_V,
                d1_V,
                d2_V,
                1.0,
                x0_end_V,
                target_ids_V,
                ENV64_V,
                nu_V,
            )


            E1_V = g1_V


            E2_V = (
                g1_V
                +
                0.5
                *
                g2_V
            )


            actual_norm_V = vec_norm_V(
                Eactual_V
            )


            r1_V = (
                vec_norm_V(
                    Eactual_V
                    -
                    E1_V
                )
                /
                max(
                    actual_norm_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            r2_V = (
                vec_norm_V(
                    Eactual_V
                    -
                    E2_V
                )
                /
                max(
                    actual_norm_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            cos1_V = vec_cos_V(
                E1_V,
                Eactual_V,
            )


            cos2_V = vec_cos_V(
                E2_V,
                Eactual_V,
            )


            norm1_V = (
                vec_norm_V(
                    E1_V
                )
                /
                max(
                    actual_norm_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            norm2_V = (
                vec_norm_V(
                    E2_V
                )
                /
                max(
                    actual_norm_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            gain_V = (
                r1_V
                /
                max(
                    r2_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            risk1_rel_V = (
                vec_norm_V(
                    E1_V
                )
                /
                target_norm_V
            )


            risk2_rel_V = (
                vec_norm_V(
                    E2_V
                )
                /
                target_norm_V
            )


            actual_rel_V = (
                actual_norm_V
                /
                target_norm_V
            )


            current_P_V = float(
                point_V[
                    "P_rel"
                ]
            )


            next_P_V = float(
                next_V[
                    "P_rel"
                ]
            )


            warning1_V = (
                risk1_rel_V
                /
                max(
                    current_P_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            warning2_V = (
                risk2_rel_V
                /
                max(
                    current_P_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            log_growth_V = math.log(
                max(
                    next_P_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
                /
                max(
                    current_P_V,
                    1e-30,
                )
            )


            ROWS_V.append(
                {
                    "case":
                        case_V,

                    "topology":
                        topology_V,

                    "nu_mult":
                        nu_mult_V,

                    "nu":
                        nu_V,

                    "tau":
                        tau_V,

                    "tau_end":
                        tau_V
                        +
                        SEGMENT_TAU_V,

                    "eps_alpha":
                        eps_V,

                    "Q_norm":
                        d_norm_V,

                    "first_residual":
                        r1_V,

                    "second_residual":
                        r2_V,

                    "improvement_factor":
                        gain_V,

                    "first_cosine":
                        cos1_V,

                    "second_cosine":
                        cos2_V,

                    "first_norm_ratio":
                        norm1_V,

                    "second_norm_ratio":
                        norm2_V,

                    "actual_Q_to_P_rel":
                        actual_rel_V,

                    "first_risk_rel":
                        risk1_rel_V,

                    "second_risk_rel":
                        risk2_rel_V,

                    "current_P_rel":
                        current_P_V,

                    "next_P_rel":
                        next_P_V,

                    "log_growth":
                        log_growth_V,

                    "warning1":
                        warning1_V,

                    "warning2":
                        warning2_V,
                }
            )


            print(
                f"  tau={tau_V:4.2f}"
                f" | r1={r1_V:7.4f}"
                f" | r2={r2_V:7.4f}"
                f" | gain={gain_V:7.2f}x"
                f" | cos2={cos2_V:.6f}"
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Save worst FIRST-order point of case for independent derivative refinement.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            if (
                worst_record_V is None
                or
                r1_V
                >
                worst_record_V[
                    "r1"
                ]
            ):

                worst_record_V = {
                    "tau":
                        tau_V,

                    "r1":
                        r1_V,

                    "U1":
                        U1_V.clone(),

                    "U2":
                        U2_V.clone(),

                    "d1":
                        d1_V.clone(),

                    "d2":
                        d2_V.clone(),

                    "eps":
                        eps_V,

                    "x0":
                        x0_end_V.clone(),

                    "g1":
                        g1_V.clone(),

                    "g2":
                        g2_V.clone(),
                }


            gc.collect()
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 7D. eps/2 CHECK AT WORST FIRST-ORDER ANCHOR
        # ==========================================================================================

        assert worst_record_V is not None


        eps2_V = (
            worst_record_V[
                "eps"
            ]
            /
            2.0
        )


        Ep2_V = response_V(
            worst_record_V["U1"],
            worst_record_V["U2"],
            worst_record_V["d1"],
            worst_record_V["d2"],
            +eps2_V,
            worst_record_V["x0"],
            target_ids_V,
            ENV64_V,
            nu_V,
        )


        Em2_V = response_V(
            worst_record_V["U1"],
            worst_record_V["U2"],
            worst_record_V["d1"],
            worst_record_V["d2"],
            -eps2_V,
            worst_record_V["x0"],
            target_ids_V,
            ENV64_V,
            nu_V,
        )


        g1half_V = (
            Ep2_V
            -
            Em2_V
        ) / (
            2.0
            *
            eps2_V
        )


        g2half_V = (
            Ep2_V
            +
            Em2_V
        ) / (
            eps2_V ** 2
        )


        g1_cos_check_V = vec_cos_V(
            worst_record_V[
                "g1"
            ],
            g1half_V,
        )


        g1_diff_check_V = (
            vec_norm_V(
                worst_record_V[
                    "g1"
                ]
                -
                g1half_V
            )
            /
            max(
                vec_norm_V(
                    g1half_V
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        g2_cos_check_V = vec_cos_V(
            worst_record_V[
                "g2"
            ],
            g2half_V,
        )


        g2_diff_check_V = (
            vec_norm_V(
                worst_record_V[
                    "g2"
                ]
                -
                g2half_V
            )
            /
            max(
                vec_norm_V(
                    g2half_V
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        CHECK_ROWS_V.append(
            {
                "case":
                    case_V,

                "topology":
                    topology_V,

                "nu_mult":
                    nu_mult_V,

                "worst_first_order_tau":
                    worst_record_V[
                        "tau"
                    ],

                "worst_first_order_residual":
                    worst_record_V[
                        "r1"
                    ],

                "g1_eps_half_cosine":
                    g1_cos_check_V,

                "g1_eps_half_difference":
                    g1_diff_check_V,

                "g2_eps_half_cosine":
                    g2_cos_check_V,

                "g2_eps_half_difference":
                    g2_diff_check_V,
            }
        )


        print()

        print(
            "  derivative check @ tau",
            worst_record_V[
                "tau"
            ],
        )

        print(
            "    g1 cosine:",
            f"{g1_cos_check_V:.10f}",
        )

        print(
            "    g1 relative diff:",
            f"{g1_diff_check_V:.3e}",
        )

        print(
            "    g2 cosine:",
            f"{g2_cos_check_V:.10f}",
        )

        print(
            "    g2 relative diff:",
            f"{g2_diff_check_V:.3e}",
        )


        # ==========================================================================================
        # CHECKPOINT
        # ==========================================================================================

        pd.DataFrame(
            ROWS_V
        ).to_csv(
            POINTS_PARTIAL_V,
            index=False,
        )


        pd.DataFrame(
            CHECK_ROWS_V
        ).to_csv(
            CHECKS_PARTIAL_V,
            index=False,
        )


        COMPLETED_V.add(
            case_V
        )


        del TRAJ_V
        del model_V

        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()


# ==================================================================================================

# ==================================================================================================

# ==================================================================================================
# 11. SECOND-ORDER WARNING — SECONDARY, NOT PRIMARY TEST-V CLAIM
#
# Compare rank correlation of:
#
#       W1 = ||E1|| / current P error
#
#       W2 = ||E2|| / current P error
#
# with next-segment LOG_GROWTH.
# ==================================================================================================

def spearman_V(
    a,
    b,
):

    a = pd.Series(
        np.asarray(
            a,
            dtype=float,
        )
    )

    b = pd.Series(
        np.asarray(
            b,
            dtype=float,
        )
    )

    mask = (
        np.isfinite(a)
        &
        np.isfinite(b)
    )

    a = a[
        mask
    ].rank(
        method="average"
    )

    b = b[
        mask
    ].rank(
        method="average"
    )


    if len(a) < 3:
        return np.nan


    return float(
        np.corrcoef(
            a.to_numpy(),
            b.to_numpy(),
        )[
            0,
            1
        ]
    )


CORR_ROWS_V = []


for case_name_V, df_V in RESULTS_V.groupby(
    "case"
):

    rho1_V = spearman_V(
        df_V[
            "warning1"
        ],
        df_V[
            "log_growth"
        ],
    )

    rho2_V = spearman_V(
        df_V[
            "warning2"
        ],
        df_V[
            "log_growth"
        ],
    )


    CORR_ROWS_V.append(
        {
            "case":
                case_name_V,

            "warning1_rho":
                rho1_V,

            "warning2_rho":
                rho2_V,
        }
    )


CORRS_V = pd.DataFrame(
    CORR_ROWS_V
)


MEDIAN_W1_RHO_V = float(
    CORRS_V[
        "warning1_rho"
    ].median()
)


MEDIAN_W2_RHO_V = float(
    CORRS_V[
        "warning2_rho"
    ].median()
)

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 23
# ==========================================================================
# ==================================================================================================
# M3D TEST W — TRUTH-FREE ACCUMULATED HIDDEN-ERROR ESTIMATION
#
# DEFECT-DRIVEN ERROR TRANSPORT
# FIRST-ORDER vs SECOND-ORDER
#
#
# MODEL:
#
#   V_{k+1} = M_k(V_k)
#
# FULL FLOW MAP:
#
#   Phi_h
#
# TRUE ERROR:
#
#   e_k = U_k - V_k
#
#
# TRUTH-FREE LOCAL DEFECT:
#
#   eta_k = Phi_h(V_k) - V_{k+1}
#
#
# FIRST-ORDER ERROR ESTIMATOR:
#
#   ehat1_{k+1}
#       =
#       eta_k
#       +
#       D Phi_h(V_k) ehat1_k
#
#
# SECOND-ORDER ERROR ESTIMATOR:
#
#   ehat2_{k+1}
#       =
#       eta_k
#       +
#       D Phi_h(V_k) ehat2_k
#       +
#       1/2 D^2 Phi_h(V_k)[ehat2_k,ehat2_k]
#
#
# CRITICAL:
# ---------
#
# The recurrence NEVER accesses truth.
#
# Truth trajectories are generated separately and used ONLY for scoring.
#
#
# 12 CASES:
#
#   4 topologies x 3 viscosities
#
# through tau=1.80.
#
#
# This is a proof-of-principle truth-free estimator.
# It is NOT YET claimed to be computationally cheap.
# ==================================================================================================

import gc
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch

    "vortex_ring",
    "periodic_shear",
    "skew_tubes",
    "mixed_vortices",
]

    0.25,
    1.0,
    4.0,
]

BASE_NU_W = 2.5e-3

TAU0_W = 40.0 / 3.0

DT_PHYS_W = 0.01

DTAU_W = (
    DT_PHYS_W
    /
    TAU0_W
)

SEGMENT_TAU_W = 0.15

SEGMENT_STEPS_W = int(
    round(
        SEGMENT_TAU_W
        /
        DTAU_W
    )
)

FINAL_TAU_W = 1.80

N_SEGMENTS_W = int(
    round(
        FINAL_TAU_W
        /
        SEGMENT_TAU_W
    )
)

DERIV_REL_W = 1e-5

RESUME_W = True

    SAVE_DIR
    /

print("resume:", RESUME_W)


# ==================================================================================================
# 2. HELPERS
# ==================================================================================================

def pair_inner_W(
    A1,
    A2,
    B1,
    B2,
):
    return (
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A1)
            *
            B1
        )
        +
        torch.sum(
            torch.conj(A2)
            *
            B2
        )
    ).real


def pair_norm_tensor_W(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return torch.sqrt(
        torch.clamp(
            pair_inner_W(
                A1,
                A2,
                A1,
                A2,
            ),
            min=0.0,
        )
    )


def pair_norm_W(
    A1,
    A2,
):
    return float(
        pair_norm_tensor_W(
            A1,
            A2,
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


def pair_cos_W(
    A1,
    A2,
    B1,
    B2,
):

    na = pair_norm_tensor_W(
        A1,
        A2,
    )

    nb = pair_norm_tensor_W(
        B1,
        B2,
    )

    naf = float(
        na.detach().cpu()
    )

    nbf = float(
        nb.detach().cpu()
    )

    if (
        naf < 1e-30
        or
        nbf < 1e-30
    ):
        return np.nan

    return float(
        (
            pair_inner_W(
                A1,
                A2,
                B1,
                B2,
            )
            /
            (
                na
                *
                nb
            )
        ).detach().cpu()
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 3. EXACT P / Q SPLIT
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def split_PQ_W(
    D1,
    D2,
    target_ids,
):

    P1 = torch.zeros_like(
        D1
    )

    P2 = torch.zeros_like(
        D2
    )

    D1f = D1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    D2f = D2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f = P1.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P2f = P2.reshape(
        3,
        -1,
    )

    P1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D1f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    P2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ] = D2f[
        :,
        target_ids
    ]

    return (
        P1,
        P2,
        D1 - P1,
        D2 - P2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 4. FULL FLOW MAP Phi_h
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def phi_W(
    U1,
    U2,
    env,
    nu,
):

    A1 = U1.clone()
    A2 = U2.clone()

    for _ in range(
        SEGMENT_STEPS_W
    ):

        A1 = full_rk4_D(
            A1,
            DT_PHYS_W,
            nu,
            env,
        )

        A2 = full_rk4_D(
            A2,
            DT_PHYS_W,
            nu,
            env,
        )

    return (
        A1,
        A2,
    )


# ==================================================================================================
# 5. MAP DERIVATIVES ALONG ONE ERROR VECTOR
#
# Returns:
#
#       J[e]
#       H[e,e]
#
# around base state V.
# ==================================================================================================

@torch.no_grad()
def map_jet_W(
    V1,
    V2,

    e1,
    e2,

    base_phi1,
    base_phi2,

    env,
    nu,

    rel_eps=DERIV_REL_W,
):

    vnorm = pair_norm_W(
        V1,
        V2,
    )

    enorm = pair_norm_W(
        e1,
        e2,
    )


    if enorm < 1e-30:

        Z1 = torch.zeros_like(
            V1
        )

        Z2 = torch.zeros_like(
            V2
        )

        return {
            "eps":
                np.nan,

            "J1":
                Z1,

            "J2":
                Z2,

            "H1":
                Z1.clone(),

            "H2":
                Z2.clone(),
        }


    eps = (
        rel_eps
        *
        vnorm
        /
        enorm
    )


    eps = float(
        np.clip(
            eps,
            1e-6,
            5e-2,
        )
    )


    P1, P2 = phi_W(
        V1
        +
        eps
        *
        e1,

        V2
        +
        eps
        *
        e2,

        env,
        nu,
    )


    M1, M2 = phi_W(
        V1
        -
        eps
        *
        e1,

        V2
        -
        eps
        *
        e2,

        env,
        nu,
    )


    J1 = (
        P1
        -
        M1
    ) / (
        2.0
        *
        eps
    )

    J2 = (
        P2
        -
        M2
    ) / (
        2.0
        *
        eps
    )


    H1 = (
        P1
        +
        M1
        -
        2.0
        *
        base_phi1
    ) / (
        eps ** 2
    )

    H2 = (
        P2
        +
        M2
        -
        2.0
        *
        base_phi2
    ) / (
        eps ** 2
    )


    return {
        "eps":
            eps,

        "J1":
            J1,

        "J2":
            J2,

        "H1":
            H1,

        "H2":
            H2,
    }


# ==================================================================================================
# 6. SCORE ONE ESTIMATE AGAINST TRUTH
#
# This function is scoring ONLY.
# ==================================================================================================

def score_error_W(
    E1,
    E2,
    T1,
    T2,
    target_ids,
):

    (
        EP1,
        EP2,
        EQ1,
        EQ2,
    ) = split_PQ_W(
        E1,
        E2,
        target_ids,
    )


    (
        TP1,
        TP2,
        TQ1,
        TQ2,
    ) = split_PQ_W(
        T1,
        T2,
        target_ids,
    )


    full_true = pair_norm_W(
        T1,
        T2,
    )

    p_true = pair_norm_W(
        TP1,
        TP2,
    )

    q_true = pair_norm_W(
        TQ1,
        TQ2,
    )


    return {
        "full_cos":
            pair_cos_W(
                E1,
                E2,
                T1,
                T2,
            ),

        "full_norm_ratio":
            pair_norm_W(
                E1,
                E2,
            )
            /
            max(
                full_true,
                1e-30,
            ),

        "full_resid":
            pair_norm_W(
                E1 - T1,
                E2 - T2,
            )
            /
            max(
                full_true,
                1e-30,
            ),

        "P_cos":
            pair_cos_W(
                EP1,
                EP2,
                TP1,
                TP2,
            ),

        "P_norm_ratio":
            pair_norm_W(
                EP1,
                EP2,
            )
            /
            max(
                p_true,
                1e-30,
            ),

        "P_resid":
            pair_norm_W(
                EP1 - TP1,
                EP2 - TP2,
            )
            /
            max(
                p_true,
                1e-30,
            ),

        "Q_cos":
            pair_cos_W(
                EQ1,
                EQ2,
                TQ1,
                TQ2,
            ),

        "Q_norm_ratio":
            pair_norm_W(
                EQ1,
                EQ2,
            )
            /
            max(
                q_true,
                1e-30,
            ),

        "Q_resid":
            pair_norm_W(
                EQ1 - TQ1,
                EQ2 - TQ2,
            )
            /
            max(
                q_true,
                1e-30,
            ),
    }
