# ==================================================================================================
# 8. CASE LOOP
# ==================================================================================================

RUN_START_W = time.perf_counter()


for topology_W in TOPOLOGIES_W:

    for nu_mult_W in NU_MULTS_W:

        nu_W = (
            BASE_NU_W
            *
            nu_mult_W
        )

        case_W = (
            f"{topology_W}"
            f"__nu{nu_mult_W:g}"
        )


        if case_W in COMPLETED_W:

            print()
            print(
                "SKIP:",
                case_W,
            )

            continue


        print()
        print("=" * 156)
        print(
            "CASE:",
            case_W,
        )
        print("=" * 156)


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 8A. COLLECT MODEL + TRUTH TRAJECTORIES IN fp32
        #
        # Truth will NOT be used by the estimator.
        # ==========================================================================================

        sync_m3d_globals_MB(
            torch.float32,
            torch.complex64,
            nu_W,
            TAU0_W,
        )


        ENV32_W = make_env_D(
            40,
            L=8.0,
        )


        U10_W, U20_W = topology_pair_L2(
            topology_W,
            ENV32_W,
        )


        truth1_W = U10_W.clone()
        truth2_W = U20_W.clone()


        model_W, init_diag_W = initialize_star2_N(
            U10_W,
            U20_W,
            ENV32_W,
        )


        target_ids_W = model_W[
            "proj"
        ][
            "target_ids"
        ]


        V01_W, V02_W = reconstruct_M(
            model_W,
            ENV32_W,
        )


        MODEL_TRAJ_W = [
            (
                V01_W.clone(),
                V02_W.clone(),
            )
        ]


        TRUTH_TRAJ_W = [
            (
                truth1_W.clone(),
                truth2_W.clone(),
            )
        ]


        print(
            "  initial RAf:",
            f"{init_diag_W['RAf_ratio']:.3e}",
        )


        for seg_W in range(
            1,
            N_SEGMENTS_W + 1,
        ):

            for _ in range(
                SEGMENT_STEPS_W
            ):

                # Truth: scoring trajectory only.
                truth1_W = full_rk4_D(
                    truth1_W,
                    DT_PHYS_W,
                    nu_W,
                    ENV32_W,
                )

                truth2_W = full_rk4_D(
                    truth2_W,
                    DT_PHYS_W,
                    nu_W,
                    ENV32_W,
                )


                # Deployed teacher-free STAR2.
                rk4_M(
                    model_W,
                    DTAU_W,
                    ENV32_W,
                )


            (
                model_W,
                diag_W,
                jump_W,
                build_W,
            ) = reanchor_star2_N(
                model_W,
                ENV32_W,
            )


            V1_W, V2_W = reconstruct_M(
                model_W,
                ENV32_W,
            )


            MODEL_TRAJ_W.append(
                (
                    V1_W.clone(),
                    V2_W.clone(),
                )
            )


            TRUTH_TRAJ_W.append(
                (
                    truth1_W.clone(),
                    truth2_W.clone(),
                )
            )


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 8B. SWITCH TO fp64 FOR THE TRUTH-FREE ESTIMATOR
        # ==========================================================================================

        sync_m3d_globals_MB(
            torch.float64,
            torch.complex128,
            nu_W,
            TAU0_W,
        )


        ENV64_W = make_env_D(
            40,
            L=8.0,
        )


        MODEL64_W = [
            (
                a.to(
                    torch.complex128
                ),

                b.to(
                    torch.complex128
                ),
            )
            for a, b
            in MODEL_TRAJ_W
        ]


        TRUTH64_W = [
            (
                a.to(
                    torch.complex128
                ),

                b.to(
                    torch.complex128
                ),
            )
            for a, b
            in TRUTH_TRAJ_W
        ]


        # ==========================================================================================
        # INITIAL ESTIMATED ERROR
        #
        # Exact initial condition is known:
        #
        #       U0 = V0
        #
        # so:
        #
        #       ehat0 = 0.
        # ==========================================================================================

        EHAT1_1_W = torch.zeros_like(
            MODEL64_W[
                0
            ][
                0
            ]
        )

        EHAT1_2_W = torch.zeros_like(
            MODEL64_W[
                0
            ][
                1
            ]
        )


        EHAT2_1_W = torch.zeros_like(
            EHAT1_1_W
        )

        EHAT2_2_W = torch.zeros_like(
            EHAT1_2_W
        )


        worst_second_W = None


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 8C. TRUTH-FREE RECURRENCE
        # ==========================================================================================

        for k_W in range(
            N_SEGMENTS_W
        ):

            tau_start_W = (
                k_W
                *
                SEGMENT_TAU_W
            )

            tau_end_W = (
                (
                    k_W
                    +
                    1
                )
                *
                SEGMENT_TAU_W
            )


            V1_W, V2_W = MODEL64_W[
                k_W
            ]

            VN1_W, VN2_W = MODEL64_W[
                k_W
                +
                1
            ]


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # TRUTH-FREE STEP 1:
            #
            # Full flow map launched from CURRENT ROM STATE.
            #
            # This is not truth.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            PHI1_W, PHI2_W = phi_W(
                V1_W,
                V2_W,
                ENV64_W,
                nu_W,
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # TRUTH-FREE LOCAL DEFECT
            #
            # eta = Phi(V_k) - V_{k+1}
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            ETA1_W = (
                PHI1_W
                -
                VN1_W
            )

            ETA2_W = (
                PHI2_W
                -
                VN2_W
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # FIRST-ORDER accumulated estimator
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            if (
                pair_norm_W(
                    EHAT1_1_W,
                    EHAT1_2_W,
                )
                <
                1e-30
            ):

                NEW_EHAT1_1_W = ETA1_W.clone()
                NEW_EHAT1_2_W = ETA2_W.clone()

                jet1_W = None

            else:

                jet1_W = map_jet_W(
                    V1_W,
                    V2_W,

                    EHAT1_1_W,
                    EHAT1_2_W,

                    PHI1_W,
                    PHI2_W,

                    ENV64_W,
                    nu_W,
                )


                NEW_EHAT1_1_W = (
                    ETA1_W
                    +
                    jet1_W[
                        "J1"
                    ]
                )

                NEW_EHAT1_2_W = (
                    ETA2_W
                    +
                    jet1_W[
                        "J2"
                    ]
                )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # SECOND-ORDER accumulated estimator
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            if (
                pair_norm_W(
                    EHAT2_1_W,
                    EHAT2_2_W,
                )
                <
                1e-30
            ):

                NEW_EHAT2_1_W = ETA1_W.clone()
                NEW_EHAT2_2_W = ETA2_W.clone()

                jet2_W = None

            else:

                jet2_W = map_jet_W(
                    V1_W,
                    V2_W,

                    EHAT2_1_W,
                    EHAT2_2_W,

                    PHI1_W,
                    PHI2_W,

                    ENV64_W,
                    nu_W,
                )


                NEW_EHAT2_1_W = (
                    ETA1_W
                    +
                    jet2_W[
                        "J1"
                    ]
                    +
                    0.5
                    *
                    jet2_W[
                        "H1"
                    ]
                )

                NEW_EHAT2_2_W = (
                    ETA2_W
                    +
                    jet2_W[
                        "J2"
                    ]
                    +
                    0.5
                    *
                    jet2_W[
                        "H2"
                    ]
                )


            # ======================================================================================
            # EVERYTHING BELOW THIS LINE IS SCORING ONLY.
            #
            # Actual truth is first touched here.
            # ======================================================================================

            U1_NEXT_W, U2_NEXT_W = TRUTH64_W[
                k_W
                +
                1
            ]


            ACTUAL1_W = (
                U1_NEXT_W
                -
                VN1_W
            )

            ACTUAL2_W = (
                U2_NEXT_W
                -
                VN2_W
            )


            score1_W = score_error_W(
                NEW_EHAT1_1_W,
                NEW_EHAT1_2_W,

                ACTUAL1_W,
                ACTUAL2_W,

                target_ids_W,
            )


            score2_W = score_error_W(
                NEW_EHAT2_1_W,
                NEW_EHAT2_2_W,

                ACTUAL1_W,
                ACTUAL2_W,

                target_ids_W,
            )


            eta_norm_W = pair_norm_W(
                ETA1_W,
                ETA2_W,
            )


            actual_norm_W = pair_norm_W(
                ACTUAL1_W,
                ACTUAL2_W,
            )


            ROWS_W.append(
                {
                    "case":
                        case_W,

                    "topology":
                        topology_W,

                    "nu_mult":
                        nu_mult_W,

                    "tau":
                        tau_end_W,

                    "local_defect_norm":
                        eta_norm_W,

                    "actual_error_norm":
                        actual_norm_W,

                    "defect_over_actual":
                        eta_norm_W
                        /
                        max(
                            actual_norm_W,
                            1e-30,
                        ),

                    # FIRST ORDER
                    "first_full_cos":
                        score1_W[
                            "full_cos"
                        ],

                    "first_full_norm_ratio":
                        score1_W[
                            "full_norm_ratio"
                        ],

                    "first_full_resid":
                        score1_W[
                            "full_resid"
                        ],

                    "first_P_cos":
                        score1_W[
                            "P_cos"
                        ],

                    "first_P_norm_ratio":
                        score1_W[
                            "P_norm_ratio"
                        ],

                    "first_P_resid":
                        score1_W[
                            "P_resid"
                        ],

                    "first_Q_cos":
                        score1_W[
                            "Q_cos"
                        ],

                    "first_Q_norm_ratio":
                        score1_W[
                            "Q_norm_ratio"
                        ],

                    "first_Q_resid":
                        score1_W[
                            "Q_resid"
                        ],

                    # SECOND ORDER
                    "second_full_cos":
                        score2_W[
                            "full_cos"
                        ],

                    "second_full_norm_ratio":
                        score2_W[
                            "full_norm_ratio"
                        ],

                    "second_full_resid":
                        score2_W[
                            "full_resid"
                        ],

                    "second_P_cos":
                        score2_W[
                            "P_cos"
                        ],

                    "second_P_norm_ratio":
                        score2_W[
                            "P_norm_ratio"
                        ],

                    "second_P_resid":
                        score2_W[
                            "P_resid"
                        ],

                    "second_Q_cos":
                        score2_W[
                            "Q_cos"
                        ],

                    "second_Q_norm_ratio":
                        score2_W[
                            "Q_norm_ratio"
                        ],

                    "second_Q_resid":
                        score2_W[
                            "Q_resid"
                        ],
                }
            )


            print(
                f"  tau={tau_end_W:4.2f}"
                f" | Q r1={score1_W['Q_resid']:7.4f}"
                f" | Q r2={score2_W['Q_resid']:7.4f}"
                f" | Q cos2={score2_W['Q_cos']:.5f}"
                f" | P r2={score2_W['P_resid']:7.4f}"
            )


            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            # Track worst second-order Q score for derivative refinement.
            # Truth is used ONLY to select a diagnostic checkpoint.
            # It never changes the estimator.
            # --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            if (
                jet2_W is not None
                and
                (
                    worst_second_W is None
                    or
                    score2_W[
                        "Q_resid"
                    ]
                    >
                    worst_second_W[
                        "Q_resid"
                    ]
                )
            ):

                worst_second_W = {
                    "tau_start":
                        tau_start_W,

                    "Q_resid":
                        score2_W[
                            "Q_resid"
                        ],

                    "V1":
                        V1_W.clone(),

                    "V2":
                        V2_W.clone(),

                    "e1":
                        EHAT2_1_W.clone(),

                    "e2":
                        EHAT2_2_W.clone(),

                    "phi1":
                        PHI1_W.clone(),

                    "phi2":
                        PHI2_W.clone(),

                    "eps":
                        jet2_W[
                            "eps"
                        ],

                    "J1":
                        jet2_W[
                            "J1"
                        ].clone(),

                    "J2":
                        jet2_W[
                            "J2"
                        ].clone(),

                    "H1":
                        jet2_W[
                            "H1"
                        ].clone(),

                    "H2":
                        jet2_W[
                            "H2"
                        ].clone(),
                }


            # Update truth-free estimators.
            EHAT1_1_W = NEW_EHAT1_1_W
            EHAT1_2_W = NEW_EHAT1_2_W

            EHAT2_1_W = NEW_EHAT2_1_W
            EHAT2_2_W = NEW_EHAT2_2_W


            gc.collect()
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()


        # ==========================================================================================
        # 8D. DERIVATIVE REFINEMENT AT WORST SECOND-ORDER-Q CHECKPOINT
        # ==========================================================================================

        assert worst_second_W is not None


        halfjet_W = map_jet_W(
            worst_second_W[
                "V1"
            ],

            worst_second_W[
                "V2"
            ],

            worst_second_W[
                "e1"
            ],

            worst_second_W[
                "e2"
            ],

            worst_second_W[
                "phi1"
            ],

            worst_second_W[
                "phi2"
            ],

            ENV64_W,
            nu_W,

            rel_eps=(
                DERIV_REL_W
                /
                2.0
            ),
        )


        J_cos_W = pair_cos_W(
            worst_second_W[
                "J1"
            ],

            worst_second_W[
                "J2"
            ],

            halfjet_W[
                "J1"
            ],

            halfjet_W[
                "J2"
            ],
        )


        J_diff_W = (
            pair_norm_W(
                worst_second_W[
                    "J1"
                ]
                -
                halfjet_W[
                    "J1"
                ],

                worst_second_W[
                    "J2"
                ]
                -
                halfjet_W[
                    "J2"
                ],
            )
            /
            max(
                pair_norm_W(
                    halfjet_W[
                        "J1"
                    ],

                    halfjet_W[
                        "J2"
                    ],
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        H_cos_W = pair_cos_W(
            worst_second_W[
                "H1"
            ],

            worst_second_W[
                "H2"
            ],

            halfjet_W[
                "H1"
            ],

            halfjet_W[
                "H2"
            ],
        )


        H_diff_W = (
            pair_norm_W(
                worst_second_W[
                    "H1"
                ]
                -
                halfjet_W[
                    "H1"
                ],

                worst_second_W[
                    "H2"
                ]
                -
                halfjet_W[
                    "H2"
                ],
            )
            /
            max(
                pair_norm_W(
                    halfjet_W[
                        "H1"
                    ],

                    halfjet_W[
                        "H2"
                    ],
                ),
                1e-30,
            )
        )


        CHECK_ROWS_W.append(
            {
                "case":
                    case_W,

                "topology":
                    topology_W,

                "nu_mult":
                    nu_mult_W,

                "check_tau_start":
                    worst_second_W[
                        "tau_start"
                    ],

                "worst_second_Q_residual":
                    worst_second_W[
                        "Q_resid"
                    ],

                "J_eps_half_cosine":
                    J_cos_W,

                "J_eps_half_difference":
                    J_diff_W,

                "H_eps_half_cosine":
                    H_cos_W,

                "H_eps_half_difference":
                    H_diff_W,
            }
        )


        print()

        print(
            "  worst-Q derivative check @ tau",
            worst_second_W[
                "tau_start"
            ],
        )

        print(
            "    J cosine:",
            f"{J_cos_W:.10f}",
        )

        print(
            "    J difference:",
            f"{J_diff_W:.3e}",
        )

        print(
            "    H cosine:",
            f"{H_cos_W:.10f}",
        )

        print(
            "    H difference:",
            f"{H_diff_W:.3e}",
        )


        # ==========================================================================================
        # CHECKPOINT
        # ==========================================================================================

        pd.DataFrame(
            ROWS_W
        ).to_csv(
            POINT_FILE_W,
            index=False,
        )


        pd.DataFrame(
            CHECK_ROWS_W
        ).to_csv(
            CHECK_FILE_W,
            index=False,
        )


        COMPLETED_W.add(
            case_W
        )


        del MODEL_TRAJ_W
        del TRUTH_TRAJ_W
        del MODEL64_W
        del TRUTH64_W
        del model_W

        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()


# ==================================================================================================
# 9. FINAL TABLES
# ==================================================================================================

RESULTS_W = pd.DataFrame(
    ROWS_W
)

CHECKS_W = pd.DataFrame(
    CHECK_ROWS_W
)


if RESULTS_W[
    "case"
].nunique() != 12:

    raise RuntimeError(
        "Test W incomplete: "
        f"{RESULTS_W['case'].nunique()}/12 cases."
    )


# Match T/V's mature interval.
MATURE_W = RESULTS_W[
    (
        RESULTS_W[
            "tau"
        ]
        >=
        0.30
        -
        1e-12
    )
    &
    (
        RESULTS_W[
            "tau"
        ]
        <=
        1.80
        +
        1e-12
    )
].copy()


RESULTS_W.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_W_truthfree_error_estimates.csv",
    index=False,
)


CHECKS_W.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_W_derivative_checks.csv",
    index=False,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 10. CASE SUMMARY
# ==================================================================================================

CASE_SUMMARY_W = (
    MATURE_W
    .groupby(
        [
            "case",
            "topology",
            "nu_mult",
        ]
    )
    .agg(
        points=(
            "tau",
            "count",
        ),

        median_first_Q_resid=(
            "first_Q_resid",
            "median",
        ),

        median_second_Q_resid=(
            "second_Q_resid",
            "median",
        ),

        median_second_Q_cos=(
            "second_Q_cos",
            "median",
        ),

        median_second_Q_norm=(
            "second_Q_norm_ratio",
            "median",
        ),

        median_first_P_resid=(
            "first_P_resid",
            "median",
        ),

        median_second_P_resid=(
            "second_P_resid",
            "median",
        ),

        median_second_P_cos=(
            "second_P_cos",
            "median",
        ),
    )
    .reset_index()
)


CASE_SUMMARY_W.to_csv(
    SAVE_DIR
    /
    "m3d_W_case_summary.csv",
    index=False,
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 11. GLOBAL METRICS
# ==================================================================================================

MEDIAN_FIRST_Q_RESID_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "first_Q_resid"
    ].median()
)


MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_RESID_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "second_Q_resid"
    ].median()
)


P90_SECOND_Q_RESID_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "second_Q_resid"
    ].quantile(
        0.90
    )
)


MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_COS_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "second_Q_cos"
    ].median()
)


P10_SECOND_Q_COS_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "second_Q_cos"
    ].quantile(
        0.10
    )
)


MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_NORM_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "second_Q_norm_ratio"
    ].median()
)


MEDIAN_FIRST_P_RESID_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "first_P_resid"
    ].median()
)


MEDIAN_SECOND_P_RESID_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "second_P_resid"
    ].median()
)


P90_SECOND_P_RESID_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "second_P_resid"
    ].quantile(
        0.90
    )
)


MEDIAN_SECOND_P_COS_W = float(
    MATURE_W[
        "second_P_cos"
    ].median()
)


SECOND_Q_IMPROVEMENT_RATE_W = float(
    (
        MATURE_W[
            "second_Q_resid"
        ]
        <
        MATURE_W[
            "first_Q_resid"
        ]
    ).mean()
)


Q_MEDIAN_GAIN_W = (
    MEDIAN_FIRST_Q_RESID_W
    /
    max(
        MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_RESID_W,
        1e-30,
    )
)


MAX_CASE_MEDIAN_Q_RESID_W = float(
    CASE_SUMMARY_W[
        "median_second_Q_resid"
    ].max()
)


MIN_CASE_MEDIAN_Q_COS_W = float(
    CASE_SUMMARY_W[
        "median_second_Q_cos"
    ].min()
)


MIN_J_COS_W = float(
    CHECKS_W[
        "J_eps_half_cosine"
    ].min()
)


MAX_J_DIFF_W = float(
    CHECKS_W[
        "J_eps_half_difference"
    ].max()
)


MIN_H_COS_W = float(
    CHECKS_W[
        "H_eps_half_cosine"
    ].min()
)


MAX_H_DIFF_W = float(
    CHECKS_W[
        "H_eps_half_difference"
    ].max()
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 12. GATES
#
# Frozen before Test-W output.
# ==================================================================================================

NUMERICS_GATE_W = bool(
    MIN_J_COS_W
    >=
    0.999
    and
    MAX_J_DIFF_W
    <=
    0.02
    and
    MIN_H_COS_W
    >=
    0.99
    and
    MAX_H_DIFF_W
    <=
    0.10
)


SECOND_ORDER_VALUE_GATE_W = bool(
    Q_MEDIAN_GAIN_W
    >=
    2.0
    and
    SECOND_Q_IMPROVEMENT_RATE_W
    >=
    0.80
)


HIDDEN_DIRECTION_GATE_W = bool(
    MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_COS_W
    >=
    0.90
    and
    P10_SECOND_Q_COS_W
    >=
    0.70
    and
    MIN_CASE_MEDIAN_Q_COS_W
    >=
    0.75
)


HIDDEN_AMPLITUDE_GATE_W = bool(
    0.70
    <=
    MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_NORM_W
    <=
    1.30
    and
    MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_RESID_W
    <=
    0.35
    and
    P90_SECOND_Q_RESID_W
    <=
    0.75
    and
    MAX_CASE_MEDIAN_Q_RESID_W
    <=
    0.60
)


OBSERVABLE_ESTIMATE_GATE_W = bool(
    MEDIAN_SECOND_P_RESID_W
    <=
    0.35
    and
    P90_SECOND_P_RESID_W
    <=
    0.75
    and
    MEDIAN_SECOND_P_COS_W
    >=
    0.90
)


TRUTH_FREE_HIDDEN_ESTIMATOR_W = bool(
    NUMERICS_GATE_W
    and
    SECOND_ORDER_VALUE_GATE_W
    and
    HIDDEN_DIRECTION_GATE_W
    and
    HIDDEN_AMPLITUDE_GATE_W
    and
    OBSERVABLE_ESTIMATE_GATE_W
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 13. PRINT CASE SUMMARY
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 156)
print("TEST W — CASE SUMMARY")
print("=" * 156)
print()

print(
    CASE_SUMMARY_W.to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
        f"{x:.7g}",
    )
)


# ==================================================================================================
# 14. FINAL DIAGNOSIS
# ==================================================================================================

print()
print("=" * 156)
print("M3D TEST W — FINAL DIAGNOSIS")
print("=" * 156)
print()

print(
    "cases:",
    MATURE_W[
        "case"
    ].nunique(),
)

print(
    "mature scored anchors:",
    len(
        MATURE_W
    ),
)

print()

print(
    "median FIRST-order hidden-Q residual:",
    f"{MEDIAN_FIRST_Q_RESID_W:.8f}",
)

print(
    "median SECOND-order hidden-Q residual:",
    f"{MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_RESID_W:.8f}",
)

print(
    "median Q-estimator improvement:",
    f"{Q_MEDIAN_GAIN_W:.6f}x",
)

print(
    "fraction Q anchors improved:",
    f"{100*SECOND_Q_IMPROVEMENT_RATE_W:.3f}%",
)

print()

print(
    "median SECOND-order hidden-Q cosine:",
    f"{MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_COS_W:.8f}",
)

print(
    "10th percentile hidden-Q cosine:",
    f"{P10_SECOND_Q_COS_W:.8f}",
)

print(
    "median hidden-Q norm ratio:",
    f"{MEDIAN_SECOND_Q_NORM_W:.8f}",
)

print(
    "90th percentile hidden-Q residual:",
    f"{P90_SECOND_Q_RESID_W:.8f}",
)

print(
    "worst case median hidden-Q residual:",
    f"{MAX_CASE_MEDIAN_Q_RESID_W:.8f}",
)

print()

print(
    "median FIRST-order P residual:",

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 25
# ==========================================================================
def sync_m3d_globals_MB(real_dtype, complex_dtype, nu, tau0):
    globals()["REAL_DTYPE_D"] = real_dtype
    globals()["COMPLEX_DTYPE_D"] = complex_dtype

    # ACTUAL baseline viscosity globals
    globals()["NU1_D"] = float(nu)
    globals()["NU2_D"] = float(nu)

    # Keep lowercase aliases too, just in case
    globals()["nu1_D"] = float(nu)
    globals()["nu2_D"] = float(nu)

    globals()["TAU0_D"] = float(tau0)

    for gd in NAMESPACES_MB:
        gd["REAL_DTYPE_D"] = real_dtype
        gd["COMPLEX_DTYPE_D"] = complex_dtype

        gd["NU1_D"] = float(nu)
        gd["NU2_D"] = float(nu)

        gd["nu1_D"] = float(nu)
        gd["nu2_D"] = float(nu)

        gd["TAU0_D"] = float(tau0)

print("viscosity sync patched")

# ---- OUTPUT --------------------------------------------------------------
# viscosity sync patched
# 

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 26
# ==========================================================================
sync_m3d_globals_MB(
    torch.float32,
    torch.complex64,
    0.000625,   # 0.25x
    40/3
)

print("NU1_D =", NU1_D)
print("NU2_D =", NU2_D)

assert abs(NU1_D - 0.000625) < 1e-15
assert abs(NU2_D - 0.000625) < 1e-15

print("✅ uppercase viscosity globals update correctly")

# ---- OUTPUT --------------------------------------------------------------
# NU1_D = 0.000625
# NU2_D = 0.000625
# ✅ uppercase viscosity globals update correctly
# 

# ==========================================================================
# CELL 27
# ==========================================================================
import pandas as pd
from pathlib import Path

SAVE_DIR = Path("/kaggle/working/M3D")

POINT_FILE_W = SAVE_DIR / "m3d_W_points_partial.csv"
CHECK_FILE_W = SAVE_DIR / "m3d_W_checks_partial.csv"

points = pd.read_csv(POINT_FILE_W)
checks = pd.read_csv(CHECK_FILE_W)

print("Before:")
print("points cases =", points["case"].nunique())
print("checks cases =", checks["case"].nunique())

# KEEP ONLY nu=1 cases
points = points[
    points["nu_mult"].astype(float).round(8) == 1.0
].copy()

checks = checks[
    checks["nu_mult"].astype(float).round(8) == 1.0
].copy()

points.to_csv(
    POINT_FILE_W,
    index=False
)

checks.to_csv(
    CHECK_FILE_W,
    index=False
)

print("\nAfter:")
print("points cases =", points["case"].nunique())
print("checks cases =", checks["case"].nunique())
print("kept:", sorted(checks["case"].tolist()))

# ---- OUTPUT --------------------------------------------------------------
# Before:
# points cases = 12
# checks cases = 12
# 
# After:
# points cases = 4
# checks cases = 4
# kept: ['mixed_vortices__nu1', 'periodic_shear__nu1', 'skew_tubes__nu1', 'vortex_ring__nu1']
#
